- new-tab / guide pages (preload-view.js; tab → main): invoke consume-startup-splash, get-uptime, get-app-version, mitm-ready-state, get-current-proxy, check-ip-geo, get-direct-ip, open-proxy-manager, get-proxy-profiles, connect-proxy-template, get-ui-pref, set-ui-pref, isolate-tab, set-tab-cookie-group, set-tab-proxy, get-cookie-groups, create-cookie-group, get-tabs, get-cookies, clear-cookies, open-cookie-manager, ext-proxy:list, ext-proxy:start, ext-proxy:stop, ext-proxy:set-port, ext-proxy:set-redirects; send navigate-to, ui-first-paint, ui-long-task-count, retry-failed-navigation, http-lab-ui-state, report-mouse-activity, report-tab-pointer/click/typing-end/scroll-end, credential-form-captured, browser-storage-activity; sync get-anti-anti-debug-sync, get-device-permissions-sync, get-paste-unlock-sync
- main → tab pages: tab-list-updated, proxy-status-changed (per-tab augmented), mitm-ready-changed, cookie-groups-updated, camera-filter-update, paste-unlock-update

**Data stores:**

- settings.json (settings-store; per-profile dir when a profile is active): toolbarToolsPlacement ('subbar' default | inline | bottom | separate), toolbarToolsMiniAlign ('right'|'left'), searchEngine ('duckduckgo'|'google'|'brave'|'yandex'|'custom'), searchEngineCustomUrl, corsBypassEnabled, onboardingComplete (forced false when there is no settings.json), homepage (getNewTabUrl), maxTabsBeforeWarning, pasteUnlock, effectiveTrafficMode
- ui-prefs.json (main-process/services/ui-prefs-store.js; userData/ui-prefs.json or userData/profiles/<vaultId>/ui-prefs.json): 'cupnet-quick-links-v2' (new-tab quick links [{icon,label,type:'url'|'proxy',url|proxyId}]), 'lastLaunchProfilePath' (Launch Profile modal), plus 'uiTheme', 'settingsUiScale', 'settingsSidebarCollapsed' (settings)
- localStorage in the tab partition for the file:// origin: 'cupnet-engine' (new-tab engine), 'cupnet-quick-links-v2' (legacy mirror/migration source), 'guide-lang' (guide language)
- SQLite (db.js): omnibox_history (url UNIQUE, host, title, visit_count, typed_count, last_visit_at, last_cookie_group_id) + history_visits (per visit: transition typed|link|reload|in_page, tab_id, cookie_group_id, session_id) + omnibox_shortcuts (typed text → url, hits). API: recordOmniboxVisit (strips #fragment, rejects placeholder titles, 1 s in_page dedupe), updateOmniboxTitle, getOmniboxCandidates/Recent/TopSites, getOmniboxSuggestions (compat, ranked), record/getOmniboxShortcut, listHistoryVisits/countHistoryVisits, deleteHistoryUrl/Visit, clearHistory({sinceMs}), purgeHistoryOlderThan(days), getHistoryStats. Ranking math lives in omnibox-model.js (frecency `(visits+3·typed)·e^(−age/14d)` × match boost). requests queried by buildTabTlsInfo (queryRequests by tabId) for the site-info 'Last logged request'; proxy profiles (getProxyProfiles for the new-tab scope badge and quick-link proxy picker); cookie groups (partitions persist:cg_<id>, 1 = shared)
- userData/ext-ports.json (ext-ports-store): external proxy ports {port, name, login, password, autoStart, followRedirects}. Written with plain fs.writeFileSync (plaintext password, not secure-fs). Default port 7777 is seeded on first run
- Session partitions: persist:cupnet_omnibox_overlay (overlay view, proxy direct), direct-ip-check (get-direct-ip hidden window), persist:cg_<cookieGroupId> (tabs)
- CSS custom properties set from JS on :root: --shell-chrome-top, --shell-status-height, --shell-tools-bar-height, --shell-tools-side-width, --shell-resources-tools-bottom
- Env vars affecting this subsystem: CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL (>=6 drops preload-view.js from tabs), CUPNET_E2E=1 / CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE=1 (skip profile gate; E2E also skips the exit dialog), CUPNET_DEVTOOLS=1 (auto-open DevTools for Proxy Manager), CUPNET_RENDERER_CONSOLE_VERBOSE=1 (forward renderer console), CUPNET_SKIP_E2E / SKIP_E2E (test runner)

**Conventions:**

- All user-facing strings must be English. The only exceptions are the guide translations (fr/es/ru) and BACK_LABELS in cupnet-guide.html. Russian text appears only in code comments
- Renderer never touches Node: the shell uses window.electronAPI from preload.js (the `sub(channel, cb)` helper returns an unsubscribe). Tab pages use preload-view.js (its on* helpers call removeAllListeners and allow one listener per channel). Small windows use dedicated minimal preloads (omniboxOverlay, siteInfoPopoverAPI, sessionProfileAPI)
- Call optional APIs defensively as `api.x?.()`. New IPC for the shell goes in preload.js AND a handler in main-process/ipc/handlers/*.js. Guard sensitive handlers with guardIpcHandler/assertTrustedShell from main-process/ipc/ipc-trusted-sender.js, and register new trusted windows through sub-windows._spawnTrustedToolWindow
- Anything that must visually overlap web content cannot be plain shell DOM, because the tab WebContentsView paints above browser.html. Use one of: a separate WebContentsView (omnibox overlay), a separate BrowserWindow (site-info popover, logging modal), detaching the tab view (setWindowSwitcherOverlayVisible), or reserving chrome space (set-toolbar-height / set-bottom-chrome-height / set-right-chrome-width)
- Shell dimension constants must stay in sync: #tab-bar 35px + #browser-toolbar 60px = CHROME_TOP_BASE_PX 95 = tab-manager TOOLBAR_HEIGHT 95; .browser-status-bar 26px = STATUS_BAR_HEIGHT 26; TOOLS_MINI_BAR_HEIGHT_PX 34 and TOOLS_SIDE_WIDTH_PX 58 = CSS var defaults; RESOURCES_DRAWER_HEIGHT_PX 260 and PROFILE_DRAWER_HEIGHT_PX 240 = drawer max-height; CRED_SAVE_BAR_RESERVE_PX 36 approximates the save-bar height
- Colours come from shared-dark-theme.css tokens (var(--bg1) etc.). Shell overrides in styles.css are scoped with body:has(#browser-toolbar), and the shell forces color-scheme: dark
- Escape dynamic text: textContent where possible, otherwise escHtml/escapeAttr/escapeHtmlCred (renderer), esc() (overlay/popover) or escHtml (new-tab) before innerHTML
- Guide content: never edit the LANGS block in cupnet-guide.html by hand. Edit scripts/guide-i18n/*.mjs (keep all 4 languages at parity) and run `npm run build:guide`
- Internal pages are addressed by aliases: cupnet://settings, cupnet://guide, cupnet://home | cupnet://new-tab (see tabs-ipc navigate-to and cupnet-runtime.getNewTabUrl). tab-manager.displayUrl maps file:// internal pages back to '' / cupnet://settings / cupnet://guide for the address bar
- Traffic rule: the UI must not add session.protocol.handle interception; network actions go through main/MITM. Keep outbound fetches, such as IP checks, in main via AzureTLS or Node, not in renderers

**Gotchas:**

- The tab WebContentsView is a native layer ABOVE browser.html. Shell DOM is only visible in the top 95px (plus extraTop), the bottom 26px (plus extraBottom) and the right strip when placement=separate. Absolutely positioned popups and toasts in the shell are hidden behind the page unless space is reserved or the view is detached
- tab-list-updated is broadcast to the shell AND every tab webContents on every title/favicon/navigation change of ANY tab (tab-manager.js:489-533, _emitTabListUpdated). Shell handlers must be idempotent and cheap
- preload-view.js is loaded for every tab page, including third-party sites. Anything added there is callable by any website, and many of its main handlers are unguarded. Setting CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL>=6 removes the preload entirely, so new-tab/guide/settings lose electronAPI: new-tab falls back to window.location for search and the guide's Back button does nothing
- The uncommitted WIP removed the Settings APIs from preload-view.js, but settings.html still opens as a TAB (open-settings-tab), so it now uses preload-view.js
- _toolbarLoading, tabs, ssCountdownEl and the _mitm* state are declared with let/const mid-file but read in callbacks. This is safe only because IPC callbacks fire after the script finishes. Do not call these functions synchronously earlier in the file (TDZ)
- The shell's document keydown listeners run in the capture phase: omnibox-controller `onKeydown` (only while the field is focused or the list is open; it stopPropagation()s the keys it consumes) and browser-renderer's Esc/Stop + Cmd+, handler (Esc-to-stop only when the omnibox is closed and unfocused)
- The omnibox overlay uses its own session partition persist:cupnet_omnibox_overlay with proxy direct: file:// on the MITM-proxied defaultSession can fail with ERR_FAILED. The view is created once and only detached on close/switchTab/window switcher (`_omniboxWanted` + `_omniboxShowPromise` guard the async create against a hide in flight, A-380); it is destroyed with the window
- setWindowSwitcherOverlayVisible is REF-COUNTED (_shellOverlayHideRefCount). Every true must be matched by exactly one false, or the tab view stays detached (blank content) and switchTab will not re-attach views
- Toolbar placement: the ONE .tool-dock element is physically moved between #tool-dock-mount-inline/subbar/bottom/separate. Code must not cache its parent. The settings default is 'subbar' (settings-store) while applyToolbarToolsPlacement falls back to 'inline' for unknown values
- get-app-metrics mixes units: Chromium rows are KB, CupNet child-process rows are bytes (see suspectedBugs)
- cupnet-guide.html is a generated artifact for the LANGS block, and build-new-tab-guide.mjs writes into the repo file in place. The e2e omni-2 test asserts NO inline autocomplete, yet the guide text still describes inline autocomplete and Tab-accept
- new-tab.html and the guide keep preferences in localStorage of the file:// origin inside the tab's session partition ('cupnet-engine', legacy 'cupnet-quick-links-v2', 'guide-lang'). Isolated tabs (persist:cg_<id>) get separate storage. Only quick links are mirrored to ui-prefs.json
- consume-startup-splash is one-shot per app run, and whichever tab page calls it first wins
- ui-prefs.json is per active profile (userData/profiles/<vaultId>/ui-prefs.json once a profile is active), so quick links and lastLaunchProfilePath change when switching profiles
- styles.css starts with legacy light-theme globals: body background #f0f2f5, padding 20px, -webkit-app-region: drag; button colour white; input width 100%. Only body:has(#browser-toolbar) resets padding. New shell elements inherit these unless overridden
- The legacy electron-app/app.js duplicates many handlers (e.g. navigate-to). Do not edit it when changing browser-ui behaviour; the live code is in main-process/

**Tests:**

- Unit (vitest, node env, tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs): `npm run test:unit`. Relevant files: keyboard-shortcuts.test.mjs (only checks list shape), omnibox-input.test.mjs (classifier table: localhost/host:port/IPv6/intranet/word.tld/search/aliases), omnibox-model.test.mjs (ranking, default match, completion, row composition, zero-suggest, command matching), omnibox-history.test.mjs (db omnibox_history/frecency; skipped if better-sqlite3 cannot load under Node), session-profile.test.mjs, utils.test.mjs (URL resolution/search engines incl. dev hosts), ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs, tab-error-page.test.mjs. DB history functions are covered by tests/test-db.js (Electron ABI). browser-renderer.js, omnibox-controller.js (DOM), new-tab.html and the modal renderer have NO unit tests
- E2E (Playwright + Electron): `npm run test:e2e` or `node scripts/playwright-e2e.mjs tests/e2e/omnibox.e2e.spec.js`. helpers.launchCupnet sets CUPNET_E2E=1, which skips the profile gate and exit dialog. Skip everything with CUPNET_SKIP_E2E=1 or SKIP_E2E. Needs network (httpbin.org)
- tests/e2e/omnibox.e2e.spec.js: omni-1 history suggestions API, omni-2 host-prefix inline completion + Tab accept (reads `window.cupnetOmniboxDebug.getState()`), omni-2b draft survives tab-list churn, omni-2c localhost:3000 → http row, omni-3 Cmd/Ctrl+K puts '>', omni-4 mod+Enter opens a new tab, omni-5 site info popover opens, omni-6 search engine setting persists, omni-7 switch-to-tab row, omni-8 Shift+Delete removes a suggestion, omni-9 History window lists visits + search, omni-10 recently closed → historyReopen restores the tab
- tests/e2e/onboarding.e2e.spec.js (onb1: fresh userData shows Welcome; completeOnboarding closes it); windows.e2e.spec.js (w1-w12: open/close tool windows); cupnet.e2e.spec.js (1-7: startup, navigation, logging, cookies, isolated tab, new tab, stop logging); p2-features.e2e.spec.js (p2-shortcuts openSettingsTab, p2-tab-proxy, p2-ext-proxy)
- Smoke: `npm run test:e2e:smoke`. Full: `npm test` (tests/run-all.sh)
- Guide sync check: none automated. It can be checked read-only by importing scripts/guide-i18n/*.mjs and comparing against the LANGS block (done for this audit: in sync)
- Missing coverage: overlay/popup visibility over the tab view, preload-view exposure versus trusted sender, status-bar metrics units, cupnet://newtab alias, typed-text preservation during tab-list updates, the window-switcher ref-count

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Electron runtime (`package.json devDependencies`) — now: electron ^34.0.0 (installed 34.5.8, Chromium 132); Electron 34 is long out of support. Upgrade to the currently supported stable major and retest WebContentsView layering, the console-message signature, sandbox defaults and CSS :has() behaviour
- console-message positional event args (`main-process/cupnet-runtime.js attachRendererConsoleForward (~1386)`) — now: webContents.on('console-message', (event, level, message, line, sourceId)); Deprecated in newer Electron (a single event object with level/message/lineNumber/sourceId). Fix this during the Electron bump
- electron-builder (`package.json`) — now: ^24.13.0; Old major. Also, package version '27.06.2026-5' is not semver, but it is displayed on new-tab/splash via getAppVersion and used for the artifact names
- TLS template version list in guide (`scripts/guide-i18n/{en,fr,es,ru}.mjs line ~112 (and proxy-manager.html tls-tpl-btn titles)`) — now: Chrome 133, Firefox 138, Safari 18, iOS 18, Edge 133, Opera 119; Update to the profiles the Go AzureTLS worker actually emits. Avoid hardcoded browser versions in docs, or generate them
- TLS profile placeholder (`session-profile-modal.html #pf-proxy-tls`) — now: placeholder="chrome_120"; Not a valid id at all; valid template ids are chrome/firefox/safari/ios/edge/opera
- Guide product version / copyright (`scripts/guide-i18n/*.mjs hero pill and footer`) — now: 'v2.0', '© CupNet 2.0'; Does not match the package version
- Guide feature descriptions (`scripts/guide-i18n/*.mjs (toolbar, isolated, settings sections)`) — now: the omnibox + history sections were rewritten 2026-09-25 (4 languages); still stale: '+🔒' isolated-tab button, mode badge always hidden, Settings opens as a tab with different sections. Also missing: CORS, HTTP Lab, Notes, status bar, profile drawer, window switcher
- Keyboard shortcut reference (`keyboard-shortcuts.js`) — now: logViewer 'Ctrl+L', credentials 'CmdOrCtrl+Shift+L', proxyManager 'CmdOrCtrl+Shift+P'; Real menu (main-window.js buildMenu): Ctrl+L = address bar, Ctrl+Shift+L = Network Activity, Ctrl+Alt+L = Credentials, Ctrl+P = Proxy Manager
- New-tab hotkeys cheat sheet (`new-tab.html #hs-body (1673-1689)`) — now: 15 static entries; Missing Ctrl/Cmd+K, Ctrl+`, Ctrl+Alt+L, Ctrl+Shift+H/N/M/A, Cmd+,. It is not generated from a single source
- New-tab search engines — fixed 2026-09-25 (A-338): new-tab.html reads Settings via preload-view getSearchEngine and persists the per-click choice with setUiPref('cupnet-newtab-engine'); list matches utils.SEARCH_ENGINES
- Legacy/dead CSS and DOM (`styles.css (proxy-selector/settings/log-viewer/screenshot/websocket blocks, .action-btn, .toolbar-right-actions, .command-palette); browser-renderer.js screenshot countdown (718-765, #screenshot-btn/#screenshot-countdown/#screenshot-flash do not exist), inline #url-omnibox-dropdown list, URL_INPUT_PLACEHOLDER_OMNIBOX, _renderModeBadge, _lastPillTabSig`) — now: present; Roughly half of styles.css is unused since only browser.html loads it
- Default ext-proxy log message (`main-process/cupnet-runtime.js ~1376`) — now: logs 'Created default external port 9001' but seeds port 7777; cosmetic

**Production gaps:**

- Security boundary: remove the privileged electronAPI from preload-view.js for non-internal origins. Expose a minimal bridge only when location is a known internal file:// page, and ALSO validate event.senderFrame.url main-side for new-tab channels (get-cookies, clear-cookies, remove-cookie, ext-proxy:*, get-direct-ip, get-proxy-profiles, connect-proxy-template, set-tab-proxy, set-tab-cookie-group, isolate-tab, get-tabs, set-ui-pref, navigate-to with file://)
- Settings page needs its own privileged bridge (a trusted preload for the settings tab or a separate trusted window) after the WIP change to preload-view.js
- CSP: add meta CSP to new-tab.html, cupnet-guide.html, site-info-popover.html and modal-logging.html by moving inline scripts to files. Drop 'unsafe-inline' script in omnibox-overlay.html. Consider sandbox:true for session-profile-modal (its preload only uses contextBridge/ipcRenderer)
- Overlays: move the credential fill popup and shell toasts into an overlay surface (a WebContentsView like the omnibox, a child window, or reserved space) so they are visible above web content
- Status bar performance: event-driven updates instead of a 3s poll that fires 3 IPCs plus synchronous ps/PowerShell. Cache metrics in main, run getPidMetrics asynchronously, and poll the resources drawer only while it is open
- browser-renderer.js is a 3.4k-line global-scope script. Split it into modules (omnibox, tabs, status bar, credentials, switcher) with unit tests; there is currently no renderer test coverage
- Accessibility: tabs are divs without role=tab/tablist or keyboard focus; the omnibox list lives in another WebContentsView, so aria-controls points to an empty list; status drawers need focus management
- i18n/consistency: a single source for the shortcut list (menu accelerators → keyboard-shortcuts.js → new-tab cheat sheet → guide), a CI check that cupnet-guide.html matches scripts/guide-i18n, and 4-language parity checks
- Favicons were removed (favicon-cache.js deleted in the WIP), so tabs and omnibox show emoji/SVG fallbacks only. The CSS .tab-favicon-img/.site-info-favicon/.item-favicon is left unused
- The Credential save bar 'Never' has no persistence (no never-save list)
- Only the dark theme: the shell forces color-scheme dark although a uiTheme ui-pref exists (used by settings)
- Launch Profile modal should round-trip unknown or unsupported fields (tab.isolated/cookieGroupId, proxy.ja3/profileName) or show them as read-only instead of silently dropping them
- Dev-browser ergonomics — done 2026-09-25: omnibox-input.js classifies localhost[:port], host:port, IPv4/IPv6, intranet/path and *.local/.test/.lan as http URLs (A-067/A-391)
- Packaging: build.files is '**/*', which ships tests/, docs/, scripts/ and the legacy electron-app/ into the app (not browser-ui specific, but it bloats the internal pages' asar)

### build-ci-tests (npm scripts, electron-builder packaging, GitHub Actions CI/release, test harnesses: node:test integration, Vitest unit, Go tests, Playwright+Electron e2e, helper/debug scripts, repo hygiene)

Everything needed to install, run, test, package and release CupNet 2: package.json scripts and electron-builder "build" config, the native-module rebuild pipeline (better-sqlite3 for Electron ABI; legacy ffi-napi for the bundled Node), the Go AzureTLS worker build (azuretls-go/bin), three GitHub workflows (PR checks, tag-triggered multi-OS release with draft GitHub Release, nightly reliability), and four test layers: tests/*.js (node:test via tests/run-all.sh), tests/unit/*.test.mjs (Vitest), azuretls-go/*_test.go (go test), tests/e2e/*.e2e.spec.js (Playwright driving real Electron). It also covers dev/debug scripts under scripts/ and junk in the repo root that electron-builder currently packages into the app.

**Files:**

- `package.json` — Scripts, dependencies, engines (node >=20 <24, npm >=10), optionalDependencies (@2060.io/ffi-napi 4.0.9, ref-napi 3.0.6, ref-struct-di), overrides (node-addon-api ^8.3.0), and the electron-builder config under "build": afterPack, appId com.cup.net, publish github, asar true, asarUnpack [azure-tls-worker.js, network-policy.js, sys-log.js, azuretls/**, node_modules/**], files [**/*, !dist, !node_modules/.cache, !azuretls/node_modules/.cache], extraResources resources/cupnet-node->cupnet-node and azuretls-go/bin->azuretls-go/bin, mac dmg for [x64, arm64] with identity null and hardenedRuntime false, win nsis, linux AppImage. version is "27.06.2026-5". The WIP diff adds argon2 ^0.41.1 and a test:mitm:live script.
- `package-lock.json` — lockfileVersion 3 and consistent with package.json (electron 34.5.8, electron-builder 24.13.3, vitest 2.1.9, @playwright/test 1.58.2, better-sqlite3 12.8.0, argon2 0.41.1). The WIP diff adds argon2.
- `.github/workflows/pr-checks.yml` — On pull_request to main, runs on macos-latest with Node 22 and Go 1.24.x: npm ci -> npm run build:go:local -> npm run test:unit:all (vitest plus go test) -> npm test (tests/run-all.sh) -> npm run test:e2e:smoke. It sets FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24 at job level. No timeout-minutes and no concurrency group.
- `.github/workflows/build-all.yml` — Release build, triggered by a v* tag push or workflow_dispatch. Matrix: macos-latest, windows-latest, ubuntu-latest. Steps: per-OS npm install (Windows uses --ignore-scripts --omit=optional followed by npm run rebuild) -> macOS 'check:native' step, which is skipped in CI -> setup-go 1.24.x -> Go worker build for the host arch only (bash build:go:local, or a PowerShell go build for win32-amd64.exe on Windows) -> dist:<os>:local with CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY=false and CUPNET_SKIP_AFTERPACK_AZURETLS_NATIVE=1 -> upload-artifact dist-<os> (dist/**, 14 days). The release-draft job, which runs on tags only, downloads dist-* and calls softprops/action-gh-release@v2 with a draft release containing *.dmg, *.exe, *.AppImage and *.blockmap. No tests run in this pipeline.
- `.github/workflows/reliability-nightly.yml` — Cron 0 2 * * * plus manual dispatch, on macos-latest with Node 22: npm ci --foreground-scripts -> npm run baseline:stability -> npm test -> uploads _debug/stability-baseline.json as the stability-baseline artifact. No Go build step, so the Go worker binary is missing and test-dns-mitm falls back to the legacy Node worker.
- `tests/run-all.sh` — The `npm test` runner. Runs 13 files with `set -e`: 11 tests/*.js files under plain `node file`, test-dns-mitm.js under `node --test --test-force-exit`, and test-db.js under `env ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1 <Electron binary>`. The Electron binary path is macOS-specific with a fallback to `which electron`, so on other OSes the db tests are skipped with a warning. Prints 'Results: N passed, M failed' and exits 1 if anything failed. It does NOT run vitest or the tests/unit files.
- `tests/test-mitm.js` — node:test file for mitm-proxy internals. It pulls parseHttpRequest out of the mitm-proxy.js source with an eval brace-matcher, tests an INLINE COPY of buildHttpResponse (not the production function), and tests generateCA, generateCAAsync and MitmProxy construction through mkProxy. It ends with setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), 500), which masks failures.
- `tests/test-dns-mitm.js` — node:test file covering MitmProxy DNS overrides and MITM CORS: setDnsOverrides, _applyDnsOverride, _mitmCorsEnabledForUrl, setGlobalCorsEnabled, _mitmCorsMatchDetail, plus exports _testApplyMitmCorsToResponse, _testShouldSkipMitmCorsForUrl and _testMatchHostPattern. It forces CUPNET_AZURETLS_CHILD_PROCESS=1 and CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER=1 and kills the spawned worker in mkProxy.
- `tests/test-db.js` — node:test file for db.js (sessions, requests, queryRequests filters, pagination, ws events, screenshots, proxy profiles including traffic_mode normalization to 'mitm', ftsSearch, getSessionsWithStats, async write queue, getWriteQueueStats). It shims 'electron' through Module._resolveFilename and require.cache, calls db.initWithPath on a temp file, and has to run under Electron-as-Node because better-sqlite3 is built for the Electron ABI.
- `tests/test-interceptor.js` — node:test file for the request-interceptor.js API surface: matchesPattern, strict URL validation, bypassInterceptMockBlockForSensitiveUrl (CF/captcha), urlMatchesStrictPattern, ruleMatchesUrl, planMitmIntercept (mock plus DNS interceptMatchUrl, script before/shortCircuit), finalizeMitmInterceptResponseAsync, validateInterceptRuleForSave, runInterceptScriptSelfTest.
- `tests/test-utils.js, tests/test-user-agent-utils.js, tests/test-page-analyzer-endpoints.js, tests/test-reliability-policy.js, tests/test-proxy-resilience.js, tests/test-traffic-mode-router.js, tests/test-safe-catch.js, tests/test-secrets-sanitization.js, tests/test-mitm-integration.js` — Small integration tests for utils.js, user-agent-utils.js, main-process/services/page-analyzer-endpoint-scan.js (plain assert with no node:test), network-policy.js, proxy-resilience.js, traffic-mode-router.js, sys-log safeCatch (electron shimmed), azuretls/azureTLS.js and .env.example placeholders, and mitm-proxy loadOrGenerateCA plus networkPolicy.mitmPort. Several overlap with tests/unit equivalents.
- `tests/unit/*.test.mjs (33 files)` — Vitest unit tests. Most load CJS sources through createRequire(import.meta.url). 27 import from 'vitest'. 6 import describe/it from 'node:test' and are never executed: mitm-http1-fallback, profile-avatars, proxy-providers, session-cookie-consolidate, session-cookie-utils, tab-error-page. Security WIP tests (ipc-trusted-sender, password-policy, traffic-log-crypto, vault-auto-lock, credentials-login-migration, proxy-save-fail-closed, profile-avatars) target files that are still untracked.
- `tests/unit/azuretls-go-worker.test.mjs` — Integration tests for the NDJSON protocol against azuretls-go/bin/azuretls-worker-<os>-<arch>, using describe.skipIf(!haveBinary). Covers __init__, __get_profiles__, __clear_sessions__, 'Invalid JSON', 'missing id', and httpbin.org GET/POST/headers/redirect/concurrency/1MB/graceful shutdown. Needs network.
- `tests/unit/omnibox-history.test.mjs` — Tests db.recordOmniboxVisit and getOmniboxSuggestions using an electron shim file plus a Module._resolveFilename override (child-process probe skips when better-sqlite3 cannot load under plain Node). The broader history API (visits, titles, shortcuts, delete/clear/purge) is covered in tests/test-db.js
- `tests/e2e/helpers.js` — Playwright Electron helpers. launchCupnet uses node_modules/electron/dist binary, args ['.', --user-data-dir=<mkdtemp cupnet-e2e-*>], env CUPNET_E2E=1 and deletes ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE. Pollers: waitForAppContext (globalThis.__cupnetAppContext.modules.tabManager), waitMitmReady (appCtx.mitm.ready), navigateAndWait (wc.loadURL plus body polling), waitForLoggedCount, waitForStableDbRequestCount, waitForBrowserEvent. Utilities: closeAllExcept (keeps the browser.html shell), createInterceptRule, deleteAllInterceptRules, openNewTabWithFreshCookieGroupAndCupnet, exportHarViaMain (appCtx.modules.harExporter.exportHar), ensureLoggingStartedNoModal, findDuplicateRequestGroups. Temp userData dirs are never cleaned up.
- `tests/e2e/*.e2e.spec.js (23 specs), tests/e2e/README.md, tests/e2e/fixtures/` — Real-Electron e2e tests, mostly against https://httpbin.org (43 references), plus ipinfo.io and 2captcha.com. All use test.describe.configure({mode:'serial'}) with one app per file. Smoke set (package.json test:e2e:smoke): cupnet, windows, intercept, mitm-azure-first-load, azuretls-go-worker. omnibox.e2e.spec.js is modified in the WIP.
- `vitest.config.js` — CJS config: require('vitest/config').defineConfig, environment 'node', include tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs, testTimeout 15000. No coverage, no setupFiles, no DOM environment.
- `playwright.config.js` — testDir tests/e2e, timeout 420s, expect 45s, workers 1, fullyParallel false, forbidOnly and retries=1 when CI is set, reporter list. With CUPNET_SKIP_E2E=1 or SKIP_E2E=1 it sets testIgnore ['**/*']. No outputDir, so test-results/ lands in the repo root.
- `scripts/playwright-e2e.mjs` — Wrapper around `npx playwright test <args>`. Adds --pass-with-no-tests when CUPNET_SKIP_E2E or SKIP_E2E is set.
- `scripts/rebuild-electron-native.js` — postinstall and `npm run rebuild`. Probes the Electron binary arch with ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1 `-e process.stdout.write(process.arch)`, then runs `npx electron-rebuild -f -o better-sqlite3 --arch <arch>`. Skipped when SKIP_ELECTRON_REBUILD=1.
- `scripts/start-force.js` — `npm run start-force`. Checks that better-sqlite3 loads under Electron-as-Node (cwd = repo root), runs rebuild-electron-native.js on failure, then `npm run start`. Skip the check with SKIP_START_FORCE_CHECK=1.
- `scripts/ensure-cupnet-node.mjs` — Run before every dist* script. Downloads the nodejs.org node-v${CUPNET_BUNDLE_NODE_VERSION:-22.14.0} for the HOST platform/arch into resources/cupnet-node/{node|node.exe, VERSION} via resources/.cupnet-node-tmp (tar, or Expand-Archive on Windows). Cached by VERSION, no checksum verification, skipped when CUPNET_SKIP_BUNDLE_NODE=1. Only the legacy FFI worker uses this binary, and that worker is disabled.
- `scripts/after-pack-azuretls-worker-native.cjs` — electron-builder afterPack hook, skipped when CUPNET_SKIP_AFTERPACK_AZURETLS_NATIVE=1 (as CI does). Finds Resources, then app.asar.unpacked or app. Runs <Resources>/cupnet-node/node plus repo node_modules/node-gyp `rebuild` inside REPO node_modules/@2060.io/{ref-napi,ffi-napi} with npm_config_arch from context.arch, then copies build/Release into the packaged node_modules. It throws if those packages are missing.
- `scripts/stability-baseline.js` — `npm run baseline:stability`. Writes <cwd>/_debug/stability-baseline.json containing ts, node, platform, arch, networkPolicy and every CUPNET_* env var with its value.
- `scripts/gen-ipc-scope-accessors.cjs` — Prints ipcScopeGet/ipcScopeSet switch statements from main-process/ipc/ipc-scope-key-list.json (152 keys) and a hard-coded writable set of 12 keys. Its output matches _ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js, but the runtime uses an inline copy in main-process/cupnet-runtime.js (~lines 1181-1359) that differs: it uses break, calls syncAppContextSnapshot() and orders keys differently.
- `scripts/gen-register-main-ipc.cjs` — DEPRECATED and DESTRUCTIVE. Slices main-process/index.js lines 2352-4645 and overwrites main-process/ipc/register-main-ipc.js. index.js is now 44 lines, so running it would wipe the IPC registration.
- `scripts/build-new-tab-guide.mjs, scripts/guide-i18n/{en,fr,es,ru}.mjs` — `npm run build:guide`. Rebuilds the `const LANGS = {` ... `    }; // end LANGS` block in cupnet-guide.html from the i18n modules; each module is a function taking kbdRow.
- `scripts/mitm-standalone-check.mjs` — Untracked WIP; `npm run test:mitm:live`. Starts MitmProxy on port 18899 without Electron (CUPNET_AZURETLS_CHILD_PROCESS=1, CUPNET_DEBUG_MITM=1), sends manual CONNECT+TLS requests to example.com and ipinfo.io, then runs stop() and start() again. Needs network and the Go binary; 45 s hard timeout.
- `scripts/tls-fingerprint-compare.mjs` (2026-09-25, untracked) — TLS/HTTP-2 fingerprint comparison without Electron or a window: drives the Go worker through a standalone MitmProxy (template via `--template`, default chrome-mac) and a locally installed Chrome with `--headless=new` (`--chrome <path>`, `--no-chrome`), both against tls.peet.ws/api/all; prints JA4 / JA4_r / JA3 hash / HTTP-2 Akamai and the extension / signature-scheme / cipher / group differences (`--json`). Re-run after every Electron or azuretls bump (PRODUCTION_PLAN "Electron/Chromium upgrade cadence"); findings in BROWSER_IDENTITY_STATUS.md §4 (2).
- `scripts/debug-nav.mjs, scripts/debug-tab-nav.mjs, scripts/bisect-tab-nav.sh` — Untracked WIP debugging tools that launch Electron through Playwright with CUPNET_E2E=1 and probe example.com navigation (curl against hard-coded 127.0.0.1:8877). bisect-tab-nav.sh temporarily replaces WIP files with their HEAD versions to find a culprit.
- `scripts/debug-ivac.js` — NOT a dev-only script: sub-windows.js runIvacScoutProcess spawns it at runtime for the IVAC Scout window. It uses the FFI azuretls/azureTLS.js client, a hard-coded Chrome/133 UA, and writes dumps to <repo>/_debug.
- `scripts/chaos-proxy-sim.js` — `npm run chaos:proxy-sim`. Random ProxyResilienceManager simulation that prints orderedCandidates and snapshot.
- `azuretls-go/build.sh, azuretls-go/build-local.sh, azuretls-go/bin/README.md, azuretls-go/*_test.go` — Go worker build. build.sh cross-compiles darwin amd64/arm64, linux amd64/arm64 and windows amd64 into bin/azuretls-worker-<os>-<arch>[.exe]. build-local.sh builds only the current GOOS/GOARCH. go test covers main/pool/profiles/request and runs offline.
- `install.sh` — macOS convenience script: npm run dist:mac, then rm -rf and copy dist/mac/CupNet.app into /Applications and open it. dist/mac is the x64 output, so it installs the x64 app on Apple Silicon.
- `.gitignore` — Ignores node_modules, dist, out, *.log, log, all dot-directories (.*/ except !.github), .roomodes, test-results/, azuretls-go/bin/* (except README), resources/cupnet-node/, resources/.cupnet-node-tmp/, _debug/, _debug2/, _debug*.zip, .env*, backup zip and cupnet-pre-rewrite.bundle. It does NOT ignore .DS_Store (which is tracked) or azuretls-go/azuretls-go-worker. data/ is untracked but must be committed.
- `.env.example` — Documentation only; there is no dotenv loader. Lists CUPNET_PROXY_URL/BEARER_TOKEN/API_BASE_URL/CAPTCHA_API_KEY placeholders (asserted by test-secrets-sanitization), stability knobs (CUPNET_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, CUPNET_DB_*, CUPNET_RETRY_*, CUPNET_WORKER_*) and Cloudflare bisect flags (CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL 1-6, CUPNET_DISABLE_*, CUPNET_INTERCEPT_*, CUPNET_HAR_*). Comments are in Russian.
- `.nvmrc` — 22
- `Repo-root junk: _debug/ (real HAR/bundle/site dumps), _debug.zip (23MB), _debug2/, _debug_eof_*.js (5, tracked), cupnet-pre-rewrite.bundle (25MB git bundle), cupnet2-workspace-backup-20260327-0815.zip (35MB), img2.png (1MB, tracked, unreferenced), critical.log, .DS_Store (tracked), debug-main.js, electron-app/app.js (legacy), todo.md, .roomodes, .ruru/, .cursor/, !MD/, docs/, test-results/, dist/ (old Mar-29 2026.3.28-8 build)` — None of this is needed at runtime. Because build.files is '**/*', electron-builder packs all of it except dist. The previous build's dist/mac/CupNet.app/Contents/Resources/app.asar (77MB) contains the workspace backup zip, the git bundle, .ruru (1452 files), tests, test-results, docs and install.sh.
- `README.md` — Outdated build instructions: `cd node/cupnet`, `npm install --ignore-scripts`, rebuild:arm64. It mentions rules-engine.js, which no longer exists, and does not cover the Go worker or tests.

**Key flows:**

- DEV INSTALL: `npm ci` runs the postinstall scripts/rebuild-electron-native.js: getElectronArch() spawns require('electron') with ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1 -> `npx electron-rebuild -f -o better-sqlite3 --arch <arch>`. After that, better-sqlite3 has the Electron ABI (Electron 34) and cannot load in plain Node 20/22. argon2 uses N-API prebuilds (node_modules/argon2/prebuilds) and loads everywhere. Optional @2060.io/ffi-napi and ref-napi compile from source with node-gyp. `npm run build:go:local` is required before the app can do MITM, because the Node FFI worker is disabled.
- DEV START: `npm start` (`ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE= electron .`) -> main.js -> main-process/index.js (single-instance lock and second-instance focus unless CUPNET_E2E=1; unhandledRejection and uncaughtException go to sysLog) -> cupnet-runtime.attachMainProcess(). If Electron runs with ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1, app is undefined and runtime-early-flags.applyCupnetEarlyChromiumFlags crashes, and sys-log writes critical.log to cwd (this is what the repo-root critical.log is). `npm run start-force` checks better-sqlite3 first and rebuilds if needed.
- UPSTREAM WORKER SELECTION (drives both packaging and tests): mitm-proxy.js createMitmAzureBackend(workerPath). With CUPNET_AZURETLS_CHILD_PROCESS=1 or inside Electron it returns new AzureTLSWorker. In plain Node it does require('./azure-tls-inprocess'), a file that DOES NOT EXIST, so it throws. AzureTLSWorker._start(): mitmPreferGoWorkerBinary() (unless CUPNET_USE_GO_WORKER=0 or CUPNET_USE_NODE_WORKER=1) -> _resolveGoWorkerBinary() tries process.resourcesPath/azuretls-go/bin/<name>, then __dirname/azuretls-go/bin/<name>, where <name> comes from _goWorkerBinaryName(): azuretls-worker-darwin-{arm64|amd64}, -linux-{arm64|amd64}, -win32-amd64.exe. It spawns that binary with no args (NDJSON on stdio). If no binary is found and CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER!=1, it sets _fatalWorkerConfigError and emits 'error' (safeCatch eventCode azure.worker.ffi.disabled). With the legacy flag set, it spawns node azure-tls-worker.js, choosing the node binary from CUPNET_AZURETLS_NODE, then _resolveCupnetBundledNodeBinary() (resources/cupnet-node), then PATH node or Electron-as-Node.
- PACKAGING (`npm run dist:mac|win|linux[:local]`): scripts/ensure-cupnet-node.mjs downloads host-arch Node 22.14.0 into resources/cupnet-node -> `cross-env USE_HARD_LINKS=false electron-builder --<os> <target> [--publish never]` -> app files '**/*' (only dist and caches excluded, plus builder default excludes) go into app.asar -> asarUnpack puts azure-tls-worker.js, network-policy.js, sys-log.js, azuretls/** and ALL node_modules/** into app.asar.unpacked -> native deps are rebuilt for the Electron ABI (electron-builder npmRebuild default) -> extraResources: Resources/cupnet-node and Resources/azuretls-go/bin (whatever binaries exist locally, including azuretls-worker-test) -> afterPack after-pack-azuretls-worker-native.cjs rebuilds ffi/ref for the bundled Node, unless skipped. Mac output: identity null, so it is unsigned and not notarized. The x64 and arm64 DMGs share the artifactName 'MacOS_CupNet-${version}.${ext}' and overwrite each other. Version '27.06.2026-5' is loosely cleaned to 27.6.2026-5.
- PR CI (pr-checks.yml, macos-latest): checkout -> setup-node 22 (npm cache) -> npm ci (postinstall rebuild) -> setup-go 1.24.x -> npm run build:go:local -> npm run test:unit:all = `vitest run` then `cd azuretls-go && GO111MODULE=on GOTOOLCHAIN=auto go test -count=1 ./...` -> npm test (tests/run-all.sh) -> npm run test:e2e:smoke (node scripts/playwright-e2e.mjs with 5 spec files). Vitest currently exits non-zero because 6 node:test files report 'No test suite found' (state fail) and the network-dependent Go-worker concurrency test fails, so PR checks are red and later steps never run.
- RELEASE (build-all.yml): push tag v<version> or run manually -> 3-OS matrix build -> artifacts dist-macos-latest, dist-windows-latest, dist-ubuntu-latest -> release-draft job (tags only, and only if no build failed) -> draft GitHub Release named 'CupNet <tag>' with generated notes and *.dmg/*.exe/*.AppImage/*.blockmap files (fail_on_unmatched_files false). The Go worker and bundled Node are built/downloaded for the RUNNER arch only (macos-latest is arm64), so the mac x64 build is broken.
- NIGHTLY (reliability-nightly.yml): npm ci --foreground-scripts -> stability-baseline.js writes _debug/stability-baseline.json (includes all CUPNET_* env values) -> npm test -> upload-artifact stability-baseline.
- RUN-ALL (`npm test`): each tests/*.js runs under node. node:test auto-runs its tests and sets exitCode=1 on failure, but test-mitm.js forces process.exit(0) after 500ms. test-dns-mitm uses --test-force-exit because the child worker leaves handles open. test-db runs under `ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1 node_modules/electron/dist/Electron.app/Contents/MacOS/Electron` and exits 0 ('skip') if require('../db') throws 'incompatible architecture'.
- E2E: node scripts/playwright-e2e.mjs -> `npx playwright test` -> spec beforeAll: launchCupnet() -> electronApp.firstWindow() -> waitForAppContext -> waitMitmReady (up to 180s). Tests drive the main process via electronApp.evaluate(() => globalThis.__cupnetAppContext...) and the renderer via mainWindow.evaluate(() => window.electronAPI.<method>()). With CUPNET_E2E=1 the app skips the single-instance lock, exit confirmation (main-window.js), tab-open confirm (tab-open-confirm.js) and profile gate / profile setup (profile-session.shouldSkipProfileGate, profile-data-migration.needsProfileSetup; CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE=1 does the same).
- IPC SCOPE REGEN: edit main-process/ipc/ipc-scope-key-list.json (and the writable set) -> `node scripts/gen-ipc-scope-accessors.cjs > main-process/ipc/_ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js` -> copy the cases by hand into the inline ipcScopeGet/ipcScopeSet in cupnet-runtime.js, which is consumed by build-ipc-scope-delegates.buildIpcScopeObject(ipcScopeKeyList, ipcScopeWritableKeys, get, set). Keep the inline syncAppContextSnapshot() call and keep ipcScopeWritableKeys (cupnet-runtime.js:1181) in sync with the generator's `writ` set.
- GUIDE: `npm run build:guide` -> build-new-tab-guide.mjs -> en/fr/es/ru(kbdRow) -> replaces the LANGS block in cupnet-guide.html, which is loaded by new-tab and screenshot/tab-error services.

**IPC channels:**

- save-proxy-profile (renderer->main invoke; exercised directly in tests/unit/proxy-save-fail-closed.test.mjs via registerProxyIpc(ctx) with a fake ipcMain.handle; must return {success:false, error:/secure storage/} when safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable() is false)
- guardIpcHandler(channel, fn) from main-process/ipc/ipc-trusted-sender.js (WIP): rejects with code IPC_UNTRUSTED_SENDER for senders registered via registerTabView and allows registerTrustedShell senders (tests/unit/ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs)
- E2E uses these preload electronAPI methods (window.electronAPI.* in browser.html; the underlying channels live in the handlers): toggleLoggingStart('e2e') -> {status: started|already_on|modal_shown}, confirmLoggingStart({mode:'continue', renameOld:null}), getCurrentSessionId, countDbRequests(filters), getDbRequests(filters, limit, offset), getBrowserEvents({sessionId, limit}), saveInterceptRule(rule) -> {id}|{error}, getInterceptRules, deleteInterceptRule(id), createCookieGroup(name) -> {success, group}, newTab(null), getTabs, setTabCookieGroup(tabId, groupId), plus sub-window openers used by openSubWindowExpectNew(methodName)
- The main-process test hook is not IPC: globalThis.__cupnetAppContext (set at main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:925 in ALL builds, including production) with .mitm.ready, .proxy {trafficMode, actProxy, profileId, profileName}, .modules.{tabManager, harExporter, mitmProxy}

**Data stores:**

- resources/cupnet-node/{node|node.exe,VERSION} (bundled Node, gitignored) and resources/.cupnet-node-tmp/ (download temp)
- azuretls-go/bin/azuretls-worker-* (gitignored build output; local copies are STALE, darwin-arm64 built 2026-04-02 while *.go changed in July) and untracked azuretls-go/azuretls-go-worker (x86_64, unused by runtime)
- azuretls/lib/libazuretls_{darwin_amd64,darwin_arm64,linux_amd64,linux_arm64,win32_amd64}.{dylib,so,dll} (tracked, legacy FFI) and azuretls/node_modules (local, untracked)
- dist/ (electron-builder output: DMG/NSIS/AppImage, *.blockmap, latest-*.yml, builder-effective-config.yaml, builder-debug.yml, mac/ or win-unpacked/ or linux-unpacked/)
- test-results/ (Playwright output, gitignored but packaged into the app)
- _debug/stability-baseline.json (baseline:stability output, CI artifact 'stability-baseline')
- data/common-passwords-10k.txt (untracked, read by main-process/services/password-policy.js COMMON_PASSWORDS_PATH; asserted by tests/unit/password-policy.test.mjs)
- cupnet-guide.html LANGS block (generated by build:guide)
- main-process/ipc/ipc-scope-key-list.json, main-process/ipc/_ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js (generated reference)
- Temp dirs: os.tmpdir()/cupnet-e2e-* (never cleaned), cupnet-db-test-*, cupnet-omnibox-*, cupnet-mitm-test-*, cupnet-debug-*, cupnet-tabnav-*, /tmp/cupnet-vitest-userdata (mocked path)
- critical.log: sys-log writes app.getPath('userData')/critical.log, or process.cwd()/critical.log when electron app is unavailable
- CI caches: ~/.cache/electron and ~/.cache/electron-builder (key <os>-electron-cache-<hash(package-lock.json)>), the npm cache from setup-node, and the Go module cache (cache-dependency-path azuretls-go/go.sum)
- GitHub Actions artifacts dist-<matrix.os> (14 days) and a draft GitHub Release
- node_modules/.vite (Vitest results cache)
- Env vars used by this subsystem: SKIP_ELECTRON_REBUILD, SKIP_START_FORCE_CHECK, CUPNET_BUNDLE_NODE_VERSION, CUPNET_SKIP_BUNDLE_NODE, CUPNET_SKIP_AFTERPACK_AZURETLS_NATIVE, npm_config_arch, USE_HARD_LINKS, CSC_IDENTITY_AUTO_DISCOVERY, CUPNET_SKIP_E2E / SKIP_E2E, CI, CUPNET_E2E, CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE, CUPNET_AZURETLS_CHILD_PROCESS, CUPNET_AZURETLS_IN_PROCESS, CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER, CUPNET_USE_GO_WORKER, CUPNET_USE_NODE_WORKER, CUPNET_AZURETLS_NODE, CUPNET_DEBUG_MITM, CUPNET_WORKER_NO_WATCHDOG, CUPNET_WORKER_FFI_CONCURRENCY, CUPNET_WORKER_CLIENT_CACHE_MAX, CUPNET_TIMEOUT_UPSTREAM_REQUEST_MS, CUPNET_HAR_MAX_EXPORT_CHARS, CUPNET_EXPORT_WS_FRAME_LIMIT, GO111MODULE, GOTOOLCHAIN, FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24, ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE, ELECTRON_IS_DEV, CUPNET_ELECTRON_REBUILD_ARCH and CUPNET_SMOKE_REQUIRE_UNIVERSAL (the last two only in scripts that point at missing files)

**Conventions:**

- Vitest unit tests go in tests/unit/<name>.test.mjs and MUST import describe/it/expect from 'vitest'. Any file importing 'node:test' is reported by Vitest as 'No test suite found' and fails the run. Load CJS sources with `const require = createRequire(import.meta.url)`.
- vi.mock('electron') does NOT affect modules loaded via createRequire (CJS). To fake electron, patch Module._resolveFilename or require.cache (patterns in tests/test-db.js, tests/test-safe-catch.js, tests/unit/omnibox-history.test.mjs). vi.resetModules() does not clear the CJS require cache, so use the modules' _resetForTests() hooks (vault-session-service, ipc-trusted-sender, password-policy _resetCommonPasswordCacheForTests).
- Integration tests in tests/*.js use node:test plus node:assert/strict and must be added by hand to tests/run-all.sh (it has no glob). Do not force process.exit(0). Use `node --test --test-force-exit` when a child worker keeps handles open.
- Any test that constructs MitmProxy in plain Node must set process.env.CUPNET_AZURETLS_CHILD_PROCESS='1' (and CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER='1' if no Go binary is present) BEFORE requiring mitm-proxy, then clearInterval(p._statTimer) and kill p.worker.proc (see tests/test-dns-mitm.js mkProxy).
- Tests that need better-sqlite3 must run under Electron-as-Node (ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1 <electron binary>), because postinstall compiles it for the Electron ABI. Otherwise guard with a canLoadBetterSqlite() skip.
- E2E specs: tests/e2e/<name>.e2e.spec.js, `test.describe.configure({ mode: 'serial' })`, one launchCupnet() per file in beforeAll and electronApp.close() in afterAll. Use helpers.js pollers instead of fixed sleeps. Add CI-critical specs to the explicit list in package.json 'test:e2e:smoke'.
- Go worker binary names are the contract azuretls-worker-{darwin,linux}-{amd64,arm64} and azuretls-worker-win32-amd64.exe. They are duplicated in mitm-proxy._goWorkerBinaryName, azuretls-go/build*.sh, tests/unit/azuretls-go-worker.test.mjs and tests/e2e/azuretls-go-worker.e2e.spec.js, so change all of them together. Rebuild with `npm run build:go:local` after touching azuretls-go/*.go; do not use a bare `go build`, which produces azuretls-go/azuretls-go-worker that the runtime never uses.
- Release process: bump package.json 'version', commit, push tag v<version>. build-all.yml then produces a DRAFT GitHub release that has to be published manually. The current scheme is DD.MM.YYYY-N (tags v27.06.2026-N). The previous scheme YYYY.M.D-N was strict semver.
- User-facing UI text must be English (project rule). Many existing comments, logs and scripts (install.sh, .env.example, README, e2e test names) are in Russian.
- MITM rule for tests and fixes: never intercept via session.protocol.handle in MITM mode. Intercept tests go through request-interceptor planMitmIntercept/finalizeMitmInterceptResponse, as in tests/test-interceptor.js.
- Do not commit new unit tests without the untracked sources they require (main-process/ipc/ipc-trusted-sender.js, main-process/services/{password-policy,traffic-log-crypto,vault-session-service,profile-session,profile-avatars,credentials-vault-crypto,secure-fs,...}.js, html-response-preview.js, data/common-passwords-10k.txt), or CI breaks.

**Gotchas:**

- Baseline runs misreport results. `npm test` shows 13/13 passed, but tests/test-mitm.js has 4 FAILING tests: 'MitmProxy: constructs without starting', 'getStats…', 'setBrowser…', 'getCACert…', each failing with Cannot find module './azure-tls-inprocess'. The file ends with setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), 500), so it still exits 0. Verified with `node tests/test-mitm.js`.
- `npm run test:unit` (vitest) fails on HEAD as well as on the WIP. Commit 045b954 introduced tests/unit/proxy-providers.test.mjs and session-cookie-utils.test.mjs using node:test, and the WIP adds 4 more. @vitest/runner marks a file with no tasks as state 'fail' ('No test suite found in file'). All 38 tests in those 6 files pass under `node --test tests/unit/<file>`.
- better-sqlite3 is built for the Electron ABI by postinstall, so plain-Node Vitest cannot use it. omnibox-history is silently skipped, har-exporter only spies on db. DB coverage lives in tests/test-db.js, which run-all.sh runs only on macOS through the Electron.app path (and package.json test:db hardcodes that macOS path).
- Stale Go worker: azuretls-go/bin/azuretls-worker-darwin-arm64 dates from 2026-04-02, before the parent-watchdog commit b94f3e5 and the WIP edits to main.go, pool.go and request.go. Run `npm run build:go:local` before trusting any MITM, e2e or azuretls-go-worker test result.
- tests/unit/azuretls-go-worker.test.mjs is skipped when the binary is missing (describe.skipIf) and otherwise needs httpbin.org. Its nextJson() uses rl.once('line'), which drops extra lines when several NDJSON responses arrive in one chunk. The 'concurrent requests return matching ids' timeout is probably this bug, not only network flakiness.
- The Node FFI worker path (azure-tls-worker.js, azuretls/azureTLS.js, @2060.io/*, resources/cupnet-node, the afterPack hook, CUPNET_SKIP_AFTERPACK_AZURETLS_NATIVE) is effectively dead in the app: AzureTLSWorker refuses it unless CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER=1. It is still used by tests/test-dns-mitm.js (flag set), by the IVAC Scout (scripts/debug-ivac.js), and by the 5 _debug_eof_*.js repro scripts.
- package.json scripts rebuild:azuretls, rebuild:azuretls:arm64|x64|universal|node, smoke:azuretls-native, check:native and check:native:universal point at scripts that have never existed in git history (scripts/azuretls-*.js, smoke-azuretls-native.js). build-all.yml runs check:native on macOS when azuretls/node_modules/@2060.io/ref-napi exists. That is never true in CI, but it is true on this dev machine, so reproducing the step locally fails. mitm-proxy.js error messages still recommend these scripts.
- Do NOT run scripts/gen-register-main-ipc.cjs: it overwrites main-process/ipc/register-main-ipc.js from an obsolete line range of index.js. Do NOT run scripts/bisect-tab-nav.sh on uncommitted work: it writes HEAD versions over WIP files, and its EXIT trap deletes the backup directory if interrupted. It also needs GNU `timeout` and `rg`, and runs pkill -f 'Electron.*cupnet2'.
- electron-builder 'files: **/*' ships repo junk. The earlier app.asar contained cupnet2-workspace-backup-20260327-0815.zip (35MB), cupnet-pre-rewrite.bundle (25MB), .ruru, .cursor, .roomodes, tests, test-results, docs, !MD, install.sh and .env.example. A local .env would be shipped too. The current tree would also add _debug/ (real HAR/session dumps), _debug.zip, _debug2, azuretls-go sources plus a 16MB stray binary and bin/azuretls-worker-test (17MB), critical.log and data/. Replace with an allowlist.
- Mac release: arch [x64, arm64] plus artifactName without ${arch} means both DMGs have the same path and one is lost (dmg-builder uses the user pattern verbatim when isUserForced). CI builds the Go worker and bundled Node only for the runner arch (arm64), so any x64 build fails at MITM start with 'Go worker required'.
- Version string '27.06.2026-5' is not strict semver. electron-builder normalizes it loosely to 27.6.2026-5, so artifact names don't match the tag. '-5' makes it a prerelease, and DD.MM.YYYY ordering is non-monotonic (27.6.2026 < 2026.4.1).
- Packaged-app detection `process.defaultApp === false && !ELECTRON_IS_DEV` (mitm-proxy.js:327, sub-windows.js:508) is never true, because defaultApp is undefined in packaged apps. Use app.isPackaged.
- CUPNET_E2E=1 and CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE=1 work in packaged builds. They bypass the profile gate / vault setup and the single-instance lock.
- Windows: npm scripts use POSIX env prefixes (start, debug, test:go, rebuild:azuretls:*) and fail under cmd.exe. build-all.yml avoids this by calling go build directly in PowerShell and using --ignore-scripts --omit=optional plus `npm run rebuild`.
- vitest.config.js and playwright.config.js are CommonJS. A Vitest 3/4 upgrade will print CJS-deprecation warnings or need an ESM config. Vitest 4 also changes pool and mocking behaviour.
- Nightly never builds the Go worker, so it effectively tests the legacy path, and its uploaded baseline JSON contains every CUPNET_* env value.
- tests/unit/password-policy.test.mjs requires the untracked data/common-passwords-10k.txt. Commit it or CI fails.
- tests/unit/html-response-preview.test.mjs runs in the 'node' environment without DOMParser, so it only checks the 'Preview unavailable' fallback. The real sanitizer path needs a jsdom or happy-dom environment.

**Tests:**

- `npm test` = bash tests/run-all.sh: 13 integration files. The baseline shows 13/13, but test-mitm.js hides 4 failures (see gotchas). Single-file variants: npm run test:utils, test:mitm, test:dns-mitm (`node --test --test-force-exit tests/test-dns-mitm.js`), test:db (macOS-only Electron path).
- `npm run test:unit` = `vitest run` over tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs (33 files). Baseline: 140 pass, 1 fail (azuretls-go-worker concurrency/httpbin), 6 files 'No test suite found' (counted as failures). The 6 node:test files can be run manually with `node --test tests/unit/{mitm-http1-fallback,profile-avatars,proxy-providers,session-cookie-consolidate,session-cookie-utils,tab-error-page}.test.mjs` (38/38 pass).
- `npm run test:go` = `cd azuretls-go && GO111MODULE=on GOTOOLCHAIN=auto go test -count=1 ./...` (offline; baseline ok). `npm run test:unit:all` runs vitest and then go.
- `npm run test:e2e` (all 23 specs) and `npm run test:e2e:smoke` (cupnet, windows, intercept, mitm-azure-first-load, azuretls-go-worker). Both need `npm run build:go:local`, a GUI session and internet (httpbin.org, ipinfo.io). `CUPNET_SKIP_E2E=1` makes them pass with no tests; `npm run test:no-e2e` = unit:all plus skipped smoke.
- `npm run test:mitm:live` = scripts/mitm-standalone-check.mjs (MITM on port 18899 through the Go worker, example.com and ipinfo.io, restart lifecycle). `npm run baseline:stability`, `npm run chaos:proxy-sim` and scripts/debug-tab-nav.mjs are diagnostics, not assertions.
- Coverage gaps: no tests for most IPC handlers (main-process/ipc/handlers/*), preloads, renderers, tab-manager, cdp-network-logging, the profile gate UI, db migrations with traffic-log encryption under Electron, or packaging (asar contents, per-arch worker presence). No coverage tooling, no lint (no ESLint config), no type checks. CI runs tests only on macOS.

**Production gaps:**

- No code signing or notarization. Mac uses identity null with no hardened runtime or notarization (Gatekeeper reports the downloaded app as damaged). Windows NSIS has no Authenticode certificate (SmartScreen). No CSC_LINK or APPLE_ID secrets are wired into build-all.yml.
- No Electron fuses and no ASAR integrity. RunAsNode stays enabled, so anyone can run the app binary as Node, and ASAR integrity and OnlyLoadAppFromAsar are off. Configure electronFuses after upgrading to electron-builder 26.
- Packaging uses a denylist ('**/*'). Switch to an explicit allowlist (main.js, main-process/**, preload*.js, *.html, *-renderer.js, required root modules, icons/**, data/**, assets). Drop the asarUnpack of all node_modules/** and unpack only native .node files and the worker binary. The earlier build was 509MB unpacked, with 101MB in app.asar.unpacked and a 111MB bundled node.
- Per-arch mac artifacts: build all Go worker targets (build.sh) or per-arch runners, and add ${arch} to artifactName or build a universal binary. Add a post-pack check that Resources/azuretls-go/bin/<expected name> exists and runs (__init__ handshake).
- The release pipeline runs no tests. build-all.yml has no needs: on unit or e2e jobs, no SBOM or SHA256SUMS, no provenance attestation, and no workflow timeouts or concurrency groups. The pr-checks workflow has no timeout-minutes, so e2e can hang for 6h.
- CI tests run only on macOS. There is no Windows or Linux test matrix, no lint (no ESLint/Prettier config), no type check (checkJs or TS), no coverage threshold, no `npm audit` / CodeQL / gitleaks gate, and no Dependabot or Renovate config.
- E2E depends on public services (httpbin.org, ipinfo.io, example.com). Add a local HTTPS fixture server (self-signed, trusted via the MITM path) so e2e is deterministic and can run offline in CI.
- No auto-update: publish: github produces app-update.yml, but there is no electron-updater dependency or update UI. The draft release has to be published manually, and generate_release_notes is the only changelog.
- Test/debug escape hatches are active in production builds: CUPNET_E2E and CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE skip the profile gate and single-instance lock, globalThis.__cupnetAppContext is always exposed, and CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER is honored. Gate all of them on !app.isPackaged.
- Repo hygiene: remove the tracked .DS_Store, img2.png, _debug_eof_*.js, debug-main.js, electron-app/app.js (legacy monolith) and the deprecated gen-register-main-ipc.cjs. Ignore .DS_Store and azuretls-go/azuretls-go-worker. Move the 60MB of backups (zip/bundle) and _debug dumps out of the working tree. Commit data/common-passwords-10k.txt.
- Dev portability: the POSIX-only npm scripts (start, debug, test:go, test:db), install.sh installing the x64 app from dist/mac, and run-all.sh are bash- and macOS-centric.
- ensure-cupnet-node downloads from nodejs.org without SHASUMS256 or signature verification (supply chain risk). This goes away once the bundled Node is removed.
- The stability-baseline artifact dumps raw CUPNET_* env values. Redact anything matching TOKEN, KEY, PASS, PROXY_URL.
- No crash reporting or telemetry pipeline (only critical.log and sysLog). No documented release checklist.

### core-runtime (Electron main bootstrap, lifecycle, IPC scope/registration, settings/ui-prefs stores, console/sys logging, misc/diagnostics/settings-toolbar/launcher/tracking/badge IPC handlers)

Boots the CupNet 2 Electron main process. main.js creates or loads the MITM CA before Electron starts. main-process/index.js takes the single-instance lock and installs the global error hooks. cupnet-runtime.js attachMainProcess() is the composition root: it holds the authoritative mutable state (windows, proxy, logging, MITM state) as closure `let` variables, builds the services (settings-store, ipc-batch, proxy-notify, fingerprint, screenshot, CDP logging, traffic-mode, proxy/MITM, sub-windows, main-window, profile-gate), starts the MITM proxy with retry, hands all state to IPC handlers through a lazily evaluated `ipcScope` object (key list in ipc-scope-key-list.json), then opens either the profile gate or the main window. On quit it tears everything down. The handlers in scope cover uptime/version/ui-prefs/homepage/site-info popover/CORS toggle/direct IP/window switcher/omnibox overlay (misc), process metrics (diagnostics), settings CRUD plus the sync preload settings (settings-toolbar), tool-window launchers and console-log export (launcher), tab pointer/click/typing/scroll screenshot triggers (tracking), and the toolbar badge reset. electron-app/app.js (5908 lines) is a legacy monolith. No require(), package.json script or build config references it; only docs/CUPNET_FULL_ANALYSIS.md mentions it. It still ships inside the asar because build.files is "**/*".

**Files:**

- `main.js` — Real entry (package.json main). Resolves userData by hand (hard-coded 'CupNet' per platform, ignores --user-data-dir and XDG_CONFIG_HOME) and calls mitm-proxy.js loadOrGenerateCA(<userData>/mitm-ca), which lives in mitm-ca.js. Sets process.env.NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS=ca-cert.pem, which only affects child processes because Node reads it at startup. Then requires main-process/index.js.
- `debug-main.js` — 10-line throwaway script that prints typeof require('electron'). Nothing references it. Dead file.
- `main-process/index.js` — Phase 0: app.requestSingleInstanceLock() (skipped when CUPNET_E2E=1); a 'second-instance' handler that restores and focuses BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()[0]; process 'unhandledRejection' -> sysLog error, 'uncaughtException' -> sysLog critical (swallowed, the process keeps running); then attachMainProcess().
- `main-process/runtime-early-flags.js` — STEALTH=Number(CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL) bisect levels. applyCupnetEarlyChromiumFlags(app) appends disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled and, when STEALTH<7, proxy-bypass-list=<local>. logCupnetBisectEnv prints the CUPNET_DISABLE_TRAFFIC_WEBREQUEST, CUPNET_DISABLE_INTERCEPT_PROTOCOL, CUPNET_DISABLE_FINGERPRINT, CUPNET_TRAFFIC_FILTER_LOG, CUPNET_FORCE_HTTP1, CUPNET_DEVTOOLS and CUPNET_RENDERER_CONSOLE_VERBOSE flags.
- `main-process/app-context.js` — createAppContext() returns a snapshot object {modules, windows, proxy, logging, fingerprint, mitm, settings, metrics, misc}. It is not the source of truth: cupnet-runtime syncAppContextSnapshot() copies closure state into it, and it is exposed as globalThis.__cupnetAppContext for E2E and scripts/debug-*.mjs.
- `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js` — 1506-line composition root (attachMainProcess, L30). Closure state at L105-198. syncAppContextSnapshot at L199. Service factories at L246-586. dSub/dMain dependency bags with get/set accessors at L615-705. createMainWindowApi/createProfileGateWindowApi/createSubWindowsApi at L676-774. certificate-error handler at L797-828. app.whenReady body at L831-1440: db/tabManager/interceptor/vault init, MITM start with retry, ipcScope build and registerAllMainIpc, default external port, browser-window-created hooks, profile gate or main window, onboarding. before-quit/will-quit/window-all-closed at L1442-1502.
- `main-process/ipc/register-all.js` — registerAllMainIpc(ctx) -> registerMainProcessIpc. Also exports noteMainProcessRegistersIpcBelow, a deprecated no-op.
- `main-process/ipc/register-main-ipc.js` — Calls the 25 handler modules in a fixed order: mitm-startup, toolbar-activity-badge, tracking, tabs, db-logging, trace-har, rules, launcher, page-analyzer, misc, proxy, screenshots, cookies-dns, log-compare-execute, settings-toolbar, diagnostics, quick-connect, mitm-tls-resilience, ext-proxy, notes, credentials, request-editor, session-profile, page-http-lab, profile. No channel is registered twice; this was checked.
- `main-process/ipc/build-ipc-scope-delegates.js` — buildIpcScopeObject(keyList, writableKeys, get, set) -> Object.create(null) with a lazy getter per key and a setter only for writable keys. Read-only keys have no setter, so assigning one throws in strict mode.
- `main-process/ipc/ipc-scope-key-list.json` — The 152 ctx keys available to handlers, such as db, tabManager, mainWindow, mitmProxy, loadSettings and sysLog. It must stay in sync with the ipcScopeGet switch in cupnet-runtime.js L1184-1340 (currently 1:1).
- `main-process/ipc/_ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js` — Output of scripts/gen-ipc-scope-accessors.cjs; nothing requires it. It is a copy of the get/set switch. Its setter uses `return` and does NOT call syncAppContextSnapshot(), unlike the inline copy in the runtime.
- `main-process/ipc/ipc-trusted-sender.js` — New, uncommitted. Two Sets of webContents ids: trusted shell windows (registerTrustedShell, called from main-window.js, sub-windows.js and profile-gate-window.js) and tab views (registerTabView, called from tab-manager.js:825). guardIpcHandler/guardIpcListener/assertTrustedShell throw err.code='IPC_UNTRUSTED_SENDER'. Only profile-ipc, credentials-ipc, proxy-ipc (get-proxy-profile-url) and cookies-dns-ipc use it; none of the in-scope handlers do.
- `main-process/services/settings-store.js` — Loads, caches and saves settings.json with a 300ms debounce and atomic writeFileSecureAsync (0600). The file is <userData>/settings.json, or <userData>/profiles/<vaultId>/settings.json after setActiveProfileVaultId. Holds SETTINGS_DEFAULTS, CURRENT_SETTINGS_VERSION=1, the normalize* helpers, materializeSettingsFromRaw (browser_proxy->mitm, strips currentProxy and capmonster), buildFactoryResetSettings, exportSettingsSafe, mergeImportedSettings and cancelPendingSave. configure({onEffectiveTrafficModeLoaded}) pushes the traffic mode back into the runtime.
- `main-process/services/ui-prefs-store.js` — <userData>/ui-prefs.json (or a per-profile copy) as a free-form key/value store. saveUiPref writes synchronously and atomically with fsync on every call.
- `main-process/services/window-state-store.js` — <userData>/window-state.json with 'main' and 'logViewer' bounds, used by main-window.js and sub-windows.js. Writes use plain fs.writeFileSync (not atomic, not per-profile). sanitizeBounds clamps positions to the display.
- `main-process/services/console-capture.js` — installConsoleCapture monkey-patches process.stdout.write and process.stderr.write. It keeps a ring buffer (10000 lines; drops 20% when full), batches 'console-log' IPC to the console viewer every 60ms, and persists to the console_logs table through onPersistBatch every 500ms. Also provides cupnetLog/pushManualEntry, and flushPersistQueue/dispose for will-quit.
- `main-process/services/ipc-batch-messenger.js` — Batched per-window sends: new-log-entry-batch (50ms/200 entries), intercept-rule-matched-batch and dns-rule-matched-batch (80ms/100), plus the de-duplicated broadcast tls-profile-changed. Keeps ring buffers of recent intercept (120) and DNS (150) events, which main-window replays on did-finish-load.
- `main-process/services/network-helpers.js` — getLocalIp, generatePassword(len=8, base64url), sanitizeProxyUrl (validates the scheme and stores the masked URL on the function object as _lastMasked), withTimeout.
- `main-process/services/os-process-metrics.js` — getPidMetrics(pid) uses synchronous execSync: `ps -p` on unix and powershell Get-Process on Windows, each with a 2s timeout. It runs on the main thread.
- `sys-log.js` — In-memory ring of 2000 entries. sysLog(level,module,msg,data) writes error/critical to stderr; critical is also appended to <userData>/critical.log, rotated to .1 above 5MB. Also: safeCatch(meta,err,level), getEntries, registerSysLogWindow (never called, so the 'sys-log-entry' push is dead), flushOnExit (re-appends every error and critical entry to critical.log), initIPC (the 'get-sys-log' handler). Loads Electron only when process.versions.electron is set.
- `utils.js` — Pure helpers covered by unit tests: resolveNavigationUrl (delegates URL-vs-search to omnibox-input.classifyOmniboxInput)/resolveNavigationUrlWithBase/resolveLoadUrlForWebContents/isWebContentsLoadUrl; search engines (SEARCH_ENGINES incl. bing, getSearchEngineUrlFromSettings, normalizeCustomSearchEngineUrl); redirect Location fix (absolutizeMitmRedirectResponse, used by mitm-proxy.js); parseProxyTemplate ({RAND:a-b}, {VAR}, {SID}); extractTemplateVars; formatBytes; shouldFilterUrl (glob -> RegExp, cached by array reference); sanitizeOutgoingRequestHeaders (strips HTTP/2 pseudo-headers); shouldSkipOmniboxInlineGhost (legacy)
- `regex-safe.js` — safeRegexTest(pattern, haystack) with length caps and a post-hoc 200ms check. Only tests/unit/regex-safe.test.mjs uses it; no production code does.
- `toast-notifications.js` — Renderer script that defines window.showToast and window.cupnetToast. Five HTML pages include it with a <script> tag; the styles are in shared-dark-theme.css.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/misc-ipc.js` — Uptime, splash, version, ui-pref get/set, check-ip-geo, homepage, TLS/site-info popover (buildTabTlsInfo reads db.getProxyProfileEncrypted and db.queryRequests), CORS bypass toggle, get-direct-ip (hidden BrowserWindow in partition 'direct-ip-check' loading https://ipinfo.io/json), window switcher (get-open-windows with capturePage thumbnails and a focus MRU list, focus-window-by-id), omnibox overlay show/hide/update/select/dismiss.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/omnibox-history-ipc.js` — Omnibox + history IPC (trusted-sender guarded): omnibox-suggest, omnibox-zero-suggest, omnibox-navigate (classify → search/url/internal, dispositions current|new-tab|isolated-tab, shortcut learning), omnibox-delete-url, history-list/count/stats, history-delete-url/visit, history-clear(range), history-get/set-settings (broadcasts searchEngine + omniboxInlineComplete to the shell), history-recently-closed, history-reopen, history-open-url, open-history-window; native menus url-bar-context-menu (Paste and go / copy / edit / Copy page URL) and nav-history-menu (Back/Forward right-click → navigationHistory entries); read-only get-search-engine for preload-view. Subscribes to history-service.onChanged → 'history-changed' to the History window + shell and rebuilds the app menu when the recently-closed ring changes
- `main-process/services/history-service.js` — `createHistoryService({db, settingsStore, loadSettings, saveSettings, tabManager, afterTabCreated, getCurrentSessionId, safeCatch})`: recordVisit (honours historyRecordIsolatedTabs), updateTitle, suggest(q, limit) → {rows ranked by omnibox-model, shortcut}, zeroSuggest → {recent, topSites}, recordShortcut, deleteUrl/deleteVisit, clear(hour|day|week|all), list/count/stats, purge() (historyRetentionDays, run at startup), recently-closed ring (10; noteClosedTab from tab-manager.closeTab with the navigationHistory entries) + reopenClosedTab (createTab in the original cookie group, falls back to shared if the group is gone, `navigationHistory.restore({entries,index})`), onChanged (debounced 150 ms). Settings keys: historyRetentionDays (90), historyRecordIsolatedTabs (true), omniboxInlineComplete (true) in settings-store.normalizeHistorySettings
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/diagnostics-ipc.js` — get-app-metrics = app.getAppMetrics() plus getPidMetrics for the AzureTLS worker (mitmProxy.worker.proc, _workerType 'go'|'node') and the IVAC scout process.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/settings-toolbar-ipc.js` — All settings mutation IPC and the synchronous preload handlers get-device-permissions-sync, get-paste-unlock-sync and get-anti-anti-debug-sync. After import or reset, applySettingsSideEffects re-applies pasteUnlock, traffic filters, device permissions, MITM CORS, the browser-settings-updated broadcast and reattachActivityMonitorToAllTabs.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/launcher-ipc.js` — Opens tool windows (proxy manager, console viewer, page analyzer, notes, credentials, IVAC scout). Console history and export (save dialogs -> fs.writeFileSync), console_logs DB queries, the MITM debug level, and cupnet-log injection.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/tracking-ipc.js` — ipcMain.on report-mouse-activity / report-tab-pointer / report-tab-click / report-tab-typing-end / report-tab-scroll-end. Maps sender webContents to a tab through ctx._wcIdToTabId (filled by cdp-network-logging) and calls ctx.requestScreenshot({skipRateLimit:true}).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/toolbar-activity-badge-ipc.js` — ipcMain.on('reset-toolbar-activity-badge') -> mainWindow 'toolbar-activity-badge-reset'.
- `main-process/services/main-window.js (neighbour)` — createMainWindow: saved bounds, preload.js, registerTrustedShell. On did-finish-load it waits for mitmStartPromise (up to 120s), then enableIpGeoChecks, creates the first tab, and runs setupNetworkLogging and interceptor.attachToSession. Also confirmExitDialog, and a close handler that sets forceAppQuit and saves bounds.
- `main-process/services/profile-gate-window.js (neighbour, new)` — Frameless profile picker (profile-gate.html, preload.js). openMainAfterLogin and openProfileSwitchGate (hides the main window). When the gate closes with no main window it calls app.quit().
- `electron-app/app.js` — Legacy 5908-line monolith. Nothing references it (checked with rg across js/json/scripts/tests/package.json). It is still packaged because build.files='**/*'. Do not edit it; delete it or move it out of the build.

**Key flows:**

- BOOT: main.js resolveUserDataDir() -> loadOrGenerateCA(<dir>/mitm-ca) -> sets NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS -> require main-process/index.js -> requestSingleInstanceLock (unless CUPNET_E2E=1) -> error hooks -> attachMainProcess(). At module time, before whenReady, attachMainProcess runs applyCupnetEarlyChromiumFlags, installConsoleCapture (patches stdout/stderr), createIpcBatchMessenger, settingsStore.configure, createProxyNotifyBroadcast, the fingerprint/screenshot/CDP-logging/traffic/proxy services, dSub/dMain, createSubWindowsApi, createMainWindowApi and createProfileGateWindowApi. It also registers app 'certificate-error' when STEALTH<3 and 'before-quit', 'will-quit' and 'window-all-closed'.
- WHEN READY (cupnet-runtime L831): applyRuntimeAppIcon -> require db and tab-manager -> db.init() -> runProfileDataMigration + ensureProfileSetupFlag -> db.purgeConsoleLogsOlderThanDays(14) -> _syncDnsOverrideHostsSet -> loadSettings() (global settings.json, because no profile is active yet) -> vaultSession.initFromSettings + onLock (sends 'credentials-locked' and 'profile-session-changed') -> tabManager.applyDevicePermissions / setProxyAll(null) -> initSysLogIPC ('get-sys-log') -> require request-interceptor; setResolveTabIdFromDetails / setOnRuleMatch (broadcastInterceptRuleMatched + maybeLogMockToNetworkActivity) / setBreakpointHandler (breakpoint-service + openBreakpointWindow) / setTrafficMode -> createAppContext -> globalThis.__cupnetAppContext -> setImmediate loads har-exporter.
- MITM START: when STEALTH<5, mitmStartPromise = startMitmWithRetry(3). Each attempt runs _clearMitmProxyInstance, waits 500ms*(attempt-1), then proxySvc.startMitmProxy() (new MitmProxy on networkPolicy.mitmPort 8877, browser 'chrome_120', worker azure-tls-worker.js or the Go worker), then wireMitmAfterStart(proxy). wireMitmAfterStart: trustMitmCA = setCertificateVerifyProc(cb(0)) on the group-1 partition, 'persist:cupnet-shared' and defaultSession, plus tabManager.setTrustMitmCA and setMitmTabUpstreamCleanup; applyEffectiveTrafficMode(..., {source:'startup', force:true, skipNotify:true}); proxy.setBrowser(settings.tlsProfile||'chrome'); syncDnsOverridesToMitm; setGlobalCorsEnabled(corsBypassEnabled). On success: _markMitmReady (mitmState='ready', ipGeoChecksEnabled=true, clearIpGeoFailCache, notifyMitmReady -> 'mitm-ready-changed', maybeLogStartupMetrics), then startMitmMonitoringIntervals. On failure: _markMitmFailed (mitmState='failed', mitmError). When STEALTH>=5 MITM is skipped and marked ready.
- MONITORING: startMitmMonitoringIntervals runs a 1s timer. It reads mitmProxy.getStats() into stabilityMetrics.gauges (queueDepth, workerRestarts, db write-queue depths), emits emitSloWarnOnce (networkPolicy.slo thresholds, 30s cooldown, sysLog 'stability'), calls syncAppContextSnapshot, requests a 'network-pending' screenshot when |pending delta| >= tracking.pendingDeltaThreshold, and sends 'mitm-stats-update' to all windows when the stats signature changes. A separate 5s timer handles 'mouse-activity' screenshots (tracking.onMouseActivity && isWindowActive && moved within 6s). Both timers are cleared in their own before-quit listeners.
- MITM RESTART: IPC 'mitm-restart' (mitm-tls-resilience-ipc) -> ctx.restartMitmProxy -> _clearMitmProxyInstance, wait 300ms, startMitmProxy, wireMitmAfterStart, _markMitmReady, startMitmMonitoringIntervals, applyEffectiveTrafficMode({source:'mitm-restart'}), reload the active tab, notifyProxyStatus. There is no mutex, mitmStartPromise is not replaced, and activeExtPorts keep a reference to the old MitmProxy as parent.
- IPC SCOPE: after MITM kick-off, inside whenReady, ipcScope = buildIpcScopeObject(ipc-scope-key-list.json, ipcScopeWritableKeys{actProxy, activeFingerprint, compareResult, connectedProfileId, connectedProfileName, connectedResolvedVars, currentSessionId, hadLoggingBeenStopped, isLoggingEnabled, lastMouseMoveTime, logEntryCount, persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl}, ipcScopeGet, ipcScopeSet). Every set also calls syncAppContextSnapshot(). Extra keys: ipcScope.onProfileLoggedIn -> profileGateApi.openMainAfterLogin(); ipcScope.onProfileSwitchGate -> openProfileSwitchGate(). Then registerAllMainIpc(ipcScope). Handlers read ctx.X lazily, so db, harExporter, mitmProxy and similar can be null at call time.
- WINDOWS: if extPortsStore has no ports, a default port is saved (port 7777, login 'cupnet', 8-char password, autoStart false). app 'browser-window-created' -> attachWindowSwitcherHotkey + attachRendererConsoleForward ('console-message' level>=2 goes to console and sysLog 'renderer-console'; all levels when CUPNET_RENDERER_CONSOLE_VERBOSE=1). profileSession.shouldSkipProfileGate() (CUPNET_E2E=1 or CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE=1) ? createMainWindow() : profileGateApi.createProfileGateWindow(). setImmediate: if settings.onboardingComplete===false -> createOnboardingWindow(). macOS 'activate' recreates the main window or the gate.
- PROFILE LOGIN (profile-ipc, neighbour): finishProfileLogin -> vaultSession.unlockVault -> applyProfileScopedStores(vaultId) = settingsStore and uiPrefsStore.setActiveProfileVaultId + reload -> migrations -> broadcastProfileSessionChanged -> ctx.onProfileLoggedIn -> openMainAfterLogin (closes the gate, creates or shows the main window). profile-logout and profile-open-switch-gate reset the stores to global (null).
- MAIN WINDOW FIRST LOAD (main-window.js): did-finish-load -> await mitmStartPromise (120s cap) -> enableIpGeoChecks -> setPasteUnlock/applyTrafficFilters -> tabManager.createTab(persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl, getNewTabUrl()) -> setupNetworkLogging + interceptor.attachToSession -> currentSessionId = tab.sessionId -> bootstrapSessionTrafficMetaLog (cupnet-network-meta-log) -> startLogStatusUpdater (5s 'update-log-status') -> db.deleteEmptySessions -> 'init-settings' -> notifyProxyProfilesList -> notifyProxyStatus -> replays the DNS and intercept batches.
- SETTINGS SAVE: a handler calls ctx.loadSettings() (returns the cached object, which is mutated in place) -> changes fields -> ctx.saveSettings(s) (sets _cached; the target path is resolved immediately; writeFileSecureAsync after 300ms; currentProxy is stripped). Import: mergeImportedSettings (whitelisted keys) -> saveSettings -> applySettingsSideEffects. Reset: buildFactoryResetSettings -> the same path. save-security-settings also calls vaultSession.initFromSettings + bumpActivity.
- QUIT: 'before-quit' -> if !forceAppQuit: preventDefault, confirmExitDialog (skipped with CUPNET_E2E=1), forceAppQuit=true, destroy all windows except main, app.quit(). The main window 'close' handler asks the same dialog when forceAppQuit is false.
