'will-quit' -> stopIvacScoutProcess, ipcBatch.disposePendingBatches, clear logStatusInterval, settingsStore.cancelPendingSave (drops the pending write), consoleCapture flushPersistQueue + dispose, clear notifyTabsDebounce, mitmProxy.stop, proxy-chain closeAnonymizedProxy, stop activeExtPorts + db.endSessionAsync, tabManager.destroyAll, db.endSessionAsync(currentSessionId) + db.close() (close drains the write queue synchronously and runs wal_checkpoint), then sys-log flushOnExit. 'window-all-closed' -> app.quit() on every platform.
- CERTIFICATES: Chromium sessions trust every certificate through setCertificateVerifyProc(cb(0)). The app 'certificate-error' handler additionally accepts certificates whose issuer/subject text matches /CupNet MITM/i, and hosts 127.0.0.1, localhost and _dnsOverrideHosts (enabled rows of db.getDnsOverrides()). syncDnsOverridesToMitm is wrapped so the host set refreshes after DNS changes.
- TRACKING SCREENSHOTS: preload-view.js DOM listeners (pointerdown/mousemove/input/keydown Enter/blur/scroll) -> ipcRenderer.send report-tab-* -> tracking-ipc resolves tabId through _wcIdToTabId, requires the tab to be active, stores _lastPointerByTabId, and calls requestScreenshot({reason:'click'|'typing-end'|'scroll-end', skipRateLimit:true}). The screenshot-service (neighbour) writes to the DB and increments logEntryCount.

**IPC channels:**

- renderer->main invoke (misc-ipc): get-uptime, consume-startup-splash, get-app-version, get-ui-pref, set-ui-pref, check-ip-geo, get-homepage, set-homepage, get-tab-tls-info, toggle-site-info-popover, site-info-popover-copy-url, site-info-popover-clear-cookies, site-info-popover-open-log, toggle-cors-bypass, get-cors-bypass-status, get-direct-ip, get-open-windows, focus-window-by-id, set-window-switcher-overlay-visible, omnibox-overlay-show, omnibox-overlay-hide, omnibox-overlay-update
- renderer->main send (misc-ipc): site-info-popover-close, omnibox-overlay-select, omnibox-overlay-dismiss
- main->renderer (misc-ipc): focus-request-url (log viewer), cors-bypass-status (main), omnibox-overlay-select and omnibox-overlay-dismiss (relayed to main)
- renderer->main invoke (diagnostics-ipc): get-app-metrics
- renderer->main invoke (settings-toolbar-ipc): onboarding-complete, reset-onboarding-wizard, get-settings-all, save-security-settings, save-search-engine-settings, save-activity-monitor-settings, export-settings-json, import-settings-json, reset-settings-to-defaults, set-paste-unlock, set-max-tabs-before-warning, set-toolbar-height, set-bottom-chrome-height, set-right-chrome-width, save-toolbar-tools-placement, save-toolbar-tools-mini-align, set-auto-screenshot, get-tracking-settings, save-tracking-settings, save-filter-patterns, save-traffic-opts, get-traffic-opts, enumerate-media-devices, save-device-permissions
- renderer->main sendSync (settings-toolbar-ipc, from preload-view): get-device-permissions-sync, get-paste-unlock-sync, get-anti-anti-debug-sync
- main->renderer (settings-toolbar-ipc): browser-settings-updated, cors-bypass-status (main window)
- renderer->main invoke (launcher-ipc): open-proxy-manager, open-console-viewer, get-console-history, get-debug-mitm-level, set-debug-mitm-level, save-console-log, save-console-log-json, save-console-log-csv, get-console-logs-db, get-console-log-sessions, find-requests-near-ts, cupnet-log, open-page-analyzer, open-notes-window, open-credentials-window, notes-embed-request, open-ivac-scout, get-ivac-scout-context, run-ivac-scout, stop-ivac-scout, open-ivac-dump-folder
- main->renderer (launcher-ipc): notes-embed-block (notes window)
- renderer->main send (tracking-ipc, from preload-view tabs): report-mouse-activity, report-tab-pointer, report-tab-click, report-tab-typing-end, report-tab-scroll-end
- renderer->main send (toolbar-activity-badge-ipc): reset-toolbar-activity-badge -> main->renderer toolbar-activity-badge-reset (main window)
- renderer->main invoke (sys-log.initIPC): get-sys-log. main->renderer sys-log-entry (dead: registerSysLogWindow is never called)
- main->renderer (cupnet-runtime): update-log-status (main + log viewers; 5s poller + sendLogStatus), mitm-stats-update (all windows, 1s when changed), credentials-locked and profile-session-changed (main, on vault auto-lock)
- main->renderer (console-capture): console-log (console viewer, 60ms batches)
- main->renderer (ipc-batch-messenger): new-log-entry-batch (log viewers), intercept-rule-matched-batch (main + log viewers + rules), dns-rule-matched-batch (main + DNS manager), tls-profile-changed (all windows)
- main->renderer (proxy-notify-broadcast, wired in runtime): proxy-status-changed, mitm-ready-changed, proxy-profiles-list
- Related channels whose ctx comes from this subsystem (other handler files): mitm-ready-state, ui-first-paint, ui-long-task-count (mitm-startup-ipc: startupMetrics/maybeLogStartupMetrics), mitm-restart / mitm-get-stats / stability-metrics-snapshot / stability-slo-status (mitm-tls-resilience-ipc: restartMitmProxy/stabilityMetrics), profile-* (profile-ipc: onProfileLoggedIn/onProfileSwitchGate)

**Data stores:**

- <userData>/settings.json (global) and <userData>/profiles/<vaultId>/settings.json (per profile after login): settings-store, atomic 0600 writes. Keys: _version, onboardingComplete, lastLogPath, filterPatterns, homepage, maxTabsBeforeWarning, pasteUnlock, effectiveTrafficMode, tracking{onUserClick,onPageLoadComplete,onNetworkPendingChange,onMouseActivity,onTypingEnd,onScrollEnd,onRuleMatchScreenshot,pendingDeltaThreshold,cooldownMs,maxPerMinute}, trafficOpts{trafficEnabled,blockImages,blockCSS,blockFonts,blockMedia,blockWebSocket,tlsPassthroughDomains,captchaWhitelist}, devicePermissions{cameraMode,cameraPriority,cameraDisabledIds,cameraDisabledLabels,microphoneMode,microphonePriority}, activityMonitorEnabled, activityMonitorRateLimit, activityMonitorStorageStackTraces, searchEngine, searchEngineCustomUrl, corsBypassEnabled, toolbarToolsPlacement, toolbarToolsMiniAlign, vaultAutoLockMinutes, trafficLogEncryption (off|redact|encrypt), trafficLogRawMode, allowPlaintextExport, tlsProfile (not in defaults; read at MITM start), autoScreenshot (legacy). currentProxy and capmonster are stripped.
- <userData>/ui-prefs.json and <userData>/profiles/<vaultId>/ui-prefs.json: ui-prefs-store free-form key/value (e.g. uiTheme, settingsUiScale, settingsSidebarCollapsed, cupnet-quick-links-v2)
- <userData>/window-state.json: window-state-store, keys 'main' and 'logViewer' -> {x,y,width,height,isMaximized}
- <userData>/ext-ports.json: ext-ports-store {ports:[{port,name,login,password,autoStart}]}; a default port 7777 is created on first run
- <userData>/critical.log (+ .1 rotation): sys-log critical entries and flushOnExit dump
- <userData>/mitm-ca/ca-key.pem (0600) and ca-cert.pem (0644): the path is computed by main.js without Electron
- <userData>/cupnet.db (better-sqlite3, db.js): tables used here are console_logs (insertConsoleLogsBatchAsync, queryConsoleLogs, getConsoleLogSessionsSummary, purgeConsoleLogsOlderThanDays(14)), requests (insertRequestAsync for mocks, queryRequests, findRequestsNearTimestamp), sessions (endSessionAsync, deleteEmptySessions), dns_overrides (getDnsOverrides), proxy profiles (getProxyProfileEncrypted), plus db.getWriteQueueStats for gauges
- <userData>/profile-migration-state.json (profile-data-migration, called at startup)
- In-memory: sys-log _entries (2000), console-capture buffer (10000), ipc-batch recent intercept (120) and DNS (150) events, stabilityMetrics.hist.requestLatencyMs (500), _lastPointerByTabId, _wcIdToTabId, logViewerInitSessions, activeExtPorts/extPortErrors, globalThis.__cupnetAppContext
- Electron session partitions touched: tabManager.partitionForGroup(1), 'persist:cupnet-shared', defaultSession (all with verify proc = accept), 'direct-ip-check' (get-direct-ip, mode direct)

**Conventions:**

- Project rule: in MITM traffic mode never use session.protocol.handle('http'/'https') for interception. Interception runs on the MITM path (request-interceptor.js planMitmIntercept/finalizeMitmInterceptResponse, called from mitm-proxy.js) because the JS/fetch path is detectable by Cloudflare/Turnstile. Prefer implementing outbound HTTP behaviour in MITM rather than in Chromium. All user-facing UI text must be English (many code comments are Russian; that is fine).
- Do not use global stealth-breaking Chromium flags such as --ignore-certificate-errors or WebGL/WebRTC switches (comment at cupnet-runtime L98-103). Certificate trust goes through setCertificateVerifyProc and 'certificate-error' only.
- Adding a ctx field for handlers takes four edits: (1) add the key to ipc-scope-key-list.json, (2) add a `case` to ipcScopeGet in cupnet-runtime.js L1184-1340, (3) if writable, add it to ipcScopeWritableKeys (L1181) and to ipcScopeSet, and to the `writ` set in scripts/gen-ipc-scope-accessors.cjs, (4) optionally regenerate _ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js. The .inc file is not loaded; the runtime switch is hand-maintained.
- A new IPC domain goes in main-process/ipc/handlers/<domain>-ipc.js, exporting register<Domain>Ipc(ctx), and is added to register-main-ipc.js in the right order. Never register the same channel twice: ipcMain.handle throws.
- Privileged channels must be wrapped with guardIpcHandler(channel, fn) or guardIpcListener from main-process/ipc/ipc-trusted-sender.js. Every new BrowserWindow must call registerTrustedShell (use sub-windows' _spawnTrustedToolWindow). Tab WebContentsViews call registerTabView and load preload-view.js; shell and tool windows load preload.js (preload-shell.js is an alias).
- Settings: always use ctx.loadSettings() -> mutate -> ctx.saveSettings(s). Normalize with the settingsStore.normalize* helpers. Add new keys to SETTINGS_DEFAULTS and materializeSettingsFromRaw, and to mergeImportedSettings.keys only when they are safe to import. Never persist currentProxy.
- Runtime services are factories: create*Service(depsBag). Mutable runtime state is passed as getter/setter closures (getX: () => x, setX: v => { x = v }) or accessor properties on the dSub/dMain bags. Never capture the value itself.
- Errors: use safeCatch({module, eventCode:'area.thing.failed', context}, err, level) instead of empty catch blocks. sysLog(level, module, msg) with level info|warn|error|critical.
- Main-to-renderer broadcasts that can fire at high rates go through ipc-batch-messenger (the *-batch channels). Check !win.isDestroyed() before webContents.send.
- Windows persist bounds through window-state-store under a key. Internal pages are file:// URLs built by getInternalPageUrl('new-tab'|'settings'|'guide'); cupnet:// aliases map to them.
- Env flags: CUPNET_E2E=1 (no single-instance lock, no exit dialog, no profile gate), CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE=1, CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL=N (bisect: >=1 disables paste-unlock, >=3 disables certificate-error, >=4 disables trustMitmCA, >=5 skips MITM, >=6 drops the tab preload, >=7 drops proxy-bypass-list), CUPNET_RENDERER_CONSOLE_VERBOSE=1, CUPNET_DEVTOOLS=1, CUPNET_DISABLE_UA_SANITIZE=1, CUPNET_DEBUG_MITM (runtime via set-debug-mitm-level).

**Gotchas:**

- The authoritative state lives in the closure `let`s of attachMainProcess(). appCtx / globalThis.__cupnetAppContext is only a snapshot, refreshed by syncAppContextSnapshot() on ipcScope sets, MITM state changes and the 1s stats tick. Window changes made through dSub setters do not trigger a sync, and without MITM there is no tick, so the snapshot can go stale.
- ipcScope getters are lazy. ctx.db, ctx.tabManager and ctx.interceptor are set in whenReady before IPC registration. ctx.harExporter is null until setImmediate, and ctx.mitmProxy is null until MITM starts, after a failure, or during a restart. Handlers must null-check.
- Any key missing from ipcScopeGet silently returns undefined (default branch). Handlers currently read two such keys: ctx.net (log-compare-execute-ipc.js:489, the net.fetch fallback of execute-request) and ctx.getMainWindow (cookies-dns-ipc.js:420, open-devtools). Assigning a read-only ctx key throws TypeError (strict mode, getter only).
- scripts/gen-ipc-scope-accessors.cjs emits `return` in the setter cases. If its output is pasted over the runtime switch (as ipc/README.md suggests), the syncAppContextSnapshot() after the switch is skipped. Keep `break`.
- main.js computes the CA directory itself (~/Library/Application Support/CupNet, %APPDATA%/CupNet, ~/.config/CupNet). It ignores --user-data-dir (E2E uses a temp dir, so E2E runs use and write the real user's mitm-ca) and XDG_CONFIG_HOME. It also runs before the single-instance lock, so two first-run launches race to generate the CA files.
- NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS set at runtime in main.js does not affect the main process's own Node TLS. It only reaches child processes, via mitm-proxy.js L332 baseEnv for the Node worker.
- Settings are profile-scoped only after login. At whenReady, loadSettings() reads the GLOBAL settings.json. vaultSession.initFromSettings, the startup TLS profile (proxy.setBrowser), CORS and onboarding are all decided from the global file. applyProfileScopedStores on login reloads the cache but re-applies nothing else (tlsProfile, corsBypassEnabled, trafficOpts, pasteUnlock, device permissions, vault auto-lock).
- settingsStore.saveSettings resolves the file path at call time on purpose (profile-switch race fix). will-quit calls cancelPendingSave(), which DROPS a save queued in the last 300ms.
- loadSettings() mutates and returns the shared cached object. Handlers mutate it in place before saveSettings, so a validation failure after mutation leaves the cache changed but unsaved.
- Several 'before-quit' listeners exist: the confirm-exit handler at L1442 plus two timer-clearing listeners registered later in startMitmMonitoringIntervals. event.preventDefault() in the first does not stop the others.
- setCertificateVerifyProc(cb(0)) is installed on defaultSession as well. Tool windows, net.fetch/net.request on the default session, and every tab partition accept ANY certificate, not just the CupNet CA. That includes tlsPassthroughDomains such as challenges.cloudflare.com, whose TLS goes directly to the origin. The Go worker also sets InsecureSkipVerify=true (azuretls-go/request.go:30), so no layer validates upstream certificates.
- Tab pages (arbitrary websites) get window.electronAPI from preload-view.js, which exposes misc/launcher channels (get-direct-ip, check-ip-geo, get-app-version, get-uptime, consume-startup-splash, set-ui-pref, open-proxy-manager), proxy channels (get-current-proxy, get-proxy-profiles, connect-proxy-template), tab and cookie channels (get-tabs, set-tab-proxy, isolate-tab, create-cookie-group) and ext-proxy:*. None of these handlers check the sender.
- In the WIP tree preload-view.js no longer exposes the settings APIs (getSettingsAll, saveSecuritySettings, getHomepage, and others). settings.html is still loaded in a tab through tabManager.navigate(getInternalPageUrl('settings')) with preload-view.js, and settings-renderer.js calls api.getSettingsAll() unguarded.
- ipcMain.on sync handlers (get-device-permissions-sync, get-paste-unlock-sync, get-anti-anti-debug-sync) must always set event.returnValue, or the renderer hangs. They run before any page script (preload).
- tracking-ipc handlers rely on ctx._wcIdToTabId, which cdp-network-logging.setupNetworkLogging fills. Tabs never passed through setupNetworkLogging produce no tracking screenshots.
- getInternalPageUrl builds `file://${path.join(...)}`. On Windows that gives backslashes and no third slash, and in packaged builds the path points inside app.asar. launcher 'open-ivac-dump-folder' creates `<_cupnetRoot>/_debug`, which is inside the read-only asar when packaged.
- networkPolicy.mitmPort (8877) is the listening port. startMitmProxy returns the existing instance when d.getMitmProxy() is set, which is why restart must clear it first.
- console-capture persists EVERY stdout/stderr line to the console_logs SQLite table in plaintext. This happens even while REC/logging is off and ignores trafficLogEncryption. The table is purged after 14 days at startup.
- The default external port log message says 9001 while the port created is 7777 (cupnet-runtime L1373-1376).

**Tests:**

- Unit tests (vitest, config vitest.config.js, include tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs). Covering this subsystem: tests/unit/utils.test.mjs (13), console-capture.test.mjs (4), regex-safe.test.mjs (6), ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs (2), cors-settings.test.mjs (2; settings-store merge/factory reset only), sys-log.test.mjs (2). Run: `npx vitest run --no-cache tests/unit/utils.test.mjs tests/unit/console-capture.test.mjs tests/unit/regex-safe.test.mjs tests/unit/ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs tests/unit/cors-settings.test.mjs tests/unit/sys-log.test.mjs`. All 29 passed on 2026-09-23 (vitest 2.1.9 warns that the Vite CJS Node API is deprecated).
- Legacy node:test scripts: `node tests/test-utils.js`, `node tests/test-safe-catch.js` (mocks electron through require.cache), `node tests/test-traffic-mode-router.js`. All legacy scripts run with `npm test` (bash tests/run-all.sh); test-db.js needs the Electron binary with ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1.
- E2E (Playwright + Electron): `npm run test:e2e:smoke` or `npm run test:e2e`. tests/e2e/helpers.js launchCupnet() sets CUPNET_E2E=1 and --user-data-dir=<tmp> and reads globalThis.__cupnetAppContext (mitm.ready, modules.tabManager, proxy, modules.harExporter). Relevant specs: cupnet.e2e.spec.js, windows.e2e.spec.js, settings.e2e.spec.js, onboarding.e2e.spec.js, traffic-proxy.e2e.spec.js (uses getSysLog). CUPNET_SKIP_E2E=1 skips them.
- No tests exist for: cupnet-runtime lifecycle (MITM retry/restart, quit sequence), settings-store load/save/migration/profile paths, ui-prefs-store, window-state-store, ipc-batch-messenger, network-helpers, os-process-metrics, build-ipc-scope-delegates, or the misc/diagnostics/settings-toolbar/launcher/tracking/badge handlers. The most valuable cheap addition is a static test that every `ctx.<key>` used in main-process/ipc/handlers/*.js exists in ipc-scope-key-list.json and that the list matches the runtime switch; it would have caught ctx.net and ctx.getMainWindow.

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Electron runtime (`package.json devDependencies`) — now: electron ^34.0.0 (installed 34.5.8); Electron 34 is out of support; only the latest 3 majors get fixes. Upgrade to a supported major (check releases.electronjs.org). Watch the console-message and other breaking changes below.
- webContents 'console-message' positional args (level, message, line, sourceId) (`main-process/cupnet-runtime.js attachRendererConsoleForward L1386`) — now: win.webContents.on('console-message', (event, level, message, line, sourceId) => ...) with numeric level comparisons; Deprecated since Electron 35 in favour of event.level ('info'|'warning'|'error'|'debug'), event.message, event.lineNumber and event.sourceId. With string levels the `level < 2` / `>= 3` checks silently stop forwarding.
- electron-builder (`package.json devDependencies`) — now: ^24.13.0 (installed 24.13.3); Current major is 26.x. Needed for newer Electron, notarization and Windows signing improvements.
- @electron/rebuild (`package.json devDependencies`) — now: ^3.6.0 (installed 3.7.2); 4.x is current; needed for newer Electron ABI (better-sqlite3, argon2, ffi-napi).
- vitest (`package.json devDependencies`) — now: ^2.1.9; The test run prints a Vite CJS Node API deprecation. Upgrade to 3.x+ and consider an ESM config (vitest.config.js is CommonJS).
- Node engines range (`package.json engines`) — now: "node": ">=20 <24"; Node 20 reached EOL in April 2026 and Node 24 is the active LTS. Widen to >=22 and allow 24.
- argon2 (`package.json dependencies`) — now: ^0.41.1; Newer 0.4x releases exist; verify prebuilt binaries for the target Electron ABI when upgrading.
- Default MITM browser profile (`main-process/services/proxy-service.js startMitmProxy L127 (called from runtime startup)`) — now: browser: 'chrome_120'; Chrome 120 (late 2023) fingerprint as the constructor default. The runtime immediately calls proxy.setBrowser(settings.tlsProfile||'chrome'), but the default should match a current Chrome, and the alias mapping for 'chrome' should be verified in mitm-proxy / the Go worker.
- Hard-coded IP lookup endpoint (`main-process/ipc/handlers/misc-ipc.js get-direct-ip L215 (also proxy-service)`) — now: https://ipinfo.io/json; Single provider with no fallback and no timeout. Anonymous rate limiting returns HTML and the lookup fails.
- Deprecated constants and shims still used (`utils.js SEARCH_ENGINE; register-all.js noteMainProcessRegistersIpcBelow; settings-toolbar-ipc 'set-auto-screenshot' (writes s.autoScreenshot)`) — now: @deprecated exports kept; Remove after checking callers. SEARCH_ENGINE is still imported by cupnet-runtime.js.
- Traffic-mode plumbing (`cupnet-runtime currentTrafficMode, settings effectiveTrafficMode, traffic-mode-router.normalizeTrafficMode`) — now: normalizeTrafficMode() always returns 'mitm'; 'browser_proxy' is migrated away; The dead mode switch survives in settings, IPC payloads and appCtx; simplify it.
- Generated accessor file (`main-process/ipc/_ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js + scripts/gen-ipc-scope-accessors.cjs`) — now: Not loaded by anything; duplicates the runtime switch with different setter semantics; Either generate the runtime switch automatically or delete the .inc file to avoid drift.
- macOS signing config (`package.json build.mac`) — now: identity: null, hardenedRuntime: false, gatekeeperAssess: false; Unsigned and not notarized; current macOS Gatekeeper blocks such downloads.

**Production gaps:**

- IPC trust boundary is incomplete: none of the in-scope handlers (misc, launcher, settings-toolbar, diagnostics, tracking) use guardIpcHandler/guardIpcListener. Security-sensitive channels (save-security-settings, import-settings-json, reset-settings-to-defaults, set-homepage, save-traffic-opts, save-filter-patterns, get-console-logs-db, save-console-log*, run-ivac-scout, get-direct-ip) should be restricted to trusted shells. preload-view.js (every website) should expose only what new-tab.html strictly needs, ideally only when location is the internal file:// new-tab page.
- No navigation hardening for trusted windows: there is no app-level 'web-contents-created' hook adding will-navigate / setWindowOpenHandler deny rules for shell and tool windows. If a tool window (notes markdown, log viewer response preview) navigates to a remote URL, that page gets the full preload.js API and stays in _trustedShellIds.
- No crash and health handling: no 'render-process-gone' or 'child-process-gone' handlers, no crashReporter, and uncaughtException is swallowed (the process keeps running in an unknown state). There is no watchdog for a hung MITM worker beyond the stats gauges.
- No auto-update (build.publish=github but no electron-updater), no code signing or notarization (mac identity null, hardenedRuntime false, Windows unsigned), no app.setAppUserModelId on Windows (the Notification import is unused), and no protocol or open-url handling in second-instance.
- Packaging hygiene: build.files='**/*' ships tests/, scripts/, docs, electron-app/app.js (dead 5.9k LOC), debug-main.js, data/, todo.md and the Go sources inside the asar. asarUnpack 'node_modules/**' unpacks every dependency.
- Certificate validation is effectively disabled end to end (Chromium verify proc returns 0 for all certs on all sessions including defaultSession, and the Go worker uses InsecureSkipVerify). For production the proc should accept only certificates chained to the in-memory CupNet CA (compare the fingerprint) and return -3 (default verification) otherwise. The upstream worker should verify by default, with a per-rule opt-out.
- Data-at-rest gaps versus the new encryption work: console_logs stores all stdout/stderr in plaintext; ext-ports.json holds the external-port login and password in plaintext without secure-fs; window-state.json, ext-ports.json, critical.log and mitm-ca are global, not per-profile; the CA private key is stored unencrypted (0600).
- Main-thread blocking: os-process-metrics uses execSync (ps or powershell, up to 2s per PID) on every get-app-metrics call. The main window calls it on every update-log-status tick and the Settings > Performance tab every 3s. ui-prefs-store writes synchronously with fsync on every set-ui-pref. confirmExitDialog uses showMessageBoxSync during OS shutdown or logout.
- Settings robustness: a corrupt settings.json is silently replaced by defaults (onboardingComplete=false) and overwritten on the next save, with no .bak backup. There is no real schema migration framework (CURRENT_SETTINGS_VERSION=1). Pending saves are cancelled instead of flushed on quit.
- Test coverage gaps for the runtime lifecycle, IPC scope consistency and settings persistence (see tests). There is no lint step in CI for undefined ctx keys.
- Observability: sys-log 'sys-log-entry' live push is never wired (registerSysLogWindow is unused). critical.log grows without bound on each exit because flushOnExit appends all error and critical entries again with no rotation.

### data-logging (traffic capture -> SQLite -> log viewer / exports / compare / replay)

Records browser traffic into the per-user SQLite DB (userData/cupnet.db) while "Rec" is on. Sources: MITM proxy HTTP callback (the main source), CDP Network.* per tab (shadow/dedup, WebSocket frames, cache hits), CDP Activity Monitor (console/exceptions/CSP/DOM storage), injected storage stack traces, screenshots, synthetic rows ("SET PROXY/DIRECT", mocks, Request Editor runs). Request headers and bodies are encrypted with the unlocked profile's vault key (AES-256-GCM, CV02 format) or redacted, depending on settings. The same code serves the Log Viewer (live batches plus DB queries, FTS5 search, annotations, sessions), exports (HAR 1.2 streaming, incident bundle JSON with redaction levels, site snapshot ZIP), request diff and session compare, replay and the Request Editor's execute-request, and the screenshot store.

**Files:**

- `db.js` — better-sqlite3 layer. Opens userData/cupnet.db (WAL, foreign_keys=ON, synchronous=NORMAL), createSchema()+migrateSchema() (ad-hoc PRAGMA table_info column checks, no user_version), cached prepared statements (_prepareStmts), write queue enqueueWrite(high|low) with busy retry and fair scheduling. Logging API: sessions (create/end/rename/delete/deleteUnnamed/deleteEmpty/createSessionFromRequestIds*), requests (insertRequest via _prepareLogEntry -> traffic-log-crypto, queryRequests list without bodies, queryRequestsFull/getRequest hydrated, countRequests, ftsSearch, setRequestAnnotation, updateRequest), ws_events (insertWsEvent + bumpWsHandshakeMessageCount, queryWsEvents with ws/wss/http/https URL variants, queryWsEventsBySession), screenshots (insertScreenshot as BLOB, getScreenshotEntriesForSession without image, getScreenshotData), browser_events, console_logs, omnibox history/top hosts, findRequestsNearTimestamp. close() drains the queue and runs wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE). Also holds proxy/rules/dns/notes/credentials tables that belong to other subsystems.
- `main-process/services/traffic-log-crypto.js` — NEW (untracked). prepareRequestLogFields(entry): picks the mode from settings trafficLogEncryption (encrypt|redact|off, default encrypt) and trafficLogRawMode. encrypt+key: encryptCredentialFieldWithKey on each field into *_enc BLOBs, log_encrypted=1. encrypt with no key (vault locked) or redact: bundle-utils redactHeaders/redactBody at level 'Balanced'. hydrateRequestLogRow(row): decrypts *_enc (the plaintext column wins if non-empty), JSON.parses headers that start with '{', returns {...row,_locked:true} when there is no key.
- `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js` — createCdpNetworkLogging(deps) -> setupNetworkLogging(wc, tabId, sessionId). Per-webContents state in a WeakMap (ongoingRequests, ongoingWebsockets, extraInfoQueue, logQueue, staleCleanupTimer). Attaches the debugger (1.3), then Fetch.disable, Network.enable (100MB/10MB buffers when logging), setCacheDisabled:false, clears extra headers, Emulation UA/timezone when a fingerprint is active, and Runtime/Log/DOMStorage.enable when the Activity Monitor is on. In MITM mode it builds CDP 'shadow' entries (_mitmCdpShadow) that are deduped against MITM through mitm-cdp-dedup. finalizeLog -> _processLogQueue (batches of 50) -> db.insert*Async + broadcastLogEntryToViewers. Detaches CDP for devtools-hostile URLs and while DevTools is open. Env CUPNET_AAD_KEEP_CDP=1 keeps CDP attached.
- `main-process/services/mitm-cdp-dedup.js` — (neighbour) MITM vs CDP dedup. tryClaimRid/releaseRid on the X-CupNet-Rid value (_ridMap, max 50k). Fallback (tab|METHOD|url|status) keys with a 12s window: markMitmLogged/markCdpLogged, shouldSkipCdpShadowAsMitmDuplicate/shouldSkipMitmAsCdpDuplicate.
- `main-process/services/logging-state.js` — Global isRecordingEnabled() getter, wired by cupnet-runtime (setGetIsLoggingEnabled). Used by tab-manager.injectStorageActivityMonitor.
- `main-process/services/cupnet-network-meta-log.js` — Synthetic rows type='cupnet', url cupnet://session/proxy|direct, method 'SET PROXY'/'SET DIRECT', status 204, body lines 'Profile:/Mode:/IP:/Location:'. insertCupnetTrafficSnapshot(ctx,snap,{force}), insertCupnetTrafficSnapshotWithGeo (calls ctx.checkCurrentIpGeo), insertSessionBootstrapTrafficRow, isGlobalProxyActive. session-egress-meta parses these rows for HAR/bundle egress info.
- `main-process/services/screenshot-service.js` — createScreenshotService(deps). requestScreenshot({reason,force,meta,skipRateLimit}) checks the tracking.* trigger flags, a per-reason cooldownMs and maxPerMinute, then calls captureScreenshot: active tab capturePage -> PNG, skips inactive window, home page, or a buffer identical to the last one. Stores via db.insertScreenshotAsync only when recording (virtualPath autoscreen::/{reason}/{time}.png in screenshot_meta), broadcasts the entry, sends 'screenshot-taken' to the main window.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/db-logging-ipc.js` — Log DB queries, session management, the Rec toggle state machine (toggle-logging-start / confirm-logging-start / toggle-logging-stop / clear-logs), get-existing-logs merge, and the browser-storage-activity listener. clearBrowserCacheOnRecStart calls tabManager.clearHttpCacheForAllTabSessions, which does not exist.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/trace-har-ipc.js` — export-har (streaming by default; CUPNET_HAR_LEGACY_SYNC, CUPNET_HAR_WS_SIDECAR), export-bundle/import-bundle, list-session-origins/list-site-export-paths/export-site-zip (archiver), diff-requests, jsondiff-format-html (dynamic ESM jsondiffpatch), replay-request (net.fetch).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/log-compare-execute-ipc.js` — Log viewer window open/focus, compare slots and compare-run pairing (uses sub-windows helpers), open-jsonl-file, rules window prefill, the Request Editor open with buildRequestEditorPrefill, and execute-request (AzureTLS worker in MITM mode, net.fetch fallback) with a cancel token map. The result is logged as type 'request-editor'.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/screenshots-ipc.js` — take-screenshot (default reason 'click'), get-screenshot-data (lazy base64), save-screenshot, copy-screenshot.
- `har-exporter.js` — HAR 1.2 export. buildHarEntryFromRow (handles the __b64__ body marker, truncation via CUPNET_HAR_MAX_EXPORT_CHARS, _webSocketMessages + _cupnetWebSocketMessages), exportHar (in memory), exportHarToFileStream (fs.writeSync, compact JSON, batches of <=500 via LIMIT/OFFSET), exportWebSocketSidecarPayload, getExportWsFrameLimit. Requires ./db directly.
- `bundle-utils.js` — Incident bundle cupnet.bundle.v1: buildBundle({db,sessionId,requestIds,protectionLevel Raw|Balanced|Strict}), redaction helpers redactHeaders/redactBody/redactStringValue (now also used by traffic-log-crypto), collectWsEventsForBundle, pickContext (intercept rules and DNS overrides, NOT redacted), validateBundle. CUPNET_BUNDLE_MAX_ROWS (default 50k).
- `diff-utils.js` — compareRequests(left,right): header diff (case-insensitive, accepts objects or JSON strings), body diff with decodeBodyMarker (__b64__ and <base64|mime|..|..>), summary/impact flags. requestMatchKey = 'METHOD path'. Used by diff-requests and compare-run.
- `site-snapshot-export.js` — Site ZIP from logged GET 200 web assets: listOriginsFromSession, listSitePathsForExport, collectSiteFilesByPath (all buffers held in memory), exportSiteZipToStream. Reads the requests table with RAW SQL (no decryption). Zip-slip is guarded by normalizePathSegments/sanitizeSegment.
- `session-egress-meta.js` — (neighbour) getSessionEgressMeta(db,sid) reads the earliest type='cupnet' row via RAW SQL response_body. formatEgressComment feeds HAR creator.comment and bundle meta.egress.
- `main-process/services/proxy-service.js` — (neighbour) startMitmProxy -> MitmProxy({browser:'chrome_120', onRequestLogged}). onRequestLogged is the main HTTP logging path: resolve tabId (active tab fallback), sessionId (entry.sessionId is always null, so currentSessionId), dedup, markMitmLogged, db.insertRequestAsync, broadcast. Also netFetchWithTimeout, used by replay.
- `main-process/services/ipc-batch-messenger.js` — (neighbour) broadcastLogEntryToViewers batches per window (50ms / 200 entries) into 'new-log-entry-batch'.
- `main-process/services/sub-windows.js` — (neighbour) createLogViewerWindow (logViewerInitSessions map, initial update-log-status). Compare helpers: _serializeCompareRowRequest, _requestsForSessionAsc (queryRequestsFull newest 10000, reversed), _reqWithCompareOptions/_stripNoiseHeaders, _pairScore, _confidence, _broadcastCompareUpdated.
- `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js` — (neighbour) Owns the state isLoggingEnabled/currentSessionId/logEntryCount/hadLoggingBeenStopped. Wires the services, db.init + purgeConsoleLogsOlderThanDays(14), lazy harExporter, maybeLogMockToNetworkActivity (type 'mock'), log-status poller (5s), sendLogStatus, bootstrapSessionTrafficMetaLog, and will-quit endSession + db.close. The IPC ctx is backed by _ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js + ipc-scope-key-list.json.
- `tab-manager.js` — (neighbour) createTab creates a sessions row per tab unless existingSessionId is passed. applyCupNetRidHeaderToSession injects the X-CupNet-Rid header via webRequest.onBeforeSendHeaders (one handler per partition). injectStorageActivityMonitor (STORAGE_ACTIVITY_SCRIPT) is gated by logging-state and settings.
- `main-process/services/settings-store.js` — (neighbour) Defaults and normalisation for the logging settings keys (see dataStores).
- `main-process/services/vault-session-service.js` — (neighbour, untracked) getMasterKey(vaultId?) returns the active profile's 32-byte key. lockVault/lockAll zero the key; auto-lock runs on lock-screen/suspend and on idle when no profile is active.
- `preload.js / preload-view.js` — (neighbour) preload.js exposes all the log viewer IPC channels to shell and tool windows. preload-view.js (tab pages) exposes window.cupnetStorageActivity.report -> 'browser-storage-activity' to every website.
- `tests/test-db.js, tests/unit/traffic-log-crypto.test.mjs, tests/unit/har-exporter.test.mjs, tests/e2e/{har-export,fts-search,activity-monitor,duplicate-requests,request-editor}.e2e.spec.js` — Existing coverage (see tests).

**Key flows:**

- STARTUP: app.whenReady -> db=require('../db'); db.init() -> initWithPath(userData/cupnet.db): hardenExistingDir, WAL/FK/NORMAL pragmas, createSchema(), migrateSchema() (adds requests *_enc/log_encrypted columns, drops legacy trace_entries/rules tables), _prepareStmts(), hardenExistingFile(db,-wal,-shm). Then runProfileDataMigration, db.purgeConsoleLogsOlderThanDays(14). har-exporter is loaded in setImmediate, but bundle-utils already requires it eagerly. createMainWindow: tabManager.createTab() -> db.createSessionAsync(proxyRules, tabId) gives tab.sessionId; setupNetworkLogging(firstTab) returns early unless logging, a fingerprint or the activity monitor is on; currentSessionId = tab.sessionId; bootstrapSessionTrafficMetaLog (no-op while not logging); startLogStatusUpdater (5s 'update-log-status'); db.deleteEmptySessions(currentSessionId).
- REC START (db-logging-ipc 'toggle-logging-start'): already on -> {status:'already_on'}. No currentSessionId -> createSessionAsync, clearBrowserCacheOnRecStart (currently a no-op because the tabManager method is missing), isLoggingEnabled=true, await insertSessionBootstrapTrafficRow (geo lookup), then for every tab tab.sessionId=current and setupNetworkLogging. hadLoggingBeenStopped or logEntryCount>0 -> createLoggingModalWindow(modalData) and the modal calls 'confirm-logging-start' {mode:'continue'|'new', renameOld}: 'continue' re-enables and re-attaches all tabs; 'new' renames and ends the old session, creates a new one, resets the counter, writes the bootstrap row, re-attaches. Otherwise it starts silently. 'toggle-logging-stop' sets isLoggingEnabled=false and hadLoggingBeenStopped=true; CDP stays attached but its handler ignores events. 'clear-logs' ends the current session and creates a new one (bootstrap row only if logging), then re-attaches all tabs, which clears their in-flight maps.
- MITM HTTP CAPTURE (primary): mitm-proxy dispatchRequest -> _doRequest (AzureTLS worker) -> this.onRequestLogged({url,method,tabId (CONNECT Proxy-Authorization or rid-derived),sessionId:null,requestId:'mitm_...',cupnetRid,status,requestHeaders,responseHeaders,requestBody,responseBody ('__b64__:'+b64 for binary content types),duration,type}). proxy-service onRequestLogged: tabId falls back to the active tab; sessionId = currentSessionId; recordLatencySample; DNS-rule broadcast; returns if !isLoggingEnabled, !db or !tabId. Dedup: tryClaimRid(cupnetRid) returning false means skip; with no rid, shouldSkipMitmAsCdpDuplicate; if the rid was not claimed, also _seenRequestIds / _lastMitmLogKey (400ms). Then markMitmLogged (synchronously, before the insert), db.insertRequestAsync -> enqueueWrite('high') -> insertRequest -> _prepareLogEntry -> traffic-log-crypto.prepareRequestLogFields -> INSERT ... RETURNING id (the FTS insert trigger fires). On resolve: incrementLogEntryCount and broadcastLogEntryToViewers({...logEntry,id:dbId}) -> ipc-batch 'new-log-entry-batch'. If the insert fails, releaseRid.
- CDP CAPTURE (setupNetworkLogging): the 'message' handler returns unless isLoggingEnabled. In MITM mode (getMitmProxy() truthy) Network.requestWillBeSent creates a shadow entry (redirect hops are ignored). Otherwise it does full redirect handling via redirectResponse + extraInfoQueue. requestWillBeSentExtraInfo merges headers and the Cookie header from associatedCookies. responseReceived and responseReceivedExtraInfo merge response headers. loadingFinished -> Network.getResponseBody (3 tries, 12s timeout; base64 bodies become '<base64|mime|{mime}|{b64}>') -> finalizeLog. loadingFailed sets entry.error. finalizeLog: with a rid, tryClaimRid()===false drops it; a shadow without a rid is dropped if shouldSkipCdpShadowAsMitmDuplicate. Strips X-CupNet-Rid, pushes to logQueue. _processLogQueue: insertRequestAsync / insertWsEventAsync / insertBrowserEventAsync, then markCdpLogged; broadcasts except for websocket_* entries. A stale timer every 60s prunes entries older than 5 minutes.
- WEBSOCKETS: only CDP captures frames. Network.webSocketCreated/FrameSent/FrameReceived/Closed/FrameError -> finalizeLog({type:'websocket_frame'|'websocket_closed'|'websocket_error', direction, url(wss://), data, connectionId=CDP requestId}) -> db.insertWsEventAsync (low priority). Closed/error payloads are '__cupnet_ws_meta__:{json}'. insertWsEvent then runs bumpWsHandshakeMessageCount (UPDATE requests where request_id=connectionId AND type='websocket') and sends 'ws-handshake-message-count' to log viewers. The handshake row itself comes from MITM (type 'websocket', requestId 'mitm_ws_...', https:// URL). queryWsEvents matches it through _wsUrlVariants.
- ACTIVITY MONITOR: settings activityMonitorEnabled && logging -> Runtime.enable, Log.enable, DOMStorage.enable. consoleAPICalled is rate limited per second (activityMonitorRateLimit) with a 'suppressed' summary row; exceptionThrown -> 'exception'; Log.entryAdded -> 'csp-violation'|'log-entry'; DOMStorage.* -> 'ls-set'/'ss-set'/'*-remove'/'*-clear'. All go through finalizeBrowserActivityLog -> insertBrowserEventAsync (low) -> broadcast as type 'browser'|'storage'|'exception'. Storage stack traces (activityMonitorStorageStackTraces): tab-manager injects STORAGE_ACTIVITY_SCRIPT, the page calls window.cupnetStorageActivity.report -> ipcMain.on('browser-storage-activity') -> insertBrowserEventAsync + broadcast.
- SCREENSHOTS: triggers are the take-screenshot IPC (renderer click/typing/scroll/etc.) and did-finish-load in cdp-network-logging (reason 'page-load', active tab only). requestScreenshot checks isScreenshotReasonEnabled(tracking), the per-reason cooldownMs and maxPerMinute, then captureScreenshot: capturePage -> PNG, dedup against the last buffer; if recording, db.insertScreenshotAsync(sessionId, tabId, pageUrl, b64, meta) (stored as a data_blob BLOB), incrementLogEntryCount, broadcast {type:'screenshot', ssDbId, ...}. The Log Viewer lazy-loads the image through 'get-screenshot-data' (id -> base64).
- SYNTHETIC ROWS: cupnet-network-meta-log (SET PROXY/SET DIRECT on Rec start / clear-logs / proxy connect via proxy-ipc and cookies-dns-ipc), cupnet-runtime.maybeLogMockToNetworkActivity (type 'mock', on interceptor rule match), log-compare-execute maybeLog (type 'request-editor', requestId 're-{ts}'), ext-proxy-ipc (external proxy ports, own session with source='external', logged even when Rec is off).
- ENCRYPTION WRITE/READ: insertRequest -> _prepareLogEntry = {...entry, ...prepareRequestLogFields(entry)} (on throw it fails closed and nulls every header/body). Mode 'encrypt' with a key: *_enc = CV02 AES-GCM blobs, plain columns null, log_encrypted=1 (BUT see the suspected bug: the original camelCase fields fall through `??`, so plaintext is also stored). 'encrypt' with no key, or 'redact': Balanced redaction into the plain columns. 'off' or trafficLogRawMode: plaintext. Read: getRequest/queryRequestsFull -> hydrateRequestLogRow. queryRequests (list), ftsSearch, session-egress-meta and site-snapshot-export do NOT hydrate.
- LOG VIEWER READ: 'get-existing-logs' = queryRequests({sessionId:current},5000) + getScreenshotEntriesForSession + getBrowserEventsForSession (10k), sorted by created_at. 'get-db-requests'/'count-db-requests' for paging and filters. 'get-request-detail' -> getRequest (hydrated). 'fts-search' -> requests_fts MATCH on quoted OR tokens. 'get-ws-events' -> queryWsEvents. 'set-request-annotation' -> tag/note/has_note. Session list: 'get-sessions-with-stats' (HAVING count>0), 'rename-session', 'delete-session' (refuses the active one), 'delete-unnamed-sessions', 'create-session-from-request-ids' (copies rows in batches of 80 through getRequest -> requestRowToInsertEntry -> insertRequest).
- EXPORTS: 'export-har' -> showSaveDialog -> harExporter.exportHarToFileStream(filePath, sid): countRequests, queryRequestsFull pages of 500 ordered id DESC, buildHarEntryFromRow (+ queryWsEvents per websocket row), writes '_cupnet' egress meta (session-egress-meta). All synchronous on the main thread. 'export-bundle' -> bundleUtils.buildBundle (queryRequestsFull up to CUPNET_BUNDLE_MAX_ROWS or getRequest per id, normalizeRequestRow, redactRequestRecord by level, collectWsEventsForBundle, pickContext) -> writeFileSync pretty JSON. 'export-site-zip' -> siteSnapshotExport.exportSiteZipToStream (raw SQL, GET 200 web assets, archiver zip level 9). 'import-bundle' -> validateBundle -> returns preview + the full bundle to the renderer.
- DIFF/COMPARE: 'diff-requests' -> getRequest x2 -> diffUtils.compareRequests. 'compare-set-slot' stores the serialized row in comparePair[left|right]. 'compare-run' {level quick|standard|deep, removeNoiseHeaders}: _requestsForSessionAsc for both sessions (newest 10000, hydrated), slices from the anchor position (LEFT_COUNT 12/20/30, RIGHT_SEARCH 60/100/140), greedy best match via _pairScore (exact 'METHOD path' key, or path similarity >= 0.55 in deep mode), compareRequests on each pair, then broadcasts 'compare-updated'. 'jsondiff-format-html' renders a jsondiffpatch HTML delta.
- REPLAY / EXECUTE: 'replay-request' -> getRequest -> JSON.parse(request_headers) -> strips forbidden headers -> ctx.netFetchWithTimeout (Electron net.fetch on defaultSession, which is proxied through MITM) -> returns {status, body, original}. 'execute-request' (Request Editor): in MITM mode with the worker ready it calls mitmProxy.worker.request({browser: tlsProfile||settings.tlsProfile||'chrome', proxy: persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl, disableRedirects:true}) raced against cancelPromise; otherwise net.fetch with an AbortController. If Rec is on, maybeLog inserts a 'request-editor' row and broadcasts it.
- SHUTDOWN: will-quit -> flush console capture, stop MITM, endSessionAsync for external ports and the current session -> db.close(): runs up to 100k queued tasks synchronously, rejects the rest, wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE), close, resets the prepared statements.

**IPC channels:**

- renderer->main invoke (db-logging-ipc): get-db-requests(filters,limit,offset), count-db-requests, get-request-detail(id), set-request-annotation(id,{tag,note}), fts-search(query,sessionId), get-omnibox-top-hosts, get-omnibox-suggestions, record-omnibox-visit, get-sessions, get-sessions-with-stats, get-current-session-id, rename-session, toggle-logging-start(hint), confirm-logging-start({mode,renameOld}), toggle-logging-stop, delete-session, delete-unnamed-sessions, create-session-from-request-ids(ids,name), open-session-in-new-window, get-initial-session-id (keyed by sender wc id), get-log-status, get-existing-logs, get-browser-events(opts), get-ws-events({sessionId,tabId,url,connectionId,limit}), clear-logs
- tab page -> main send (ipcMain.on, NOT trusted-sender guarded): browser-storage-activity (from preload-view window.cupnetStorageActivity.report, exposed to every website)
- renderer->main invoke (trace-har-ipc): export-har(sessionId), export-bundle({sessionId,protectionLevel,requestIds,notes}), list-session-origins, list-site-export-paths({sessionId,origin|origins}), export-site-zip, import-bundle, diff-requests(leftId,rightId), jsondiff-format-html(leftText,rightText), replay-request(id)
- renderer->main invoke (screenshots-ipc): take-screenshot(reason,meta), get-screenshot-data(id), save-screenshot(b64,filename), copy-screenshot(b64)
- renderer->main invoke (log-compare-execute-ipc): open-log-directory(dirPath), select-log-directory, open-log-viewer, open-log-viewer-with-url, open-log-viewer-focus-request, open-compare-viewer, compare-get, compare-set-slot(side,id), compare-clear-slot, compare-run(options), open-jsonl-file, open-rules-window, open-rules-window-with-mock, open-request-editor(idOrPayload), open-request-editor-new-window, execute-request(payload), cancel-execute-request(token)
- main->renderer: new-log-entry-batch (log viewers, batched 50ms/200); update-log-status (main window + log viewers; 5s poller + sendLogStatus + on log viewer did-finish-load); ws-handshake-message-count (log viewers); screenshot-taken (main window); focus-request-url / focus-request-id (live log viewer); compare-updated (compare viewer + log viewers); prefill-intercept-rule (rules window)
- None of the logging/export channels are wrapped with ipc-trusted-sender guardIpcHandler (only cookies/credentials/proxy-url are). They are reachable from any window using preload.js.

**Data stores:**

- SQLite file: app.getPath('userData')/cupnet.db (+ -wal, -shm), hardened to 0600/0700 via secure-fs at init only.
- Table sessions(id, started_at, ended_at, proxy_info, tab_id, notes = user-visible name, source 'browser'|'external', ext_port).
- Table requests(id, session_id FK CASCADE, tab_id, request_id, url, method, status, type, duration_ms, request_headers, response_headers, request_body, response_body, error, host, tag, note, has_note, ws_message_count, request_headers_enc, response_headers_enc, request_body_enc, response_body_enc (CV02 AES-GCM BLOBs), log_encrypted, created_at). Indexes on session, tab, url, status, created, duration, host. No index on request_id.
- FTS5 virtual table requests_fts(url, response_body, request_id UNINDEXED; content='requests') with triggers requests_fts_insert/_delete/_update. The update trigger fires on ANY column update.
- Table ws_events(id, session_id FK CASCADE, tab_id, url, direction send|recv, payload plaintext, connection_id, created_at), index idx_ws_events_conn(session_id, tab_id, url, connection_id).
- Table screenshots(id, session_id FK CASCADE, tab_id, url, data_b64 legacy, data_blob PNG BLOB, screenshot_meta JSON, created_at).
- Table browser_events(id, session_id FK CASCADE, tab_id, event_type console|exception|csp-violation|log-entry|ls-set|ss-set|ls-remove|ss-remove|ls-clear|ss-clear, level, summary <=8000, detail <=2MB, source_url, source_line, origin, created_at).
- Table console_logs(session_id FK CASCADE, ts, level, source, module, stream, text), purged after 14 days at startup.
- Table note_request_links(note_id, request_id FK CASCADE); omnibox_history (per-URL aggregate) + history_visits + omnibox_shortcuts (browsing history, see history-service.js); omnibox top hosts from requests.host only as a fallback
- Settings keys (settings-store): trafficLogEncryption 'encrypt'|'redact'|'off' (default 'encrypt'); trafficLogRawMode (default false; true = plaintext); activityMonitorEnabled (false); activityMonitorRateLimit (100, clamped 50-500); activityMonitorStorageStackTraces (false); filterPatterns (default ['*google.com*','*cloudflare.com*','*analytics*','*tracking*']); tracking.{onUserClick,onPageLoadComplete,onNetworkPendingChange,onMouseActivity,onTypingEnd(default true),onScrollEnd,onRuleMatchScreenshot,cooldownMs 2000 [200-30000],maxPerMinute 12 [1-120]}; lastLogPath; tlsProfile; vaultAutoLockMinutes (15).
- Env vars: CUPNET_HAR_MAX_EXPORT_CHARS | CUPNET_HAR_MAX_BODY_CHARS (default 1 MiB, '0' = unlimited); CUPNET_EXPORT_WS_FRAME_LIMIT (default 5,000,000, max 500M); CUPNET_HAR_EXPORT_BATCH (<=500); CUPNET_HAR_LEGACY_SYNC=1; CUPNET_HAR_WS_SIDECAR=1 (writes *-websocket.json); CUPNET_BUNDLE_MAX_ROWS (50,000); CUPNET_AAD_KEEP_CDP=1; CUPNET_DISABLE_CUPNET_RID=1; CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL (>=1 disables storage inject); CUPNET_DB_BUSY_RETRIES/_BASE_DELAY_MS/_MAX_DELAY_MS; CUPNET_DB_WRITE_QUEUE_MAX_HIGH (20000) / _LOW (5000); CUPNET_TIMEOUT_REPLAY_MS / CUPNET_TIMEOUT_REQUEST_EDITOR_MS (30000).
- In-memory: mitm-cdp-dedup _ridMap (<=50k) / _mitmKeys / _cdpKeys (12s window); cupnet-runtime _seenRequestIds (<=5000), _lastMitmLogKey (MITM_DEDUP_MS=400), logViewerInitSessions (wcId -> sessionId), comparePair/compareResult, logEntryCount; per-webContents CDP state WeakMap; screenshot lastScreenshotBuffer / cooldown map / 60s limiter window.

**Conventions:**

- Hot-path writes go through the *Async wrappers (enqueueWrite). 'high' priority for requests/sessions/annotations; 'low' for ws_events, browser_events, screenshots, console_logs, rule hit counters. Reads are synchronous better-sqlite3 calls.
- Read request rows ONLY through db.getRequest / db.queryRequestsFull, which hydrate and decrypt. Raw getDb().prepare('SELECT ... FROM requests') bypasses decryption (session-egress-meta.js and site-snapshot-export.js currently do this).
- After hydration, request_headers/response_headers can be an OBJECT (log_encrypted rows) or a JSON STRING (other rows). Always parse with a tolerant helper that accepts both (diff-utils.parseHeaders or sub-windows._parseHeadersMaybe style), never a bare JSON.parse.
- Binary body markers stored in body columns: MITM '__b64__:<base64>'; CDP '<base64|mime|<mime>|<base64>>'. Every consumer must handle both (see diff-utils.decodeBodyMarker, site-snapshot parseBase64Body, log-viewer-renderer).
- Synthetic row types: 'cupnet' (url cupnet://session/proxy|direct, method 'SET PROXY'/'SET DIRECT', status 204, body lines 'Profile:/Mode:/IP:/Location:'), 'mock', 'request-editor'. WS meta payload prefix '__cupnet_ws_meta__:'.
- Timestamps are ISO strings from strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now'). created_at is the INSERT time, i.e. the request completion time, not its start.
- Services are factories createXxx({getters...}) with dependency injection through getter functions, e.g. getDb: () => db, because modules load late.
- IPC handlers are registerXxxIpc(ctx) using ctx.ipcMain.handle(channel, ...). ctx is the IPC scope object: a new ctx key must be added to main-process/ipc/ipc-scope-key-list.json AND to the get/set switches in main-process/ipc/_ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js and cupnet-runtime.js. Channel names must not be renamed (preload.js depends on them).
- Live updates reach the Log Viewer only through broadcastLogEntryToViewers (batched 'new-log-entry-batch'). Entries should carry the DB id so 'get-request-detail' works.
- MITM/CDP dedup: X-CupNet-Rid (tryClaimRid) first, then the fallback (tab, METHOD, url, status) window keys. Any new logging source must participate, or duplicate rows will appear.
- Project rules: never use session.protocol.handle for interception in MITM mode; prefer MITM/AzureTLS worker for outbound HTTP (replay-request violates this); all user-facing text in English.
- Every session switch must call setupNetworkLogging for every tab, so that CDP state.sessionId matches currentSessionId.

**Gotchas:**

- traffic-log-crypto is require()'d lazily INSIDE db.js functions (_prepareLogEntry, getRequest, queryRequestsFull) because of a cycle: traffic-log-crypto -> bundle-utils -> har-exporter -> db. Do not hoist it to a top-level require.
- Encryption uses the ACTIVE profile's vault key (vault-session getMasterKey()). When the vault is locked (screen lock, suspend, idle without a profile, e2e tests), mode 'encrypt' silently falls back to lossy, irreversible Balanced redaction. Rows carry no 'redacted' flag and no key id/vault_id. Rows written under profile A decrypt to null under profile B with no indication (_locked is only set when there is no key at all).
- hydrateRequestLogRow prefers the plaintext column over *_enc when both exist and JSON.parses headers only for log_encrypted rows, so header types differ from row to row.
- queryRequests (list endpoint) never returns bodies, except for type='cupnet' session rows via a CASE expression, and is not hydrated. The FTS index, session-egress-meta and site-snapshot-export read plaintext columns. All of these will lose data once the plaintext leak (suspectedBugs #1) is fixed; fix them together.
- MITM onRequestLogged ignores entry.sessionId (always null) and writes to the global currentSessionId, while CDP rows use the per-tab state.sessionId captured at setupNetworkLogging time. Any session switch must re-run setupNetworkLogging for all tabs, which also wipes in-flight CDP requests.
- MITM rows with no tabId are dropped (`if (!tabId || sessionId == null) return`); the tabId falls back to the ACTIVE tab, so background traffic without a CONNECT tab id is misattributed.
- CDP is detached on devtools-opened and for devtools-hostile URLs (devtools-hostile-sites.js); logging is then MITM-only (no WS frames, no Activity Monitor).
- The Activity Monitor enables CDP Runtime/Log domains, a known anti-bot detection vector (Cloudflare/Turnstile); keep it off by default. Also, window.cupnetStorageActivity / electronAPI / cupnetCredentials are exposed to every website by preload-view and are detectable.
- The FTS5 table is external-content with triggers; every UPDATE on requests (annotations, the per-WS-frame ws_message_count bump) deletes and re-inserts the FTS row, including the full response_body.
- Cascades rely on PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON (a per-connection setting). deleteSession explicitly deletes requests/browser_events in a transaction; ws_events/screenshots/console_logs/note links go through FK cascade.
- The write queue runs one task per setImmediate, synchronously. Overflow rejects the promise and logs a warn per item; busy retries re-queue at the front and bypass the cap. db.close() drains up to 100k tasks synchronously during will-quit.
- All exports (HAR stream, bundle, site ZIP) run synchronously in the main process, which also hosts the MITM proxy: a big export stalls browsing.
- get-existing-logs loads only the newest 5000 requests but ALL screenshots and 10k browser events, so the timeline can be lopsided for big sessions.
- tabs created via the 'new-tab' IPC (toolbar +), 'open-settings-tab' and the menu Cmd+Shift+D item get their own new session row and no setupNetworkLogging / interceptor.attachToSession call. Only open-in-new-tab and the Cmd+T / Cmd+Shift+T menu items wire logging.
- Electron allows ONE webRequest.onBeforeSendHeaders per session. tab-manager.applyCupNetRidHeaderToSession (X-CupNet-Rid) replaces it on every createTab for the shared partition, and request-interceptor._removeAllHooks (detachFromSession when the refcount hits 0) removes it.
- screenshot dedup compares against the last PNG buffer globally (across tabs). Captures happen (capturePage) even when not recording; only the DB insert is gated.
- delete-session compares the id with === against currentSessionId; a string id bypasses the guard.
- The log DB also contains credentials/notes/proxy tables; the 'data-logging' functions are only a part of db.js (lines ~595-1410, 2599-2922).

**Tests:**

- tests/test-db.js — node:test suite for db.js (sessions, requests, queries, ws_events incl. https<->wss matching, screenshots, FTS, getSessionsWithStats, async write path, queue stats). Mocks 'electron' and uses initWithPath on a temp file. Run: `npm run test:db` (ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1 Electron binary, because better-sqlite3 is built for the Electron ABI). Also part of `npm test` (tests/run-all.sh). Does NOT cover encryption/hydration with an unlocked key.
- tests/unit/traffic-log-crypto.test.mjs (vitest) — prepareRequestLogFields + hydrateRequestLogRow in isolation with a real vault unlock. Does not go through db.insertRequest, so it misses the plaintext fallthrough bug.
- tests/unit/har-exporter.test.mjs (vitest) — buildHarEntryFromRow with string headers, broken JSON, WS messages, env limits, sidecar. Does not test object (hydrated) headers or the CDP <base64|...> marker.
- Other vitest tests touching this area: tests/unit/omnibox-history.test.mjs, console-capture.test.mjs, vault-auto-lock.test.mjs. Run all with `npm run test:unit` (vitest.config.js includes tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs).
- Playwright e2e (`npm run test:e2e`, scripts/playwright-e2e.mjs): har-export.e2e.spec.js (HAR shape, replay-request), fts-search.e2e.spec.js, activity-monitor.e2e.spec.js (console -> browser_events, ls-set with inject, settings save), duplicate-requests.e2e.spec.js (no MITM/CDP dup rows on httpbin), request-editor.e2e.spec.js. They do not unlock a profile vault, so they exercise the redact fallback, not the encrypt path.
- NO tests for: bundle-utils (redaction levels), diff-utils, site-snapshot-export, cdp-network-logging, mitm-cdp-dedup, screenshot-service, cupnet-network-meta-log, session-egress-meta, db-logging/trace-har/log-compare-execute/screenshots IPC handlers, compare-run pairing.
- Verification harness used during this audit (scratch only): run a script under `ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1 node_modules/electron/dist/Electron.app/Contents/MacOS/Electron script.js` that mocks require.cache['electron'], monkeypatches vault-session-service.getMasterKey to return a random 32-byte key, then calls db.initWithPath(tmp) + db.insertRequest + raw SELECT.

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Electron runtime (`package.json devDependencies`) — now: electron ^34.0.0 (installed 34.5.8); Electron 34 has been out of support for a long time (Sept 2026). The logging code depends on webContents.debugger (CDP 1.3), capturePage, net.fetch and WebContentsView. Upgrade to the current stable major (check releases.electronjs.org), rebuild better-sqlite3/argon2, and re-test CDP event shapes (requestWillBeSentExtraInfo, webSocket*).
- Default MITM upstream browser/TLS profile (`main-process/services/proxy-service.js startMitmProxy (new d.MitmProxy({ browser: 'chrome_120' }))`) — now: chrome_120; Chrome 120 is from Dec 2023. The fingerprint of all logged MITM traffic uses it unless a profile overrides it. Bump it to a current Chrome profile supported by azuretls-go.
- HAR creator version (`har-exporter.js exportHar + exportHarToFileStream`) — now: creator { name: 'CupNet', version: '2.0' }; Hardcoded; package.json version is '27.06.2026-5'. Pass app.getVersion().
- HAR httpVersion (`har-exporter.js buildHarEntryFromRow`) — now: 'HTTP/1.1' for request and response; The upstream may be h2/h3 via AzureTLS; the protocol is not recorded in the DB at all.
- CDP UA override without Client Hints (`main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js setupNetworkLogging (Emulation.setUserAgentOverride {userAgent, acceptLanguage})`) — now: no userAgentMetadata; Modern Chrome sends Sec-CH-UA*; overriding only the UA string gives a detectable UA vs UA-CH mismatch. Pass userAgentMetadata (brands, fullVersionList, platform, platformVersion, mobile, architecture).
- Buffer#slice (`db.js _truncBody`) — now: body.slice(0, MAX_BODY_BYTES) on a Buffer; Deprecated alias (DEP0158); use subarray.
- Test/build tooling majors (`package.json`) — now: vitest ^2.1.9, electron-builder ^24.13.3; Both are several majors behind; the upgrade affects the test:unit and dist scripts.
- Ad-hoc schema migrations (`db.js migrateSchema`) — now: PRAGMA table_info column probes on every startup, no PRAGMA user_version; No schema version is tracked; migrations are not transactional (except the vault/intercept_rules rebuilds).
- Stale service docs (`main-process/services/README.md`) — now: mentions handleRuleActions inside cdp-network-logging.js; That function no longer exists (rule actions moved to the MITM path).

**Production gaps:**

- No retention/size limits for requests, ws_events, screenshots (full-resolution PNG BLOBs) or browser_events; only console_logs is purged (14 days). No VACUUM/auto_vacuum, no DB size indicator, no per-session size stats.
- Encryption at rest is partial even after fixing the leak: url/host (query tokens), ws_events.payload, browser_events.detail (console args, localStorage values), screenshots, console_logs, requests.error, sessions.proxy_info stay plaintext. There is no PRAGMA secure_delete, so plaintext remnants from before the mode switch survive, and old plaintext rows are never migrated or re-encrypted.
- No key id / vault_id stored per encrypted row, no 'redacted' or 'decrypt_failed' flags, and no UI indication. Multi-profile use and lock/unlock periods produce silently empty or redacted rows.
- No FTS strategy for encrypted bodies (after the leak fix, body search only works for plaintext rows); the FTS update trigger is not column-scoped.
- Exports (HAR, bundle, site ZIP) and compare-run run synchronously on the main process that also hosts MITM: no worker_threads, no progress, no cancellation. Bundle and site ZIP build everything in memory.
- Logging/export IPC is not protected by ipc-trusted-sender (guardIpcHandler). open-log-directory accepts an arbitrary renderer path for shell.openPath. browser-storage-activity accepts unauthenticated, unthrottled payloads from any website.
- HAR 1.2 compliance: time/timings.wait can be -1, entries are newest-first, no pageref, empty cookies arrays, empty redirectURL for 3xx, startedDateTime is the completion time, the CDP <base64|...> body marker is not decoded.
- Test coverage gaps: nothing for bundle-utils, diff-utils, site-snapshot-export, cdp-network-logging, mitm-cdp-dedup, screenshot-service, the IPC handlers, or encrypt-mode end-to-end (insert -> DB -> export). e2e always runs with a locked vault.
- Write-queue drops are only logged; there is no UI surfacing or backpressure (stabilityMetrics has gauges, but the logging UI does not show dropped rows).
- Replay and Request Editor fallback use Electron net.fetch instead of the MITM AzureTLS worker, against the project rule to prefer MITM; replayed traffic is logged against the active tab.
- No schema versioning or migration tests; migrations run on every startup.

### log-viewer (Network Activity window) + Compare viewer + System Console viewer + shared HTML response preview

Renderer-side tool windows for inspecting captured traffic and app logs. (1) "Network Activity" (log-viewer.html + log-viewer-renderer.js) shows a virtualised list of HTTP rows, WebSocket handshakes, screenshots, Activity Monitor browser events (console/exception/storage/CSP) and CupNet system rows. They come live from the MITM, CDP, screenshot, mock and request-editor producers, or from SQLite sessions. It has a detail pane (Headers/Cookies/Request/Response/Raw/Messages/Event/Comment tabs, HTML Preview/Only text/Forms subviews, in-tab find), tagging and notes, a sessions sidebar, multi-select "session from selection", HAR, bundle and site-ZIP export, bundle import, and handoffs to Request Editor, Compare, the Rules mock editor, Notes and launch profiles. (2) Compare viewer (compare-viewer.html/-renderer.js) pairs request sequences from two anchors (A/B) and renders the diffs. (3) System Console (console-viewer.*) shows main-process stdout/stderr captured by console-capture.js, live or from the console_logs table, and controls the MITM debug level. (4) html-response-preview.js is a shared sanitiser that builds a sandboxed srcdoc HTML preview and extracts plain text.

**Files:**

- `log-viewer.html` — Network Activity window markup (lines 2227-2714) plus about 2200 lines of inline CSS (lines 9-2225). The meta CSP is: default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data:. Loads shared-dark-theme.css, then html-response-preview.js, then log-viewer-renderer.js. Key ids: #lv-sidebar/#session-list, #lv-toolbar (search-input, fts-checkbox, ms-type/ms-status/ms-tab/ms-activity multi-selects, sc-only/hide-options/hide-screenshot checkboxes, lv-session-from-sel-btn, lv-rec-btn, auto-scroll-btn, export-har-btn, export-bundle-btn, export-site-zip-btn, import-bundle-btn, open-rules-btn, clear-logs). List: #lv-scroll > #lv-spacer-top/#lv-rows/#lv-spacer-bottom. Detail: #lv-detail (the .multi-sel class hides everything except #lv-detail-multi-banner), #lv-replay-bar, #lv-mark-section, #lv-tabs, #lv-detail-find, tab panes #tab-headers/cookies/request/response/raw/messages/event/comment, #resp-html-preview-iframe (sandbox="allow-same-origin"). Modals (.lv-overlay toggled with .visible): #protection-modal, #site-export-modal, #compare-side-modal, #session-from-sel-modal; #wrt-ctx-menu. Dead legacy DOM: hidden #filter-session select, #request-body-hidden, #lv-replay-result/#replay-body/#replay-status-badge/#lv-replay-diff.
- `log-viewer-renderer.js` — All Network Activity logic (5419 lines, classic script, global `api=window.electronAPI`, `HtmlPreview=window.HtmlResponsePreview||{}`). Map: state and DOM refs 1-145; Rec toggle 146-169; browser-event badge helpers 171-222; utilities (esc, formatTime, statusCls, truncUrl, URL-parts popover) 224-494; cupnet system-row presentation getCupnetSessionTrafficPresentation 496-532; annotation/type/screenshot helpers 533-617; tab badges 619-710; body decoding (formatBody, pickRequestBodyRaw, normalizeRequestDetailRow, decodeStoredBody, parseBase64Body, guessFileInfo, sniffImageMime) 711-927; multipart image overlay 929-1013; WS message expand toggles 1015-1064; buildCurlCommand/formatWebSocketRawLog/buildRawHttp 1071-1177; modal promises chooseProtectionLevel/promptNewSessionNameFromSelection/openCompareSidePickerForEntry 1179-1289; virtual scroll calcWindow/renderVirtual/scheduleRenderVirtual (ROW_HEIGHT 28, BUFFER 24) 1291-1327; selection handleRowClick/showMultiSelectionDetail 1329-1428; buildRow 1430-1574; scroll/keyboard 1576-1605; live batching addEntry/_flushBatch (BATCH_MS 80, MAX_LIVE_ENTRIES 10000) 1614-1684; filters entryPassesFilter/applyFilters/updateTabFilter 1686-1813; sessions sidebar 1815-2067; selectEntry/showDetail 2069-2657; mark panel and annotations 2659-2795; form/multipart/JSON/table field renderers 2804-3342; HTML detection, form parsing and HTML subviews (setupResponseHtmlViews, setResponseHtmlView, mountResponseHtmlPreview, renderHtmlFormsPanel, buildHtmlFormEditorPrefill) 3286-3697; headers/cookies tab 3699-4131; WS Messages panel 4133-4204; activateTab 4206; in-tab find 4216-4500; Editor/Mock/Raw buttons 4539-4614; toolbar handlers, Ctrl+A, clear, exports, site-ZIP modal, bundle export/import 4616-5053; focus-request handlers 5057-5085; live subscriptions and intercept toasts 5087-5154; ResizeObserver 5156-5166; init() 5169-5199; multi-select widgets 5201-5260; split resizer 5262-5287; sidebar collapse (localStorage 'cupnet-lv-sidebar-open') 5289-5310; Web Request Tools context menu 5312-5419.
- `html-response-preview.js` — New file, untracked in git. Shared helpers exposed both as window.HtmlResponsePreview and via module.exports: isLikelyHtmlContent, buildSandboxPreviewDoc (DOMParser; strips script/noscript/meta-refresh/base; replaces iframe/frame/object/embed; removes non-data:/blob: src/href/srcset/poster/ping and on* attributes; rebuilds the document with a strict meta CSP: default-src 'none', img/font data: blob:, style-src 'unsafe-inline', form-action 'none'), extractHtmlPlainText, normalizePlainText, sanitizeSrcsetValue, splitSrcsetEntries, isSandboxSafeResourceUrl, previewDocHash (FNV-1a). Not wrapped in an IIFE, so its top-level functions and `const exportsObj` live in the page's global scope. Used by log-viewer.html and compare-viewer.html (the header says the Request Editor too).
- `compare-viewer.html` — Compare window markup plus inline CSS. Same CSP as the log viewer. Links ./node_modules/jsondiffpatch/lib/formatters/styles/html.css and loads html-response-preview.js then compare-viewer-renderer.js. Toolbar: #compare-btn, #refresh-btn, #compare-level (quick/standard/deep), #noise-toggle, #dimension-mode (all/transport/data/ui/security). Panes: #left-list, #right-list, svg#pane-links, #detail-body.
- `compare-viewer-renderer.js` — Compare UI: state {left,right,result} from compare-get/compare-updated; renderColumns (pair badges, SVG bezier links drawn by drawPaneLinks); renderDetail (async: metrics, executive summary, riskLevel, header diff tables, body split or jsondiffpatch HTML via formatJsonDiffHtml, analyzeHtmlSnapshot HTML impact with sandboxed iframes sandbox="" + srcdoc, security signals); collectJsonDiffKinds path list with filters and focus toggles; runCompare → compare-run; auto-runs once when both anchors are set and there is no result. Redefines a global buildSandboxPreviewDoc wrapper that delegates to window.HtmlResponsePreview.
- `console-viewer.html` — System Console markup: tabs all/mitm/system/bookmarks, #cv-debug-mitm select (0-4), #cv-search, #cv-exclude, #cv-regex, #cv-db-session, action buttons (autoscroll, pause, wrap, group, copy, save .log/.json/.csv, clear), level chips, collapsible #cv-extra-filters (source checkboxes mitm/system/worker/ffi/dns/main/app, datetime-local range), #cv-log-scroll/#cv-viewport/#cv-log, #cv-detail aside with correlation list, #cv-status-bar, #cv-ctx-menu.
- `console-viewer-renderer.js` — Console logic: normalizeEntry (level/source inferred again if missing), classifyCategory (mitm vs system by prefix), MAX_LINES 50000/TRIM_BATCH 10000, virtual list ROW_H 22/VIRTUAL_BUFFER 45, wrap mode capped at WRAP_MAX_ROWS 4000, filters (level, source, time, search/regex, exclude), group consecutive repeats, pause, bookmarks (localStorage 'cupnet-console-bookmarks-v1'), extra-filter toggle (sessionStorage 'cupnet-console-extra-filters-open'), exports, DB session browsing (getConsoleLogsFromDb, max 5000 rows ascending), correlate (findRequestsNearTs, ±2500 ms), MITM debug level sync, shortcuts Cmd/Ctrl+F/K/S.
- `console-viewer.css` — Console styles built on shared-dark-theme.css tokens (--bg0..3, --accent-bg, --danger, --warn, --ok, --text-muted, --font-mono, --radius-sm). [hidden] overrides for #cv-mitm-toolbar, #cv-detail and #cv-empty.
- `preload.js` — contextBridge `electronAPI` (one preload shared by ALL tool windows). Log-viewer APIs are at lines 61, 88-157, 240; console APIs at 304-314; notesEmbedRequest at 341; launch-profile APIs at 448-450. sub(channel, cb) returns an unsubscribe function.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/db-logging-ipc.js` — get-db-requests, count-db-requests, get-request-detail, set-request-annotation, fts-search, get-sessions-with-stats, get-current-session-id, rename-session, toggle-logging-start/confirm-logging-start/toggle-logging-stop, delete-session, delete-unnamed-sessions, create-session-from-request-ids, open-session-in-new-window, get-initial-session-id, get-log-status, get-existing-logs, get-browser-events, get-ws-events, clear-logs, and the ipcMain.on 'browser-storage-activity' listener (inserts a browser_events row and broadcasts it).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/trace-har-ipc.js` — export-har, export-bundle, list-session-origins, list-site-export-paths, export-site-zip, import-bundle, diff-requests, jsondiff-format-html, replay-request (legacy, only tests use it). Uses ctx.logViewerWindow as the dialog parent.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/log-compare-execute-ipc.js` — open-log-viewer, open-log-viewer-with-url (sends focus-request-url), open-log-viewer-focus-request (sends focus-request-id), open-compare-viewer, compare-get, compare-set-slot, compare-clear-slot, compare-run (pairing algorithm), open-jsonl-file, open-rules-window, open-rules-window-with-mock (sends prefill-intercept-rule), open-request-editor (buildRequestEditorPrefill), execute-request (AzureTLS worker in MITM mode, otherwise net.fetch; logs to DB and broadcasts).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/launcher-ipc.js` — Console IPC: open-console-viewer, get-console-history, get/set-debug-mitm-level (mitm-proxy.js getDebugMitmLevel/setDebugMitmLevel), save-console-log(-json/-csv), get-console-logs-db, get-console-log-sessions, find-requests-near-ts, cupnet-log, notes-embed-request (sends notes-embed-block to the notes window).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/session-profile-ipc.js` — open-session-profile-modal, export-launch-profile-from-request, apply-launch-profile-from-request (via services/export-launch-profile.js buildLaunchProfileFromRequest). Used by the log-viewer context menu.
- `main-process/services/sub-windows.js` — createLogViewerWindow(sessionId) at L76: _spawnTrustedToolWindow with webPreferences {preload only}, window-state key 'logViewer', cascade offset, logViewerInitSessions.set(wcId, sessionId), sends update-log-status on did-finish-load. _serializeCompareRowRequest/_comparePayload/_requestsForSessionAsc/_pairScore/_confidence/_stripNoiseHeaders at L138-260. _broadcastCompareUpdated at L263 (also sends to log viewers). createCompareViewerWindow at L281 (singleton). getLiveLogViewerWindow at L304 (first window whose init session is null). createConsoleViewerWindow at L310 (singleton). createLoggingModalWindow at L746 (positions the modal from the button hint using mainWindow coordinates).
- `main-process/services/ipc-batch-messenger.js` — broadcastLogEntryToViewers: per-window queue, flushes every 50 ms or at 200 entries as 'new-log-entry-batch' to EVERY log-viewer window. broadcastInterceptRuleMatched → 'intercept-rule-matched-batch' (80 ms / 100) to main, log viewers and rules window.
- `main-process/services/console-capture.js` — installConsoleCapture patches process.stdout/stderr.write. buildConsoleEntryFromLine({text,ts,level,source,module,stream,pid}); ring buffer bufferMax 10000 (drops 20% when full); 'console-log' batches every 60 ms to the console window; persistence queue every 500 ms (onPersistBatch → db.insertConsoleLogsBatchAsync with session_id=currentSessionId); cupnetLog/pushManualEntry for structured logs.
- `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js` — Holds window state (logViewerWindow/logViewerWindows/logViewerInitSessions L109-112, compareViewerWindow/consoleViewerWindow L118-119, comparePair/compareResult L125-126), installs console capture (L128) and the ipc batch messenger (L141), defines loadJsonDiffModules (L14, dynamic ESM import of jsondiffpatch + formatters/html), maybeLogMockToNetworkActivity (L313), startLogStatusUpdater/sendLogStatus (L360-390, 'update-log-status'), and purges console_logs older than 14 days at startup (L853).
- `main-process/services/proxy-service.js` — MITM onRequestLogged (around L220-255): builds logEntry {id,url,method,status,type (default 'Document'),request{headers,body},response{statusCode,headers},duration,duration_ms,responseBody}, then db.insertRequestAsync, then broadcasts with the DB id. There is no created_at on live MITM entries.
- `main-process/services/cdp-network-logging.js` — CDP producer: finalizeLog/_processLogQueue inserts requests (L380-397) and broadcasts, skipping websocket_frame/closed/error types (L419-426). Inserts WS frames and sends 'ws-handshake-message-count' (L366-372). Also inserts browser events (console/exception/storage).
- `main-process/services/screenshot-service.js` — Live screenshot entries {type:'screenshot', path 'autoscreen::/<reason>/<time>.png', url, ssDbId, tabId, session_id, created_at, screenshotMeta} broadcast after insertScreenshotAsync (L90-105).
- `main-process/services/cupnet-network-meta-log.js` — insertSessionBootstrapTrafficRow: type 'cupnet' rows with url cupnet://session/proxy|direct, status 204, text body with Profile/IP/Location lines. The log viewer renders these as SYS rows.
- `main-process/services/traffic-log-crypto.js` — WIP (untracked). prepareRequestLogFields encrypts headers/bodies into *_enc columns when trafficLogEncryption='encrypt' and the vault is unlocked; redacts (Balanced) when the mode is 'redact' or the vault is locked. hydrateRequestLogRow decrypts: headers come back as parsed OBJECTS for encrypted rows but stay strings for plaintext rows; when the vault is locked it returns {...row, _locked:true} with null fields.
- `db.js` — SQLite (better-sqlite3). Used here: getSessionsWithStats (L911, HAVING COUNT>0), renameSession, deleteSession (cascade), deleteUnnamedSessions, createSessionFromRequestIdsAsync (L1797, via requestRowToInsertEntry L959), setRequestAnnotation (L1095), queryRequests (L1101, list columns only, ORDER BY id DESC), getRequest (L1138, hydrated), queryRequestsFull (L1149), ftsSearch (L1168, limit 100), queryWsEvents (L1241, url variants https↔wss), getScreenshotEntriesForSession (L1303, ids 'ss-N'), getScreenshotData (L1323), getBrowserEventsForSession (L1389, max 10000), queryConsoleLogs (L2789, max 5000), getConsoleLogSessionsSummary (L2816), findRequestsNearTimestamp (L2835), purgeConsoleLogsOlderThanDays (L2857).
- `har-exporter.js / bundle-utils.js / site-snapshot-export.js` — Export backends behind the log-viewer buttons. exportHarToFileStream writes synchronously with fs.writeSync over queryRequestsFull batches. buildBundle/validateBundle/redactHeaders/redactBody. listOriginsFromSession/listSitePathsForExport/exportSiteZipToStream read raw SQL columns and are not encryption-aware.

**Key flows:**

- Open window: the toolbar invokes 'open-log-viewer'. log-compare-execute-ipc focuses getLiveLogViewerWindow() or calls createLogViewerWindow(null) (sub-windows.js L76), which loads log-viewer.html, records logViewerInitSessions[wcId]=sessionId|null, pushes the window to logViewerWindows, sets ctx.logViewerWindow=win, and sends 'update-log-status' on did-finish-load. The sidebar ↗ button uses 'open-session-in-new-window' → createLogViewerWindow(id); the renderer's init() then calls getInitialSessionId() → activateSidebarSession(id,false).
