- Renderer init() (L5169): getLogStatus → updateRecBtn; loadSessionSidebar (get-sessions-with-stats(200) + get-current-session-id) → renderSessionList (named sessions first, then a 'Delete all unnamed' button, then unnamed ones). Then either activateSidebarSession(initId) or getExistingLogs. The main process merges queryRequests({sessionId: current},5000) + getScreenshotEntriesForSession + getBrowserEventsForSession(≤10000), sorted by created_at. Result: allEntries → updateTabFilter → applyFilters. setupMarkPanel() and setupWsMessagesToggle() run after init() starts.
- Live entries: producers (proxy-service MITM onRequestLogged, cdp-network-logging _processLogQueue, screenshot-service, maybeLogMockToNetworkActivity, insertSessionBootstrapTrafficRow, execute-request maybeLog, browser-storage-activity) insert into the DB first, then call _broadcastLogEntryToViewers(entry with DB id). ipc-batch-messenger queues per window (50 ms / 200) and sends 'new-log-entry-batch'. In the renderer, api.onNewLogEntryBatch → addEntry (ensureAnnotationFields) → _batchQueue → _flushBatch after 80 ms: push to allEntries, collect knownTabs, entryPassesFilter → filteredEntries. When allEntries exceeds 10000 it trims the oldest and rebuilds filteredEntries. Then scheduleRenderVirtual + scrollToBottom when autoScrollEnabled, and a sidebar live-count patch.
- Filtering: entryPassesFilter(e). _browserEvent rows are kept only if their browserEventCategory is in selectedActivityTypes. Other rows go through selectedTypes (case-insensitive; websocket_frame hidden by default), selectedStatuses (success/redirect/client-error/server-error/error), selectedTabs, sc-only (countSetCookies), hide OPTIONS, hide screenshot, sessionFilterMode (null=live/all, number=session) and a URL substring match. applyFilters(): when FTS is on with a query, fts-search(q, currentSessionId) → db.ftsSearch (FTS5 over url/response_body/request_id, OR of quoted tokens, 100 rows), merged with browser events whose summary/detail match, sorted by created_at. Otherwise it runs allEntries.filter. Both paths reset the selection and re-render.
- Selection and detail: handleRowClick(idx,e) handles plain, Shift-range and Ctrl/Cmd-toggle clicks. selectedRequestIds holds DB ids; screenshots and browser events are single-select only. selectEntry(idx): flushCommentAutosave → drop camelCase bodies of the previous entry → showDetail(rowData) → for HTTP rows with an id and not _detailLoaded, get-request-detail → db.getRequest (hydrateRequestLogRow) → normalizeRequestDetailRow → Object.assign(entry) → showDetail again if gen===_selectGen. showDetail branches: (a) screenshot: tabs hidden, lazy get-screenshot-data(ssDbId), hover zoom, click marker from screenshotMeta.click; (b) _browserEvent: only the Event tab, prettified JSON detail; (c) HTTP: renderDetailRequestLine, meta row, replay/mark bar visibility (canAnnotate / showReplay), headers (renderHeaders, fields/table toggle), query params, request body (decodeStoredBody; form-urlencoded → renderFormBody, multipart → renderMultipartBody with image preview/save, JSON → renderJsonBody flatten, otherwise text; requestBodyMissingMessage), Raw tab (buildRawHttp + dataset.curl=buildCurlCommand; websocket rows append formatWebSocketRawLog from get-ws-events guarded by _rawWsGen), Response (binary image or file download, JSON flatten, text; HTML → setupResponseHtmlViews: Body/Preview (HtmlPreview.buildSandboxPreviewDoc → iframe.srcdoc, hash-deduped)/Only text (extractHtmlPlainText)/Forms (parseHtmlFormsFromString → renderHtmlFormsPanel → '✏ Submit' → open-request-editor with buildHtmlFormEditorPrefill)), renderCookiesTab, tab badges, then activateTab(lastActiveTab). activateTab('messages') on a websocket row calls loadWsMessagesPanel → get-ws-events.
- Annotations: setupMarkPanel renders the TAG_COLORS swatches. saveAnnotation({tag,note}) → set-request-annotation(id,{tag,note}) → db.setRequestAnnotationAsync (note trimmed, has_note) → updates entry, renderVirtual, syncMarkPanel. The comment textarea autosaves after NOTE_AUTOSAVE_MS=500, and also on blur, Cmd/Ctrl+Enter and row change.
- Actions: '✏ Editor' → open-request-editor(id) → buildRequestEditorPrefill (db.getRequest) → openRequestEditorWindow → 'request-editor-init'. '+ Compare' → compare-get → side picker → compare-set-slot(side,id) → comparePair[side] → _broadcastCompareUpdated → opens the compare viewer if the other side is set. '⚡ Mock' → open-rules-window-with-mock({name,url_pattern,params{body,mimeType,status}}) → rules window 'prefill-intercept-rule'; interception itself runs on the MITM path. '📋 Note' → notes-embed-request(blockData) → notes window 'notes-embed-block'. Context menu (HTTP rows only): apply-launch-profile-from-request(id,{newTab}), export-launch-profile-from-request, open-session-profile-modal, open-request-editor, copy as curl.
- Sessions: activateSidebarSession(id,isLive) resets the view. Live → getExistingLogs. Historical → get-db-requests({sessionId},5000,0) reversed (requests only, no screenshots or browser events), sets currentSessionId and sessionFilterMode. Other sidebar actions: rename-session (inline input), delete-session (the main process refuses the active session), delete-unnamed-sessions (keeps current). '+ Session from selection' → promptNewSessionNameFromSelection overlay → create-session-from-request-ids(ids,name) → db.createSessionFromRequestIdsAsync copies rows in batches of 80 → the new session is activated.
- Recording: the Rec button calls toggle-logging-start(buttonRect) or toggle-logging-stop. Main process: if there is no session, create one and start; if logging was stopped before or has entries, show createLoggingModalWindow → 'confirm-logging-start' {mode: continue|new, renameOld}. Each start calls clearBrowserCacheOnRecStart, insertSessionBootstrapTrafficRow and setupNetworkLogging for every tab. Status is pushed with 'update-log-status' by sendLogStatus and a 5 s poll.
- Clear button: clear-logs ends the current session, creates a new one, resets logEntryCount, writes the bootstrap row if logging is on, and re-attaches setupNetworkLogging on all tabs. The renderer empties its view and reloads the sidebar. Nothing is deleted.
- Exports. HAR: export-har(currentSessionId|null) → save dialog → harExporter.exportHarToFileStream (sync; CUPNET_HAR_LEGACY_SYNC=1 uses the in-memory exportHar; CUPNET_HAR_WS_SIDECAR=1 also writes -websocket.json). Bundle: chooseProtectionLevel (Raw/Balanced/Strict) → confirm 'selected only?' → export-bundle → bundleUtils.buildBundle. Import: import-bundle → validateBundle → preview confirm → allEntries = bundle.traffic.requests reversed, each marked _fromBundle. Site ZIP: resolveSessionIdForDbExport → list-session-origins → modal with ≤10 host patterns (exact host, or glob when * or ? is present; OR semantics) → list-site-export-paths preview → export-site-zip → siteSnapshotExport.exportSiteZipToStream(archiver).
- Cross-window focus: open-log-viewer-with-url sends 'focus-request-url' {url} → search input = url → applyFilters → select the first row. open-log-viewer-focus-request sends 'focus-request-id' {id} → select it if present in filteredEntries/allEntries, clearing search and FTS first if needed.
- Compare run: the compare viewer calls refresh() (compare-get) and auto-runs runCompare once. compare-run(options): _requestsForSessionAsc for both anchor sessions (queryRequestsFull 10000 then reverse); leftSlice = anchor..+LEFT_COUNT (quick 12, standard 20, deep 30); rightPool = anchor..+RIGHT_SEARCH (60/100/140). Greedy best match per left row via _pairScore (exact requestMatchKey +100, same method +18, path-token Jaccard ×12 or ×24 on deep, query-key Jaccard ×14, status class +6, minus index penalty ≤40; accepted if exact key, or on deep if same method and pathSim ≥0.55). diffUtils.compareRequests on noise-stripped headers produces pairs (match / missing-right / missing-left) in compareResult → 'compare-updated' → renderColumns/drawPaneLinks/renderDetail. renderDetail calls jsondiff-format-html for JSON bodies (jsondiffpatch.diff + html formatter).
- Console: console-capture patches stdout/stderr → captureLine → buffer + batch → 'console-log' (60 ms) to consoleViewerWindow, and persistQueue → db.insertConsoleLogsBatchAsync (500 ms, session_id=currentSessionId). Renderer: loadBookmarks → syncDebugMitmSelect (get-debug-mitm-level) → populateDbSessions (get-console-log-sessions, 80) → getConsoleHistory snapshot → normalizeEntry → rebuildFilteredIndices. onConsoleLog → addEntry (ignored when paused or in DB mode). DB select → get-console-logs-db {sessionId, limit 5000, order asc}. Context menu 'correlate' → find-requests-near-ts {tsMs, windowMs 2500} (main uses currentSessionId) → the list; clicking a row only copies 'request id N'. MITM select → set-debug-mitm-level(n) → setDebugMitmLevel (sets process.env.CUPNET_DEBUG_MITM).

**IPC channels:**

- renderer→main invoke (log viewer): get-existing-logs, get-db-requests(filters,limit,offset), get-request-detail(id), set-request-annotation(id,{tag,note}), get-screenshot-data(ssDbId), fts-search(query,sessionId), get-ws-events({sessionId,tabId,url,connectionId?,limit?}), clear-logs, get-sessions-with-stats, get-current-session-id, rename-session(id,name), delete-session(id), delete-unnamed-sessions, create-session-from-request-ids(ids,name), open-session-in-new-window(id), get-initial-session-id, get-log-status, toggle-logging-start(hint), toggle-logging-stop, export-har(sid), export-bundle({sessionId,protectionLevel,requestIds,notes}), import-bundle, list-session-origins(sid), list-site-export-paths({sessionId,origins}), export-site-zip({sessionId,origins}), open-compare-viewer, compare-get, compare-set-slot(side,id), open-rules-window, open-rules-window-with-mock(data), open-request-editor(id|{method,url,headers,body}), notes-embed-request(blockData), open-session-profile-modal, export-launch-profile-from-request(id), apply-launch-profile-from-request(id,{newTab})
- main→renderer push (log viewer): new-log-entry-batch (entries[]), update-log-status ({enabled,sessionId,count}), ws-handshake-message-count ({handshakeDbId,ws_message_count}), focus-request-url ({url}), focus-request-id ({id}), intercept-rule-matched-batch ([{type,ruleName,url,...}]), compare-updated (sent but unused). Subscribed but never sent: new-log-entry, intercept-rule-matched.
- renderer→main invoke (compare viewer): compare-get, compare-run({level,removeNoiseHeaders}), jsondiff-format-html(leftText,rightText). main→renderer: compare-updated ({left,right,result}).
- renderer→main invoke (console viewer): get-console-history, get-debug-mitm-level, set-debug-mitm-level(n), save-console-log(text), save-console-log-json(array), save-console-log-csv(text), get-console-logs-db({sessionId,limit,offset,order}), get-console-log-sessions, find-requests-near-ts({tsMs,windowMs}). main→renderer: console-log (entry[] batches every 60 ms).
- Openers invoked from other windows (browser-renderer.js, notes-quill-custom-blocks.js, dns-manager-renderer.js): open-log-viewer, open-log-viewer-with-url(url), open-log-viewer-focus-request(id), open-console-viewer, open-compare-viewer.
- Side-channel sends triggered by log-viewer actions: main→rules window 'prefill-intercept-rule'; main→notes window 'notes-embed-block'; main→request editor 'request-editor-init'; main→logging modal 'modal-logging-init' (answered by the invoke 'confirm-logging-start' {mode,renameOld}).
- Producers into the log stream: ipcMain.on 'browser-storage-activity' (tab preload → main; inserts a browser_events row, then broadcasts).

**Data stores:**

- SQLite (db.js, better-sqlite3): sessions (id, started_at, ended_at, proxy_info, notes = display name, source, ext_port); requests (id, session_id, tab_id, request_id, url, method, status, type, duration_ms, request_headers, response_headers, request_body, response_body, error, host, tag, note, has_note, ws_message_count, created_at, request_headers_enc/response_headers_enc/request_body_enc/response_body_enc BLOB, log_encrypted); requests_fts (FTS5 on url, response_body, request_id; triggers requests_fts_insert/delete/update); ws_events (session_id, tab_id, url, direction send|recv, payload, connection_id, created_at); screenshots (session_id, tab_id, url, data_blob/data_b64, screenshot_meta JSON, created_at); browser_events (session_id, tab_id, event_type console|exception|csp-violation|ls-*|ss-*|log-entry, level, summary, detail, source_url, source_line, origin, created_at); console_logs (session_id, ts ms, level, source, module, stream, text, created_at). FK ON DELETE CASCADE from sessions, with foreign_keys=ON.
- localStorage (log viewer): `cupnet.lv.view.<sectionKey>` = 'fields'|'table'. sectionKeys: req-headers, resp-headers, query-params, req-body-form, req-body-multipart, req-body-json, resp-body-json, cookies-sent, cookies-recv, resp-form-<n>, headers. Also `cupnet-lv-sidebar-open` = 'true'|'false'.
- localStorage (console): `cupnet-console-bookmarks-v1` (JSON array of seq numbers). sessionStorage: `cupnet-console-extra-filters-open` = '1'|'0'.
- Settings keys read in this flow: trafficLogEncryption ('encrypt' default | 'redact' | 'off'), trafficLogRawMode, activityMonitorEnabled, activityMonitorStorageStackTraces, tlsProfile (execute-request), lastLogPath.
- Window state store: windowStateStore key 'logViewer' (bounds/isMaximized) in main-process/services/window-state-store.js.
- Main-process in-memory state (cupnet-runtime.js): logViewerWindows[], logViewerWindow, logViewerInitSessions Map<webContentsId, sessionId|null>, compareViewerWindow, consoleViewerWindow, comparePair {left,right}, compareResult, currentSessionId, isLoggingEnabled, hadLoggingBeenStopped, logEntryCount. Also the console-capture ring buffer (10000) and the ipc-batch-messenger queues.
- Env vars: CUPNET_DEBUG_MITM (0-4, set at runtime by set-debug-mitm-level), CUPNET_HAR_LEGACY_SYNC, CUPNET_HAR_WS_SIDECAR, CUPNET_HAR_EXPORT_BATCH, CUPNET_HAR_MAX_BODY_CHARS, CUPNET_HAR_MAX_EXPORT_CHARS, CUPNET_EXPORT_WS_FRAME_LIMIT, CUPNET_BUNDLE_MAX_ROWS.
- Files written through save dialogs (defaulting to the Downloads folder): cupnet-session-<sid>.har (+ -websocket.json), cupnet-bundle-<sid>-<ts>.json, cupnet-site-<slug>-<ts>.zip, cupnet-console-<ts>.log/.json/.csv, cupnet-launch-request-<id>.json. Renderer-side downloads use <a download> with blob:/data: URLs (response-*.ext, request-body-*.txt|bin, screenshot-*.png, multipart-*).

**Conventions:**

- Renderers are plain classic <script> files with no bundler or modules, 'use strict', and `const api = window.electronAPI`. Every new capability needs three things: a preload.js entry (ipcRenderer.invoke, or sub() for push channels), a handler in main-process/ipc/handlers/*.js with ctx.ipcMain.handle, and registration through register-main-ipc.js. Tool windows are created via _spawnTrustedToolWindow, which calls registerTrustedShell.
- Escape every interpolation into innerHTML with esc() (log viewer), esc() (compare) or escapeHtml() (console). Prefer building DOM with textContent, as the field and card renderers already do.
- All user-facing UI strings must be English (project rule). Code comments may be Russian.
- Entry objects mix two shapes, so always read both. DB rows: snake_case (request_headers/response_headers as JSON strings or hydrated objects, request_body, response_body, duration_ms, tab_id, session_id, created_at, tag, note, has_note, ws_message_count). Live broadcasts: camelCase/nested (request.headers, request.body, response.statusCode, response.headers, responseBody, duration, tabId, sessionId). Use pickRequestBodyRaw, normalizeRequestDetailRow, parseHeaders and findHeader (case-insensitive).
- Binary body markers: '__b64__:<base64>' (MITM), '<base64|mime|<type>|<data>>' (CDP), and legacy raw base64 for multipart/octet-stream/image. WS meta payloads use the prefix '__cupnet_ws_meta__:' followed by JSON {kind:'closed'|'error',...}. Screenshot rows: DB id 'ss-<n>' + ssDbId, live rows have no id; path 'autoscreen::/<trigger>/...'. Browser events: _browserEvent:true with ids from the browser_events table. CupNet system rows: type 'cupnet', url cupnet://session/proxy|direct.
- Guard async detail rendering with generation counters (_selectGen, _rawWsGen) and `_currentDetailEntry === entry` checks. showDetail must be idempotent because it runs twice per selection.
- Section view mode is persisted in localStorage `cupnet.lv.view.<sectionKey>` ('fields'|'table') and wired through injectViewToggle/createViewModeToggle. New key/value sections should use renderKeyValueList + a sectionKey.
- Modals are .lv-overlay elements toggled with the .visible class and resolved through Promises. window.prompt is unsupported in Electron; alert/confirm are used for simple cases.
- Main-process DB writes go through the enqueueWrite queue (*Async functions, 'high'/'low' priority). A log entry is broadcast only after its DB insert resolves, and it carries the SQLite id.
- Traffic interception and mocking must stay on the MITM path (request-interceptor planMitmIntercept/finalizeMitmInterceptResponse). The log viewer only opens the Rules window with a prefill and must never use session.protocol.handle.
- Push channels to the log viewer are batched (new-log-entry-batch, intercept-rule-matched-batch). Keep batching for any new high-rate stream.

**Gotchas:**

- Shared global scope. html-response-preview.js declares top-level functions and `const exportsObj` in the page's global scope. log-viewer-renderer.js re-declares isLikelyHtmlContent, and compare-viewer-renderer.js re-declares buildSandboxPreviewDoc, overwriting the global. This works only because exportsObj captured the originals. The module's internal calls resolve through globals, so a same-named top-level let/const or a second `const exportsObj` in any script is a SyntaxError that kills that whole renderer.
- Index-based list. selectedIndex and the click closures reference positions in filteredEntries. Any re-filter or trim (applyFilters, the 10000 cap in _flushBatch) invalidates positions; selectedRequestIds holds DB ids.
- Id spaces collide. browser_events ids overlap requests ids; screenshot ids are strings. Anything that turns an entry id into a request id must first exclude _browserEvent and screenshot rows.
- Live broadcasts go to every log-viewer window, including windows showing a historical session. Those entries are still pushed into allEntries (and only filtered out by sessionFilterMode).
- Row caps: getExistingLogs and get-db-requests load at most 5000 requests, FTS returns 100, browser events are capped at 10000, WS frames at 10000. The UI never shows a truncation notice.
- queryRequests (the list query) returns no request headers or bodies. response_headers is the raw DB column, which is null when encrypted. response_body is returned only for cupnet session rows. Full data comes only from get-request-detail → getRequest → hydrateRequestLogRow. That function returns _locked:true with null fields when the vault is locked, and header OBJECTS (not strings) for encrypted rows, so JSON.parse(row.request_headers) elsewhere breaks.
- The log-viewer CSP (default-src 'self', no connect-src) blocks fetch('data:...'), and fetch is what the 'Copy image' buttons use. External images/scripts/frames are blocked too.
- The Clear button does not delete data: clear-logs rotates to a new session and re-attaches logging on all tabs, even when recording is off.
- getSessionsWithStats hides sessions with 0 requests, so the LIVE session appears only after its first request.
- ctx.logViewerWindow is the most recently created log-viewer window. It is used as the dialog parent for HAR, bundle, site-ZIP and import dialogs.
- The 'new-log-entry' and 'intercept-rule-matched' single channels have no senders; only the *-batch variants are sent. 'compare-updated' is also sent to log viewers, which ignore it.
- Console viewer: bookmarks are keyed by entry.seq, which is a local counter for live lines or the DB id for DB rows, and are persisted in localStorage. console-capture persists every stdout/stderr line (session_id=currentSessionId) even when recording is off. MITM debug level ≥3 prints request/response bodies, which then land in console_logs in plaintext.
- console-capture's cupnetLog must not write to stdout/stderr, because the patched writers would capture the line twice.
- compare-run loads up to 10000 FULL rows per side (decrypting bodies) on every run. If an anchor falls outside the newest 10000 rows, findIndex returns -1 and the slice silently starts at 0.
- The log-viewer window is created with webPreferences {preload} only. It relies on Electron defaults (contextIsolation true, sandbox true) and has no will-navigate or setWindowOpenHandler guard, while exposing the full preload electronAPI and a trusted-shell webContents id.
- Inline CSS in log-viewer.html redefines :root colour tokens, overriding shared-dark-theme.css, so the log viewer uses a different palette from the console viewer.
- The Activity multi-select starts with all four categories checked. Its 'Clear filter' empties the set, which HIDES all activity rows; for the other multi-selects an empty set means 'all'.

**Tests:**

- tests/unit/html-response-preview.test.mjs (vitest): sanitiser, srcset filtering, plain-text extraction, hash. Run `npm run test:unit` (vitest.config.js).
- tests/unit/console-capture.test.mjs: buildConsoleEntryFromLine, inferSourceModule, inferLevel.
- tests/unit/har-exporter.test.mjs and tests/unit/traffic-log-crypto.test.mjs: HAR row building (string headers only) and the encrypt/hydrate round-trip. Neither covers exporters or IPC with hydrated object headers.
- tests/test-db.js (run with `npm run test:db`; needs the Electron binary as node via ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE): queryRequests filters and pagination, getRequest, ftsSearch, queryWsEvents (including https↔wss matching), getScreenshotEntriesForSession, getSessionsWithStats, deleteSession cascade, async write queue.
- Playwright e2e (`npm run test:e2e`, smoke via `npm run test:e2e:smoke`): tests/e2e/windows.e2e.spec.js (w1 Log Viewer opens via openLogViewer, w7 Console Viewer, w11 Compare Viewer), fts-search.e2e.spec.js (fts1/fts2), har-export.e2e.spec.js (har1 shape, har2 replay-request), activity-monitor.e2e.spec.js (browser_events rows), cupnet.e2e.spec.js test 3 (Network Activity DB logging).
- `npm test` runs tests/run-all.sh (node unit scripts). `npm run test:no-e2e` runs unit + Go tests and skips e2e.
- Gap: no tests for log-viewer-renderer.js, compare-viewer-renderer.js or console-viewer-renderer.js logic (filters, batching, selection, body decoding, multipart parsing, curl/raw builders, site-export glob). The functions are not exported, so testing them needs extraction into modules or DOM e2e tests.

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Electron runtime (`package.json devDependencies`) — now: electron ^34.0.0 (installed 34.5.8); Electron 34 (Chromium 132) is long out of support as of 2026. Behaviour of the CSP, clipboard, sandbox and iframe srcdoc paths used here should be re-verified after upgrading to a supported major.
- Deprecated escape() used for UTF-8 decoding (`log-viewer-renderer.js:2527`) — now: decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(respParsed.data))); Use a Uint8Array + TextDecoder (with a charset taken from Content-Type) instead.
- Obsolete CSS property (`console-viewer.css:378`) — now: -webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch; No-op in desktop Chromium; remove.
- Weak / incomplete CSP meta tags (`log-viewer.html:5-6, compare-viewer.html:5-6, console-viewer.html:5-6`) — now: default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data:; Missing object-src 'none', base-uri 'none', form-action 'none', an explicit connect-src (breaks fetch(data:) copy-image), and frame-src for the srcdoc preview. Consider moving the inline CSS to files so 'unsafe-inline' can be dropped.
- Implicit BrowserWindow security prefs and no navigation guards (`main-process/services/sub-windows.js:90-100 (createLogViewerWindow)`) — now: webPreferences: { preload } only; no will-navigate / setWindowOpenHandler; Set contextIsolation:true, sandbox:true and nodeIntegration:false explicitly, and deny navigation/new windows for all tool windows.
- Dead legacy code/channels (`log-viewer.html (#filter-session, #request-body-hidden, #lv-replay-result/#replay-body/#replay-status-badge/#lv-replay-diff); log-viewer-renderer.js (LV_ACT_LABELS except editor, loadSessions(), replay vars); preload.js:96,101 (onNewLogEntry, onInterceptRuleMatched with no senders); trace-har-ipc.js 'replay-request' (net.fetch replay, only used by e2e)`) — now: present; Remove, or document. replay-request uses the Chromium net.fetch path, which contradicts the 'prefer MITM' rule.
- Blocking native dialogs for UX (`log-viewer-renderer.js (window.confirm/alert in delete session, bundle export/import, site export, WS copy); console none`) — now: alert()/confirm(); Replace with in-app overlays/toasts; the confirm OK/Cancel semantics in bundle export ('OK = selected only') are confusing.
- HAR export ordering/creator metadata (`har-exporter.js:291-325`) — now: entries written in id DESC order via OFFSET paging; creator version '2.0' hardcoded; synchronous fs.writeSync on the main thread; HAR consumers expect chronological entries. OFFSET paging over a live session can skip or duplicate rows. The synchronous write blocks the UI.
- jsondiffpatch without diff-match-patch (`main-process/cupnet-runtime.js:14-25 loadJsonDiffModules`) — now: jsondiffpatch ^0.7.3 default entry (text diffs disabled); Use 'jsondiffpatch/with-text-diffs' if inline string diffs are wanted. The current setup is functional.

**Production gaps:**

- No UI for encrypted or locked traffic rows. When the vault is locked, get-request-detail returns _locked:true with null headers and bodies, and the viewer silently shows 'No body' / '(empty)'. Needs a 'Vault locked — unlock to view' banner and should disable Editor/Export actions.
- No pagination or 'load more' beyond 5000 requests (live and historical), 100 FTS hits or 5000 console DB lines, and no truncation indicator. Historical sessions load neither screenshots nor browser events.
- No size guards for huge bodies. renderJsonBody flattens every leaf into DOM cards; innerHTML holds multi-MB text; DOMParser runs 2-3 times per selection (setupResponseHtmlViews + countResponseTabItems + the second showDetail); detail-find walks the whole DOM; Raw for WebSocket rows loads up to 10000 frames twice (Raw + Messages).
- The sidebar and live state do not react to session changes pushed in 'update-log-status' (a new session after Rec → 'new', clear-logs from another window). The LIVE badge and currentSrvSessId go stale, and the live view mixes sessions.
- Traffic IPC channels are not wrapped in guardIpcHandler (ipc-trusted-sender.js): get-request-detail (returns decrypted bodies), get-db-requests, fts-search, delete-session, delete-unnamed-sessions, export-*, import-bundle, set-debug-mitm-level, get-console-logs-db. Only proxy, cookies, credentials and profile handlers are guarded.
- Privileged tool windows lack will-navigate / setWindowOpenHandler / drag-drop navigation guards (see the HTML-injection bug).
- console_logs are stored in plaintext regardless of trafficLogEncryption (and at MITM debug ≥3 they contain bodies). They are persisted even while recording is off, with retention fixed at 14 days and no setting.
- Error surfacing is inconsistent. HAR export failures are silent; compare-set-slot failures are silent; open-request-editor prefill failures open an empty editor; console save failures are swallowed.
- Console correlation only copies 'request id N'. It should call the existing electronAPI.openLogViewerFocusRequest(id) and use the session being viewed.
- Multi-window correctness: export and import dialogs are parented to ctx.logViewerWindow (the last created window) instead of BrowserWindow.fromWebContents(event.sender). Clear in a historical-session window rotates the global live session.
- Bundle import produces a read-only 'view' that still fetches details, annotations, compare and editor data from the local DB by foreign ids. It mixes with incoming live entries, cannot show WS frames, and has no 'close imported bundle' action.
- The tab filter lists raw tab ids (no tab titles). The Type filter lacks values for mock/cupnet/request-editor/Other/Media.
- i18n: the WS message expand/collapse labels, alerts and the cupnet row tooltip are in Russian. The 'Tapping' label should read 'Typing'.
- Accessibility: rows are divs without listbox/row roles, there are no focus-visible styles on rows, and keyboard navigation covers only arrows/Home/End. Menus are not keyboard-operable.
- The monolithic 5.4k-line renderer has no module boundaries and no unit tests. Pure helpers (decodeStoredBody, parseMultipartBody, buildCurlCommand, buildRawHttp, parseSetCookie, entryPassesFilter, site-export glob) should be extracted into testable modules.
- The HAR export is synchronous on the main thread and freezes the app for large sessions; the site-ZIP and bundle builders likewise run in the main process.
- The compare engine is memory-heavy (10k full decrypted rows per side per run) and does not persist comparePair across restarts.

### mitm-intercept: local MITM proxy (127.0.0.1:8877) + AzureTLS Go upstream worker + intercept rules/breakpoints/DNS+CORS overrides + traffic-mode routing + MITM/CDP log dedup

Every Chromium tab session is proxied to a local MITM on 127.0.0.1:${CUPNET_MITM_PORT||8877} (bypass list is only '<local>'). For HTTPS the MITM answers CONNECT, terminates Chromium's TLS with a per-host leaf cert signed by its own in-memory CA (mitm-ca.js), parses the HTTP/1.1 requests, applies DNS overrides then intercept rules (block/mock/modifyHeaders/script/breakpoint) via request-interceptor.planMitmIntercept, re-sends the real request upstream through a child-process AzureTLS Go worker (NDJSON over stdio) so the upstream TLS/HTTP2 fingerprint imitates Chrome/Firefox/Safari, buffers the response, optionally post-processes it (response-header edits, script 'after' phase, CORS injection), writes it back to Chromium, and logs it to SQLite deduped against CDP logging. Interception must never use session.protocol.handle (Cloudflare/Turnstile detect the JS/fetch path); it runs only on the MITM path. Also covers TLS passthrough for challenges.cloudflare.com, raw WebSocket tunnelling over Node TLS, fail-closed egress blocking, the 502 gateway error page, and authenticated ExternalProxyPort ports that reuse the pipeline.

**Files:**

- `mitm-proxy.js` — Core (~2466 lines). AzureTLSWorker class (child process NDJSON, restart/backoff, stdin queue, pending map, concurrency guards). MitmProxy class: start()/stop(), _handleConnection, _handleConnect (TLS-terminate + per-request pipeline dispatchRequest), _handleConnectPassthrough (TLS passthrough tunnel), _readFullHttpRequestThenHandle + _handleHttp (plain HTTP), _doRequest (the central request path: DNS override, egress-block check, planMitmIntercept, worker.request, HTTP/1.1 retry, native fallback, finalize), _build502Response, _applyDnsOverride, WebSocket tunnel (_tunnelWebSocketUpgradeMitm/_continueWsTunnelAfterPlan/_openRawTlsToUpstream), CORS injection (applyMitmCorsToResponse), buildHttpResponse, parseHttpRequest, per-tab upstream map, setEgressBlocked/setForceHttp1/setDnsOverrides/setGlobalCorsEnabled/setTlsPassthroughDomains. Also ExternalProxyPort class.
- `mitm-ca.js` — Pure-Node ASN.1 CA + leaf cert generation (P-256 ECDSA, no openssl). generateCA/generateCAAsync, loadOrGenerateCA(dir) persists ca-key.pem/ca-cert.pem, getFakeCert/getFakeCertAsync build per-host leaf certs (LRU cache max 500, in-flight dedup map), getCACertPem.
- `mitm-connect.js` — Empty placeholder module (only a comment + empty exports). Reserved for a future CONNECT-pipeline extraction; imported nowhere meaningful.
- `mitm-gateway-error.js` — 502 gateway error HTML page + short summaries. isLikelyDocumentNavigation, resolveMitmGatewayDnsContext (infers DNS/localhost from Go dial-tcp error text and /etc/hosts), summarizeMitmGatewayError, buildMitmGatewayErrorResponse. Page is dark-themed English HTML.
- `request-interceptor.js` — Intercept engine (~1064 lines). planMitmIntercept(opts,planOptions) is called from mitm-proxy._doRequest; iterates enabled rules (loadRules, 3s cache), matches url/method/headers/body, runs breakpoint handler, returns {done:true,response} for block/mock/script-shortCircuit or {done:false,opts,postProcess} for modifyHeaders/script-forward. finalizeMitmInterceptResponseAsync runs response-header edits + script 'after' phase. Script rules run in node:vm runInNewContext (NOT a real sandbox). Rule save/validate, strict-URL mode, sensitive-URL bypass, session attach/detach (protocol.handle intentionally NOT registered).
- `network-policy.js` — Central env-driven config: timeouts (workerRequestMs 30000, tlsHandshakeMs 15000, upstreamRequestMs 30000...), retry, concurrency caps (workerMaxPending 1000, workerMaxInflight 200, workerStdinQueueMax 2000), breaker, mitmPort (8877), mitmClientProxyAuth {globalUsername:'_cupnet_global', password:'cupnet'}, db, slo, featureFlags. retryableStatus, computeBackoffMs.
- `traffic-mode-router.js` — Traffic mode is always 'mitm' (browser_proxy removed). normalizeTrafficMode returns 'mitm', resolveSessionProxyConfig builds proxyRules 'http=127.0.0.1:PORT;https=127.0.0.1:PORT', toProxyRules helper (used only by tests now).
- `main-process/services/traffic-mode-service.js` — createTrafficModeService: applyEffectiveTrafficMode(mode,upstreamProxyUrl,ctx) updates interceptor.setTrafficMode, mitm.setUpstream, tabManager.setProxyAll, reapplyMitmTrustToSharedSession, defaultSession.setProxy, persists effectiveTrafficMode. applyTrafficFilters sets tlsPassthroughDomains. BYPASS_RULES = '<local>'.
- `main-process/services/mitm-cdp-dedup.js` — Dedup between MITM logging and CDP shadow logging. Primary key: X-CupNet-Rid via tryClaimRid/releaseRid (first writer wins, map cap 50000). Fallback: (tab,url,method,status)+12s window via markMitmLogged/markCdpLogged/shouldSkip*.
- `main-process/services/breakpoint-service.js` — Pending breakpoint registry: registerBreakpoint(id,resolve) with 30s auto-forward timeout, resumeBreakpoint(id,result).
- `breakpoint-preload.js` — contextBridge exposes breakpointAPI.onBreakpointSet + resume(payload)->ipcRenderer.invoke('breakpoint-resume').
- `breakpoint-renderer.js` — Breakpoint window UI logic: fills URL/method/headers/body, Forward/Apply&forward -> resume({action:'forward',patch}), Block -> resume({action:'block'}).
- `breakpoint.html` — Breakpoint window markup (CSP, dark theme, form fields, loads breakpoint-renderer.js).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/mitm-startup-ipc.js` — IPC: 'mitm-ready-state' (ready/state/error/ts), 'ui-first-paint', 'ui-long-task-count' (startup metrics).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/mitm-tls-resilience-ipc.js` — IPC: mitm-get-stats, mitm-restart, stability-metrics-snapshot, stability-slo-status, proxy-resilience-state, mitm-get-ca-cert, mitm-set-browser, mitm-set-upstream, get/set-tls-profile, connect-direct.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/rules-ipc.js` — IPC: get-monaco-vs-path, breakpoint-resume, format-intercept-rule-diff-html, get/save/delete/reorder/export/import-intercept-rules, get-intercept-rule-history, select-mock-file, test-intercept-notification/script/url-match, export-rules-activity-log. Save/delete call interceptor.invalidateRulesCache + ctx.reattachInterceptorToAllTabs.
- `main-process/services/proxy-service.js` — startMitmProxy() constructs MitmProxy with onGatewayError + onRequestLogged (the DB logging + dedup + dns/cors broadcast pipeline), wires worker events; check-ip-geo uses mitm.worker directly. Also quickChangeProxy/connectProxyWithFailover (proxy-chain anonymize).
- `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js` — Owns mitmProxy/mitmReady/mitmState/mitmError, startMitmWithRetry/restartMitmProxy/wireMitmAfterStart, certificate-error handler (_isCupnetMitmCertificate), interceptor.setOnRuleMatch/setBreakpointHandler/setResolveTabIdFromDetails, stats broadcast interval ('mitm-stats-update').
- `tab-manager.js` — Per-tab session proxy = 127.0.0.1:PORT; applyCupNetRidHeaderToSession injects X-CupNet-Rid header (tabId_uuid) via onBeforeSendHeaders; setTrustMitmCA/reapplyMitmTrustToSharedSession; setProxyAll.
- `azuretls-go/request.go` — Go worker request handler: incomingLine/outgoingLine NDJSON structs, createPooledSession (InsecureSkipVerify=true), buildAzureRequest, handleHTTPRequest with conn-error idempotent retry (fresh session, then bare fallback).
- `azuretls-go/main.go` — Go worker stdio loop: emits {id:'__init__',status:'ready'}, dispatches __clear_sessions__/__get_profiles__/http requests.
- `azuretls-go/profiles.go` — Browser -> azuretls preset + UA/HTTP2 map (Chrome 133, Firefox 138, Safari 18, iOS 18, Edge 133, Opera 119). Maps names like chrome_120 to azuretls.Chrome.
- `db.js` — intercept_rules table (type CHECK block/modifyHeaders/mock/script; params JSON; priority/method/delay_ms/tags/stop_on_match/breakpoint_enabled) + intercept_rule_history; getAllInterceptRules ORDER BY priority DESC,id; saveInterceptRule/incrementInterceptRuleHitAsync/setInterceptRuleErrorAsync.

**Key flows:**

- HTTPS request flow: net server _handleConnection sees 'CONNECT host:port' -> _handleConnect. If host matches _tlsPassthroughDomains (default challenges.cloudflare.com) -> _handleConnectPassthrough opens a raw TCP tunnel (direct or via upstream CONNECT) and never MITMs. Otherwise reply 'HTTP/1.0 200 Connection Established', pause socket, getFakeCertAsync(host) builds a leaf cert, wrap socket in tls.TLSSocket(isServer). tlsSocket 'data' accumulates decrypted bytes, parseHttpRequest splits each request; body parsed by Content-Length (gzip/br/deflate/multipart/binary -> bodyBase64 else body). WebSocket upgrade requests branch to _tunnelWebSocketUpgradeMitm; all others go to dispatchRequest.
- dispatchRequest builds url 'https://host[:port]path', strips proxy-authorization + (when body) content-length/transfer-encoding from orderedHeaders, strips X-CupNet-Rid (kept as cupnetRid), derives tabId from CONNECT Proxy-Authorization (_mitmTabIdFromProxyAuthHead) or from cupnetRid, pushes an ordered pipeline entry, calls _doRequest, then applyMitmCorsToResponse + buildHttpResponse and writes responses in FIFO order via flushPipeline (out-of-order completions wait). onRequestLogged fires with url/method/tabId/cupnetRid/status/headers/body/type/dnsOverride.
- _doRequest (central): stats bump; _applyDnsOverride rewrites host->IP, sets Host header (or rewriteHost) and interceptMatchUrl, forces HTTP/1.1; _resolveUpstreamForTab returns {proxy,browser,ja3,forceHttp1}; if _egressBlocked && no tab/global proxy -> return 502 'Egress blocked'; require('./request-interceptor').planMitmIntercept(mitmOpts) — if plan.done return plan.response (block/mock/script shortCircuit), applying responseDelayMs; else applyOutboundUserAgentToMitmHeaders on plan.opts, decide forceHttp1 (env CUPNET_FORCE_HTTP1, tab profile, youtube/googlevideo host, dnsOverride), build workerReqBase, worker.request; on HTTP/2-ish worker error or 502-ish gateway body retry once with forceHttp1; on connection error for idempotent methods try _nativeHttpsFallback; finalizeMitmInterceptResponseAsync applies postProcess (modifyHeaders response edits or script 'after'); return {statusCode,headers,bodyBase64,dnsOverride}.
- planMitmIntercept: loadRules() (enabled rules, 3s cache, priority DESC). For each rule: ruleMatchesUrl(matchUrl) using interceptMatchUrl (logical URL before DNS host->IP), bypassInterceptMockBlockForSensitiveUrl (skips CF/Turnstile/captcha unless CUPNET_INTERCEPT_ALLOW_MOCK_CF=1), ruleMatchesMethod, ruleMatchesExtraConditions (matchHeaders/matchBody). Optional breakpoint handler pauses. block -> 403 'Blocked by CupNet'. mock -> status+mimeType+body/file. modifyHeaders -> _patchRequestOptsForMitm mutates request headers, defers response-header edits to postProcess, honors stop_on_match. script -> runInterceptScriptPhase 'before' (can set ctx.shortCircuit to answer without network, or mutate url/method/headers/body), 'after' deferred via postProcess. delay_ms accumulates responseDelayMs (max 60000). _bumpRuleHit + _logRuleApplied + _onRuleMatch on each match.
- AzureTLS worker lifecycle: createMitmAzureBackend -> AzureTLSWorker(workerPath). _start picks Go binary (mitmPreferGoWorkerBinary/_resolveGoWorkerBinary) when present; else, unless CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER=1, sets a fatal config error and the worker never becomes ready (Node FFI path disabled by default). Spawns child (Go binary directly, or Node running azure-tls-worker.js). stdout NDJSON: {id:'__init__'} sets ready + emits 'ready'; response lines resolve pending. On exit: reject pending, exponential backoff restart (1s..30s) unless _stopped. request() enforces workerMaxPending/workerMaxInflight, per-request timeout (workerRequestMs), writes JSON+newline through _writeToWorker/_drainStdinQueue.
- WebSocket tunnel: _isWebSocketUpgrade detects GET Upgrade:websocket. _tunnelWebSocketUpgradeMitm applies DNS override + planMitmIntercept (can short-circuit); _continueWsTunnelAfterPlan builds a raw HTTP/1.1 wire request, _openRawTlsToUpstream connects TLS to origin (directly or via upstream CONNECT, ALPN http/1.1, rejectUnauthorized:true), reads the 101 response, then pipes clientTls<->upSock as a raw duplex. Logged as type 'websocket'.
- Log dedup: tab sessions inject X-CupNet-Rid=tabId_uuid (tab-manager applyCupNetRidHeaderToSession). MITM onRequestLogged (proxy-service) tryClaimRid(cupnetRid): false=>drop (CDP already wrote), true=>write and markMitmLogged. CDP shadow (cdp-network-logging finalizeLog) does the mirror: tryClaimRid or shouldSkipCdpShadowAsMitmDuplicate. X-CupNet-Rid is stripped before upstream and before DB storage.
- Traffic mode / proxy connect: connect-proxy-template / global-proxy-connect resolves the profile URL, connectProxyWithFailover -> quickChangeProxy (proxy-chain anonymizeProxy to a local port), mitm.setUpstream(anonymized), setBrowser(tls_profile), _activeJa3, setForceHttp1. On failure it fails closed: setEgressBlocked(true) so _doRequest returns 502 instead of leaking the real IP. connect-direct / disconnect call setEgressBlocked(false).

**Conventions:**

- Interception happens ONLY on the MITM path (planMitmIntercept/finalizeMitmInterceptResponse* called from mitm-proxy). Never register session.protocol.handle('http'|'https') for intercept — Cloudflare/Turnstile detect the JS/fetch path (.cursor/rules/electron-protocol-handle-mitm-cloudflare.mdc). _syncProtocolHandlers actively unhandles http/https.
- Prefer implementing outbound HTTP behaviour in MITM, not Chromium (e.g. UA sanitize is applyOutboundUserAgentToMitmHeaders on the wire, not session.setUserAgent).
- All user-facing UI text must be English (gateway error page, breakpoint window, rule logs).
- orderedHeaders (array of [key,value] preserving case+order) is the source of truth for the upstream wire request; mutations to the headers object must be merged back via mergeHeadersIntoOrdered/_patchRequestOptsForMitm.
- AzureTLS returns already-decompressed bodies, so buildHttpResponse strips content-encoding + content-length + hop-by-hop headers and always recomputes Content-Length; bodies travel as bodyBase64 end-to-end.
- Binary/multipart request+response bodies are stored with a '__b64__:' prefix so raw bytes survive the TEXT column.
- tabId is never in proxyRules; it is carried per-request via the X-CupNet-Rid header (tab-manager) or CONNECT Proxy-Authorization (username=tabId, password from networkPolicy.mitmClientProxyAuth).
- MITM-restricted/config IPC is guarded: shell/tool windows are registered via registerTrustedShell; tab WebContentsViews are registered via registerTabView and are never trusted (guardIpcHandler/assertTrustedShell).
- Adding a value to the IPC ctx scope requires editing ipc-scope-key-list.json and regenerating _ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js (scripts/gen-ipc-scope-accessors.cjs).
- Env flags gate everything: CUPNET_MITM_PORT, CUPNET_DEBUG_MITM (0-4), CUPNET_FORCE_HTTP1, CUPNET_USE_GO_WORKER/CUPNET_USE_NODE_WORKER/CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER, CUPNET_AZURETLS_CHILD_PROCESS/IN_PROCESS/NODE, CUPNET_INTERCEPT_STRICT_URLS, CUPNET_INTERCEPT_ALLOW_MOCK_CF, CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL, CUPNET_DISABLE_CUPNET_RID, CUPNET_DISABLE_UA_SANITIZE.

**Gotchas:**

- Intercept script rules run in node:vm runInNewContext, which is NOT a security sandbox: ctx.constructor.constructor('return process')() reaches the real Node process (verified). Any saved 'script' rule is arbitrary main-process code execution; rules are user-authored but there is no isolation. This is the single biggest security/production concern in this subsystem.
- createMitmAzureBackend's in-process path does require('./azure-tls-inprocess') but that file does not exist in the tree; so CUPNET_AZURETLS_IN_PROCESS=1 (and any non-Electron test that doesn't force CUPNET_AZURETLS_CHILD_PROCESS=1) throws MODULE_NOT_FOUND. tests/test-mitm.js 'MitmProxy: constructs' cases fail for exactly this reason under plain node.
- With no Go binary and CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER!=1 the worker sets _fatalWorkerConfigError and never becomes ready; MitmProxy.start() awaits worker.waitReady() and rejects — MITM never starts. Easy to hit in a fresh checkout that skipped npm run build:go.
- The pipeline in _handleConnect writes responses strictly in request arrival order (FIFO). A single slow request (e.g. 30s upstream timeout) head-of-line-blocks every later response on the same TLS connection, even ones that finished. Same pattern in ExternalProxyPort.
- Go worker createPooledSession sets InsecureSkipVerify=true and _nativeHttpsFallback uses rejectUnauthorized:false — upstream server certs are never validated (MITM-of-MITM). Intended for fingerprinting but means no upstream TLS trust; a MITM between CupNet and origin is undetected.
- matchesPattern escapes regex metachars but NOT '?' — a pattern like 'https://x/api?id=*' compiles '?' as a regex quantifier and fails to match real URLs (verified). Rule authors must avoid raw '?' in glob patterns; the plain-URL fast path (ruleMatchesUrl) avoids this only for patterns with no '*'.
- modifyHeaders response-header edits (finalizeMitmInterceptResponse) run per matching rule, but the plain modifyHeaders path with stop_on_match=false only keeps mergedModifyPost accumulation; script rules with stop_on_match are effectively always terminal (they return immediately), so ordering of mixed script+modifyHeaders rules is subtle.
- DNS override forces HTTP/1.1 and rewrites the URL host to the IP while keeping the logical Host header; interceptMatchUrl must be used for rule matching or domain-pattern rules silently stop matching after a DNS override.
- Egress fail-closed only blocks when there is NO proxy for the tab; if a per-tab override still has a stale proxy it will egress through it. setUpstream(url) auto-clears _egressBlocked, so any later setUpstream with a truthy URL silently unblocks.
- onRequestLogged/DB insert path in proxy-service is large and duplicated between MITM and CDP; the rid claim is released on DB insert failure but there are two independent dedup mechanisms (rid vs time-window) that can disagree if X-CupNet-Rid is present on one path but not the other.
- buildHttpResponse writes header bytes as 'latin1'; non-ASCII header values from upstream (rare, non-spec) could be corrupted.
- _mitmTabIdFromProxyAuthHead returns null on wrong password, and CONNECT is always accepted — so a wrong/absent Proxy-Authorization silently drops per-tab routing to the global upstream rather than erroring.
- mitm-connect.js is an empty placeholder that exports nothing usable; do not assume CONNECT logic lives there — it is all in mitm-proxy.js.

**Tests:**

- Unit (node --test, run via tests/run-all.sh): tests/test-mitm.js (parseHttpRequest/buildHttpResponse/CA; MitmProxy-construct cases currently fail due to missing azure-tls-inprocess.js), tests/test-dns-mitm.js (setDnsOverrides/_applyDnsOverride/CORS; forces CUPNET_AZURETLS_CHILD_PROCESS=1 + CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER=1), tests/test-interceptor.js (planMitmIntercept mock/script/shortCircuit/after, strict URL, ruleMatchesUrl), tests/test-mitm-integration.js (CA load + port range), tests/test-traffic-mode-router.js.
- Vitest (npm run test:unit, tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs): mitm-gateway-error.test.mjs, mitm-http1-fallback.test.mjs (_shouldAutoRetryHttp1/_responseSuggestsHttp1Retry), traffic-mode-router.test.mjs, cors-settings.test.mjs, traffic-log-crypto.test.mjs, ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs, regex-safe.test.mjs.
- Go (npm run test:go, cd azuretls-go && go test ./...): request_test.go, pool_test.go, profiles_test.go, main_test.go.
- E2E (npm run test:e2e via Playwright): tests/e2e/intercept.e2e.spec.js (block/mock/modifyHeaders/script against httpbin), mitm-azure-first-load.e2e.spec.js, azuretls-go-worker.e2e.spec.js, dns-overrides.e2e.spec.js, duplicate-requests.e2e.spec.js (dedup), traffic-proxy.e2e.spec.js, user-agent.e2e.spec.js.
- Live/manual: npm run test:mitm:live (scripts/mitm-standalone-check.mjs), npm run smoke:azuretls-native.

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Default MITM browser profile string (`mitm-proxy.js L787, L910, L2456; main-process/services/proxy-service.js L127`) — now: 'chrome_120'; Maps to azuretls.Chrome in Go but the label is stale (Go profiles emit Chrome 133). Cosmetic/log only, not the real fingerprint.
- Fallback default Chrome UA (`user-agent-utils.js L25 DEFAULT_CHROME_UA`) — now: Chrome/120.0.0.0 (Mac); Used when a request has no User-Agent. Older than the Go worker's Chrome 133 UA and the proxy-tls-ua-sync/proxy-manager-renderer Chrome 133 strings — inconsistent UA across paths.
- Go browser fingerprint profiles (`azuretls-go/profiles.go L18-56, azure-tls-worker.js L77-107`) — now: Chrome 133, Firefox 138, Safari 18/iOS 18, Edge 133, Opera 119; As of 2026 these are ~a year behind current stable Chrome/Firefox; the JA3/HTTP2 presets come from azuretls-client v1.11.0 (v1.12.x available in the module cache). Update azuretls-go/go.mod + profiles for current fingerprints.
- azuretls-client Go dependency (`azuretls-go/go.mod`) — now: github.com/Noooste/azuretls-client v1.11.0; v1.12.12 is already present in the local module cache; bumping would refresh TLS/HTTP2 fingerprints and utls.
- Electron runtime (`package.json devDependencies`) — now: electron ^34.0.0 (installed 34.5.8); Chromium in Electron 34 corresponds to ~Chrome 132; renderer navigator.userAgent and TLS from Chromium lag the emulated Chrome 133 upstream fingerprint — a mismatch anti-bot systems can flag.
- Legacy Node FFI AzureTLS worker + monolith (`azure-tls-worker.js, azuretls/azureTLS.js, electron-app/app.js`) — now: present but disabled by default (needs CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER=1); electron-app/app.js is the pre-refactor monolith; Dead/legacy code paths kept for tests and fallback; electron-app/app.js duplicates the whole MITM wiring and is believed unused (main.js -> main-process/index.js).

**Production gaps:**

- Intercept 'script' rules execute arbitrary code in the main process via node:vm with no real isolation (see gotchas). For production this needs a hardened out-of-process/worker sandbox or a capability-limited DSL, plus a warning/consent when enabling a script rule.
- Restore or delete the missing in-process backend: createMitmAzureBackend requires './azure-tls-inprocess' which does not exist, so the in-process branch and several unit tests crash. Either ship the file or remove the branch and its env flag.
- Build/dependency preflight: if the Go worker binary is absent MITM silently never becomes ready. Production should fail fast with a clear UI error (there is _fatalWorkerConfigError but startup just surfaces a timeout) and CI should guarantee build:go ran for the target arch.
- Head-of-line blocking on a single CONNECT TLS connection (FIFO pipeline) can stall a page when one upstream request is slow; consider bounded out-of-order writing or per-request framing.
- Upstream server certificate validation is disabled everywhere (InsecureSkipVerify / rejectUnauthorized:false). A production build should validate origin certs (or make it an explicit, logged opt-out) so CupNet isn't itself trivially MITM-able upstream.
- Fingerprint freshness pipeline: Chrome/Firefox versions, JA3/HTTP2, UAs and Electron Chromium are on three different versions. Production needs one source of truth and a routine bump so emulated and real fingerprints agree.
- glob matchesPattern does not escape '?' — validate/normalize rule patterns on save (there is strict mode but it is opt-in via CUPNET_INTERCEPT_STRICT_URLS).
- No explicit body-size cap on buffered MITM responses before base64 (10MB caps exist on request inbuf and plain-HTTP req, but the response is fully buffered in memory from the worker); large downloads load fully into memory in both worker and main process.

### Proxy (upstream proxy profiles, providers, global/per-tab connect, MITM upstream wiring, IP/geo checks, proxy tests, resilience/failover, external proxy ports, Proxy Manager UI)

Lets the user define proxy profiles (manual URL templates or provider-generated: Decodo, Oxylabs, IPRoyal, NodeMaven), store them encrypted in SQLite, and connect one globally or per tab. Chromium always talks to the local MITM (127.0.0.1:8877). The real upstream is set inside MITM: for authenticated proxies it is a proxy-chain anonymized local URL; per-tab upstreams are raw URLs. Connecting also applies the profile's fingerprint (UA/timezone/language) and TLS settings (AzureTLS profile, custom JA3, force HTTP/1.1). The subsystem also shows the egress IP/geo (check-ip-geo), tests proxies, tracks proxy health (circuit breaker and quarantine), fails closed on connect failure (egress block), records synthetic SET PROXY/SET DIRECT rows for HAR egress metadata, and exposes local authenticated "external proxy ports" that reuse the MITM pipeline.

**Files:**

- `main-process/services/proxy-service.js` — createProxyMitmService(d). Contains startMitmProxy (new MitmProxy with port networkPolicy.mitmPort, browser 'chrome_120', workerPath azure-tls-worker.js, onGatewayError -> 'page-gateway-error', onRequestLogged -> dedup + db.insertRequestAsync + broadcastLogEntryToViewers, worker stability events). Also: checkCurrentIpGeo/checkCurrentIpGeoInternal (L423/L603: in-flight map, 60s ok-cache, 45s fail-cache, node https direct / AzureTLS worker / Chromium fallbacks), testProxy (L796, hidden BrowserWindow in a proxy-test-<ts> partition), quickChangeProxy/_doQuickChangeProxy (L886, proxy-chain anonymize with local port reuse, serialized by _proxySwitchChain), connectProxyWithFailover (L955, resilience ordering/breaker), parseFallbackProxyList, getRealIp (unused), netFetchWithTimeout (used by trace-har replay). Heavily modified in the uncommitted WIP.
- `main-process/services/global-proxy-connect.js` — connectGlobalProxyProfile(ctx, profileId, ephemeralVars) and disconnectGlobalProxy(ctx, {skipSnapshot}). This is the canonical global connect/disconnect used by 'connect-proxy-template', 'disconnect-proxy' and session-profile-loader. Applies traffic mode, fingerprint, TLS/JA3/forceHttp1, fail-closed egress block, geo update and snapshot rows.
- `main-process/services/proxy-notify-broadcast.js` — createProxyNotifyBroadcast(getters). notifyProxyStatus -> 'proxy-status-changed' to all BrowserWindows and to tab webContents (the tab part is broken, see bugs); deduped by a JSON signature. notifyMitmReady -> 'mitm-ready-changed' {ready,state,error,ts}. notifyProxyProfilesList -> 'proxy-profiles-list' to the Proxy Manager and main window.
- `main-process/services/proxy-profile-resolve.js` — resolveProxyTemplateFromDbRow (safeStorage.decryptString(url_encrypted), else url_display when it contains no '***', then parseProxyTemplate). resolveSessionProfileProxyUpstream (exported, only used by tests). maskProxyUrlForLog.
- `main-process/services/session-profile-proxy.js` — upsertLastSessionProxyProfile: upserts a profile named 'last_session_proxy' (LAST_SESSION_PROXY_NAME) from a session file's inline proxy.template plus fingerprint. Used by session-profile-loader._prepareGlobalProxyForSession and export-launch-profile.
- `main-process/services/ext-ports-store.js` — Loads/saves userData/ext-ports.json ({ports:[{port,name,login,password,autoStart,followRedirects}]}) with plain fs.writeFileSync. Passwords are stored in plaintext.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/proxy-ipc.js` — IPC: get-current-proxy, connect-proxy-template, apply-quick-proxy-change, disconnect-proxy, save-proxy-profile-full, test-proxy-template, get-proxy-profiles, save-proxy-profile (legacy), get-proxy-profile-url (the only guarded channel: guardIpcHandler), delete-proxy-profile, test-proxy-profile (legacy), test-proxy-url. The WIP makes saves fail closed when safeStorage is unavailable.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/quick-connect-ipc.js` — IPC 'quick-connect-profile'(profileId|null): a separate, reduced connect/disconnect path (no failover, no TLS/JA3, no fail-closed, no snapshot). Exposed as preload.js quickConnectProfile; no UI caller found.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/ext-proxy-ipc.js` — IPC ext-proxy:list/create/start/stop/delete/reset-session/get-local-ip/set-port/set-redirects. startExtPort creates a DB external session (createExternalSessionAsync('ext:PORT','ext_PORT',port)) and an ExternalProxyPort(mitmProxy) that logs via db.insertRequestAsync and _broadcastLogEntryToViewers. State lives in ctx.activeExtPorts and ctx.extPortErrors (Maps in cupnet-runtime).
- `proxy-providers.js` — Dual-mode (window.cupnetProxyProviders / CommonJS) provider registry PROXY_PROVIDERS with buildTemplate() for decodo, oxylabs, iproyal and nodemaven (the latter has optionFields: gateway/protocol/region/city/isp/filter/ipv4). Also: reserved vars (__connectionMode, __provider, __accountId (legacy, unused), __country, __sessionMode, __durationMin, __opt_*, __username; legacy __password), maskProxyUrlForDisplay, resolveProviderCredentials (legacy vars only). **2026-09-24:** provider accounts (localStorage CRUD, account test planner, `testCountries/testMinPass`) were removed — credentials are per profile (A-393). resolveProviderCredentials.
- `decodo-endpoints.js` — Static Decodo endpoint table DECODO_ENDPOINT_ROWS [code,name,region,host|null,stickyMin,stickyMax,portRotating]. decodoPortSegment returns the fixed rotating port or '{RAND:min-max}' for sticky. Exposed as window.cupnetDecodoEndpoints / CommonJS. It also seeds the NodeMaven country list.
- `proxy-resilience.js` — ProxyResilienceManager: per-proxy circuit breaker (closed/open/half-open), quarantine, 50-sample sliding window error rate, latency EWMA, LRU-pruned map (200). Keyed by protocol//host:port with credentials stripped. orderCandidates, canAttempt, registerSuccess/Failure, snapshot. Config from networkPolicy.breaker.
- `session-egress-meta.js` — getSessionEgressMeta(db, sessionId): raw SQL on requests with type='cupnet' (SET PROXY/SET DIRECT rows) and parses 'Profile:/Mode:/IP:/Location:' lines. formatEgressComment is used by har-exporter.js and bundle-utils.js.

Proxy Manager window markup (CSP script-src 'self'). Contains the status pill, MITM stats, provider accounts section and modal, profile list with a built-in 'Direct' entry, and an editor with Connection/Identity/Optimization tabs (provider/manual mode, country picker, sticky duration, template vars table, UA/timezone/language, TLS template buttons Chrome 133/Firefox 138/Safari 18/iOS 18/Edge 133/Opera 119, custom JA3, Force HTTP/1.1 checkbox tls-force-http1 (WIP), traffic optimization). Loads decodo-endpoints.js, proxy-providers.js, proxy-tls-ua-sync.js and proxy-manager-renderer.js in that order.

Covers profile list/filter, editor (provider compile via pp.buildProviderTemplate, manual template, parseTemplateVars, resolvePreview), saveProfileFromForm -> saveProxyProfileFull, performConnectGlobal -> connectProxyTemplate or connectDirect, performTestProxy -> testProxyTemplate, account modal test -> testProxyUrl, Direct profile saved in localStorage 'direct_profile', UA_PRESETS/TLS_TEMPLATE_DESCS/TLS_JA3_PRESETS, traffic opts, live MITM stats.

Default browser 'chrome_120'.

It RETURNS EARLY when mode and proxy string are unchanged and force is not set.

Proxy Manager) exposes the full proxy API. preload-view.js (ALL tab pages, including remote websites) exposes checkIpGeo, getDirectIp, getCurrentProxy, getProxyProfiles, connectProxyTemplate, setTabProxy, openProxyManager, onProxyStatusChanged, extProxyList/Start/Stop/SetPort/SetRedirects.
- `db.js (neighbour)` — proxy_profiles table (schema ~L678, migrations L248-272 incl. force_http1). getProxyProfiles (L1429, computes needsReSave), saveProxyProfile (L1447, UPSERT BY NAME), updateProxyProfileById, getProxyProfileEncrypted (L1498, no id/country), updateProxyProfileTest, updateProxyProfileGeo, deleteProxyProfile, and the *Async write-queue variants. createExternalSession for ext ports.

- `main-process/cupnet-runtime.js (neighbour)` — Owns the state vars (persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl, actProxy, connectedProfileId/Name/ResolvedVars, activeFingerprint, currentTrafficMode, mitmProxy, activeExtPorts, extPortErrors) and wires proxyNotify, proxyResilience, trafficSvc and proxySvc. Also: startMitmWithRetry/restartMitmProxy/wireMitmAfterStart, the ipcScope (ipcScopeWritableKeys), the default ext port (7777) on first run, and will-quit cleanup.

**Key flows:**

- STARTUP: cupnet-runtime creates proxyNotify (createProxyNotifyBroadcast), proxyResilience (new ProxyResilienceManager(networkPolicy.breaker)), trafficSvc and proxySvc. whenReady: tabManager.setProxyAll(null), then startMitmWithRetry(3) -> proxySvc.startMitmProxy() -> MitmProxy.start(). Then wireMitmAfterStart: trustMitmCA (setCertificateVerifyProc cb(0)) on persist:cg_1, persist:cupnet-shared and defaultSession unless STEALTH>=4; applyEffectiveTrafficMode('mitm', persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl, {source:'startup', force, skipNotify}); proxy.setBrowser(settings.tlsProfile||'chrome'); DNS overrides and CORS. If ext-ports.json has no ports, a default {port:7777, login:'cupnet', random password, autoStart:false} is written.
- applyEffectiveTrafficMode(mode, upstream, ctx) (traffic-mode-service): the mode is always 'mitm'. It returns early if upstream === persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl and !ctx.force. Otherwise: interceptor.setTrafficMode, mitm.setUpstream(upstream) (non-null clears _egressBlocked), tabManager.setProxyAll(upstream) (sessions keep proxying to 127.0.0.1:8877; WebRTC policy is applied only if upstream is set), reapplyMitmTrustToSharedSession, defaultSession.setProxy(MITM, bypass '<local>'), settings.effectiveTrafficMode saved, notifyProxyStatus().
- GLOBAL CONNECT (Proxy Manager Connect -> api.connectProxyTemplate -> 'connect-proxy-template' -> connectGlobalProxyProfile): db.getProxyProfileEncrypted(id), merge saved variables with ephemeralVars, resolveProxyTemplateFromDbRow (decrypt url_encrypted or plaintext url_display, then parseProxyTemplate). It then calls parseProxyTemplate a SECOND time to fill resolvedVars, which produces different SID/RAND values. parseFallbackProxyList(FALLBACK_PROXIES|fallback_proxies|fallbackProxies), then connectProxyWithFailover. In connectProxyWithFailover: orderCandidates (if CUPNET_FF_PROXY_HEALTH_WEIGHTED), canAttempt (CUPNET_FF_PROXY_BREAKER), then per candidate quickChangeProxy -> _doQuickChangeProxy: close the old anonymized proxy, setActProxy(masked), ProxyChain.anonymizeProxy({url, port: oldPort}), setPersistentAnonymizedProxyUrl, registerSuccess/Failure + emitStabilityEvent. Back in connectGlobalProxyProfile: applyEffectiveTrafficMode(mode, anon, {force}); activeFingerprint={user_agent,timezone,language} -> applyFingerprintToAllTabs; mitm.setBrowser(tls_profile), mitm._activeJa3 = custom JA3 or null, mitm.setForceHttp1(force_http1), broadcastTlsProfileChanged; setEgressBlocked(false); connectedProfileId/Name/ResolvedVars; buildMenu, notifyProxyStatus. Async: checkCurrentIpGeo() -> db.updateProxyProfileGeoAsync(id, ip, 'City, CC') -> notifyProxyProfilesList -> insertCupnetTrafficSnapshot(mode 'proxy'). ON FAILURE: close anon, clear state, mitm.setEgressBlocked(true, reason), applyEffectiveTrafficMode(mode, null) WITHOUT force, return {success:false, fallback:'blocked'}.
- GLOBAL DISCONNECT ('disconnect-proxy' -> disconnectGlobalProxy): closeAnonymizedProxy, actProxy='', connected* cleared, setForceHttp1(false), setEgressBlocked(false), applyEffectiveTrafficMode(mode, null, {force}), resetFingerprintOnWebContents for all tabs if activeFingerprint is set, buildMenu, notifyProxyStatus, SET DIRECT snapshot (unless skipSnapshot). Alternative paths with different side effects: 'connect-direct' (mitm-tls-resilience-ipc: also sets TLS profile and persists settings.tlsProfile, resets _activeJa3, does NOT reset the fingerprint) and 'quick-connect-profile'(null) (no force, so MITM upstream is not cleared).
- PER-TAB PROXY ('set-tab-proxy' in cookies-dns-ipc, API only, no UI caller): tabManager.setTabProxy(tid, pid) -> applyMitmAndFingerprintForTabProxy -> resolveProxyTemplateFromDbRow -> mitm.setTabUpstream(tid, {upstream: RAW URL with credentials, browser, ja3, forceHttp1}) -> applyFingerprintFromProfile; setupNetworkLogging; notifyProxyStatus; snapshot '<name> · tab'. MITM maps requests to tabs via the Proxy-Authorization username on CONNECT (_mitmTabIdFromProxyAuthHead; networkPolicy.mitmClientProxyAuth '_cupnet_global'/'cupnet') and _resolveUpstreamForTab(tabId). On tab close: setMitmTabUpstreamCleanup -> removeTabUpstream.
- CHECK IP/GEO ('check-ip-geo'(tabId) -> checkCurrentIpGeo): the in-flight key and cache key is `${anon}|${mode}|${sessionStoragePath}|${tab.proxyProfileId??'global'}`. ok-cache CUPNET_IP_GEO_CACHE_MS (default 60s), fail-cache CUPNET_IP_GEO_FAIL_CACHE_MS (45s). No global anon: Node https.get to ipinfo.io (direct). With anon: _awaitWorkerReadyForIpGeo -> _resolveEgressForIpGeo (mitm._resolveUpstreamForTab) -> mitm.worker.request GET ipinfo.io. Chromium fallbacks: _awaitMitmEgressReady (wait mitmReady up to 30s, worker.waitReady, reapply trust, sleep 450ms) -> resolveProxy log -> tab webContents.executeJavaScript(fetch ipinfo) with retryOnAbort x3 -> session.fetch(bypassCustomProtocolHandlers) -> hidden BrowserWindow -> api.ipify.org. Returns {ip, city, region, country, country_name (=ISO code), org, timezone}.
- PROXY TEST ('test-proxy-template'(id, eph) / 'test-proxy-url'(url, {checkUrl}) / legacy 'test-proxy-profile'(id)) -> testProxy: ProxyChain.anonymizeProxy -> in-memory partition proxy-test-<ts>.setProxy(anon) -> hidden BrowserWindow.loadURL(checkUrl), retrying ipinfo.io on ERR_TUNNEL/ERR_PROXY/ERR_CONNECTION/ERR_NAME_NOT_RESOLVED -> parseProxyCheckResponse (ip.oxylabs.io special case) -> {success, data:{ip,country,city,org}}. Handler then calls updateProxyProfileTestAsync(id, latency, ip, geo) + notifyProxyProfilesList. This uses the Chromium network stack, NOT MITM/AzureTLS.
- PROVIDER PROFILE AUTHORING (renderer): 'Add account' modal -> runAcctModalProviderTest: planProviderAccountTests(3) -> buildProviderAccountTestTemplate -> resolveProviderTemplateForTest -> api.testProxyUrl (1 retry). Passes if successes >= testMinPass (2); then addProviderAccount writes localStorage 'cupnet.proxy.providerAccounts.v2' in plaintext. Editor: provider select value 'acc:<accountId>' or '__custom__'; compileProviderTemplate -> buildProviderTemplate -> provider.buildTemplate (password encodeURIComponent, sticky {SID} and Decodo {RAND:} port). variables = buildProviderVariables (__connectionMode, __provider, __accountId, __country, __sessionMode, __durationMin, __opt_*). Save -> 'save-proxy-profile-full': requires safeStorage; encryptString(template); url_display = template with the password replaced by '***' (first occurrence); updateProxyProfileByIdAsync(id) or saveProxyProfileAsync (upsert by NAME); notifyProxyProfilesList.
- SESSION PROFILE LOAD: session-profile-loader._prepareGlobalProxyForSession -> disconnectGlobalProxy({skipSnapshot:true}) -> upsertLastSessionProxyProfile ('last_session_proxy') or proxy.profileId -> setTabProxy(null)+removeTabUpstream for all tabs -> connectGlobalProxyProfile(ctx, id, proxy.variables).
- EXTERNAL PROXY PORTS: 'ext-proxy:create'(opts) validates port 1024-65535, appends to ext-ports.json, then startExtPort: db.createExternalSessionAsync('ext:P','ext_P',P) -> new ExternalProxyPort(mitmProxy,{port,login,password,name,sessionId,followRedirects,onRequestLogged}) -> start() (listens 127.0.0.1:P, Basic auth) -> requests go through parent._doRequest with tabId 'ext_P' (global upstream) -> onRequestLogged -> db.insertRequestAsync + _broadcastLogEntryToViewers. stop -> instance.stop + endSessionAsync. reset-session starts a new DB session. The UI shows getLocalIp() (LAN IP). Ports are stopped on will-quit.
- STATUS BROADCAST: notifyProxyStatus builds {active: !!anon, proxyName: connectedProfileName||actProxy, mode: direct|proxy|none, trafficMode, effectiveMode, profileId, resolvedVars, tabProxyProfileId, tabProxyName, displayProxyName} and sends it to every BrowserWindow (the Proxy Manager gets the payload without tab info). The per-tab send loop is effectively dead (exhausted iterator). The Proxy Manager reacts in onProxyStatusChanged: it sets connectedId, re-checks geo after 1.2s and opens the Direct editor when mode is direct. 'get-current-proxy' returns the same shape on demand, with correct per-tab name resolution.
- HAR/BUNDLE EGRESS: har-exporter/bundle-utils -> getSessionEgressMeta(db, sessionId) -> SELECT response_body FROM requests WHERE type='cupnet' -> the first SET PROXY/SET DIRECT row -> parse IP/Location/Profile -> formatEgressComment into the HAR creator comment.

**IPC channels:**

- invoke renderer->main 'get-current-proxy' (proxy-ipc; preload.js + preload-view.js)
- invoke 'connect-proxy-template'(profileId, ephemeralVars) -> connectGlobalProxyProfile (proxy-ipc; preload.js + preload-view.js/new-tab shortcuts)
- invoke 'apply-quick-proxy-change'(proxyUrl) (proxy-ipc; preload.js applyQuickProxyChange; no UI caller)

- invoke 'get-proxy-profile-url'(id) (proxy-ipc; guardIpcHandler, trusted windows only)
- invoke 'delete-proxy-profile'(id) (proxy-ipc)
- invoke 'test-proxy-profile'(id) legacy (proxy-ipc)
- invoke 'test-proxy-url'(url, {checkUrl}) (proxy-ipc; account test modal)
- invoke 'quick-connect-profile'(profileId|null) (quick-connect-ipc; preload.js quickConnectProfile; no UI caller)
- invoke 'ext-proxy:list' | 'ext-proxy:create'(opts) | 'ext-proxy:start'(port) | 'ext-proxy:stop'(port) | 'ext-proxy:delete'(port) | 'ext-proxy:reset-session'(port) | 'ext-proxy:get-local-ip' | 'ext-proxy:set-port'(old,new) | 'ext-proxy:set-redirects'(port, follow) (ext-proxy-ipc; list/start/stop/set-port/set-redirects also exposed to tab pages via preload-view.js)
