- invoke 'identity:get' | 'identity:set'(patch) | 'identity:preview'(settings) (identity-ipc, guardIpcHandler; preload.js getIdentity/setIdentity/previewIdentity), 'open-identity-manager' (preload openIdentityManager), sync 'get-identity-navigator-patch-sync' (preload-view early inject); send main->renderer 'identity-changed' (identity-service.applyEffective → all windows; preload onIdentityChanged). 'get-tls-profile'/'set-tls-profile'/'connect-direct' now map onto the identity service (2026-09-23).
- Toolbar Browser ID button opens the Browser ID window directly (browser-renderer.js Browser ID IIFE → preload openIdentityManager({mode?})); the active identity name shows in the shell footer `#status-identity`, the button carries only `#identity-btn-dot`. No dropdown menu (a tab WebContentsView paints above shell HTML, so a DOM menu under the toolbar is invisible and a native Menu.popup was rejected by the owner). 'open-identity-manager'({mode?}) pre-switches the window mode (`#mode=…` hash / 'identity-manager-command' when already open).
- send main->renderer 'proxy-status-changed' (notifyProxyStatus -> all BrowserWindows; the per-tab send is broken)
- send main->renderer 'mitm-ready-changed' {ready,state,error,ts} (notifyMitmReady -> windows + tabs)
- send main->renderer 'proxy-profiles-list' (notifyProxyProfilesList -> Proxy Manager + main window)
- send main->renderer 'page-gateway-error' (MITM onGatewayError -> main window, active tab only)
- send main->renderer 'tls-profile-changed' (broadcastTlsProfileChanged via ipcBatch) and 'mitm-stats-update'
- dead: 'proxy-selected', 'load-proxies', 'load-proxy-profiles' (declared in preload.js; no main-side handler or sender)

**Data stores:**

- SQLite table proxy_profiles (db.js): id, name (not unique; saveProxyProfile upserts by name), url_encrypted BLOB (safeStorage), url_display TEXT (masked template), country, is_template, variables TEXT JSON (reserved __connectionMode/__provider/__accountId/__country/__sessionMode/__durationMin/__opt_*, legacy __username/__password, user template vars, FALLBACK_PROXIES), notes, last_tested_at, last_latency_ms, last_ip, last_geo ('City, CC'), sort_order, user_agent, timezone, language, tls_profile, tls_ja3_mode ('template'|'custom'), tls_ja3_custom, traffic_mode (always 'mitm'), force_http1 (WIP), created_at; computed needsReSave
- SQLite requests rows type='cupnet', method 'SET PROXY'/'SET DIRECT', url cupnet://session/proxy|direct, body 'Profile:/Mode:/IP:/Location:' (response_body or response_body_enc when traffic-log encryption is on); sessions rows for ext ports (createExternalSession: proxy_info 'ext:PORT', source/ext_port)
- userData/ext-ports.json: {ports:[{port,name,login,password(plaintext),autoStart,followRedirects}]}
- Renderer localStorage (Proxy Manager, file:// origin): 'cupnet.proxy.providerAccounts.v2' {accounts:[{id,providerId,username,password,updatedAt}]}, legacy 'cupnet.proxy.providerAccounts', 'direct_profile' {user_agent,timezone,language,tls_profile,tls_ja3_mode,tls_ja3_custom,force_http1}
- settings-store keys: tlsProfile, effectiveTrafficMode, trafficOpts (incl. tlsPassthroughDomains), corsBypassEnabled, trafficLogEncryption/trafficLogRawMode (affect egress meta)
- In-memory main state (cupnet-runtime): persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl, actProxy, connectedProfileId, connectedProfileName, connectedResolvedVars (incl. __usedFallbackProxy), activeFingerprint, currentTrafficMode, activeExtPorts Map, extPortErrors Map, proxyResilience.state; MitmProxy: upstream, _tabProxyMap, _egressBlocked/_egressBlockReason, _activeJa3, forceHttp1, browser; proxy-service: _ipGeoCache, _ipGeoFailCache, _checkIpGeoInFlight, _proxySwitchChain
- Electron partitions: persist:cg_<group> (tabs), persist:cupnet-shared (legacy), proxy-test-<ts> (in-memory, per test), direct-ip-check (get-direct-ip)
- Env vars: CUPNET_MITM_PORT (8877), CUPNET_TIMEOUT_PROXY_OP_MS, CUPNET_TIMEOUT_PROXY_TEST_MS, CUPNET_TIMEOUT_IP_GEO_MS, CUPNET_TIMEOUT_UPSTREAM_REQUEST_MS, CUPNET_IP_GEO_CACHE_MS (60000), CUPNET_IP_GEO_FAIL_CACHE_MS (45000), CUPNET_BREAKER_ENABLED/MIN_SAMPLES/CONSEC_FAILS/ERROR_RATE_PCT/COOLDOWN_MS, CUPNET_PROXY_QUARANTINE_MS (capped to cooldown), CUPNET_PROXY_EWMA_ALPHA_PCT, CUPNET_FF_PROXY_BREAKER, CUPNET_FF_PROXY_HEALTH_WEIGHTED, CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL, CUPNET_DEVTOOLS

**Conventions:**

- All user-facing UI text must be English. Code comments may be in Russian.
- In MITM mode, never use session.protocol.handle for interception. Outbound HTTP behaviour belongs in MITM (mitm-proxy.js + AzureTLS worker), not in Chromium. Chromium sessions always proxy to 127.0.0.1:networkPolicy.mitmPort, and the upstream is set only via mitm.setUpstream / setTabUpstream.
- Services are factories createXxx(d) that receive getter-based deps (getMitmProxy(), getPersistentAnonymizedProxyUrl()...). Never capture state values directly; always read through getters.
- IPC handlers live in main-process/ipc/handlers/*-ipc.js as registerXxxIpc(ctx). They are registered in a fixed order in register-main-ipc.js and return {success, error} objects rather than throwing. A new ctx field requires: ipc-scope-key-list.json + ipcScopeGet/Set in cupnet-runtime (regenerate _ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js via scripts/gen-ipc-scope-accessors.cjs); mutable fields must also be added to ipcScopeWritableKeys.
- Proxy URLs are stored encrypted with Electron safeStorage in proxy_profiles.url_encrypted, with a masked url_display. The WIP policy is fail closed: refuse to save when safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable() is false.
- Mask credentials before logging: sanitizeProxyUrl(url) then sanitizeProxyUrl._lastMasked, maskProxyUrlForLog, or maskProxyUrlForDisplay (renderer).
- Sensitive IPC must be wrapped with guardIpcHandler(channel, fn) from ipc-trusted-sender.js. Tab WebContentsViews are registered as untrusted via registerTabView.
- Errors use safeCatch({module, eventCode, context}, err, level) and sysLog(level, tag, msg). Proxy connect health events use emitStabilityEvent('info'|'warn', 'proxy.connect_success'|'proxy.connect_failed'|'proxy.quarantined'|'proxy.circuit_opened'|'proxy.candidate_skipped', data) and stabilityMetrics.counters.
- Wrap timeouts with withTimeout(promise, networkPolicy.timeouts.X, msg). Env overrides are documented in network-policy.js.
- After a proxy state change call buildMenu(), notifyProxyStatus(), notifyProxyProfilesList() as relevant, and insert a SET PROXY/SET DIRECT snapshot via cupnet-network-meta-log.
- DB writes go through the *Async enqueueWrite variants (high/low priority).
- Provider definitions are objects in PROXY_PROVIDERS with buildTemplate({username,password,country,sessionMode,durationMin,options}). Reserved profile vars are prefixed '__'; provider options are stored as '__opt_<key>'. Credentials are not stored in profile variables (they come from provider accounts via __accountId).
- proxy-providers.js and decodo-endpoints.js must remain dual-mode (window global + module.exports) and dependency-free. Script order in proxy-manager.html: decodo-endpoints.js before proxy-providers.js.

**Gotchas:**

- Traffic mode is always 'mitm' (normalizeTrafficMode ignores its input). The proxy_profiles.traffic_mode column and the profileTrafficMode logic are vestigial.
- Global upstream: for AUTHENTICATED proxies it is the proxy-chain local anonymized URL http://127.0.0.1:<port>. For credential-less proxies, ProxyChain.anonymizeProxy returns the RAW upstream URL unchanged, so closeAnonymizedProxy on it is a no-op. Per-tab upstream (setTabUpstream) is always the RAW resolved URL with credentials. The two paths behave differently.
- ProxyChain.anonymizeProxy never dials the upstream; it only starts a local server. So 'connect success' does not mean the proxy works, and failover, breaker and fail-closed never react to dead upstreams.
- _doQuickChangeProxy reuses the previous local port, so persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl stays the same string across profile switches. Anything keyed on that string (applyEffectiveTrafficMode sameProxy early-return, the check-ip-geo cache key, AzureTLS worker client cache) sees 'no change'. Always pass {force:true} to applyEffectiveTrafficMode and invalidate caches explicitly.
- applyEffectiveTrafficMode returns early when (mode, proxy) are unchanged and force is false. Callers that null persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl before calling it will NOT clear the MITM upstream.
- MitmProxy.setUpstream(non-null) silently lifts _egressBlocked. The egress-block check is `_egressBlocked && !tabUpstream.proxy`, so any stale non-null upstream bypasses the block.
- Only the anonymize step is serialized (_proxySwitchChain). Connect, disconnect, connect-direct and quick-connect can interleave writes to connectedProfileId, activeFingerprint and egressBlocked.
- dialog.showErrorBox inside _doQuickChangeProxy is synchronous and blocks the main-process event loop. The MITM server runs in the main process, so all browsing stalls while the modal is open.
- restartMitmProxy creates a new MitmProxy instance. _tabProxyMap, _activeJa3, forceHttp1, _egressBlocked and the connected profile's TLS are NOT restored (wireMitmAfterStart applies settings.tlsProfile).
- global-proxy-connect writes the private field mitmProxy._activeJa3 directly. mitm-tls-resilience-ipc does the same.
- checkCurrentIpGeo calls _trustSessionForMitm (setCertificateVerifyProc accept-all) on tab sessions, even when CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL>=4 intentionally skipped trust. setCertificateVerifyProc(cb(0)) disables ALL certificate validation for that session.
- ipinfo `country_name` is mapped from `country` (ISO code). last_geo is stored as 'City, CC', and the renderer's countryCodeFromLastGeoString relies on the trailing ISO2.
- notifyProxyStatus dedups on a JSON signature. State not included in the signature (egress blocked, MITM TLS) will not trigger a rebroadcast. tabManager.getAllTabs() returns a single-use Map iterator.
- tab objects from tabManager.getTab/getAllTabs are raw; fields like cupnetEnabled/direct/isolated exist only on getTabList() DTOs.
- db.saveProxyProfile upserts by NAME (name is not UNIQUE in the schema). Creating a profile with an existing name overwrites that profile. last_session_proxy relies on this behaviour.
- getProxyProfileEncrypted returns no id/country. getProxyProfiles returns parsed variables and computes needsReSave (url_encrypted IS NULL AND url_display LIKE '%@%').
- resolveProxyTemplateFromDbRow falls back to plaintext url_display when decryption fails or is unavailable (legacy rows). test-proxy-template does NOT use this fallback, so the two paths behave differently.
- The 'Direct' built-in profile (id '__direct__') exists only in the renderer; its UA/timezone/language/TLS live in localStorage 'direct_profile'. Only tls_profile is sent to 'connect-direct'.
- Provider templates embed the account password at SAVE time. Editing an account in localStorage does not update existing profiles until each profile is re-saved.
- Decodo sticky port is a {RAND:min-max} placeholder resolved on each connect/test. Oxylabs uses uppercase CC. NodeMaven's socks5 option switches scheme and port to 1080.
- testProxy validates through Chromium's stack (hidden BrowserWindow). Results say nothing about the AzureTLS/MITM path or its TLS fingerprint.
- TLS passthrough domains (default challenges.cloudflare.com, settings trafficOpts.tlsPassthroughDomains) bypass AzureTLS and use raw TCP via the GLOBAL upstream only.
- Large uncommitted WIP touches proxy-service.js (geo rewrite), global-proxy-connect.js (fail-closed), proxy-ipc.js (fail-closed save, force_http1, guard), proxy-notify-broadcast.js (mitm state/error), and the renderer/html (force_http1, Re-save badge). Diff against HEAD before refactoring.
- Dead or legacy code: proxy-selector.js; preload.js selectProxy/onLoadProxies/onLoadProxyProfiles/saveProxyProfile/testProxyProfile; getRealIp; _fetchIpinfoViaNet; resolveSessionProfileProxyUpstream (tests only); ipGeoChecksEnabled/getIpGeoChecksEnabled/getMitmStartPromise deps (set but never read); quick-connect-profile and apply-quick-proxy-change (no UI caller).

- node tests/test-proxy-resilience.js  (3 tests: circuit opening, half-open recovery, candidate ordering). Passed in this audit. Also run by `bash tests/run-all.sh` / `npm test`.
- npm run test:unit (vitest, include tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs): proxy-profile-resolve.test.mjs, proxy-save-fail-closed.test.mjs (save-proxy-profile rejects without safeStorage), proxy-tls-ua-sync.test.mjs, traffic-mode-router.test.mjs, utils.test.mjs (parseProxyTemplate). NOTE: proxy-providers.test.mjs imports node:test, which vitest probably cannot collect (verify).

- E2E gotcha (Electron 44, 2026-09-24): `electronApp.firstWindow()` may resolve to the Onboarding or gate window — every spec now takes the shell via `getMainWindow(electronApp)` (tests/e2e/helpers.js); `CUPNET_E2E=1` also adds `--use-mock-keychain` so safeStorage never opens the macOS Keychain prompt.
- E2E (Playwright + Electron, live network): npm run test:e2e -- tests/e2e/proxy-profiles.e2e.spec.js (save/delete profile, TLS profile roundtrip, connect-direct/disconnect) and tests/e2e/traffic-proxy.e2e.spec.js (MITM ready, httpbin via direct, check-ip-geo, isolated tab, getDirectIp, no cert errors).
- npm run chaos:proxy-sim (scripts/chaos-proxy-sim.js exercises ProxyResilienceManager).
- NO tests for: global-proxy-connect.js, proxy-service.js (checkCurrentIpGeo/testProxy/quickChangeProxy/connectProxyWithFailover), proxy-notify-broadcast.js, quick-connect-ipc.js, ext-proxy-ipc.js, ext-ports-store.js, session-egress-meta.js, session-profile-proxy.js, decodo-endpoints table sanity, proxy-manager-renderer.js. A local authenticated proxy-chain Server would make hermetic connect/failover tests possible.

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Electron runtime (`package.json devDependencies.electron`) — now: ^34.0.0 (installed 34.5.8, Chromium 132); Electron 34 is out of support. The Chromium engine is older than the Chrome 133 UA/TLS the app claims, so the mismatch is detectable. Upgrade to the current stable Electron and re-verify setCertificateVerifyProc, the session.fetch bypassCustomProtocolHandlers option, the console-message event signature and webContents.setWebRTCUDPPortRange.
- proxy-chain library (`package.json dependencies.proxy-chain; used in proxy-service.js, global-proxy-connect.js, quick-connect-ipc.js, mitm-tls-resilience-ipc.js, cupnet-runtime will-quit`) — now: ^2.5.9 (installed 2.5.9; npm latest reports 3.0.1); Major version bump available. Re-check the anonymizeProxy/closeAnonymizedProxy API, raw-URL passthrough for credential-less proxies, and SOCKS/HTTPS upstream support.
- Default MITM TLS client profile (`proxy-service.js startMitmProxy (browser: 'chrome_120'); mitm-proxy.js constructor L787, setBrowser fallback L910, L2456`) — now: chrome_120; Stale identifier. It is overridden after start by settings.tlsProfile, but any path that does not call setBrowser (fallbacks, ExternalProxyPort defaults) uses Chrome 120. Align with azuretls-go profile names (profiles.go).
- User-Agent presets in Proxy Manager (`proxy-manager-renderer.js UA_PRESETS L117-123`) — now: Chrome/133.0.0.0 (Win/Mac), Firefox/138.0, Safari Version/17.3.1 macOS 14_3_1, iPhone OS 17_3_1; Outdated as of 2026-09 (Chrome and Firefox are many majors newer; Safari/iOS moved to 26+). Also inconsistent with proxy-tls-ua-sync.js, which uses Safari/iOS 18.3.
- TLS template labels/descriptions (`proxy-manager.html tls-tpl-btn (~L1289-1316) and proxy-manager-renderer.js TLS_TEMPLATE_DESCS L142-149`) — now: Chrome 133, Firefox 138, Safari 18, iOS 18, Edge 133, Opera 119; Must match the actual azuretls-go profiles and current browser versions.
- UA table synced to TLS templates (`proxy-tls-ua-sync.js L10-24`) — now: Chrome/133, Firefox/138, Safari 18.3 (macOS 14_7_4), iOS 18_3, Edg/133, OPR/119 (+ legacy list incl. 17.3.1); Update together with the TLS profiles. The UA and TLS/HTTP2 fingerprint must describe the same browser version.
- Static JA3 presets (`proxy-manager-renderer.js TLS_JA3_PRESETS L152-159`) — now: Fixed JA3 strings 'measured via tls.peet.ws' (Chrome with 17613/65037/4588; the Firefox preset also contains 17613); JA3 is unstable for Chrome since Chrome 110 randomizes extension order; JA4 is the modern standard. The Firefox preset containing ALPS 17613 looks wrong. Regenerate from the current azuretls profiles.
- AzureTLS Go client (`azuretls-go/go.mod`) — now: github.com/Noooste/azuretls-client v1.11.0, go 1.24.0; Verify the latest release and its browser profile set (Chrome 14x/15x, Firefox 14x/15x, Safari 26) before refreshing the UI labels.
- Decodo endpoint table (`decodo-endpoints.js DECODO_ENDPOINT_ROWS`) — now: Static list of ~140 countries with sticky ranges/rotating ports; Provider tables drift. Suspicious rows: 'bd' portRotating 41001 (the pattern is xx000) and 'bd'/'fi' stickyMax 41099 (the pattern is xx999). Re-sync with Decodo docs.
- Hard-coded provider country lists (`proxy-providers.js PROXY_PROVIDERS (oxylabs 10 countries, iproyal 9, nodemaven = Decodo list)`) — now: Static arrays; Oxylabs and IPRoyal support far more countries. NodeMaven's list is borrowed from Decodo's table.
- Geo/IP lookup services (`proxy-service.js (ipinfo.io, api.ipify.org), misc-ipc get-direct-ip, provider tests`) — now: Unauthenticated https://ipinfo.io/json; Subject to rate limits. Make the provider and token configurable.
- Deprecated clipboard fallback (`proxy-manager-renderer.js L230-253`) — now: document.execCommand('copy'); Deprecated API; navigator.clipboard is sufficient in Electron.

**Production gaps:**

- Real upstream verification on connect: probe a check URL through the AzureTLS worker via the new upstream before declaring success, and feed the result into connectProxyWithFailover, ProxyResilienceManager and the fail-closed egress block. Today a dead proxy 'connects'.
- One serialized proxy state machine (single mutex, single source of truth) replacing the five divergent paths: connectGlobalProxyProfile, disconnectGlobalProxy, connect-direct, quick-connect-profile, apply-quick-proxy-change (+ set-tab-proxy). It must consistently handle fingerprint, TLS/JA3/forceHttp1, egress block, snapshots and caches.
- A complete kill switch: the egress block must cover TLS passthrough, WebSocket tunnels, WebRTC (policy must follow per-tab and blocked state), ext ports, and check-ip-geo's Node-direct request. Expose egressBlocked in the proxy-status payload and show a UI banner.
- Lock down the web-exposed API: preload-view.js must expose proxy/IP/ext-proxy methods only to internal file:// pages (new-tab.html). All proxy, ext-proxy, get-direct-ip and mitm-set-upstream handlers need sender validation (guardIpcHandler or senderFrame URL checks).
- Move provider account credentials out of renderer localStorage into main-process storage encrypted with safeStorage or the credentials vault. Stop storing secrets in proxy_profiles.variables (legacy __password, FALLBACK_PROXIES, user {PASS} vars). Encrypt ext-ports.json passwords and write with secure-fs permissions.
- Per-tab proxy is API-only (no UI caller for set-tab-proxy). Either build UI or remove it. Per-tab assignments must survive MITM restart and app restart.
- Geo check correctness: invalidate the cache on every proxy state change, key it by connection generation and profile id, resolve per-tab upstream even when there is no global proxy, never make direct requests while egress is blocked, and never run fetch inside a visited site's main world.
- Profile management: enforce a unique name constraint or prevent overwrite in the UI; reapply or warn when editing the connected profile; handle deleting a connected profile or tab-assigned profile; use or remove the sort_order column; import/export.
- Sticky session UX: show the real SID/RAND actually used, add a 'rotate SID' action, and use a provider-specific SID format (IPRoyal 8-char).
- Hermetic automated tests with a local authenticated upstream (proxy-chain Server). Cover connect/disconnect/failover/fail-closed, the check-ip-geo branches, testProxy timeouts, notifyProxyStatus, the ext-proxy IPC and session-egress-meta with encrypted logs.
- Error surfacing: replace dialog.showErrorBox with non-blocking UI notifications and return structured error codes (auth failed, DNS, connect refused, 407).
- Ext ports: decide the LAN-exposure policy (currently binds 127.0.0.1 while the UI advertises the LAN IP). Implement autoStart, duplicate-port checks, restart on set-port, and timing-safe auth.
- Remove dead code: proxy-selector.js and its legacy preload APIs/handlers, getRealIp, _fetchIpinfoViaNet, resolveSessionProfileProxyUpstream, ipGeoChecksEnabled plumbing.
- Keep the provider catalog up to date (endpoints/countries), ideally from provider APIs or a versioned data file.

### tabs-shell-main

Owns the browser shell of CupNet 2: the main BrowserWindow (browser.html + preload.js) and app menu; tab lifecycle in tab-manager.js (each tab is a WebContentsView on a persistent partition persist:cg_<cookieGroupId>, proxied to the local MITM at 127.0.0.1:<mitmPort>, tagged with X-CupNet-Rid, with per-session traffic filters, UA sanitization, media-permission handlers, and page-world injects); layout/z-order (toolbar+status-bar reserves, shell overlays that detach the active tab view, the omnibox suggestion overlay WebContentsView); error pages + reload/retry; all secondary tool windows (sub-windows.js) and their trusted-sender registration; keyboard hotkeys that must fire while a tab has focus; managed detached DevTools + DevTools-hostile-site registry + anti-anti-debug shim; cookie-jar ops, cookie groups and DNS overrides; launch/session profiles (parse, load into tab, export from a logged request); and the preload surfaces (shell preload.js, tab preload-view.js, and small preloads for omnibox overlay, session-profile modal, site-info popover).

**Files:**

- `/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/tab-manager.js` — Core tab engine (module singleton). State: tabs Map<tabId,tab>, activeTabId, _shellOverlayHideRefCount, extra top/bottom/right offsets, _upstreamProxyRules, _trafficOpts, omniboxOverlayView. Exports init, createTab (object OR legacy positional signature proxyRules,url,isolated,existingSessionId), switchTab, closeTab, navigate, ensureActiveTabViewVisible, getTabList, getActiveTab/Id, getTab, getAllTabs, getTabIdByWebContentsId, setProxy/setProxyAll, setTabProxy (metadata only), setTabCookieGroup (destroys+recreates the WebContentsView), isolateTab (deprecated), relayout/resizeActiveView, broadcastTabList (80ms debounce), setWindowSwitcherOverlayVisible (ref-counted detach), show/hide/updateOmniboxOverlay, setTrafficOpts, applyDevicePermissions, reapplyMitmTrustToSharedSession, reloadActiveTab (re-exported from tab-error-page). Builds/injects camera filter, credential-capture, storage-activity, paste-unlock scripts. Owns per-session webRequest onBeforeSendHeaders (X-CupNet-Rid) and onBeforeRequest (traffic filter).
- `/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/main-process/services/main-window.js` — createMainWindowApi(d): builds the main BrowserWindow, registerTrustedShell on its webContents, loads browser.html, and in did-finish-load awaits MITM start (120s race) then creates+switches the first tab, wires setupNetworkLogging+interceptor.attachToSession, sends init-settings. Owns before-input-event for F12 (managed DevTools) and F5/Ctrl+R (reloadActiveTab). tabManager.init onEvent callback: handles open-in-new-tab (confirmOpenAnotherTab + createTab + services), forwards tab-* events to the shell, and notifies cookie-manager/notes/credentials context. buildMenu() defines File/Edit/View accelerators and the confirm-exit dialog + secondary-window teardown on close.
- `/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/main-process/services/sub-windows.js` — createSubWindowsApi(d): factory for every secondary window (Log Viewer, Compare, Console, Page Analyzer, Notes, Credentials, API Scout/IVAC, Cookie Manager, DNS Manager, session-profile modal, logging modal, site-info popover, Proxy Manager, Onboarding, Rules, Breakpoint, Request Editor). All go through _spawnTrustedToolWindow/_spawnRequestEditorBrowserWindow which call registerTrustedShell. Also compare-pair scoring helpers, notifyCookieManagerTabs (150ms debounce), notifyCookieGroupsListsUpdated, syncDnsOverridesToMitm, reattachInterceptorToAllTabs, and the IVAC scout child-process runner.

**Key flows:**

- App boot: main.js loads MITM CA (NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS) before electron, then main-process/index.js (single-instance lock, error hooks) -> cupnet-runtime.js. Runtime requires tab-manager, db.init, sets interceptor resolveTabIdFromDetails via tabManager.getTabIdByWebContentsId, then either createMainWindow() or the profile gate (profileSession.shouldSkipProfileGate).
- First tab: main-window.js createMainWindow -> registerTrustedShell(mainWindow.webContents) -> loadFile browser.html. did-finish-load awaits getMitmStartPromise (120s race), enableIpGeoChecks, then tabManager.createTab(persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl, getNewTabUrl()) + switchTab; wires setupNetworkLogging + interceptor.attachToSession + currentSessionId; sends init-settings; notifyProxyProfilesList; 250ms delay; notifyProxyStatus.
- createTab(opts): tabId = tab_<Date.now()>_<n>; partition persist:cg_<cookieGroupId>; tabSession = session.fromPartition. _trustMitmCA(session) (setCertificateVerifyProc), setProxy(getProxyOptsForTab -> http=/https=127.0.0.1:mitmPort, bypass <local>), applyTrafficFiltersToSession, applyCupNetRidHeaderToSession(session,tabId), applyRendererUserAgentToSession, installMediaPermissionHandlers. new WebContentsView(buildTabViewWebPreferences: contextIsolation true, nodeIntegration false, webSecurity ALWAYS true, preload preload-view.js unless STEALTH>=6), registerTabView(wc), db.createSessionAsync, attachTabListeners, loadURL. View is NOT added to contentView until switchTab.
- attachTabListeners: page-title-updated, page-favicon-updated, will-navigate (notePendingGetNavigation + tab-will-navigate), did-navigate (tab.url, db.recordOmniboxVisit with typed flag from _pendingOmniboxTypedTabIds, url-updated), did-navigate-in-page, did-start/stop-loading, did-fail-load (mainFrame, skip -3 ERR_ABORTED, loadTabErrorPage), did-finish-load (after 32ms checks lastMainFrameStatus via shouldShowHttpErrorPage>=400 -> error page; else injectPasteUnlock, maybeRestorePageHttpLab, and, if not devtools-hostile, injectCredentialFormCapture + injectStorageActivityMonitor), setWindowOpenHandler (deny + emit open-in-new-tab), attachWindowSwitcherHotkeyToTabWebContents, attachTabWebRequestToolsContextMenu, attachMainFrameStatusTracker (onCompleted).
- switchTab(tabId): destroy omnibox overlay + force-close-omnibox, remove prev active view from mainWindow.contentView, set activeTabId, add new view (unless a shell overlay is hiding tab surface), resizeActiveView, url-updated + set-loading-state, broadcastTabList(true), onEvent tab-switched (-> notifyCookieManagerTabs, cookie-manager set-active-tab, notes/credentials context).
- Navigation from URL bar: browser-renderer sends navigate-to -> tabs-ipc resolves (cupnet aliases, resolveLoadUrlForWebContents with search-engine fallback) -> tabManager.navigate(url, undefined, {omniboxTyped:true}) -> ensureActiveTabViewVisible + _loadTabUrl (resolves relative to current URL, loadURL, on error emits tab-load-error -300).
- Error page + retry: did-fail-load or HTTP>=400 -> loadTabErrorPage sets tab.displayUrlOverride/isErrorPage and loads error-page.html?params. Reload button -> retry-failed-navigation IPC -> reloadActiveTab -> reloadFailedTabNavigation replays the real URL (POST body via buildLoadOptionsFromNavigationRetry when captured).
- Overlays & layout: resizeActiveView positions the active view at y=TOOLBAR_HEIGHT(95)+extraTop, reserving STATUS_BAR_HEIGHT(26)+extraBottom at bottom and extraRight. set-toolbar-height/set-bottom-chrome-height/set-right-chrome-width IPC drive the extra offsets. setWindowSwitcherOverlayVisible ref-counts detaching the tab view so HTML overlays (window switcher, command palette) paint above it. Omnibox overlay is a separate WebContentsView on session persist:cupnet_omnibox_overlay (direct proxy) added above the tab.
- Per-tab proxy: set-tab-proxy IPC -> tabManager.setTabProxy (stores proxyProfileId) then applyMitmAndFingerprintForTabProxy: mitmProxy.setTabUpstream(tabId,{upstream,browser tls,ja3,forceHttp1}) + applyFingerprintFromProfile; MITM keys the upstream by tabId parsed from X-CupNet-Rid (_tabIdFromCupnetRid). removeTabUpstream on null/close.
- setTabCookieGroup: detach interceptor from old session, capture URL, build new partition/session (trust CA, proxy, filters, RID, UA, media perms), destroy old WebContentsView, create new one, attachTabListeners, re-add to contentView, reload URL. Caller re-runs setupNetworkLogging + interceptor.attachToSession + proxy/fingerprint.
- Launch profile: session-profile modal (sessionProfileAPI.loadSessionProfile) -> session-profile-ipc load-session-profile -> loadSessionProfileFromJson -> loadSessionProfile: optional global proxy (disconnect + upsert last_session_proxy + connect), createTab or reuse active, fingerprint, clear/set cookies, navigate, _scheduleAfterNavigation (storage inject + runAfterLoad script on did-finish-load), attach logging+interceptor; result broadcast to shell.
- Secondary windows: menu/toolbar/IPC -> sub-windows create* -> _spawnTrustedToolWindow -> registerTrustedShell so guardIpcHandler-protected channels accept them. On close each nulls its d.<window> handle; credentials window also clearCredentialsVaultSession unless a profile session is active.

**Conventions:**

- All user-facing UI strings must be English (project rule); Russian only in code comments.
- Interception is MITM-only: never register session.protocol.handle('http'/'https') or webRequest header hooks for interception (Cloudflare/Turnstile detect the JS/fetch path). Only two webRequest listeners are allowed per tab session and tab-manager owns them: onBeforeSendHeaders (X-CupNet-Rid) and onBeforeRequest (traffic filter). tab-error-page adds exactly one onCompleted (mainFrame status) and comments warn not to add onBeforeRequest/onBeforeSendHeaders there.
- Every tab is a WebContentsView (not BrowserView/BrowserWindow) added to mainWindow.contentView; only switchTab adds the active view — createTab must not add it (avoids z-order/relayout on the wrong webContents).
- webSecurity is ALWAYS true for tab views (false breaks Cloudflare Turnstile). WebRTC leak policy is applied per-webContents, never via global Chromium flags.
- Tab webContents use preload-view.js (reduced API); shell + tool windows use preload.js (full API) and must be registered via registerTrustedShell. Tab views are registered via registerTabView and are never trusted for vault/proxy/cookie IPC.
- Sensitive IPC handlers are wrapped with guardIpcHandler/guardIpcListener (ipc-trusted-sender.js). New cookie/proxy/credential/profile channels should be guarded or ownership-checked (see _cookieSessionTab).
- Electron partition per cookie group: partitionForGroup(id) = 'persist:cg_'+id; group 1 = Default. Legacy 'persist:cupnet-shared' kept only for CA-trust/permission fan-out.
- Internal pages are file:// (new-tab.html/settings.html/cupnet-guide.html) surfaced as cupnet://settings|guide|home aliases via getInternalPageUrl and getTabDisplayUrl; error-page.html is detected by substring.
- DB writes go through the async queue (createSessionAsync, createCookieGroupAsync, saveDnsOverrideAsync, etc.); reads are sync.
- STEALTH tiers via CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL: >=1 disables page injects (paste/camera/storage/credential), >=2 disables interceptor attach, >=4 skips MITM CA trust, >=6 drops the tab preload entirely.
- Main-process ctx for IPC handlers is the ipcScope proxy built from ipc-scope-key-list.json (152 keys) + explicit getter/setter switch in cupnet-runtime.js; adding a ctx dependency requires adding it to both the JSON list and the switch.

**Gotchas:**

- Only ONE listener per Electron session per webRequest event. tab-manager owns onBeforeSendHeaders + onBeforeRequest; tab-error-page owns onCompleted. Cookie groups are shared across tabs, so recreating listeners on a shared session can clobber another tab's — attachMainFrameStatusTracker filters by webContentsId and is guarded by tab._statusTrackerAttached, but it is re-run for each tab sharing a session, replacing the single onCompleted handler each time.
- X-CupNet-Rid header (<tabId>_<uuid>) is how MITM/AzureTLS learns the per-tab upstream — CUPNET_DISABLE_CUPNET_RID=1 removes it and per-tab proxy overrides silently stop working. The regex in _tabIdFromCupnetRid requires a UUID suffix; a tabId containing an underscore+uuid-like tail could be mis-parsed.
- createTab has TWO signatures (object and legacy positional). Menu items and some IPC still call the positional form (createTab(proxyRules,url,isolated,existingSessionId)); passing an object as first arg switches modes.
- setTabCookieGroup / isolateTab destroy and recreate the WebContentsView and its session, which drops MITM upstream, network logging and interceptor — every caller must re-run setupNetworkLogging + interceptor.attachToSession (+ proxy/fingerprint). Easy to forget on a new call site.
- closeTab does NOT call interceptor.detachFromSession (only the close-tab IPC handler does); calling tabManager.closeTab directly leaks the interceptor session ref-count.
- Shared cookie group: closing a tab whose partition is still used by another tab and then clearing storage for that group would wipe the other tab's cookies. delete-cookie-group refuses if any tab still uses the group, but partition storage is otherwise shared silently.
- Overlay hide is ref-counted (_shellOverlayHideRefCount); an unbalanced show/hide leaves the active tab surface detached (blank content). ensureActiveTabViewVisible force-resets it before navigation as a safety net.
- broadcastTabList debounces 80ms and getTabList recomputes num=index+1 each call, so tab numbers are positional and shift when a tab closes; DevTools/window-switcher titles derived from findIndex can race a just-closed tab.
- did-finish-load waits 32ms then re-checks status; fast client-side redirects or SPA nav can make the HTTP-error-page heuristic fire on a stale lastMainFrameStatus. It only fires when !tab.isErrorPage.
- installMediaPermissionHandlers replaces the session permission handler globally per session; cameraMode 'none' denies video for ALL tabs on that partition, and switching to all/custom sets it null (loses any other permission gating).
- Omnibox overlay uses its own session persist:cupnet_omnibox_overlay set to direct proxy because file:// on the MITM-proxied default session can fail ERR_FAILED (-2). Do not route it through MITM.
- reapplyMitmTrustToSharedSession / applyDevicePermissions still fan out to SHARED_PARTITION ('persist:cupnet-shared') even though tabs now use persist:cg_1 — legacy partition kept alive only for these fan-outs.
- keyboard-shortcuts.js is documentation only and is out of sync with the real menu accelerators (e.g. it lists Ctrl+L and Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+P, but the menu binds Ctrl+Shift+L for Network Activity and Ctrl+P for Proxy Manager).
- runAfterLoad profile scripts are executed with executeJavaScript(script, true) in the page main world — a launch profile file is effectively arbitrary code; parseLaunchProfile only length-limits it.

**Tests:**

- Unit (vitest, node env, tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs; npm run test:unit): tab-error-page.test.mjs (buildLoadOptionsFromNavigationRetry POST/GET), session-cookie-utils.test.mjs + session-cookie-consolidate.test.mjs (cookie helpers/consolidation), session-profile.test.mjs (parser, 11 cases), ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs (trust registry + guardIpcHandler), devtools-hostile-sites.test.mjs, anti-anti-debug-script.test.mjs, omnibox-history.test.mjs, keyboard-shortcuts.test.mjs. NOTE: several use node:test not vitest (tab-error-page, session-cookie-utils, session-cookie-consolidate) — run with `node --test <file>`; vitest.config.js include only matches *.test.mjs but the node:test files still need the node runner (run-all.sh / test:no-e2e drives them).
- Verified locally: `node --test tests/unit/tab-error-page.test.mjs tests/unit/session-cookie-utils.test.mjs tests/unit/session-cookie-consolidate.test.mjs` => 13 pass / 0 fail.
- E2E (Playwright + Electron, tests/e2e; npm run test:e2e, smoke = test:e2e:smoke): cupnet.e2e.spec.js (startup, navigation, isolated tab cookie isolation, new tab), windows.e2e.spec.js (open/close every secondary window + stress), cookie-manager.e2e.spec.js (cm1-cm6 set/get/remove/clear/share + cookie-group tab switch), omnibox.e2e.spec.js (history suggestions, no autocomplete, Ctrl/Cmd+K palette, mod+Enter new tab, site-info popover, tab switch), settings.e2e.spec.js (homepage get/set, activity-monitor persist), dns-overrides / traffic-proxy / mitm-azure-first-load also exercise tab sessions. CUPNET_E2E=1 disable single-instance lock and confirm dialogs.
- Gaps: no automated coverage for setTabCookieGroup re-wiring, managed DevTools lifecycle, the shell-overlay ref-count detach/restore, error-page HTTP>=400 heuristic, or launch-profile runAfterLoad execution.

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Default Chrome User-Agent fallback string (`/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/user-agent-utils.js:25 (DEFAULT_CHROME_UA)`) — now: ...Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36; Hardcoded Chrome 120; installed Electron is 34.5.8 (Chromium ~132). When the default-session UA is unavailable this leaks a stale/mismatched Chrome major vs the real Chromium engine — a fingerprinting tell for Cloudflare. Should track the actual Chromium major.
- Electron major (`/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/package.json devDependencies.electron`) — now: ^34.0.0 (installed 34.5.8); Electron 34 / Chromium 132 line; not the latest stable. UA sanitization strips CupNet/Electron tokens but the emulated Chrome version elsewhere should be bumped in lockstep with Electron upgrades.
- App/product name embedded in the base UA (`/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/package.json (name 'cupnet', productName 'CupNet')`) — now: CupNet; Electron's default session UA contains 'CupNet/<ver> Electron/<ver>'. sanitizeUserAgentChromeOnly removes them for MITM + tab sessions, but any code path that reads navigator.userAgent before the session UA override is applied could still expose it.
- DevTools-hostile host list (`/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/main-process/services/devtools-hostile-sites.js:32 (DEFAULT_HOSTILE_HOSTS)`) — now: ['ozforensics.com']; Single hardcoded host; runtime add/set APIs exist but are not wired to a user-editable setting, so new anti-debug sites require a code change.
- IVAC scout default URL and TLS profile default (`/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/main-process/services/sub-windows.js getIvacScoutContext`) — now: https://appointment.ivacbd.com/ , tlsProfile default 'chrome'; Hardcoded target/site; fine for this app's domain but tied to a specific external service.

**Production gaps:**

- No render-process-gone / 'unresponsive' / child-process-gone handling on tab webContents — a renderer crash leaves a blank WebContentsView with no recovery UI (only the network error page path exists).
- No will-download / download manager wiring in the tabs subsystem; downloads on the tab session are unhandled.
- No find-in-page (findInPage/stopFindInPage) and no page zoom controls for tabs, though keyboard-shortcuts.js advertises Find in page (Cmd/Ctrl+F).
- No session/tab persistence across restarts — open tabs are not restored; only window bounds (window-state.json) persist.
- keyboard-shortcuts.js reference list is stale vs the real accelerators; the documented shortcuts table shown in Settings can mislead users.
- DevTools-hostile hosts and the anti-anti-debug allowlist are not user-configurable (no settings UI).
- setTabCookieGroup/isolateTab require each caller to manually re-attach logging+interceptor+proxy+fingerprint; this cross-cutting re-wiring is duplicated across ~4 call sites with no single helper, so a new call site can silently drop MITM upstream or logging.
- open-in-new-tab and per-tab proxy inheritance: a tab opened from a link inherits the default (global) proxy, not the opener tab's per-tab proxy override, which can leak the real IP for popups from a proxied tab.
- ipc-trusted-sender.js, preload-shell.js, profile-gate-window.js and several security-hardening pieces are still uncommitted WIP; the trust boundary is only partially applied (tabs-ipc navigation/new-tab handlers and many misc-ipc overlay handlers are unguarded).
- Launch-profile runAfterLoad executes arbitrary JS in the page main world with only a length limit — no sandbox, allowlist, or user confirmation before running an imported profile's script.
- get-direct-ip and ipinfo.io usage in getIvacScoutContext/misc make live external calls on a direct (non-proxied) session — a deanonymization vector if triggered while the user expects proxy-only traffic.

### tool-windows-a: Request Editor, Rules & Interceptor UI, Page Analyzer (+ Endpoint Scout), in-page HTTP Lab, Captcha solver stub, API/IVAC Scout

Repo root: /Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2 (all paths below are relative to it). This subsystem is a set of separate trusted tool windows plus one script injected into pages. Each window loads preload.js (contextIsolation, window.electronAPI) and talks to the main process over ipcRenderer.invoke. (1) Request Editor: a Postman-like window that sends HTTP requests through the Go AzureTLS worker in MITM mode, or through Electron net.fetch otherwise. It has history, collections, environments, cURL/Postman import and code generation. (2) Rules & Interceptor: a UI for the intercept_rules table (block / modifyHeaders / mock / script). The rules are enforced by request-interceptor.js on the MITM path, never by protocol.handle. The window also has a live Activity feed. (3) Page Analyzer: inspects a chosen tab by running scripts in the page's main world. It covers forms, captcha widget detection, meta, Web Storage (read and replace) and Endpoint Scout, which fetches the page's JS bundles and scans them for API paths. (4) HTTP Lab ("Web Request Tools"): a panel injected into the page's main world that sends fetch() calls with the page's own cookies. (5) captcha-solver.js: a stub. Automated solving is disabled. (6) API Scout (IVAC scout): a window that runs scripts/debug-ivac.js as a child process and streams its log and summary.

**Files:**

- `request-editor.html` — Request Editor markup. CSP: script-src 'self'. Top bar (method, URL, Send/Cancel, env select, Save/Import/Code/cURL, proxy badge, Env…, Sidebar, +), a History/Collections sidebar, request tabs (Params/Headers/Body/Auth), response tabs (Raw/Pretty/HTML/Image/Headers/Cookies), and <dialog>s import-dialog, codegen-dialog, env-dialog. Loads curl-parser, then codegen, then the renderer.
- `request-editor-renderer.js` — All Request Editor logic (about 1700 lines). state{params,headers,formFields,multipart,bodyType}; applyTemplate({{var}} from the selected env); mergeAuthIntoRequest; buildUrlWithParams; sendRequest -> api.executeRequest; renderResponse/renderError; buildCurl; history in localStorage (pushHistorySnapshot/applyHistoryEntry); collections tree (loadCollectionsTree/loadCollectionRequest); env dialog; import/codegen dialogs; initFromData (the 'request-editor-init' payload); CodeMirror init via api.getRequestEditorCodeMirror, falling back to a textarea; JSON tree (buildJsonNode); sandboxed HTML preview (renderHtmlPreview injects a CSP meta).
- `request-editor.css` — Request Editor styles on top of shared-dark-theme.css tokens: .panel-hidden, #cancel-btn.visible, #resp-content.visible, #history-pane.collapsed, .jt-* JSON tree, dialog.re-modal, .body-missing-warn.
- `request-editor-codegen.js` — window.CupNetCodegen.generateRequestCode(lang, {method,url,headers,body}) for curl|fetch|axios|python|go|php. Escaping uses escJs (JSON.stringify) and escPy.
- `request-editor-curl-parser.js` — window.CupNetParseCurl(text) -> {method,url,headers,body} via tokenizeCurl, and window.CupNetParsePostman(json), which returns only the first request found in a Postman v2.1 collection.
- `rules.html` — Rules window markup (modified in the working tree: font-src added to the CSP). CSP allows 'unsafe-inline' and 'unsafe-eval' for Monaco. Contains the toolbar (search, type filters, template select, New/Duplicate/Export/Import/History/Test toast), sidebar list, editor form (name, pattern, test URL, methods, group, tags, delay slider, stop_on_match, breakpoint, type-specific params, match headers/body), Activity panel, history modal and onboarding overlay. Loads node_modules/sortablejs/Sortable.min.js, node_modules/jsondiffpatch/.../html.css, rules-monaco-setup.js and rules.js.
- `rules.js` — Rules UI. SCRIPT_PRESETS plus INTERCEPT_AI_PROMPT_BASE ('Copy LLM prompt'). RULE_TEMPLATES (block-trackers, cors-everywhere, disable-csp, slow-network, mock-404). fillEditor/collectRuleFromForm; renderRuleList grouped by group_name with Sortable reorder; saveRule/duplicateRule; runUrlTest; Activity log (_actAll, max 2000, filters, export); showHistoryModal; onboarding (localStorage 'cupnet.rules.onboarding.v1'); keyboard shortcuts (Cmd+N/S/D/F/E, arrow keys, Delete/Backspace).
- `rules.css` — Rules window layout: .rule-row, .rule-group-head, .activity-panel(.collapsed), .monaco-wrap, .history-modal(.visible), #onboarding-overlay(.visible), .match-ok/.match-bad.
- `rules-monaco-setup.js` — window.CupNetRulesMonaco {init, getValue, setValue, syncFromTextareas}. Loads Monaco AMD file://<vs>/loader.js from the path returned by 'get-monaco-vs-path' and creates 5 editors bound to textareas (edit-req-headers, edit-resp-headers, edit-mock-body as json; edit-script-before, edit-script-after as javascript). Falls back to plain textareas.
- `page-analyzer.html` — Page Analyzer markup with inline CSS. Tabs: Forms, Captcha, Page Info (meta), Storage, Endpoint Scout. CSP default-src 'self'. The captcha notice text is Russian. The CSS for .captcha-solver-* is unused.
- `page-analyzer-renderer.js` — Page Analyzer logic. populateTabSelect ('__current__' plus tab list); runAnalysis({force, includeScout}) calls analyzePageForms/Captcha/Meta/Storage[/Endpoints] in parallel; auto-refresh every 3 s (setupAutoRefresh); renderForms with inline editing (pageAnalyzerAction set-value); renderCaptcha with copy params; Web Storage inspector (JSON textarea, read-only tree, applyStorageToPage); renderScout table. Many UI strings are Russian.
- `main-process/services/page-analyzer-injected-scripts.js` — String sources executed with webContents.executeJavaScript in the page's MAIN world: _analyzeFormsScript, _analyzeCaptchaScript (reCAPTCHA v2/v3/enterprise, hCaptcha, Turnstile, GeeTest v3, inline-script sitekey sniffing), _analyzeMetaScript, _analyzeEndpointsCollectScript (inline script texts, script src URLs, performance resource names, 'Just a moment' challenge hint). Exposed on ctx through cupnet-runtime.js.
- `main-process/services/page-analyzer-endpoint-scan.js` — Pure Node heuristics for Endpoint Scout: PATH_PATTERNS/LINE_PATTERNS regexes, extractApiEndpoints, classifyEndpoint, isLikelyApiEndpoint, extractObjectKeysFromText, buildEndpointReport({pageUrl,statusHint,inlineScripts,externalScripts,perfNames,startedAt}). MAX_SCRIPT_CHARS = 4,000,000. Unit tested.
- `main-process/services/page-http-lab.js` — Reads injected/page-http-lab.inject.js at require time (INJECT_SRC). isHttpLabEligibleUrl (http/https, or file:// excluding internal pages); injectPageHttpLab(wc, opts, tabId) runs executeJavaScript(`(${INJECT_SRC})(${JSON.stringify(opts)})`, userGesture=true); httpLabActiveTabIds Set; maybeRestorePageHttpLab (called by tab-manager.js after navigation); handleHttpLabUiState; attachTabWebRequestToolsContextMenu, which replaces the native context menu on eligible pages.
- `main-process/injected/page-http-lab.inject.js` — function cupnetPageHttpLab(options), which runs in the page's main world. It creates #cupnet-http-lab (panel), #cupnet-http-log (left log drawer), #cupnet-http-bubble and #cupnet-http-toast. CONFIG covers url, method, referer, bodyMode (none/urlencoded/multipart/json/raw), query, headers, fields, files and rawBody. submit() calls fetch(url, {credentials:'include', referrer}). Can import page forms (readPageForms, mergePageIntoConfig, CSRF tokens). Persists state in the page's localStorage. Sets window.CupNetHttpLab. Reports UI state through window.electronAPI.reportHttpLabUiState.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/page-analyzer-ipc.js` — registerPageAnalyzerIpc. Channels: analyze-page-forms/-captcha/-meta/-storage, apply-page-storage (replace semantics), analyze-page-endpoints (sequential session.fetch per script, FETCH_SCRIPT_MS = 25000, then buildEndpointReport), page-analyzer-action (focus/show/hide/toggle-disabled/toggle-password-visibility/set-value), inject-turnstile-token, solve-turnstile-captcha (always DISABLED).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/page-http-lab-ipc.js` — registerPageHttpLabIpc: inject-page-http-lab (tabId defaults to the active tab), inject-active-page-http-lab, and ipcMain.on('http-lab-ui-state') coming from tab views (preload-view.js reportHttpLabUiState).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/request-editor-ipc.js` — registerRequestEditorIpc: collection CRUD (list/save/delete nodes), environment CRUD, request-editor-pick-file (dialog.showOpenDialog), request-editor-build-multipart (reads files with fs in main, builds the multipart body with boundary ----cupnetFormBoundary<ts>, returns bodyBase64, and warns about missing files).
- `captcha-solver.js` — Stub. Exports CaptchaSolverError(code, message, meta) and solveTurnstileWithCapMonster(), which always throws DISABLED. CaptchaSolverError is on ctx and used by solve-turnstile-captcha.
- `ivac-scout.html` — API Scout window: URL input (default https://appointment.ivacbd.com/), Start/Stop/Open _debug, Proxy/TLS/State badges, metric cards, endpoints table, live log, and a Russian 'what to do next' box.
- `ivac-scout-renderer.js` — API Scout logic. refreshContext every 1 s (get-ivac-scout-context); Start calls runIvacScout({url}); log lines beginning with '/' are collected as endpoints; applySummary(summary) on 'ivac-scout-done'; copy endpoints; open dump folder.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/log-compare-execute-ipc.js (neighbour)` — Owns open-request-editor (buildRequestEditorPrefill from db.getRequest or a payload), open-request-editor-new-window, execute-request (MITM worker path or net.fetch fallback, maybeLog into the DB and live log viewers), cancel-execute-request (requestEditorExecState Map), open-rules-window, and open-rules-window-with-mock (sends 'prefill-intercept-rule').
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/rules-ipc.js (neighbour)` — Owns every rules channel: get-monaco-vs-path, format-intercept-rule-diff-html (jsondiffpatch), get/save/delete-intercept-rule, select-mock-file, test-intercept-notification, test-intercept-script, test-intercept-url-match, export/import-intercept-rules, reorder-intercept-rules, get-intercept-rule-history, export-rules-activity-log, breakpoint-resume.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/launcher-ipc.js (neighbour)` — open-page-analyzer, open-ivac-scout, get-ivac-scout-context, run-ivac-scout, stop-ivac-scout, open-ivac-dump-folder (<ctx._cupnetRoot>/_debug).
- `main-process/services/sub-windows.js (neighbour)` — Window factories: openRequestEditorWindow (singleton, re-sends 'request-editor-init'), openRequestEditorNewWindow (extra windows), createRulesWindow (replays 80 recent intercept events), createPageAnalyzerWindow plus _sendAnalyzerTabs/notifyCookieManagerTabs ('analyzer-tabs-updated'), createIvacScoutWindow, runIvacScoutProcess, stopIvacScoutProcess, getIvacScoutContext, reattachInterceptorToAllTabs.
- `preload.js (neighbour)` — Renderer API for these windows: lines ~5-20 (lazy CodeMirror require), ~156-173 (rules), ~240-253 (request editor), ~317-331 (page analyzer and HTTP Lab), ~415-422 (IVAC scout), ~436/445 (getCurrentProxy/onProxyStatusChanged).
- `request-interceptor.js (neighbour)` — Rule engine: validateInterceptRuleForSave, ruleMatchesUrl/matchesPattern, planMitmIntercept (called from mitm-proxy.js), finalizeMitmInterceptResponse(Async), runInterceptScriptSelfTest, and env flags CUPNET_INTERCEPT_*.
- `db.js (neighbour)` — listRequestEditorCollectionTree/insert/update/deleteRequestEditorCollectionNode, list/upsert/deleteRequestEditorEnvironment (~2486-2597); getAllInterceptRules/saveInterceptRule (history snapshot of the previous row)/reorder/getInterceptRuleHistory/deleteInterceptRule (~1540-1655); schema at ~703 and ~815-845.
- `scripts/debug-ivac.js (neighbour)` — The API Scout child process. Uses the legacy azuretls/azureTLS.js (ffi) client and a hard-coded Chrome/133 UA. Writes a dump and summary.json to ../_debug.
- `tab-manager.js (neighbour)` — Tab ids are strings 'tab_<ts>_<n>'. Line ~604 calls maybeRestorePageHttpLab after navigation. Line ~619 calls attachTabWebRequestToolsContextMenu.

**Key flows:**

- Open Request Editor. Entry points: toolbar api.openRequestEditor(null), log viewer openRequestEditor(id|prefill), and the command palette. The call goes to invoke 'open-request-editor', then buildRequestEditorPrefill(ctx, idOrPayload). For a DB id it uses db.getRequest(id) and returns {method,url,headers:JSON.parse(request_headers),body}; a '__b64__:' body is replaced with the text '[Binary body, base64 in DB]'. Then ctx.openRequestEditorWindow(data) runs. If the singleton window exists, it is shown and 'request-editor-init' is sent. Otherwise a new BrowserWindow is created with preload.js, registered via registerTrustedShell, loads request-editor.html, and sends the payload on did-finish-load. The '+' button calls 'open-request-editor-new-window', which runs openRequestEditorNewWindow and tracks the window in ctx.requestEditorExtraWindows. In the renderer, initFromData(data) sets method/URL/params/headers and detects the body type (JSON parse, or content-type json / x-www-form-urlencoded). For POST/PUT/PATCH without a body it shows a .body-missing-warn notice.
- Send a request (request-editor-renderer.js sendRequest). buildUrlWithParams() takes the base URL through applyTemplate and appends the enabled params, encodeURIComponent'd. pushHistorySnapshot() records the request. Header values go through applyTemplate, then mergeAuthIntoRequest applies bearer, basic (btoa), apikey (header or query) or a custom header. The body depends on bodyType: json adds Content-Type; form-urlencoded; form-data calls api.requestEditorBuildMultipart and gets bodyBase64 plus the boundary Content-Type. Then api.executeRequest({method,url,headers,body|bodyBase64,cancelToken:'re_<ts>_<rand>'}) is invoked on 'execute-request'. The handler runs sanitizeOutgoingRequestHeaders (drops pseudo-headers). If ctx.getCurrentTrafficMode()==='mitm' and ctx.mitmProxy.worker.ready, it calls ctx.mitmProxy.worker.request({proxy: ctx.persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl, browser: payload.tlsProfile||loadSettings().tlsProfile||'chrome', disableRedirects:true, timeout: networkPolicy.timeouts.requestEditorMs (CUPNET_TIMEOUT_REQUEST_EDITOR_MS, default 30000)}) and races it against the cancel promise; the response bodyBase64 is decoded as UTF-8. Otherwise it falls back to ctx.net.fetch with the EXEC_FORBIDDEN header filter and an AbortController timeout. maybeLog() runs only if isLoggingEnabled && currentSessionId: db.insertRequestAsync(sessionId, null, {type:'request-editor', requestId:'re-<ts>'...}), then logEntryCount++, sendLogStatus, _broadcastLogEntryToViewers. Back in the renderer, renderResponse fills the status pill, the Raw view (CodeMirror or pre fallback), Pretty (buildJsonNode), HTML (sandboxed iframe with a CSP meta injected), Image (Blob from the text body), Headers and Set-Cookie. It auto-selects a tab by content-type and calls updateHistoryResult.
- Cancel. The Cancel button calls 'cancel-execute-request'(token). requestEditorExecState.get(token) is marked cancelled, abort() runs (fetch path only), and _cancelReject('Cancelled') makes the race return {success:false,cancelled:true}.
- Collections and environments. Save (Cmd+S) prompts for a name, builds a payload with parent_id = selectedCollectionFolderId, *_json strings and auth_json, and calls 'request-editor-save-collection-node'. That runs db.insertRequestEditorCollectionRow, or updateRequestEditorCollectionRow when row.id is set; folder updates null all request fields. Table request_editor_collections has parent FK ON DELETE CASCADE and foreign_keys=ON. The Collections tab calls 'request-editor-list-collections', renders rows sorted by depth, and clicking a request runs loadCollectionRequest. The Env… dialog uses 'request-editor-list-environments' and 'request-editor-upsert-environment'({id?, name, variables_json}); the JSON is validated in the renderer. The #env-select value picks the variables applyTemplate uses.
- Import, Code and cURL. The Import dialog treats text starting with '{' as JSON and runs CupNetParsePostman (v2.1, first request only); otherwise CupNetParseCurl. The result fills method/URL/headers/body (JSON detection). The Code dialog calls CupNetCodegen.generateRequestCode(lang, merged request with templates and auth applied). The toolbar cURL button and the response 'Copy cURL' use buildCurl() (single-quote escaping, -F for multipart). Note that buildCurl does not apply env templates to headers.
- Rules window boot. It opens from the app menu or toolbar via 'open-rules-window', which runs createRulesWindow; on did-finish-load it sends 'intercept-rule-matched-batch' with ipcBatch.getRecentInterceptEventsSlice(80). DOMContentLoaded runs boot(): buildTypeFilters, buildMethodCheckboxes, initMockMimeSelect, wireEvents (subscribes onPrefillInterceptRule, onInterceptRuleMatched, onInterceptRuleMatchedBatch), bindKeyboard, then CupNetRulesMonaco.init. That calls 'get-monaco-vs-path' (monacoVsDir finds node_modules/monaco-editor/min/vs), loads file://.../loader.js and require(['vs/editor/editor.main']). Finally loadRules() calls 'get-intercept-rules' (db.getAllInterceptRules ORDER BY priority DESC LIMIT 1000), then renderRuleList (groups, Sortable) and maybeOnboarding.
- Save or modify a rule. collectRuleFromForm() builds the params per type: modifyHeaders {requestHeaders, responseHeaders} from JSON editors; mock {status, mimeType, mockSource:'text'|'file', body|mockFilePath}; script {beforeSource, afterSource}; plus optional matchHeaders[] and matchBody{}. A new rule gets priority = max+10. 'save-intercept-rule' runs requestInterceptor.validateInterceptRuleForSave (strict URL mode, script syntax). Then db.saveInterceptRuleAsync, which inserts the previous row JSON into intercept_rule_history. Then ctx.interceptor.invalidateRulesCache() and reattachInterceptorToAllTabs(). The latter only unhandles protocol http/https; real enforcement is planMitmIntercept/finalizeMitmInterceptResponse inside mitm-proxy.js. The enable checkbox saves {...rule, enabled}. Sortable onEnd sends 'reorder-intercept-rules' with pairs {id, priority:(n-i)*100} and saves any group changes. Templates are saved directly. Delete is Delete/Backspace on the keyboard only: 'delete-intercept-rule', which also deletes the rule's history.
- Rules activity and mock prefill. request-interceptor's _onRuleMatch goes to cupnet-runtime broadcastInterceptRuleMatched, then ipc-batch-messenger, which sends 'intercept-rule-matched' / 'intercept-rule-matched-batch' to the window. rules.js pushActivity keeps the last 2000 entries and filters by text, type and date range. Export uses 'export-rules-activity-log'({entries, format:'csv'?}). The log viewer's 'mock this response' action calls api.openRulesWithMock({name, url_pattern: full URL, params:{body,mimeType,status}}), which invokes 'open-rules-window-with-mock'; main sends 'prefill-intercept-rule' and the renderer runs fillEditor(mock, unsaved).
- Page Analyzer. 'open-page-analyzer' runs createPageAnalyzerWindow, which sends 'analyzer-tabs-list' on load; tab changes send 'analyzer-tabs-updated', debounced 150 ms, from notifyCookieManagerTabs. runAnalysis() fires on load, on tab change, on Refresh, and every 3 s while Auto is on (skipped while inline editing or while the storage textarea has focus). It runs Promise.all of analyze-page-forms/-captcha/-meta/-storage. Each handler resolves ctx.tabManager.getTab(tabId) and runs executeJavaScript(`(${ctx._analyze*Script})()`) in the page MAIN world. Field buttons call 'page-analyzer-action'({type, formIndex, fieldIndex, value}), which builds a script addressing document.querySelectorAll('form')[fi].elements[fld]. For storage, the textarea JSON goes through normalizeStorageEntries and then 'apply-page-storage'({target:'session'|'local', entries}). That removes keys not in entries, sets the rest as strings, and re-reads.
- Endpoint Scout. Clicking Explore runs runAnalysis({includeScout:true}), which adds 'analyze-page-endpoints'. The handler runs _analyzeEndpointsCollectScript in the page, then fetches each script URL with wc.session.fetch(u, {bypassCustomProtocolHandlers:true, cache:'no-store'}), sequentially, with a 25 s timeout each. buildEndpointReport regex-scans the inline scripts, fetched scripts and performance names, filters with isLikelyApiEndpoint, classifies with classifyEndpoint, and collects hits (source, line, preview), methods and payloadKeys. renderScout shows the table; 'Copy endpoints' copies the list.
- HTTP Lab. Entry points: the tab context menu 'Web Request Tools' (attachTabWebRequestToolsContextMenu, 4 options), the app menu 'Web Request Tools (HTTP Lab)' Cmd+Shift+H (main-window.js, importForms:true), and the toolbar httpLabBtn via 'inject-active-page-http-lab'. All go through injectPageHttpLab(wc, opts, tabId): isHttpLabEligibleUrl, then executeJavaScript(`(${INJECT_SRC})(${JSON.stringify(opts)})`, true) in the page main world. cupnetPageHttpLab(opts) runs loadLs, optionally mergePageIntoConfig, then mountPanel and the bubble/log panel. Send runs submit(), which uses page fetch with credentials:'include' and a custom referrer, so traffic goes Chromium -> session proxy -> MITM. UI state goes to page localStorage 'cupnet_http_lab_ui_v1' and to window.electronAPI.reportHttpLabUiState, which sends 'http-lab-ui-state' to handleHttpLabUiState. After navigation, tab-manager calls maybeRestorePageHttpLab. That restore is broken; see suspectedBugs.
- API Scout (IVAC scout). 'open-ivac-scout' runs createIvacScoutWindow, which sends 'ivac-scout-state' on load. The renderer polls 'get-ivac-scout-context' every 1 s: active tab URL or the IVAC default, tlsProfile from settings, and proxy label/URL from ctx.persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl and connectedProfileName. Start calls 'run-ivac-scout'({url}), which runs runIvacScoutProcess and spawns node (or process.execPath with ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE=1 when packaged) scripts/debug-ivac.js --url --proxy --browser. stdout/stderr lines are sent as 'ivac-scout-log'. On close it reads <root>/_debug/summary.json and sends 'ivac-scout-state' and 'ivac-scout-done'{ok, exitCode, summary}. Stop sends SIGTERM. Closing the window also stops the process.
- Captcha. The analyze-page-captcha script only detects widgets. 'solve-turnstile-captcha' always returns {ok:false, error:{code:'DISABLED', retryable:false}} using ctx.CaptchaSolverError. 'inject-turnstile-token'(tabId, {token, sitekey, action, autoSubmit}) sets the cf-turnstile-response inputs, invokes data-callback, known global callbacks and ___turnstile_cfg callbacks, and can optionally requestSubmit the form. No UI currently calls it (preload exposes injectTurnstileToken).

**IPC channels:**

- Request Editor, renderer->main invoke: open-request-editor(idOrPayload), open-request-editor-new-window, execute-request({method,url,headers,body,bodyBase64,tlsProfile?,cancelToken}), cancel-execute-request(token), request-editor-list-collections, request-editor-save-collection-node(row), request-editor-delete-collection-node(id), request-editor-list-environments, request-editor-upsert-environment(row), request-editor-delete-environment(id), request-editor-pick-file, request-editor-build-multipart({text[],files[]}), get-current-proxy, open-cookie-manager(null).
- Request Editor, main->renderer: request-editor-init(payload), proxy-status-changed.
- Rules, renderer->main invoke: open-rules-window, open-rules-window-with-mock(data), get-intercept-rules, save-intercept-rule(rule) -> {id}|{error}, delete-intercept-rule(id), reorder-intercept-rules(pairs), get-intercept-rule-history(id,limit), test-intercept-url-match(pattern,url) -> {ok}, test-intercept-script({beforeSource,afterSource}), test-intercept-notification, select-mock-file -> {filePath,size}, export-intercept-rules, import-intercept-rules -> {ok,imported}, export-rules-activity-log({entries,format}), get-monaco-vs-path. The main-side handler format-intercept-rule-diff-html(leftJson,rightJson) exists, but preload does NOT expose formatInterceptRuleDiffHtml.
- Rules, renderer->main send: reset-toolbar-activity-badge('rules'). Main->renderer: intercept-rule-matched(info), intercept-rule-matched-batch(items), prefill-intercept-rule(data).
- Page Analyzer, renderer->main invoke: open-page-analyzer, get-tabs, analyze-page-forms(tabId), analyze-page-captcha(tabId), analyze-page-meta(tabId), analyze-page-storage(tabId), apply-page-storage(tabId,{target,entries}), analyze-page-endpoints(tabId), page-analyzer-action(tabId,{type,formIndex,fieldIndex,value}), inject-turnstile-token(tabId,payload), solve-turnstile-captcha(tabId). Main->renderer: analyzer-tabs-list(tabs), analyzer-tabs-updated(tabs).
- HTTP Lab: invoke inject-page-http-lab(tabId,opts) and inject-active-page-http-lab(opts) from the shell (browser-renderer httpLabBtn). Send 'http-lab-ui-state'(state) goes tab view -> main via preload-view.js reportHttpLabUiState, which is callable from page JS because preload-view exposes 'electronAPI' in the tab main world.
- API Scout, renderer->main invoke: open-ivac-scout, get-ivac-scout-context, run-ivac-scout({url}) (resolves when the process exits), stop-ivac-scout, open-ivac-dump-folder. Main->renderer: ivac-scout-log(line), ivac-scout-state({running}), ivac-scout-done({ok,exitCode,summary,error?}).

**Data stores:**

- SQLite request_editor_collections (id, parent_id FK ON DELETE CASCADE, node_type 'folder'|'request', name, sort_order, method, url, headers_json, body, body_type, auth_json, params_json, form_fields_json, multipart_json, created_at, updated_at). Stores auth secrets in plaintext.
- SQLite request_editor_environments (id, name, variables_json, sort_order, created_at, updated_at).
- SQLite intercept_rules (id, name, enabled, url_pattern, type CHECK block|modifyHeaders|mock|script, params JSON, priority, method, group_name, hit_count, last_hit_at, delay_ms, tags, last_error, stop_on_match, breakpoint_enabled, created_at).
- SQLite intercept_rule_history (id, rule_id, snapshot = full previous row JSON, changed_at). Never pruned; cleared only when the rule is deleted.
- SQLite requests (read by db.getRequest for editor prefill; written by db.insertRequestAsync with type 'request-editor' when logging is enabled).
- Request Editor window localStorage: 'cupnet-request-editor-layout-v2' {reqWidth, historyCollapsed}; 'cupnet-request-editor-history-v1' (max 48 entries, includes auth tokens and passwords in plaintext).
- Rules window localStorage: 'cupnet.rules.onboarding.v1'.
- Page (tab origin) localStorage written by HTTP Lab: 'cupnet_http_lab_v1' (CONFIG), 'cupnet_http_lab_ui_v1' {active, minimized, bubbleX, bubbleY}, 'cupnet_log_width', 'cupnet_log_autoscroll'; 'cupnet_http_lab_history_v1' is declared but unused.
- Settings: loadSettings().tlsProfile (request editor MITM path, IVAC scout context).
- Env vars: CUPNET_TIMEOUT_REQUEST_EDITOR_MS (network-policy.js, default 30000); CUPNET_INTERCEPT_STRICT_URLS=1; CUPNET_INTERCEPT_SCRIPT_MS (default 400); CUPNET_INTERCEPT_ALLOW_MOCK_CF=1; IVAC_PROXY/HTTP_PROXY (debug-ivac.js); ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE, ELECTRON_IS_DEV (runIvacScoutProcess).
- Filesystem: <cupnetRoot>/_debug/ (debug-ivac dumps + summary.json; opened by open-ivac-dump-folder); node_modules/monaco-editor/min/vs (Monaco AMD); files chosen for multipart (read in main); mock rule files (params.mockFilePath read by the interceptor); export JSON/CSV in ~/Documents by default.
- In-memory: requestEditorExecState Map (cancel tokens) in log-compare-execute-ipc.js; httpLabActiveTabIds Set in page-http-lab.js; ipcBatch _recentInterceptEvents; ctx.ivacScoutProcess; ctx.requestEditorWindow / requestEditorExtraWindows / rulesWindow / pageAnalyzerWindow / ivacScoutWindow.

**Conventions:**

- All user-visible UI text must be English. This subsystem currently breaks that rule: page-analyzer.html/renderer storage and captcha texts, and ivac-scout.html/renderer next-actions are Russian. Code comments in Russian are tolerated.
- Renderers only use window.electronAPI from preload.js (contextIsolation:true, nodeIntegration:false). To add a feature: (1) add ipcMain.handle in main-process/ipc/handlers/<domain>-ipc.js; (2) add a preload.js binding (invoke, or sub() for main->renderer events); (3) if the handler needs a new ctx field, add it to main-process/ipc/ipc-scope-key-list.json and regenerate _ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js with `node scripts/gen-ipc-scope-accessors.cjs > main-process/ipc/_ipc-scope-accessors.inc.js`, also updating the cupnet-runtime.js switch (see main-process/ipc/README.md).
- Tool windows are created through sub-windows.js with registerTrustedShell(win.webContents) (_spawnTrustedToolWindow / _spawnRequestEditorBrowserWindow). Sensitive IPC should be wrapped with guardIpcHandler(channel, fn) from main-process/ipc/ipc-trusted-sender.js. None of this subsystem's channels are wrapped yet.
- Interception must stay on the MITM path. Never add session.protocol.handle('http'/'https') or webRequest hooks. Rules are applied in request-interceptor.planMitmIntercept/finalizeMitmInterceptResponse called from mitm-proxy.js; reattachInterceptorToAllTabs only unhandles protocols.
- Outbound HTTP from main should prefer the MITM/AzureTLS worker (ctx.mitmProxy.worker.request) over Chromium net.fetch; net.fetch is only a fallback.
- Scripts injected into pages are strings run with webContents.executeJavaScript in the MAIN world. Any value interpolated into them must be passed through JSON.stringify (see apply-page-storage and inject-turnstile-token); page-analyzer-action does not do this (see suspectedBugs).
- Renderer HTML building uses innerHTML with a local esc()/escHtml(). Always escape page- or DB-derived strings, including in attributes (the page-analyzer esc escapes quotes; request-editor esc does not, so use it only in text context).
- Tab ids are strings ('tab_<ts>_<n>'). Never coerce them with Number().
- Rule params are stored as a JSON string in intercept_rules.params. tags are stored as a JSON array string. stop_on_match and breakpoint_enabled are ints. Priority is higher-first (ORDER BY priority DESC).
- Request Editor per-window state lives in localStorage (history, layout). Durable data (collections, environments) lives in SQLite via db.js.

**Gotchas:**

- The Request Editor window is created without sandbox:false, so under Electron 34 it is a sandboxed renderer. The preload's lazy require('codemirror') and require('@codemirror/*') cannot work there. Returning class constructors and EditorView instances across contextBridge would not work either. Expect the textarea fallback (body-fallback-ta / resp-pre-fallback) to be the real path. Do not rely on CodeMirror features such as the Cmd+F search panel.
- Request Editor in MITM mode calls the Go worker directly (ctx.mitmProxy.worker.request). It does NOT pass through mitm-proxy's intercept rules, DNS overrides, per-tab proxy profile or session cookies. It uses the global ctx.persistentAnonymizedProxyUrl and the global tlsProfile. The fallback net.fetch uses Electron's default session, not a tab session.
- execute-request decodes response bodies as UTF-8 strings (res.bodyBase64 -> toString('utf8') / resp.text()). Binary data is lost before it reaches the renderer.
- EXEC_FORBIDDEN (content-length, host, connection, accept-encoding, …) is filtered only on the net.fetch fallback. The MITM worker path forwards those headers as given, including stale Content-Length from replayed log entries.
- Page Analyzer and injected scripts run in the page MAIN world through executeJavaScript. The page can observe or tamper with them (prototype hooks, DOM clobbering), and auto-refresh repeats them every 3 s. This matters for Cloudflare/anti-bot targets. Consider executeJavaScriptInIsolatedWorld.
- HTTP Lab is highly visible to the page: window.CupNetHttpLab, #cupnet-* DOM nodes, localStorage cupnet_* keys, and requests carry X-Requested-With by default. preload-view.js also exposes window.electronAPI to every page.
- The IPC channels here are not guarded by guardIpcHandler. Tab views cannot reach them today only because preload-view.js does not expose them. request-editor-build-multipart reads any path in main; apply-page-storage and page-analyzer-action mutate pages.
- Rules: enforcement happens only for traffic routed through the CupNet MITM (planMitmIntercept). 'reattachInterceptorToAllTabs' / 'syncMockProtocolHandlers' now only unhandle protocols; the names are legacy.
- ruleMatchesUrl treats a plain http(s) URL without '*' as a strict prefix (query/hash ignored). Glob patterns go through matchesPattern regex conversion, which does not escape '?', so '?' acts as a regex quantifier.
- Script rules run in a Node vm in the main process with a timeout (CUPNET_INTERCEPT_SCRIPT_MS). vm is not a security boundary. Imported rule files can contain script rules.
- jsondiffpatch 0.7.3 is ESM-only but is require()'d in rules-ipc.js. This works only because Electron 34.5.8 ships Node 20.19.1 with require(esm). Keep that in mind when changing Electron or Node.
- Monaco is loaded via the AMD loader from file://<node_modules>/monaco-editor/min/vs. rules.html CSP therefore needs 'unsafe-eval', blob: workers and font-src data:. Monaco editors are created while their panels are display:none (automaticLayout fixes the size on show).
- Editor windows are singletons except Request Editor extra windows. openRequestEditorWindow re-sends 'request-editor-init' into an existing window, and initFromData then partially overwrites state (see suspectedBugs).
- run-ivac-scout's promise resolves only when the child exits, so the renderer's await blocks for the whole run. The summary and dump go to <cupnetRoot>/_debug, which is inside app.asar (read-only) in packaged builds.
- package.json build.files is '**/*', so _debug, _debug.zip, backup zips, logs and test-results are packed into the app. The API Scout's _debug dir ships with it.

**Tests:**

- tests/test-page-analyzer-endpoints.js: plain Node assert tests for page-analyzer-endpoint-scan (extractApiEndpoints, isLikelyApiEndpoint, buildEndpointReport). Run `node tests/test-page-analyzer-endpoints.js`; also included in `npm test` (tests/run-all.sh).
- tests/test-interceptor.js: rule engine (request-interceptor.js) used by the Rules window. Run `node tests/test-interceptor.js` or `npm test`.
- tests/e2e/request-editor.e2e.spec.js: re1 executeRequest GET httpbin, re2 replay last DB request. Needs network and MITM ready. Run `node scripts/playwright-e2e.mjs tests/e2e/request-editor.e2e.spec.js`.
- tests/e2e/intercept.e2e.spec.js: i1-i6 block/mock/modifyHeaders/script via IPC against httpbin (part of `npm run test:e2e:smoke`).
- tests/e2e/captcha-demo-detection.e2e.spec.js: runs the Page Analyzer captcha script against 2Captcha demo pages (network-dependent).
- tests/e2e/site-smoke.e2e.spec.js (2026-09-25): normal-site compatibility smoke through the MITM — per site: main-document status < 400 (PerformanceNavigationTiming.responseStatus), no anti-bot interstitial / block page, body text + links, instant scroll, focus + webContents.insertText into the primary input. Default list: example.com, Wikipedia, DuckDuckGo, GitHub login, Stack Overflow, npmjs, mozilla.org, BBC News. Engine identity by default; CUPNET_SITE_SMOKE_TEMPLATE=<catalog template id> applies a template via electronAPI.setIdentity; CUPNET_SITE_SMOKE_SITES overrides the list; CUPNET_SKIP_EXTERNAL=1 skips (network-dependent). Documented in docs/ai/BROWSER_IDENTITY_STATUS.md §3c.
- tests/unit/ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs covers the guard helper, but no channel in this subsystem uses it.
- Not covered: request-editor-curl-parser.js, request-editor-codegen.js, request-editor-ipc.js multipart builder, rules.js UI logic (templates, reorder, keyboard), rules-monaco-setup.js, page-analyzer-renderer.js, page-analyzer-ipc.js actions/storage, page-http-lab.js / inject script, ivac scout process (runIvacScoutProcess), captcha-solver.js. Vitest unit tests (`npm run test:unit`, tests/unit/*.test.mjs) are the natural place for the parsers and codegen.

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Electron runtime (`package.json devDependencies "electron": "^34.0.0" (installed 34.5.8)`) — now: Electron 34.5.8 = Chromium 132.0.6834.210, Node 20.19.1; Electron 34 is past end-of-life. Upgrade to a currently supported major. Recheck the sandbox/preload behaviour (CodeMirror require), require(esm) of jsondiffpatch, and net.fetch semantics after upgrading.
- Hard-coded Chrome User-Agent (`scripts/debug-ivac.js CHROME_HEADERS['User-Agent']`) — now: Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) ... Chrome/133.0.0.0 Safari/537.36; Stale UA, and it does not match the TLS profile passed with --browser. Derive it from user-agent-utils / the TLS profile instead.
- Legacy ffi AzureTLS client used by API Scout (`scripts/debug-ivac.js require('../azuretls/azureTLS.js')`) — now: ffi-napi based azuretls (skipped on Windows release builds per recent commits); Migrate to the Go NDJSON worker (azuretls-go) used by the MITM proxy.
- Monaco AMD loader (`rules-monaco-setup.js / rules-ipc.js monacoVsDir()`) — now: monaco-editor 0.55.1 via min/vs/loader.js + require(['vs/editor/editor.main']); AMD/min build loading is legacy. Check the ESM build before upgrading Monaco. It also forces a CSP with 'unsafe-eval'.
- ESM-only jsondiffpatch loaded with require() (`main-process/ipc/handlers/rules-ipc.js`) — now: jsondiffpatch 0.7.3 ("type":"module") via require('jsondiffpatch') and require('jsondiffpatch/formatters/html'); Works only with Node >= 20.19 require(esm). Use dynamic import (as cupnet-runtime.js does) or pin.
- Deprecated DOM APIs (`rules.js copyInterceptAiPrompt (document.execCommand('copy')); request-editor-renderer.js mergeAuthIntoRequest and request-editor-curl-parser.js -u (btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent())))`) — now: execCommand / unescape; Use navigator.clipboard only, and TextEncoder plus base64 for Basic auth.
- cURL import coverage (`request-editor-curl-parser.js parseCurlCommand`) — now: Handles -X/-H/-d/--data*/-b/-u only; Modern 'Copy as cURL' output from Chrome/Firefox uses $'…' ANSI-C quoting, --data-raw, -F, --url, -I, --compressed, -G, --data-urlencode. Several of these are mis-parsed (see suspectedBugs).
- Postman import (`request-editor-curl-parser.js parsePostmanCollectionV21`) — now: schema must contain '2.1'; first request only; raw/urlencoded bodies only; No v2.0, no formdata/graphql bodies, no auth, no variables, and only one request per collection.
