- Captcha detection catalogue (`main-process/services/page-analyzer-injected-scripts.js _analyzeCaptchaScript`) — now: reCAPTCHA v2/v3/enterprise, hCaptcha, Turnstile, GeeTest v3; 'other' always empty; No GeeTest v4, Arkose/FunCaptcha, AWS WAF, DataDome or Turnstile implicit-render detection. The UI says 'in development'.
- Permissive CSP (`rules.html meta CSP`) — now: script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval' blob:; 'unsafe-inline' is only needed because of an inline onclick in createRuleRow (rules.js). Remove it and keep 'unsafe-eval' only if Monaco needs it.

**Production gaps:**

- No trusted-sender guard (guardIpcHandler) on execute-request, request-editor-build-multipart (arbitrary file read in main), apply-page-storage, page-analyzer-action, inject-turnstile-token, rules CRUD/import, or run-ivac-scout.
- Secrets at rest. Request Editor auth (bearer tokens, basic passwords, API keys) is stored in plaintext in localStorage history and in request_editor_collections.auth_json. It is not covered by the vault or traffic-log encryption hardening.
- No unit tests for the cURL parser, codegen, multipart builder, HTTP Lab or rules UI. The e2e tests depend on the live httpbin.org and 2captcha demo sites.
- English-only UI rule is violated in page-analyzer.html (captcha notice), page-analyzer-renderer.js (Storage panel buttons, statuses, errors), ivac-scout.html ('Что делать дальше' box) and ivac-scout-renderer.js (next-action strings).
- Rules window has no visible Delete button (Delete/Backspace key only). No confirmation before importing rule files that contain executable script rules. Export/Import cancel is shown as an error toast.
- Request Editor: no redirect-follow option (disableRedirects is always true), no per-tab session/cookie jar selection, no binary response handling or download, no response size limit, and no streaming.
- Collections UI: no drag-and-drop or move-between-folders; new folders are always created at root; the tree is not rendered hierarchically; no export/import of collections or environments.
- Page Analyzer: auto-refresh polling (3 s of main-world script execution) should be event-driven (did-navigate / dom-ready) and optional. Endpoint Scout fetches scripts sequentially with no global timeout, concurrency cap or progress UI.
- Unused and dead code to prune or finish: solveTurnstileWithCapMonster, the solve-turnstile-captcha / inject-turnstile-token UI, .captcha-solver-* CSS, focusRuleByName in rules.js, the HTTP Lab HISTORY key and jpegConvert, the preload searchKeymap export.
- API Scout is hard-wired to one target (ivacbd), writes to the app dir, depends on system `node` in dev, and has no cancellation feedback beyond SIGTERM.
- intercept_rule_history grows without bound (every enable toggle writes a full snapshot).

### tool-windows-b: Settings page, DNS Manager, Cookie Manager, Notes (Quill editor + custom request blocks), note domain utils

User-facing tool UIs for CupNet 2. (1) settings.html/settings-renderer.js/settings.css is the app preferences page, with tabs general, appearance, network, capture, certificate, devices, shortcuts, performance, security and about. It is opened as an internal browser TAB (file://<root>/settings.html, displayed as cupnet://settings), not as a separate window. (2) dns-manager.* is a trusted BrowserWindow for CRUD on MITM-only DNS overrides (host -> IPv4, optional Host rewrite) plus a live 'Activity' feed of rule matches. (3) cookie-manager.* is a trusted BrowserWindow that views/edits/deletes/clears/consolidates/shares/imports/exports the cookies of a chosen tab's Electron session, plus CRUD for cookie groups (partitions). (4) notes.* is a trusted BrowserWindow: a Quill 2 rich-text notes app bound to site patterns. It has tags, pin, duplicate, export (md/txt/html), optional per-note password encryption, and embeddable HTTP 'request' cards taken from the Network Activity DB. notes-quill-custom-blocks.js holds the Quill BlockEmbed registry. notes-cn-block-export.js is a CommonJS text exporter that only the tests use. note-domain-utils.js is main-process code (tldts) that derives the eTLD+1 index domain and matches url_match patterns against the page URL.

**Files:**

- `settings.html` — Settings page markup (577 lines). 10 sections #sp-tab-{general,appearance,network,capture,certificate,devices,shortcuts,performance,security,about}. Cards carry data-sp-search tags for the hub search. CSP: default-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; script-src 'self' (no inline styles allowed). Loads shared-dark-theme.css, settings.css, toast-notifications.js (global showToast), keyboard-shortcuts.js (window.CUPNET_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUTS), settings-renderer.js. The Security section is new in the uncommitted WIP.
- `settings-renderer.js` — All Settings logic. switchTab() (L129) drives perf polling and camera refresh. Debounced savers: scheduleHomepageSave 500ms, scheduleSearchEngineSave 400ms, scheduleFiltersSave 600ms, scheduleTrackingSave 250ms, scheduleDeviceSave 250ms, scheduleActivityMonitorSave 400ms, scheduleSecuritySave 400ms, scheduleMaxTabsBeforeWarningSave 550ms. A beginSave/endSave counter drives the #settings-persist-status text. Other pieces: camera list drag/drop (renderCameraRows, wireCameraCheckboxes, mergeCameraOrder, syncCameraDisabledLabelsFromIds); perf table (startPerfPoll every 3s, renderPerfRows); CA download/copy (fetchMitmCaPem); export/import/reset; initSidebarCollapse and initAppearance (ui-prefs); init() L1042 loads everything through getSettingsAll.
- `settings.css` — Settings layout and theme (dark only, px-based sizes). Classes: .settings-shell, .settings-sidebar-wrap(.is-collapsed), .sp-tab-btn, .sp-tab-body.active, .card, .sp-toggle*, .tracking-row, .device-row, .perf-table, .field-error, .settings-persist-status[data-state]. It has no rules for [data-settings-theme].
- `dns-manager.html` — DNS Manager markup and inline CSS. Tabs 'rules' and 'activity'; filter bar (#filter-host, #filter-ip, #filter-enabled-only); table #dns-tbody; edit panel (#e-host, #e-ip, #e-rewrite-host, #e-enabled); status bar. CSP allows inline styles.
- `dns-manager-renderer.js` — DNS rules UI. Client validation: isValidDnsHost, isValidIpv4, isValidDnsRewriteHost. applyFilters and renderTable. Delegated tbody click handles copy, delete, state toggle and edit. Save handler L329 blocks new wildcard rules. Activity feed: addActivityEvent keeps at most 300 entries, grouped by extractPage(url); 'Request' opens openLogViewerWithUrl; Clear sends resetToolbarActivityBadge('dns').
- `cookie-manager.html` — Cookie Manager markup. Top bar has group CRUD (Grp select, +, Cp, Ren, ✕), a Tab select, JSON/NS export, Imp, Share, Consolidate (new in WIP), Clear all and + Cookie. Filter bar has a 'Current tab' toggle, domain/name/value fields, HttpOnly/Secure/Session checkboxes, a page-URL match badge and a count. Also a sortable table, an edit panel and a share modal. Loads cookies-page-match.js (window.CupNetCookiePageMatch) before the renderer.
- `cookie-manager-renderer.js` — State: allCookies, filteredCookies, activeTabId, allTabs, editingCookie, sortCol/sortAsc. loadCookies/loadCookiesSilent run on a 3s auto-refresh that pauses while the edit panel is open. syncDomainFilter/setActiveDomain lock the domain filter to the active tab's hostname. Also: applyFilters, sortCookies, renderTable (escHtml); openEdit and a save handler that calls setCookie with a built url; deleteCookie (removeCookie with the full cookie object); consolidate, clear-all, export (Blob download of filteredCookies), import (JSON array or Netscape), share modal. IPC listeners: onCookieTabsList, onSetActiveCookieTab, onTabsUpdated, onCookieGroupsUpdated. Group CRUD uses window.prompt.
- `notes.html` — Notes window markup and large inline CSS. Sidebar: search, tag filter, #notes-list. Title row: New, title, Pin, Copy, Export menu (md/txt/html), save status, Save, Delete. Hidden #chk-encrypt with #encrypt-row (#pw1, Set password). Site pattern input #notes-match-input, Encrypt toggle, tags input. Editor stack: #editor-lock-overlay (unlock form), find bar, #notes-editor. Also word count, meta footer, and the DOM prompt #cn-req-prompt that replaces window.prompt. CSP adds img-src 'self' data:. Loads ./node_modules/quill/dist/quill.snow.css and quill.js, then notes-quill-custom-blocks.js, toast-notifications.js and notes-renderer.js.
- `notes-renderer.js` — Creates the Quill 2 editor with a custom toolbar that includes a 'cnRequest' handler → openCnRequestPrompt. Adds captions to the toolbar buttons. Implements selectNote/selectNoteSkipDirtyCheck (via notesGetForSelect with pwdCache), tryUnlock, newNote, saveNoteInternal/saveNote, the autosave path (markDirty → scheduleAutosave 1500ms → doAutosave; skipped while encrypted or locked), deleteNote, togglePin, duplicateNote, doExport. The list is rendered in two sections by loadList/renderListGrouped. Also: find bar (rebuildFindMatches), image paste/drop as data URLs, baseline/dirty tracking (captureBaseline, isDirtyNow, scheduleBaselineAfterLoad using a double rAF), and applyNoteBodyHtml (hydrateCupnetBlocksHtml → quill.root.innerHTML). Subscribes to onNotesInit, onNotesContextUpdate, onNotesEmbedBlock. Ctrl/Cmd+S saves; Ctrl/Cmd+F opens find.
- `notes-quill-custom-blocks.js` — Browser IIFE. Registers the Quill BlockEmbed 'cnBlock': a div.ql-cn-block whose data-cn-payload attribute holds JSON. Block kind registry: registerBlockKind('demo'|'request') with normalize/render/exportText/actions. The 'request' render has method/status/url, a meta row, collapsible headers/bodies and action buttons. Delegated action handlers via window.setupCnBlockDelegatedActions(root): 'request:open-request-editor' → electronAPI.openRequestEditor(id|payload); 'request:find-in-network' → openLogViewerFocusRequest(id) or openLogViewerWithUrl(url). Exposes window.insertCnBlock, window.hydrateCupnetBlocksHtml (turns ```cupnet fences into cards) and window._cnBlockRegistry.
- `notes-cn-block-export.js` — CommonJS exporters (cnBlockExportText, exporters.demo/request, formatHeaders, fmtBytes) that duplicate the renderer's exportText. Not required by any production code; only tests/unit/notes-cn-block-export.test.mjs uses it.
- `note-domain-utils.js` — Main-process helpers using tldts. getNoteDomainFromUrl returns the eTLD+1 (localhost/IP kept as-is). matchGlobPattern does glob → regex over the full URL. noteMatchesUrlMatch: a pattern with '*' or a scheme uses the glob; otherwise a host/subdomain match on the first path segment. getNoteIndexDomainFromMatch fills the DB 'domain' index column. Used by notes-ipc.js and sub-windows.js (createNotesWindow, broadcastNotesWindowContext).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/settings-toolbar-ipc.js` — Handlers the Settings page calls: get-settings-all, save-security-settings (also calls vaultSession.initFromSettings and bumpActivity), save-search-engine-settings, save-activity-monitor-settings (reattachActivityMonitorToAllTabs), export/import-settings-json, reset-settings-to-defaults (applySettingsSideEffects), set-paste-unlock, set-max-tabs-before-warning, save-toolbar-tools-placement/mini-align (broadcastBrowserChromeSettings → 'browser-settings-updated'), get/save-tracking-settings, save-filter-patterns, enumerate-media-devices (runs JS inside mainWindow), save-device-permissions, reset-onboarding-wizard, and the sync IPCs used by preload-view. None of these are wrapped by the trusted-sender guard.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/misc-ipc.js` — get-ui-pref, set-ui-pref (unguarded), get-homepage (reads settingsStore.getCached()), set-homepage (no validation), get-app-version.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/diagnostics-ipc.js` — get-app-metrics: app.getAppMetrics() plus collectCupNetChildProcessMetrics (rows flagged cupnet:true).
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/mitm-tls-resilience-ipc.js` — mitm-get-ca-cert (L37): mitmProxy.getCACert(), falling back to userData/mitm-ca/ca-cert.pem.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/cookies-dns-ipc.js` — Cookie IPCs. get-cookies and set-cookie go through _cookieSessionTab (the tab owned by the sender, or a trusted shell). remove-cookie, clear-cookies and consolidate-cookies are NOT guarded. share-cookies uses guardIpcHandler. DNS IPCs: open-dns-manager, dns-overrides-list/save/delete/toggle. Also open-cookie-manager, cookie group CRUD (get/create/copy/rename/delete-cookie-group) and per-tab set-tab-proxy/set-tab-cookie-group/isolate-tab.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/notes-ipc.js` — Notes backend. Turndown instance td with a cnBlock rule (outputs ```cupnet JSON fences); getMarked() (ESM marked via a dynamic import singleton); mdToHtml, htmlToMd, mdToPlainText; resolveNoteDomains; _decryptNoteBody (master key, then password); migrateNotesToMasterKey (CN01→CN02 on profile login); normalizeRequestRowForNotesEmbed. Handlers: notes-list, notes-get, notes-save, notes-delete, notes-pin, notes-export (save dialog + fs.writeFile), notes-get-request-for-embed.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/launcher-ipc.js` — open-notes-window (L79) and notes-embed-request (L82). The latter creates or focuses the Notes window and sends 'notes-embed-block', waiting for did-finish-load + 120ms if the window is still loading.
- `main-process/ipc/handlers/tabs-ipc.js` — open-settings-tab (L22): reuses a tab whose URL contains settings.html, otherwise creates a tab with getInternalPageUrl('settings'). get-tabs returns tabManager.getTabList().
- `main-process/services/sub-windows.js` — Window factories: createNotesWindow L340 (notes-init on first load; notes-context-update on refocus), broadcastNotesWindowContext L369, notifyCookieManagerTabs L597 (150ms debounce, 'tabs-updated'), notifyCookieGroupsListsUpdated L613, main-side isValidDnsHost/isValidIpv4 L628-649, syncDnsOverridesToMitm L651, createCookieManagerWindow L665, createDnsManagerWindow L684 (replays up to 100 recent DNS events). All tool windows use _spawnTrustedToolWindow → registerTrustedShell and preload.js.
- `main-process/services/main-window.js` — ~L225-241: tab-manager events trigger notifyCookieManagerTabs, send 'set-active-tab' to the Cookie Manager on tab-switched, and call broadcastNotesWindowContext/broadcastCredentialsWindowContext on tab-url-changed/tab-switched.
- `main-process/services/settings-store.js` — settings.json persistence: SETTINGS_DEFAULTS, materializeSettingsFromRaw, normalize* helpers, buildFactoryResetSettings, exportSettingsSafe, mergeImportedSettings (key whitelist). saveSettings updates the cache immediately and writes after a 300ms debounce (writeFileSecureAsync); the path is resolved at call time. Per-profile file: userData/profiles/{vaultId}/settings.json.
- `main-process/services/ui-prefs-store.js` — ui-prefs.json (global or per-profile). Keys used here: settingsSidebarCollapsed, uiTheme, settingsUiScale.
- `main-process/services/session-cookie-utils.js` — buildCookieRequestUrl, cookieIdentityKey/cookiesMatchIdentity, normalizeRemoveCookieArgs, removeSessionCookiePrecise (expires the cookie through set() with a past date over candidate URLs), cookieMatchesDomainFilter, consolidateCookiesToParent (computeParentDomain strips one label), purgeCookiesForHostnameAllTabs.
- `main-process/services/ipc-batch-messenger.js` — broadcastDnsRuleMatched batches events into 'dns-rule-matched-batch' for the main window and the DNS window, and keeps a recent-events ring read through getRecentDnsEventsSlice.
- `main-process/services/proxy-service.js` — L140-170: a MITM log entry with entry.dnsOverride or entry.dnsCorsMatch triggers broadcastDnsRuleMatched({ruleName, host, ip, url, method, tabId, sessionId}).
- `mitm-proxy.js` — setDnsOverrides L919 builds a Map host→{ip, rewriteHost} and CORS patterns from enabled rules. _applyDnsOverride L1570 swaps the URL hostname for the IP, sets the Host header (rewriteHost, or host[:port]) and keeps interceptMatchUrl.
- `notes-crypto.js` — CN01 (magic 'CN01', scrypt(password, salt) + AES-256-GCM) and CN02 (magic 'CN02', AES-256-GCM with the 32-byte vault master key). decryptNotePayload rejects CN02.
- `db.js` — Tables cookie_groups (L696), dns_overrides (L723, plus migrated columns mitm_inject_cors and rewrite_host), user_notes (L733, plus migrated url_match, is_pinned, tags, profile_id), note_request_links. Functions: getDnsOverrides/saveDnsOverride (upsert by host)/toggle/delete L1658-1735; cookie group CRUD L1739-1760; listUserNotes L1903, getUserNote, saveUserNote, setUserNotePinned, deleteUserNote, migrateNotesToProfile; *Async wrappers go through enqueueWrite.
- `preload.js` — Full privileged window.electronAPI for the trusted windows (main, DNS, Cookie, Notes). Includes every settings/cookie/dns/notes API listed in ipcChannels. sub(channel, cb) returns an unsubscribe function.
- `preload-view.js` — Preload for browser tabs, which includes the settings.html tab. The WIP removed all settings APIs from it. It still exposes getTabs, getCookies, clearCookies, getUiPref/setUiPref, setTabProxy, setTabCookieGroup, connectProxyTemplate and others to every web page.
- `main-process/ipc/ipc-trusted-sender.js` — registerTrustedShell / registerTabView / isTrustedShell / assertTrustedShell / guardIpcHandler. Tool windows are trusted; tab views never are.
- `cookies-page-match.js` — window.CupNetCookiePageMatch.countCookiesForPageUrl(cookies, url): a simplified RFC6265 check of domain/path/secure/expiry, used for the green 'N for this URL' badge.
- `keyboard-shortcuts.js` — CUPNET_KEYBOARD_SHORTCUTS: the static rows rendered in the Settings 'Shortcuts' tab.

**Key flows:**

- SETTINGS OPEN: Ctrl/Cmd+, or menu → browser-renderer api.openSettingsTab() → invoke 'open-settings-tab' (tabs-ipc.js L22). It finds an existing tab whose URL includes settings.html or creates one with ctx.getInternalPageUrl('settings') (cupnet-runtime.js L484). The tab WebContentsView uses preload-view.js (tab-manager.js buildTabViewWebPreferences L150); with CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL>=6 there is no preload at all. settings-renderer init() (L1042): initSidebarCollapse (getUiPref 'settingsSidebarCollapsed') → initAppearance (getUiPref 'uiTheme', 'settingsUiScale') → initShortcutsTable → getAppVersion → getSettingsAll → fill fields → getHomepage → switchTab('general') → setPersistState('saved'). Any exception in init sets 'Failed to load settings'.
- SETTINGS SAVE: a field event calls schedule*Save(): markUnsaved → setTimeout → beginSave → api.<saver>() → endSave(ok). Main side (settings-toolbar-ipc.js) does s = ctx.loadSettings(), mutates s through settingsStore.normalize*, then ctx.saveSettings(s) (cache update + 300ms debounced writeFileSecureAsync to settings.json). Side effects: set-paste-unlock → tabManager.setPasteUnlock → 'paste-unlock-update' to tabs. save-device-permissions → tabManager.applyDevicePermissions → 'camera-filter-update'. save-toolbar-tools-* → 'browser-settings-updated' to mainWindow. save-activity-monitor-settings → reattachActivityMonitorToAllTabs (setupNetworkLogging + injectStorageActivityMonitor). save-security-settings → vaultSession.initFromSettings + bumpActivity. Keyboard Ctrl/Cmd+S → saveFiltersNow + saveHomepageNow.
- SETTINGS PERFORMANCE/DEVICES/CA: switchTab('performance') → startPerfPoll → get-app-metrics every 3s (stops when the tab or document is hidden) → renderPerfRows. switchTab('devices') → refreshCameraDevices → enumerate-media-devices (main: mainWindow.webContents.executeJavaScript(enumerateDevices videoinput)) → mergeCameraOrder with cameraPriority → render; drag/drop or checkbox → scheduleDeviceSave → save-device-permissions. Certificate: fetchMitmCaPem → mitm-get-ca-cert → Blob download 'cupnet-mitm-ca.pem' or navigator.clipboard.writeText.
- SETTINGS EXPORT/IMPORT/RESET: export-settings-json → settingsStore.exportSettingsSafe (drops currentProxy/effectiveTrafficMode, adds _exportMeta) → Blob download cupnet-settings-YYYY-MM-DD.json. Import: hidden file input → confirm → import-settings-json → mergeImportedSettings (whitelist: filterPatterns, homepage, maxTabsBeforeWarning, pasteUnlock, currentProxy, effectiveTrafficMode, onboardingComplete, activityMonitor*, lastLogPath, searchEngine*, corsBypassEnabled, plus tracking/trafficOpts/devicePermissions) → saveSettings → applySettingsSideEffects → renderer location.reload(). Reset: reset-settings-to-defaults → buildFactoryResetSettings → applySettingsSideEffects → reload. Onboarding reset: reset-onboarding-wizard → onboardingComplete=false + createOnboardingWindow.
- DNS MANAGER: api.openDnsManager → 'open-dns-manager' → sub-windows.createDnsManagerWindow (trusted, preload.js). On did-finish-load it sends 'dns-overrides-updated' (db.getDnsOverrides()) and 'dns-rule-matched-batch' (ipcBatch.getRecentDnsEventsSlice(100)). Renderer loadRules → 'dns-overrides-list' → applyFilters/renderTable.
- DNS SAVE: #btn-save-rule → client validation (isValidDnsHost; a wildcard is only allowed when editing the same legacy rule, and then needs no IP or rewrite; rewrite needs an IPv4; isValidIpv4 unless it is a legacy CORS-only rule) → 'dns-overrides-save' {id, host, ip, enabled, rewrite_host} → cookies-dns-ipc: ctx.isValidDnsHost/isValidIpv4; keeps the existing mitm_inject_cors → db.saveDnsOverrideAsync (UPDATE by id, or upsert by host) → ctx.syncDnsOverridesToMitm (mitmProxy.setDnsOverrides(enabled rules) + 'dns-overrides-updated' to the DNS window). If rewrite_host is set and differs from the host → purgeCookiesForHostnameAllTabs(host). Toggle: 'dns-overrides-toggle'. Delete: 'dns-overrides-delete' (after confirm).
- DNS RUNTIME/ACTIVITY: the MITM request path calls mitm-proxy._applyDnsOverride (hostname → IP, Host header = rewriteHost or host[:port], interceptMatchUrl keeps the original URL). The log entry carries dnsOverride → proxy-service L146 → ipcBatch.broadcastDnsRuleMatched → batched 'dns-rule-matched-batch' to the main window (toolbar badge) and the DNS window → addActivityEvent (keeps 300) → renderActivity when the Activity tab is active. 'Request' button → 'open-log-viewer-with-url'. Clear → local reset + send 'reset-toolbar-activity-badge' ('dns').
- COOKIE MANAGER OPEN/TRACK: api.openCookieManager(tabId) → 'open-cookie-manager' → createCookieManagerWindow(initialTabId). On did-finish-load it sends 'set-active-tab' first, then 'tabs-list' (getTabList). The renderer also calls getTabs(). When the main window switches tabs, main-window.js sends 'set-active-tab' plus the debounced 'tabs-updated'. The renderer follows the active tab, recomputes currentTabDomain/currentTabUrl and re-syncs the locked domain filter.
- COOKIE READ/WRITE: loadCookies → 'get-cookies'(tabId, {}) → _cookieSessionTab (sender-owned tab, or assertTrustedShell) → tab.tabSession.cookies.get({}). Auto-refresh every 3s (loadCookiesSilent + cookiesChanged). Save: build details {name, value, domain, path, httpOnly, secure, url=(secure?https:http)://domain-without-dot+path, sameSite?, expirationDate?} → 'set-cookie' → cookies.set + flushStore. Delete: 'remove-cookie'(tabId, cookieObj) → normalizeRemoveCookieArgs → removeSessionCookiePrecise → verify the identity is gone. Clear all: 'clear-cookies'(tabId, domainFilter|null) → clearStorageData({storages:['cookies']}), or precise removal of cookies matching cookieMatchesDomainFilter; returns {success, count, remaining}.
- COOKIE CONSOLIDATE/SHARE/IMPORT/EXPORT: Consolidate → 'consolidate-cookies'(tabId, anchor = filter or tab host) → consolidateCookiesToParent: parent = computeParentDomain(anchor); cookies are grouped by name/path/secure/httpOnly/sameSite; every group member is removed, then the canonical cookie is set on .parent; a final pass removes leftover subdomain cookies. Share → modal → 'share-cookies'(from, to, domain) (guardIpcHandler) → cookies.get({domain}) → set each on the target session. Export: JSON or Netscape of filteredCookies via Blob + a.download. Import: .json array or Netscape text → one 'set-cookie' per cookie (url derived from secure/domain/path).
- COOKIE GROUPS: loadCookieGroups → 'get-cookie-groups' (db.getCookieGroups). + / Cp / Ren / ✕ use window.prompt/confirm → 'create-cookie-group' | 'copy-cookie-group' (copies all cookies from partitionForGroup(from) into the new partition) | 'rename-cookie-group' | 'delete-cookie-group' (refused while a tab uses the group; clearStorageData on the partition) → notifyCookieGroupsListsUpdated → 'cookie-groups-updated' to the main window, the Cookie Manager and all tabs.
- NOTES OPEN/CONTEXT: api.openNotesWindow → 'open-notes-window' → createNotesWindow. It computes pageUrl (active tab, http(s) only) and domain = getNoteDomainFromUrl(pageUrl) (eTLD+1). New window: did-finish-load → 'notes-init' {pageUrl, domain} → renderer newNote() + loadList(). Existing window: focus + 'notes-context-update'. Later tab-url-changed/tab-switched → broadcastNotesWindowContext → 'notes-context-update' → renderer updates ctxPageUrl/ctxDomain; if a draft is open it overwrites #notes-match-input with ctxDomain and recaptures the baseline; then loadList().
- NOTES LIST: loadList → 'notes-list' {limit:500, search, tag} (all sites) plus, if ctxDomain is set, 'notes-list' {…, domain: ctxDomain, refineByUrlMatch: true, pageUrl}. Main: _profileNoteFilter adds profileId = profileSession.getActiveVaultId() → db.listUserNotes (profile_id, domain =, LIKE on title/body_plain, tag instr on space-stripped tags, ORDER BY is_pinned DESC, updated_at DESC) → optional filter with noteMatchesUrlMatch(url_match, pageUrl). The renderer builds the 'This site' and 'All sites' sections with DOM APIs.
- NOTES LOAD/UNLOCK: selectNote(id) (asks to confirm if dirty) → notesGetForSelect → 'notes-get'(id, pwdCache.get(id)). Plain note: body_plain (markdown) → mdToHtml (marked, gfm+breaks) → bodyHtml. Encrypted without a password: _decryptNoteBody(row, '') tries decryptNotePayloadWithKey(buf, vault master key); on failure it returns {locked:true}. Encrypted with a password: decryptNotePayload (CN01 only), else throws 'Wrong password'. Renderer: a locked note shows #editor-lock-overlay, disables Quill and makes the fields read-only; tryUnlock → 'notes-get'(id, pw) → pwdCache.set. applyNoteBodyHtml → window.hydrateCupnetBlocksHtml (replaces <pre><code class=language-cupnet>JSON</code></pre> with rendered ql-cn-block divs) → quill.root.innerHTML → scheduleBaselineAfterLoad (double rAF + setTimeout).
- NOTES SAVE: any change → markDirty → (if not encrypted and not locked) scheduleAutosave 1500ms → doAutosave → saveNoteInternal → 'notes-save' {id, title, bodyHtml: quill.root.innerHTML, page_url: ctxPageUrl, url_match, tags, is_pinned, is_encrypted, password?}. Main: resolveNoteDomains (url_match → getNoteIndexDomainFromMatch, else page_url eTLD+1, else '(no site)'); bodyMd = htmlToMd (Turndown + cnBlock rule → ```cupnet {payload JSON}```). Encrypted: encryptNotePayload(JSON {body: md}, password) (always CN01) → db.saveUserNoteAsync {body_encrypted}. Plain: body_plain = md. profile_id = active vault id or 0. Renderer then loadList(), and for plain notes calls notes-get to refresh meta; encrypted notes use pwdCache and selectNoteSkipDirtyCheck. Encrypted notes save only through Save / Ctrl+S / 'Set password'.
- NOTES PIN/DUP/DELETE/EXPORT: 'notes-pin'(id, bool) → db.setUserNotePinnedAsync. Duplicate: notes-get → notes-save with id null and title '(copy)' (needs the cached password when encrypted). Delete: confirm → 'notes-delete' → newNote(true). Export: 'notes-export' {id, format md|txt|html, password} → main decrypts (CN01) → md = raw markdown; txt = mdToPlainText; html = marked output → dialog.showSaveDialog (default Documents/<title>.<ext>) → fs.writeFile.
- NOTES REQUEST EMBED: (a) toolbar '+ Request' → openCnRequestPrompt (DOM overlay) → 'notes-get-request-for-embed'(id) → db.getRequest → normalizeRequestRowForNotesEmbed → window.insertCnBlock(quill, 'request', data) → Quill insertEmbed 'cnBlock' → CnBlockBlot.create → def.normalize + def.render. (b) Log viewer 'Embed in note' (log-viewer-renderer.js ~L2763) → 'notes-embed-request'(blockData) → launcher-ipc creates/focuses the Notes window → 'notes-embed-block' → onNotesEmbedBlock → insertCnBlock (refused while locked). Card buttons use capture-phase delegation on quill.root (setupCnBlockDelegatedActions) keyed by data-cn-action.
- NOTES KEY MIGRATION: profile-ipc.finishProfileLogin → migrateNotesToMasterKey(db, profilePassword). For every CN01 encrypted note of the active profile it tries decryptNotePayload(buf, profilePassword); on success it re-encrypts with encryptNotePayloadWithKey(master key) as CN02 via db.saveUserNote. Notes whose password differs from the profile password stay CN01.

**IPC channels:**

- renderer→main invoke (Settings page, via electronAPI; the settings tab's preload-view no longer exposes most of these): get-settings-all, save-filter-patterns, set-paste-unlock, set-max-tabs-before-warning, get-homepage, set-homepage, save-search-engine-settings, reset-onboarding-wizard, save-activity-monitor-settings, save-security-settings, save-tracking-settings, get-app-metrics, enumerate-media-devices, save-device-permissions, mitm-get-ca-cert, get-ui-pref, set-ui-pref, save-toolbar-tools-placement, save-toolbar-tools-mini-align, export-settings-json, import-settings-json, reset-settings-to-defaults, get-app-version
- main→renderer side effects of settings saves: browser-settings-updated (→ mainWindow), paste-unlock-update and camera-filter-update (→ tab webContents), cors-bypass-status (→ mainWindow, on import/reset)
- renderer→main invoke to open the tool UIs (main window / new-tab / log viewer): open-settings-tab, open-dns-manager, open-cookie-manager(tabId), open-notes-window, notes-embed-request(blockData)
- DNS Manager renderer→main invoke: dns-overrides-list, dns-overrides-save({id, host, ip, enabled, rewrite_host}), dns-overrides-delete(id), dns-overrides-toggle(id, enabled), open-log-viewer-with-url(url). ipcRenderer.send: reset-toolbar-activity-badge('dns')
- DNS main→renderer: dns-overrides-updated(rules[]) (from createDnsManagerWindow did-finish-load and syncDnsOverridesToMitm), dns-rule-matched-batch(events[]) (ipc-batch-messenger + replay on open). dns-rule-matched (single) is subscribed but never emitted.
- Cookie Manager renderer→main invoke: get-tabs, get-cookies(tabId, filter), set-cookie(tabId, details), remove-cookie(tabId, cookieObj|url, name?), clear-cookies(tabId, domain|null), consolidate-cookies(tabId, anchorHost), share-cookies(fromTabId, toTabId, domain|null) [guardIpcHandler], get-cookie-groups, create-cookie-group(name), copy-cookie-group(fromId, name), rename-cookie-group(id, name), delete-cookie-group(id)
- Cookie Manager main→renderer: tabs-list(tabList) on load, set-active-tab(tabId) (on load and on main-window tab-switched), tabs-updated(tabList) (notifyCookieManagerTabs, 150ms debounce), cookie-groups-updated (notifyCookieGroupsListsUpdated → main window, cookie manager, all tabs)
- Notes renderer→main invoke: notes-list(filter), notes-get(id, password|null), notes-save(payload) → id, notes-delete(id), notes-pin(id, bool), notes-export({id, format, password}) → {canceled, filePath}, notes-get-request-for-embed(requestId). From card actions: open-request-editor(id|payload), open-log-viewer-focus-request(id), open-log-viewer-with-url(url)
- Notes main→renderer: notes-init({pageUrl, domain}) (first did-finish-load only), notes-context-update({pageUrl, domain}) (refocus, tab-url-changed, tab-switched via broadcastNotesWindowContext), notes-embed-block(blockData) (from notes-embed-request)
- Sync IPC used by preload-view in every tab, including the settings tab: get-device-permissions-sync, get-paste-unlock-sync, get-anti-anti-debug-sync (settings-toolbar-ipc.js L319-363)

**Data stores:**

- settings.json at userData/settings.json, or userData/profiles/{vaultId}/settings.json when a profile is active. Keys used here: filterPatterns, pasteUnlock, maxTabsBeforeWarning, homepage, searchEngine, searchEngineCustomUrl, tracking.{onUserClick, onPageLoadComplete, onNetworkPendingChange, onMouseActivity, onTypingEnd, onScrollEnd, onRuleMatchScreenshot, pendingDeltaThreshold, cooldownMs, maxPerMinute}, devicePermissions.{cameraMode, cameraPriority, cameraDisabledIds, cameraDisabledLabels, microphoneMode, microphonePriority}, activityMonitorEnabled, activityMonitorRateLimit, activityMonitorStorageStackTraces, toolbarToolsPlacement, toolbarToolsMiniAlign, onboardingComplete, vaultAutoLockMinutes, trafficLogEncryption, trafficLogRawMode, allowPlaintextExport, corsBypassEnabled, effectiveTrafficMode, trafficOpts, lastLogPath, _version.
- ui-prefs.json at userData/ui-prefs.json or userData/profiles/{vaultId}/ui-prefs.json. Keys: settingsSidebarCollapsed, uiTheme, settingsUiScale (the Settings page only).
- SQLite (db.js / better-sqlite3), table user_notes: id, domain (eTLD+1 index), url_match, page_url, title, body_plain (markdown), body_encrypted (BLOB, CN01|CN02), is_encrypted, is_pinned, tags (comma string), profile_id, created_at, updated_at. Indexes on domain, created_at and profile_id.
- SQLite table note_request_links (note_id, request_id): defined, but the notes flow never uses it.
- SQLite table dns_overrides: id, host (UNIQUE), ip, enabled, mitm_inject_cors, rewrite_host, created_at, updated_at.
- SQLite table cookie_groups: id, name (UNIQUE), created_at. id=1 is 'Default' and cannot be renamed or deleted.
- SQLite table requests: read by notes-get-request-for-embed through db.getRequest(id) (url, method, status, request/response headers and bodies, duration_ms, session_id, created_at).
- Electron session partitions: per cookie group via tabManager.partitionForGroup(gid), plus per-tab isolated sessions (tab.tabSession). The Cookie Manager reads and writes these jars.
- userData/mitm-ca/ca-cert.pem: fallback source for mitm-get-ca-cert.
- In-memory stores: renderer pwdCache Map(noteId→password); ipcBatch _recentDnsEvents ring (DNS activity replay); mitmProxy._dnsOverrides Map and _dnsCorsPatterns.
- Env var CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL: >=1 disables paste-unlock injection; >=6 means tabs, including the settings tab, get no preload.

**Conventions:**

- Every tool window script does `const api = window.electronAPI`. Trusted tool windows (DNS, Cookie, Notes) must be created with sub-windows._spawnTrustedToolWindow (→ registerTrustedShell) and preload.js; guarded channels (share-cookies, get/set-cookie on non-owned tabs, vault/profile IPC) throw IPC_UNTRUSTED_SENDER otherwise.
- New privileged renderer APIs go into preload.js only. preload-view.js (tabs, including settings.html) must stay minimal because every website gets it (see SECURITY.md 'Preload split').
- All user-facing UI text must be English (project rule), including status messages, confirms and date locales.
- Do not use window.prompt: Electron 34 overrides it to throw 'prompt() is not supported.'. Use DOM modals like notes.html #cn-req-prompt. window.confirm works.
- HTML-string rendering must escape with the local helpers (escapeHtml in settings, escHtml in dns/cookie, esc in custom blocks). Prefer createElement/textContent (the notes list already does). Row actions use delegated listeners on tbody (cookie/dns), and cnBlock actions use data-cn-action delegation because quill.root.innerHTML wipes per-node listeners.
- Cookie/DNS/group IPC handlers return {success, error, ...} objects. Notes and settings handlers return raw values or throw; notes-renderer strips the 'Error invoking remote method' prefix with formatNotesIpcError.
- Adding a setting takes these steps: SETTINGS_DEFAULTS + materializeSettingsFromRaw/normalize* in settings-store.js; a field in get-settings-all; a save handler (load → mutate → ctx.saveSettings) in settings-toolbar-ipc.js; the preload.js API; UI plus a schedule*Save using beginSave/endSave; and mergeImportedSettings if it should be importable.
- Settings autosave UX: debounce, then markUnsaved/beginSave/endSave update #settings-persist-status (states saved|saving|unsaved|error). Field validation errors use showFieldError/clearFieldError with the <p class=field-error> sibling.
- The Notes body is persisted as Markdown (Turndown in main), not as HTML or a Quill Delta. Custom blocks round-trip as ```cupnet JSON fences. A new block kind needs registerBlockKind + registerCnBlockActions in notes-quill-custom-blocks.js, and ideally an exporter in notes-cn-block-export.js.
- Delete cookies with removeSessionCookiePrecise (session-cookie-utils), never cookies.remove(url, name), which can drop several same-name entries.
- DNS overrides and CORS injection act only on the MITM path (mitm-proxy.js). Do not add session.protocol.handle interception (project rule 1). The global CORS bypass is the toolbar setting corsBypassEnabled; per-rule mitm_inject_cors is legacy.
- DB writes from IPC use the *Async wrappers (enqueueWrite 'high' priority queue); reads are synchronous better-sqlite3 calls.
- Settings and ui-prefs are profile-scoped (userData/profiles/{vaultId}/settings.json and ui-prefs.json) once a profile is unlocked; notes are profile-scoped via the user_notes.profile_id column.

**Gotchas:**

- settings.html is NOT a BrowserWindow. It is a browser tab (cupnet://settings → file://…/settings.html) running preload-view.js, and in the current WIP that preload no longer exposes getSettingsAll or any saver, so the Settings page cannot load or save anything. Tests don't catch this because e2e calls those IPCs through mainWindow's preload.js.
- settings.html CSP is style-src 'self' without 'unsafe-inline', so inline style="" attributes (50 in settings.html plus those in innerHTML strings in renderPerfRows) are ignored. Only CSSOM assignments (el.style.x = …) take effect. dns-manager.html and cookie-manager.html allow 'unsafe-inline' styles; notes.html allows img-src data: (pasted images) and blocks remote images.
- Script order in notes.html matters: quill.js must load before notes-quill-custom-blocks.js (its IIFE returns early without window.Quill), and setupCnBlockDelegatedActions(quill.root) must run after the Quill constructor.
- Notes text-format pipeline: renderer HTML → main Turndown → DB markdown → main marked → renderer innerHTML. Anything Turndown can't express is lost, and marked passes raw HTML through without sanitizing.
- Notes encryption has two formats. CN01 uses a per-note password (scrypt); CN02 uses the vault master key. New encrypted saves are always CN01. CN02 exists only through migrateNotesToMasterKey at profile login. decryptNotePayload throws on CN02. pwdCache (renderer Map id→password) lives only in memory. Autosave is disabled for encrypted notes.
- Notes 'domain' column is an eTLD+1 index derived from url_match (getNoteIndexDomainFromMatch), not the raw host. 'This site' = listUserNotes({domain: ctxDomain}) AND noteMatchesUrlMatch(url_match, pageUrl). Notes window context comes only from http(s) active tabs.
- notes-init runs newNote() + loadList() and would wipe the open note. That is why createNotesWindow sends notes-init only on first did-finish-load and notes-context-update on refocus.
- Cookie Manager receives 'set-active-tab' BEFORE 'tabs-list' on load, and also calls getTabs(). Handlers must tolerate an empty allTabs. Tab ids are strings 'tab_<ts>_<n>'.
- get-cookies from a tab renderer is allowed when the tab owns the tabId, and it returns cookies.get({}) for the whole session partition (all domains, HttpOnly included).
- enumerate-media-devices runs inside the main window (file:// origin). Because deviceIds are origin-specific, disabled cameras are also matched by label (cameraDisabledLabels).
- DNS window replays the last 100 match events from ipcBatch on every open; the activity 'Clear' button is local-only.
- dns_overrides.host is UNIQUE and saveDnsOverride without an id upserts by host, so a 'New rule' for an existing host overwrites it. Editing the host to an existing one raises a unique-constraint error.
- settings-store.saveSettings is a 300ms debounced write with the path resolved at call time. A second saveSettings within 300ms cancels the first timer, so only the latest object is written.
- CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL env: >=1 disables paste-unlock injection; >=6 removes preload-view from tabs entirely, so the settings tab gets no window.electronAPI at all and settings-renderer throws on its first api access.
- cookie-manager auto-refresh (3s) is skipped while editingCookie is set. Closing the edit panel resumes it.

**Tests:**

- Unit (vitest): `npm run test:unit` (run from repo root /Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2). Relevant files: tests/unit/note-domain-utils.test.mjs (getNoteDomainFromUrl, matchGlobPattern, noteMatchesUrlMatch, getNoteIndexDomainFromMatch; has no case for '*.example.com' or for '?'), tests/unit/notes-cn-block-export.test.mjs, tests/unit/session-cookie-utils.test.mjs, tests/unit/session-cookie-consolidate.test.mjs, tests/unit/cors-settings.test.mjs, tests/unit/keyboard-shortcuts.test.mjs, tests/unit/ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs, tests/unit/vault-auto-lock.test.mjs.
- E2E (Playwright + Electron): `npm run test:e2e`, or a single spec with `node scripts/playwright-e2e.mjs tests/e2e/settings.e2e.spec.js` (likewise notes.e2e.spec.js, cookie-manager.e2e.spec.js, dns-overrides.e2e.spec.js, p2-features.e2e.spec.js 'openSettingsTab'). These specs call the IPC through mainWindow.evaluate(window.electronAPI.*), which is preload.js, so they do NOT exercise settings.html in its tab, the tool-window renderers, Quill round-trips or the prompt-based group buttons.
- Node scripts: tests/test-dns-mitm.js (MITM DNS override behaviour) and tests/test-db.js (DB layer); `npm test` runs tests/run-all.sh.
- Gaps: no tests for settings-renderer.js, dns-manager-renderer.js, cookie-manager-renderer.js, notes-renderer.js, notes-quill-custom-blocks.js, the notes-ipc Turndown/marked round-trip, notes-crypto CN01/CN02 interplay with notes-get/notes-export, or settings-store merge/reset.

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Electron runtime (`package.json devDependencies 'electron'`) — now: ^34.0.0 (installed 34.5.8); Electron 34 is out of support. Upgrade to a current supported major; re-verify WebContentsView, contextBridge and preload behaviour, window.prompt (still unsupported), and CSP handling in tool windows.
- Quill 2 used with Quill 1 idioms (`notes-renderer.js applyNoteBodyHtml / captureBaseline / saveNoteInternal; notes.html CSS '.ql-editor pre.ql-syntax'`) — now: quill ^2.0.3 with quill.root.innerHTML read/write; Quill 2 renders bullets and checklists as <ol><li data-list=…> and code blocks as div.ql-code-block. Use quill.clipboard.convert or dangerouslyPasteHTML plus getSemanticHTML(), or store the Delta. The pre.ql-syntax styling targets Quill 1 markup.
- Turndown without the GFM plugin and without Quill-2-aware rules (`main-process/ipc/handlers/notes-ipc.js td = new TurndownService(...)`) — now: turndown ^7.2.2; Strike, task lists, underline, colors and Quill 2 code blocks are lost. Add turndown-plugin-gfm and custom rules, or move away from markdown storage.
- marked HTML passthrough without a sanitizer (`notes-ipc.js mdToHtml / notes-export html`) — now: marked ^17.0.5 (ESM, dynamic import); Add DOMPurify (renderer) or sanitize-html (main) on the mdToHtml output.
- window.prompt usage (unsupported in Electron) (`cookie-manager-renderer.js L69, L85, L102 (also credentials-renderer.js L512/625/1216/1245/1266/1356)`) — now: prompt('New cookie group name:') etc.; Electron overrides window.prompt to throw 'prompt() is not supported.' Replace with DOM modals.
- Hard-coded Russian locale in UI date formatting (`cookie-manager-renderer.js formatExpires L353`) — now: toLocaleDateString('ru-RU') / toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU'); Breaks the English-UI rule; use 'en-GB'/'en-US' or ISO format.
- Inline styles under a strict CSP (`settings.html (50 style="" attributes), settings-renderer.js renderPerfRows`) — now: CSP style-src 'self'; The inline styles are silently dropped; move them into settings.css classes.
- Deprecated beforeunload returnValue pattern (`notes-renderer.js L1213-1215`) — now: e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ''; Still needed in Electron but deprecated on the web; with no dialog handler the window silently refuses to close. Consider a will-prevent-unload handler in main to show a proper dialog.
- Duplicate block exporter kept only for tests (`notes-cn-block-export.js vs notes-quill-custom-blocks.js exportText`) — now: Two copies of the fmtBytes, formatHeaders and request exporters; Drift risk; production export uses neither.

**Production gaps:**

- Settings must move to a trusted BrowserWindow (preload.js/preload-shell.js, created with _spawnTrustedToolWindow), or open-settings-tab must host it somewhere privileged. Right now the page is non-functional, and its IPC channels (get-settings-all, save-security-settings, import/export/reset, set-homepage, set-ui-pref…) have no guardIpcHandler protection.
- No renderer-level tests (unit or UI e2e) for any of the four tool UIs; e2e only calls the IPC from the main window.
- Theme (uiTheme) and UI scale controls are placebo: no light theme CSS, nothing reads uiTheme, and settings.css is px-based. 'CupNet windows that read this preference' don't exist.
- Settings hub search only filters cards inside the active tab; there is no cross-tab result navigation.
- Security-reducing settings (trafficLogRawMode, allowPlaintextExport, trafficLogEncryption=off, vault auto-lock Never) have no confirmation or re-authentication and no audit log.
- Settings import/export asymmetry: export includes toolbar and security keys that import ignores. Import/reset don't re-init vaultSession, don't apply effectiveTrafficMode, and don't validate filterPatterns type.
- Notes: storage is lossy (markdown) and unsanitized; the list is capped at 500 with no pagination; search is LIKE (no FTS); no import, backup or versioning; pasted images are stored as base64 inside the markdown with no size limit; Find breaks with embeds.
- Notes at rest: plain notes are stored in plaintext even when the vault is unlocked. Embedded request cards copy full request/response headers (Cookie/Authorization) and bodies into notes, bypassing trafficLogEncryption/redaction. New encrypted notes don't use the vault master key.
- Notes lack profile_id enforcement on get/save(update)/delete/pin/export by id; note_request_links is defined but unused.
- Cookie Manager: edits don't preserve host-only scope or replace the old entry; no validation for expires, SameSite=None+Secure or __Host-/__Secure- prefixes; Netscape import/export loses HttpOnly and host-only; no bulk selection; the group selector isn't tied to the tab's cookie group; group CRUD is broken (prompt).
- Cookie consolidation isn't public-suffix aware (no tldts) and removes before it writes, so partial failures can lose cookies.
- DNS Manager: IPv4 only (no IPv6 or multiple IPs), no import/export, wildcard creation disabled, activity not persisted, the cookie purge on rewrite save is undisclosed, and HTTPS SNI behaviour for IP-rewritten URLs needs verification.
- Accessibility and i18n: emoji/icon-only buttons (📋 ✕ ⧉ ✎), mixed locales (en-GB, en-US, ru-RU), and Russian code comments in shipped files. Fine for comments, but UI strings need review.
- No auto-update channel (About says to check the distribution channel), and packaging 'files: ["**/*"]' ships tests, docs and the untracked data/ directory. Neighbouring concern: review before release.

### vault-security (profiles = credentials vaults, profile gate, vault session key, field encryption CV01/CV02, notes encryption CN01/CN02, TOTP, password policy, secure-fs, paste-unlock, trusted-sender IPC guard)

Local "workspace profile" + password-vault layer of CupNet 2. Each row of SQLite table credentials_vault is BOTH a credentials vault AND a user profile (1:1, profile id == vault id). At startup a frameless profile gate (profile-gate.html) asks the user to pick a profile and enter its password (or run one-time setup). The password is verified against the vault's verify_blob (Argon2id CV02 or legacy scrypt CV01); a 32-byte session key is derived and held only in main-process memory (vault-session-service), the password string is dropped. That key encrypts credential fields (AES-256-GCM "CV02"), migrated notes ("CN02") and traffic-log bodies/headers (traffic-log-crypto.js). Profile login also switches settings/ui-prefs to userData/profiles/{vaultId}/ and scopes notes by user_notes.profile_id. The Credentials tool window (credentials.html + credentials-renderer.js) is a Bitwarden-like manager (logins/cards/identities/secure notes, folders, URIs, custom fields, TOTP, import from other managers, health report, encrypted export, change master password). Browser integration: form-capture save bar, toolbar autofill. Privileged IPC is restricted to trusted shell windows via ipc-trusted-sender.js. IMPORTANT: almost the whole subsystem is UNCOMMITTED WIP (many files are untracked '??'); HEAD (48e1d46) still has the old design (CV01 only, master password string kept in a Map in credentials-ipc.js). Compare with `git show HEAD:<file>`.

**Files:**

- `/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/credentials-crypto.js` — Crypto primitives. CV01 (legacy, HEAD format): 'CV01'|0x01|salt16|iv12|tag16|ct, key=scryptSync(pw,salt,32) with Node defaults (N=16384) per field. CV02 field: 'CV02'|0x02|iv12|tag16|ct with 32-byte session key. CV02 verify blob: 'CV02'|0x02|salt16|iv12|tag16|ct('CUPNET_VAULT_OK'), key=Argon2id(m=65536 KiB,t=3,p=1,32B) via optional native 'argon2' (falls back to scrypt silently if require fails). Exports encrypt/decryptCredentialField(CV01), encrypt/decryptCredentialFieldWithKey(CV02, CV01 needs password fallback), createVaultVerifyBlob(+Sync, unused), verifyVaultPassword(+Sync, unused), deriveMasterKey(+Sync), deriveSessionKeyFromVerifyBlob (session key = Argon2id(pw, blob[5..21]) i.e. the SAME key that encrypts the marker), encryptFieldForStorage/decryptFieldFromStorage, isCv02Blob, MAGIC_CV01/CV02.
- `/Users/eliseyvrublevskiy/Work/PBH/RRR/node/cupnet2/notes-crypto.js` — Notes payload crypto. CN01: 'CN01'|0x01|salt16|iv12|tag16|ct with scrypt(per-note password). CN02: 'CN02'|0x01|iv12|tag16|ct with vault session key. encryptNotePayload/decryptNotePayload (CN01 only; CN02 throws 'CN02 note requires master key'), encryptNotePayloadWithKey/decryptNotePayloadWithKey(buf,key,passwordFallback).

Loaded by credentials.html <script>. Does not parse otpauth:// URIs.
