**Key flows:**

- STARTUP: cupnet-runtime app ready -> db.init() -> runProfileDataMigration({db,sysLog}) + ensureProfileSetupFlag -> loadSettings() (GLOBAL userData/settings.json, no profile yet) -> vaultSession.initFromSettings(settings) (autoLockMinutes, powerMonitor lock-screen/suspend -> lockAll) + vaultSession.onLock(cb sends 'credentials-locked' and 'profile-session-changed' {active:false} to mainWindow) -> registerAllMainIpc(ipcScope) with ipcScope.onProfileLoggedIn=openMainAfterLogin, onProfileSwitchGate=openProfileSwitchGate -> if profileSession.shouldSkipProfileGate() (CUPNET_E2E=1 | CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE=1) createMainWindow() else profileGateApi.createProfileGateWindow().
- GATE STATUS: profile-gate-renderer initGate() -> invoke 'profile-gate-status' -> needsProfileSetup(db) decides mode 'setup'|'signin'; profiles = db.listVaults().map(mapProfileRow) (+credentialCount, avatarColor, needsSetup via profileRowNeedsSetup); legacyVaultId = migration.primaryVaultId or the only vault; renderPicker(); maybeAutoOpenSetup() opens edit screen in 'setup' mode for the legacy/Default profile or when zero profiles.
- SIGN IN: doSignIn -> 'profile-login' {vaultId,password} -> reject if needsProfileSetup -> db.getCredentialsVaultMeta -> verifyVaultPassword(meta.verify_blob,pw) (direct, NOT rate-limited) -> vaultSession.lockAll('profile-switch') + clearCredentialsVaultSession() -> finishProfileLogin: vaultSession.unlockVault({vaultId,name,password,verifyBlob}) [_checkRateLimit -> verifyVaultPassword -> deriveSessionKeyFromVerifyBlob (verifies again + Argon2id(pw, blob[5..21])) -> zero old key -> _vaultKeys.set -> _activeVaultId -> profileSession.activateProfile -> _scheduleIdleLock -> if blob not CV02: upgradedVerifyBlob=createVaultVerifyBlob(pw) (NEW random salt)] -> db.updateCredentialsVaultVerifyBlob(upgraded) -> db.updateVaultLastLogin -> applyProfileScopedStores(vaultId) (settingsStore+uiPrefsStore setActiveProfileVaultId, reloadSettings, reloadUiPrefs) -> migratePlaintextCredentialFields(db,vaultId) + migrateNotesToMasterKey(db,password) (errors swallowed) -> broadcastProfileSessionChanged -> ctx.onProfileLoggedIn -> profileGateApi.openMainAfterLogin() (close gate, createMainWindow or show existing, syncAppContextSnapshot).
- FIRST-TIME / LEGACY SETUP: doEditSave(mode 'setup') -> 'profile-complete-setup' {name,password,confirm,avatarColor,legacyVaultId} -> needsProfileSetup must be true -> validatePassword -> blob=createVaultVerifyBlob(password) -> target vault = payload.legacyVaultId || migration.primaryVaultId || the only vault -> if existing: db.updateCredentialsVaultVerifyBlob(vaultId, blob) (overwrites WITHOUT verifying the old master password), renameVault, updateVaultAvatar(initials,color), ensureProfileDir, seedProfileFromGlobal; else createVault + updateVaultAvatar + initNewProfileDataFiles + seedProfileFromGlobal + db.migrateNotesToProfile -> markProfileSetupComplete() -> finishProfileLogin.
- CREATE PROFILE: 'profile-create' {name,password,confirm,avatarColor} -> validatePassword -> createVaultVerifyBlob -> db.createVault -> updateVaultAvatar -> initNewProfileDataFiles (factory settings, empty ui-prefs) -> if needsProfileSetup: markProfileSetupComplete -> finishProfileLogin.
- SWITCH / LOGOUT: browser-renderer profile drawer -> 'profile-open-switch-gate' -> lockAll('switch-gate') + clearCredentialsVaultSession + settings/uiPrefs scope reset to global + reload + broadcast -> ctx.onProfileSwitchGate -> openProfileSwitchGate (mainWindow.hide(), gate shown). Gate closed without login -> 'closed' handler re-shows main (or app.quit if no main). 'profile-logout' -> lockAll + stores back to global + broadcast (main window stays open, no gate). 'profile-gate-quit' -> app.quit().
- CREDENTIALS WINDOW BOOT: sub-windows createCredentialsWindow() (trusted, preload.js) -> 'credentials-init' {pageUrl,domain} -> credentials-renderer bootstrap(): credentialsVaultStatus + profileSessionStatus -> no vault & profile active -> vault screen; unlocked -> enterVault(); vaultCount>1 -> picker; else unlock screen. enterVault -> refreshAll (credentials-list limit 2000, credentials-type-counts, credentials-folders-list, credentials-count-trash) -> loadRows. On window close: if !profileSession.isProfileActive() clearCredentialsVaultSession().
- UNLOCK FROM CREDENTIALS UI / TOOLBAR: 'credentials-unlock'(pw,vaultId?), 'credentials-vault-switch'(id,pw), 'credentials-unlock-and-get-matches'{password,pageUrl,vaultId?}, 'credential-capture-unlock-and-save'(pw) all call _unlockVaultRecord(meta,pw): vaultSession.unlockVault -> persist upgradedVerifyBlob -> updateVaultLastLogin -> settingsStore.setActiveProfileVaultId(meta.id) (uiPrefsStore NOT updated, no reload/broadcast) -> migratePlaintextCredentialFields -> 'credentials-toolbar-refresh' to mainWindow. Note unlockVault also calls profileSession.activateProfile, so this silently switches the active profile.
- SAVE / READ ITEM: renderer saveCurrentCredential -> 'credentials-save' payload {id,item_type,domain,label,login,password,extra{totpSecret,card,identity,secureNoteContent},notes,tags,is_favorite,folder_id,uris[],customFields[]} -> _requireMaster -> password & extra JSON via vaultCrypto.enc (CV02) -> login_encrypted/notes_encrypted via encryptCredentialFieldWithKey (plain columns set '') -> db.saveCredential (vault_id=active) -> saveCredentialUris -> saveCredentialCustomFields (value_encrypted CV02). 'credentials-get'(id) -> getCredential -> _decField password/extra/custom fields (no password fallback, CV01 -> ''), _plainLogin/_plainNotes, listCredentialUris.
- FORM CAPTURE: tab-manager injected script -> window.cupnetCredentials.reportCapture({login,password,source}) (preload-view) -> ipcMain.on('credential-form-captured') (unguarded) -> wc.getURL() must be http(s) -> domain via getNoteDomainFromUrl -> tab proxy profile + checkCurrentIpGeo -> _pendingCapture -> mainWindow 'show-credential-save-bar' {login,domain,needsUnlock,vaultId,profileName} -> user: 'credential-capture-confirm' (_saveCapturedCredential into _resolveCaptureVaultId) | 'credential-capture-dismiss' | 'credential-capture-unlock-and-save'(pw). _saveCapturedCredential finds existing by plaintext r.login, writes plaintext login column + CV02 password.
- AUTOFILL: browser-renderer toolbar -> 'credentials-site-match-count' {pageUrl} (works locked; counts in active or first vault) / 'credentials-site-matches' -> list {id,login,label} from db.listCredentials({domain,vaultId}) filtered by noteMatchesUrlMatch -> 'credentials-fill-active-tab' {credentialId} -> getCredential -> decrypt password -> tab.view.webContents.executeJavaScript(_fillScript(row.login,password)) (fills first non-password input in the form + input[type=password], dispatches input/change) -> updateCredentialLastUsedMeta (ip/proxy) -> toolbar refresh.
- CHANGE MASTER: modal -> 'credentials-change-master' {oldPassword,newPassword,confirm} -> validatePassword(new) -> verifyVaultPassword(old) (not rate-limited) -> require vault unlocked -> newBlob=createVaultVerifyBlob(new) -> newKey=deriveMasterKey(new,newBlob[5..21]) -> for each db.getAllCredentialCipherRows(vid): decrypt password_encrypted/extra_encrypted with (active key | oldPw fallback for CV01) -> updateCredentialCipherFields(newKey CV02) -> re-encrypt credential_custom_fields -> db.updateCredentialsVaultVerifyBlob -> vaultSession.unlockVault(new). Not in a DB transaction; login_encrypted, notes_encrypted, CN02 notes and encrypted traffic logs are NOT re-keyed. Renderer then always calls credentialsVaultSetHint(hint).
- EXPORT: 'credentials-export' {format:'vault'|'cupnet-vault-export'|'json'|'csv', exportPassword} -> json/csv require settings.allowPlaintextExport + dialog.showMessageBoxSync confirm -> items from listCredentials({vaultId,limit 2000}) + getCredential + decrypt -> vault format: validatePassword(exportPassword) + _buildEncryptedVaultExport (scrypt(exportPw,salt16) AES-256-GCM, JSON {format:'cupnet-vault-export',version:1,salt,iv,tag,data} base64) -> showSaveDialog -> writeFileSecureAsync. Renderer asks export password with window.prompt().
- IMPORT: renderer parseImportSource (bitwarden-json/csv, chrome-csv, firefox-csv, lastpass/1password/keepass-csv) or batch (delimiter/regex parsers) -> 'credentials-import-batch' {items, grabIp?, pageUrl?} (max 5000) -> optional checkCurrentIpGeo -> better-sqlite3 transaction -> _insertCredentialFromImport (password/extra CV02, login & notes stored PLAINTEXT) -> {imported,skipped,ids}.
- IDLE / POWER LOCK: activity -> 'vault-session-activity' (browser-renderer mousemove throttled 5s + URL changes; credentials-renderer every mousedown/keydown/scroll/touchstart) and every requireUnlockedMasterKey() -> bumpActivity -> _scheduleIdleLock (autoLockMinutes*60s, unref). Timer callback returns immediately if profileSession.isProfileActive() (always true after unlockVault). powerMonitor 'lock-screen'/'suspend' -> lockAll -> profileSession.clearProfileSession -> onLock callbacks -> main window toast 'Vault locked due to inactivity' and profile UI inactive; main window and profile-scoped settings stay.
- NOTES: 'notes-save' -> resolveNoteDomains -> htmlToMd -> encrypted: encryptNotePayload(JSON {body}, explicit password) CN01 | plain: body_plain; profile_id = profileSession.getActiveVaultId()||0 -> db.saveUserNoteAsync. 'notes-get'(id,pw): plain -> mdToHtml; encrypted without pw -> _decryptNoteBody (CN02 via vault key) else {locked:true}; with pw -> decryptNotePayload (CN01 only). 'notes-list' filtered by profileId only when a profile is active. At profile login migrateNotesToMasterKey converts CN01 notes decryptable with the profile password to CN02. 'notes-export' always requires a password and uses CN01-only decrypt.
- TRAFFIC LOG ENCRYPTION (consumer of the vault key): db request logging -> traffic-log-crypto.prepareRequestLogFields(entry): mode=settings.trafficLogEncryption; key=vaultSession.getMasterKey(); encrypt -> CV02 blobs into requests.request_headers_enc/response_headers_enc/request_body_enc/response_body_enc, log_encrypted=1; locked+encrypt or redact -> bundle-utils redactHeaders/redactBody('Balanced'). Reads: hydrateRequestLogRow decrypts with the CURRENT active key or returns {_locked:true}.
- PASTE UNLOCK: tab preload-view.js sendSync('get-paste-unlock-sync') -> settings-toolbar-ipc returns {script: getPasteUnlockScript()} unless settings.pasteUnlock===false or CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL>=1 -> webFrame.executeJavaScript(script) (main world, before page scripts); tab-manager.injectPasteUnlock also runs on load; 'set-paste-unlock'(enabled) -> saveSettings + tabManager.setPasteUnlock -> reinject via 'paste-unlock-update'.

**IPC channels:**

- renderer->main invoke (guarded) profile: profile-gate-status, profile-list, profile-session-status, profile-complete-setup, profile-login, profile-create, profile-logout, profile-open-switch-gate, profile-gate-quit, vault-session-activity
- renderer->main invoke (guarded) vault: credentials-vault-status, credentials-vault-list, credentials-vault-create, credentials-vault-setup, credentials-unlock(password, vaultId?), credentials-vault-switch(vaultId, password), credentials-vault-delete(vaultId, password), credentials-vault-rename(vaultId, name), credentials-lock, credentials-vault-set-hint(hint), credentials-change-master
- renderer->main invoke (guarded) items: credentials-list(filter), credentials-get(id), credentials-save(payload), credentials-import-batch, credentials-delete(id, permanent), credentials-restore, credentials-purge-trash, credentials-count-trash, credentials-type-counts, credentials-favorite, credentials-folders-list, credentials-folder-create, credentials-folder-rename, credentials-folder-delete, credentials-move-to-folder, credentials-uris-get, credentials-uris-save, credentials-fields-get, credentials-fields-save, credentials-export
- renderer(shell)->main invoke (guarded) browser integration: credentials-get-tab-proxy-ip, credentials-site-matches, credentials-site-match-count, credentials-unlock-and-get-matches, credentials-fill-active-tab, credential-capture-confirm, credential-capture-dismiss, credential-capture-unlock-and-save
- tab renderer->main send (UNGUARDED ipcMain.on): credential-form-captured {login,password,source} (via window.cupnetCredentials.reportCapture in preload-view.js)
- renderer->main invoke (UNGUARDED ipcMain.handle): notes-list, notes-get(id,pw), notes-save, notes-delete, notes-pin, notes-export, notes-get-request-for-embed (notes-embed-request/open-notes-window registered elsewhere)
- renderer->main: open-credentials-window (invoke), set-paste-unlock (invoke, settings-toolbar-ipc), get-paste-unlock-sync (sendSync from preload-view)
- main->renderer (mainWindow): credentials-toolbar-refresh, show-credential-save-bar {login,domain,needsUnlock,vaultId,profileName}, credentials-locked, profile-session-changed {active,vaultId,name,avatarColor,initials}
- main->renderer (credentialsWindow): credentials-init {pageUrl,domain}, credentials-context-update {pageUrl,domain}
- main->tab renderer: paste-unlock-update {script}

**Data stores:**

- SQLite cupnet.db (userData; opened by db.js initWithPath, WAL, foreign_keys ON, file/dir chmod 600/700).
- Table credentials_vault(id PK AUTOINCREMENT, name, verify_blob BLOB NOT NULL, hint, last_login_at, created_at, avatar_icon, avatar_color). One row = one profile. Legacy singleton CHECK(id=1) removed by migrateCredentialsVaultMultiProfile.
- Table credentials(id, vault_id FK CASCADE, item_type login|card|identity|note, domain, url_match, label, login (plaintext, legacy), login_encrypted BLOB, password_encrypted BLOB, extra_encrypted BLOB (JSON: totpSecret, card, identity, secureNoteContent, customFields), notes (plaintext, legacy), notes_encrypted BLOB, last_ip, last_proxy_profile_id, last_proxy_profile_name, last_used_at, tags, is_favorite, folder_id FK SET NULL, deleted_at (trash), created_at, updated_at).
- Tables credential_folders(id, vault_id, name, parent_id, sort_order), credential_uris(credential_id FK CASCADE, uri, match_type 0..6, sort_order), credential_custom_fields(credential_id FK CASCADE, name, value_encrypted BLOB, field_type text|hidden|checkbox, sort_order).
- Table user_notes(id, domain, url_match, page_url, title, body_plain, body_encrypted BLOB CN01/CN02, is_encrypted, is_pinned, tags, profile_id INTEGER default 0, created_at, updated_at) + note_request_links.
- Table requests: request_headers_enc/response_headers_enc/request_body_enc/response_body_enc BLOB (CV02 with vault key), log_encrypted flag (traffic-log-crypto).
- File userData/profile-migration-state.json {version, migratedAt, primaryVaultId, vaultsEnsured, notesAssigned, settingsSeededFromGlobal, uiPrefsSeededFromGlobal, profileSetupComplete, profileSetupCompletedAt}.
- Files userData/profiles/{vaultId}/settings.json and ui-prefs.json (profile-scoped). Global fallbacks: userData/settings.json, userData/ui-prefs.json.
- Settings keys: vaultAutoLockMinutes (default 15, 0=off), trafficLogEncryption ('off'|'redact'|'encrypt', default 'encrypt'), trafficLogRawMode (false), allowPlaintextExport (false), pasteUnlock (true), currentProxy (stripped on save/export).
- Data file data/common-passwords-10k.txt (password blocklist).
- Export file *.cupnet-vault-export: JSON {format:'cupnet-vault-export', version:1, salt, iv, tag, data} (base64; scrypt + AES-256-GCM; payload {version, exportedAt, items[]}). Plaintext *.json / *.csv only with allowPlaintextExport.
- Env vars: CUPNET_E2E=1 and CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE=1 (skip gate and setup), CUPNET_STEALTH_LEVEL (>=1 disables paste-unlock injection, >=6 no tab preload).
- In-memory main-process state: vault-session-service _vaultKeys Map<vaultId,{key,name}>, _activeVaultId, _unlockAttempts, _autoLockMinutes, _idleTimer, _onLockCallbacks; profile-session _active {vaultId,name}; credentials-ipc _pendingCapture; ipc-trusted-sender _trustedShellIds/_tabViewIds; settingsStore/uiPrefsStore _activeProfileVaultId.
- Renderer: credentials-renderer module state only (the old localStorage 'credentials-autolock-minutes' was removed).

**Conventions:**

- Register every privileged handler through the local helper `const handle = (channel, fn) => ctx.ipcMain.handle(channel, guardIpcHandler(channel, fn));` (credentials-ipc.js:157, profile-ipc.js:114). guardIpcHandler makes the handler async and throws Error{code:'IPC_UNTRUSTED_SENDER'} for untrusted senders.
- Every BrowserWindow that loads preload.js must be created via sub-windows.js _spawnTrustedToolWindow / _spawnRequestEditorBrowserWindow or call registerTrustedShell(win.webContents) itself (main-window.js, profile-gate-window.js); tab WebContentsViews are registerTabView'ed and use preload-view.js, which must never expose credentials/profile/notes APIs (SECURITY.md CI grep: `rg "credentials-get" preload-view.js` expects 0).
- Never keep the master/profile password string after unlock. Derive the 32-byte session key via vault-session-service.unlockVault; get it only through vaultSession.getMasterKey()/credentials-vault-crypto.requireUnlockedMasterKey(). SECURITY.md CI grep: `rg "password:\s*String\(password\)" main-process/` expects 0.
- Encrypt new secret fields with credentials-vault-crypto.enc() (CV02) or encryptCredentialFieldWithKey(value, key); decrypt with vaultCrypto.dec(buf, passwordFallback). Blob type is identified only by 4-byte magic (CV01/CV02/CN01/CN02) + 1 version byte.
- IPC handlers return plain objects `{ success: boolean, error?: string, ... }` (export returns `{ canceled, filePath?, error? }`) instead of throwing; CREDENTIALS_LOCKED is mapped to 'Vault is locked'. Exceptions: credentials-list and credentials-get throw when locked (renderer checks that the error text contains 'locked').
- Server-side password validation is authoritative: validatePassword() (>=12 chars + blocklist) for vault/profile/export passwords; renderer checks are only UX.
- Secret-bearing files are written with secure-fs writeFileSecure/writeFileSecureAsync (atomic tmp+rename, 0600) inside ensureSecureDir (0700) directories.
- Profile scoping: profile id == credentials_vault.id; per-profile files under userData/profiles/{vaultId}/ (settings.json, ui-prefs.json) selected with settingsStore/uiPrefsStore.setActiveProfileVaultId(); notes scoped by user_notes.profile_id; credentials by credentials.vault_id; proxy_profiles remain shared.
- All user-facing strings (errors, labels, toasts, dialogs) must be English (project rule).
- Handler modules double as libraries: profile-ipc imports clearCredentialsVaultSession/migratePlaintextCredentialFields from credentials-ipc.js and migrateNotesToMasterKey from notes-ipc.js; sub-windows.js imports clearCredentialsVaultSession. Keep these exports stable.
- Unit tests are vitest `tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs` loading CJS via createRequire; stateful services expose `_resetForTests()` (vault-session-service, ipc-trusted-sender, password-policy `_resetCommonPasswordCacheForTests`).
- Do not use session.protocol.handle for interception in MITM mode and prefer MITM-side outbound behaviour (project rules) — relevant because paste-unlock/capture scripts run in the page main world and are detectable.

**Gotchas:**

- The subsystem is UNCOMMITTED WIP: profile-ipc.js, vault-session-service.js, profile-*.js, secure-fs.js, password-policy.js, credentials-vault-crypto.js, preload-shell.js, profile-gate.*, SECURITY.md and 4 test files are untracked; credentials-crypto.js/credentials-ipc.js/notes-*.js are modified. Real users' DBs are in the HEAD format: CV01 verify blob + CV01 per-field scrypt encryption with the master password (see `git show HEAD:credentials-crypto.js`). Any change must keep a migration path for CV01 data.
- The session key IS the verify-blob key: key = Argon2id(password, verify_blob[5..21]). Replacing verify_blob (upgrade, profile-complete-setup, change-master) changes the data key; every CV02/CN02/traffic blob written with the old key becomes undecryptable unless re-encrypted in the same operation. Verified in scratchpad: after a CV01 unlock, the upgraded blob yields a different key and fields written in the first session fail with 'Unsupported state or unable to authenticate data'.
- CV01 fields can only be decrypted when a password is passed (decryptCredentialFieldWithKey third arg). Since the password is no longer retained, the only code paths able to read CV01 data are those that still have the password in scope: vault unlock handlers, finishProfileLogin, and credentials-change-master (which passes oldPw).
- credentials-crypto silently falls back to scrypt when `require('argon2')` fails, and blobs don't record which KDF produced them. A blob created with the fallback won't verify once argon2 loads (and vice versa) -> user lockout. argon2@0.41.1 ships N-API prebuilds for darwin-arm64/x64, linux-arm64/x64, win32-x64, freebsd-x64 only (no win32-arm64).
- Each profile login runs 4-5 Argon2id derivations (64 MiB, t=3): profile-login verify, unlockVault verify, deriveSessionKeyFromVerifyBlob's second verify, the actual derive, plus createVaultVerifyBlob on upgrade. CV01 paths use scryptSync and block the main thread (change-master over many CV01 rows can freeze the UI).
- Idle auto-lock is effectively disabled: vault-session-service._scheduleIdleLock's timer returns early while profileSession.isProfileActive(), and unlockVault always activates the profile. tests/unit/vault-auto-lock.test.mjs asserts this on purpose, which contradicts SECURITY.md ('Idle lock' checklist item 1). Also vaultAutoLockMinutes is read once at startup from GLOBAL settings (initFromSettings) and never re-read after a profile login or a Settings change.
- lockAll() is the only way to lock: credentials-lock (the Lock button in the Credentials window), clearCredentialsVaultSession, powerMonitor events and profile switches all lock EVERY vault AND clear the profile session (the main window gets profile-session-changed {active:false}) — but settingsStore/uiPrefsStore stay pointed at the profile dir and the main window stays usable.
- Unlocking a different vault from the Credentials window or toolbar (_unlockVaultRecord) calls profileSession.activateProfile and settingsStore.setActiveProfileVaultId, but not uiPrefsStore, reloads or broadcastProfileSessionChanged, so the app's active profile changes silently and inconsistently.
- Traffic-log encryption uses the active vault key with no vault id stored on the row. Rows logged under profile A can't be read under profile B, and every re-key (change master, blob upgrade) orphans them.
- db.listCredentials clamps limit to 2000 (db.js ~2143) even when callers pass 10000/5000: migratePlaintextCredentialFields, export, health scan, site matches and the UI list all silently cap at 2000 rows; trashed rows (deleted_at not null) are never migrated.
- db.saveCredential UPDATE uses `login_encrypted = COALESCE(?, login_encrypted)` / `notes_encrypted = COALESCE(?, ...)`, so passing null keeps the old ciphertext. It also overwrites folder_id, item_type (defaults 'login') and vault_id with whatever the record contains.
- After migration the plaintext `credentials.login` column is '' — every code path reading `r.login`/`row.login` (list subtitle, site-matches, fill-active-tab, capture de-duplication, db search) gets empty values; only credentials-get/export use _plainLogin().
- needsProfileSetup() returns false under CUPNET_E2E=1 / CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE=1, and the runtime skips the gate entirely. E2E tests therefore never exercise the gate/setup flows, and no profile is active in E2E (notes get profile_id 0, the vault behaves like the old standalone flow).
- profile-migration-state.json drives setup mode: if it is deleted or corrupted, runProfileDataMigration rewrites it with profileSetupComplete:false, which forces setup mode again and (because of profile-complete-setup) resets the legacy vault's password.
- Electron renderers don't support window.prompt(), yet credentials-renderer uses it for the encrypted export password, vault switch password, vault delete password, and folder create/rename/move. These flows don't work as written; a custom modal is needed.
- In Node/vitest, `require('electron')` returns a path string, so powerMonitor/app are undefined. vault-session-service loads fine (powerMonitor errors are swallowed), but profile-session.getProfileDir() throws in tests.
- notes-ipc handlers are registered with plain ctx.ipcMain.handle (no trusted-sender guard), unlike credentials/profile. Safe today only because preload-view.js doesn't expose notes APIs.
- Password min length is 12 on the server, but UI copy still says 8 in several places (credentials.html setup label 'min 8 characters' + renderer check `pw.length < 8`, change-master label 'min 8', profile-gate.html placeholder 'Minimum 8 characters').
- preload-shell.js is not used by any window. Every trusted window, including the pre-auth profile gate, gets the full preload.js API.

**Tests:**

- Run the subsystem unit tests (vitest, config vitest.config.js includes tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs): `npx vitest run tests/unit/credentials-crypto.test.mjs tests/unit/credentials-login-migration.test.mjs tests/unit/vault-auto-lock.test.mjs tests/unit/password-policy.test.mjs tests/unit/ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs tests/unit/traffic-log-crypto.test.mjs tests/unit/totp-generator.test.mjs tests/unit/profile-avatars.test.mjs` (SECURITY.md lists `npm run test:unit -- <files>`). Full suite: `npm run test:unit`; with Go worker: `npm run test:unit:all`.
- credentials-crypto.test.mjs: CV01 round-trip, CV02 round-trip with deriveMasterKey, CV02 verify blob. It does not cover the CV01->CV02 upgrade/key-stability, deriveSessionKeyFromVerifyBlob, or the argon2 fallback.
- credentials-login-migration.test.mjs: migratePlaintextCredentialFields with a fake db (login/notes -> *_encrypted, plain cleared).
- vault-auto-lock.test.mjs: asserts there is NO idle lock while the profile is active, and an idle lock when the profile session is cleared (fake timers).
- password-policy.test.mjs: min length, fallback list, loads >9000 entries from data/common-passwords-10k.txt, rejects a >=12-char listed password, accepts a strong passphrase.
- ipc-trusted-sender.test.mjs: trusted shell allowed; tab view rejected with IPC_UNTRUSTED_SENDER.
- totp-generator.test.mjs: decodeBase32, secondsRemaining, deterministic code for JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP.
- profile-avatars.test.mjs uses `node:test` + `node:assert` (not vitest) with ESM named imports from a CJS module. Under `vitest run` this file probably reports 'No test suite found'. Verify, then convert it to vitest or run it with `node --test`.
- traffic-log-crypto.test.mjs covers the traffic-log encrypt/redact path that depends on the vault key.
- NOT covered: credentials-ipc handlers (save/get/change-master/export/import/capture/fill), profile-ipc (login/setup/create/switch/logout), notes-ipc crypto paths (CN01/CN02, migrateNotesToMasterKey, export), profile-data-migration, secure-fs, paste-unlock, db vault functions (a db test exists only as `npm run test:db`, tests/test-db.js under ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE). E2E (`npm run test:e2e`) runs with CUPNET_E2E=1, which bypasses the profile gate.
- Manual checklist: SECURITY.md 'Manual verification checklist' (sqlite3 checks of credentials.login, requests.log_encrypted, file modes, tab DevTools `window.electronAPI?.credentialsGet`, rate limit).

**Outdated / to-update:**

- Electron runtime major version (`package.json devDependencies.electron`) — now: ^34.0.0 (installed 34.5.8); Electron 34 is long out of support (Sep 2026; current majors are ~40+). Affects powerMonitor, BrowserWindow options, and security fixes. Also the gate sets `backgroundMaterial: 'ultra-dark'` on darwin (profile-gate-window.js:76), but backgroundMaterial is a Windows-only option and 'ultra-dark' is a removed macOS vibrancy value.
- argon2 native module (`package.json dependencies.argon2; credentials-crypto.js:16-21`) — now: ^0.41.1; Newer releases exist. The installed version has no win32-arm64 prebuild. Its load failure is silently masked by the scrypt fallback.
- scrypt KDF parameters (`credentials-crypto.js:24 (CV01), notes-crypto.js:26/44 (CN01), credentials-ipc.js:140 (_buildEncryptedVaultExport)`) — now: crypto.scryptSync(pw, salt, 32) with Node defaults N=16384,r=8,p=1; Below current OWASP guidance (N=2^17). The encrypted export and per-note passwords use this weaker KDF instead of Argon2id.
- KDF parameters not versioned in blob format (`credentials-crypto.js CV02 verify blob layout`) — now: 'CV02'|0x02|salt|iv|tag|ct, Argon2id m=65536,t=3,p=1 hardcoded (ARGON2_MEMORY/TIME/PARALLELISM); Argon2 cost can't be raised, and argon2 and scrypt blobs can't be told apart, without a new format version.
- Unit test framework (`package.json devDependencies.vitest`) — now: ^2.1.9; Vitest 3/4 are current. profile-avatars.test.mjs uses node:test instead.
- Common-password blocklist (`data/common-passwords-10k.txt used by password-policy.js`) — now: 10k list, only ~10 entries are >=12 chars; Effectively a no-op against the 12-char minimum. Consider a larger breached-password list or a zxcvbn-style estimator.
- Renderer password strength heuristic (`credentials-renderer.js:49 scoreMasterPassword`) — now: character-class counting (length>=8/12/16, cases, digits, symbols); Doesn't match the server policy; used for Health 'weak' detection too.
- Passphrase wordlist (`credentials-renderer.js:63 EFF_WORDS`) — now: ~440 words (a BIP39 'abandon..exact' prefix, not the EFF list); ~8.8 bits/word, so the default 4 words is ~35 bits. Use the EFF large wordlist (7776 words).
- window.prompt() (`credentials-renderer.js:512,625,1216,1245,1266,1356`) — now: prompt(...) for export password, vault password, folder names; Not supported in Electron renderers.
- Stale CSP allowances (`credentials.html:6`) — now: img-src https://www.google.com; connect-src https://api.pwnedpasswords.com; Favicon fetching was removed and there is no HIBP code; tighten the CSP.
- SECURITY.md claims (`SECURITY.md (Runtime controls, Data at rest, Preload split)`) — now: Documents idle auto-lock, 'Logins, notes encrypted on save', preload-shell split; Code disables idle lock under profiles, import/capture store plaintext logins, and preload-shell.js is unused. Keep docs/SECURITY.ru.md in sync.

**Production gaps:**

- No CV01->CV02 migration of existing credential fields (password_encrypted, extra_encrypted, credential_custom_fields.value_encrypted) at unlock time while the password is available. Legacy users see empty passwords after upgrading.
- No key-wrapping design: the data key is derived straight from the password (no random DEK wrapped by a password KEK). Password change/upgrade has to re-encrypt everything (credentials, notes, traffic logs) and currently doesn't. Adopt a DEK stored encrypted in credentials_vault plus HKDF subkeys per domain (credentials/notes/traffic).
- Re-key operations (change-master, blob upgrade, profile setup) are not atomic: no single SQLite transaction and no crash recovery.
- No import path for the encrypted .cupnet-vault-export backup, and the export is lossy (omits item_type, uris, customFields, folder). The backup can't be restored.
- Rate limiting exists only inside vaultSession.unlockVault, in memory. profile-login, credentials-change-master and credentials-vault-delete call verifyVaultPassword directly and bypass it. No persistent lockout.
- No re-authentication gate after screen lock/suspend: the main window remains usable (with profile-scoped settings) after lockAll. Idle auto-lock is disabled while a profile is active.
- Profile switch doesn't re-apply settings side effects (settings-toolbar-ipc applySettingsSideEffects: paste unlock, traffic filters, device permissions, CORS, chrome settings) or vault auto-lock minutes, and doesn't reset tabs/sessions/cookies of the previous profile.
- Profile/vault deletion leaves orphans: userData/profiles/{id}/ directory, user_notes rows with profile_id=id, traffic logs encrypted with that key. There is no profile delete in the gate UI.
- Windows: secure-fs chmod is a no-op, so there is no ACL hardening for cupnet.db/settings/profile dirs. powerMonitor 'lock-screen' isn't emitted on Linux.
- No blind index for encrypted login search (documented limitation). The 2000-row cap across list/export/migration needs pagination.
- No test coverage for the IPC flows (profile login/setup, change-master, export/import, capture, autofill, notes CN02). E2E bypasses the gate.
- No AEAD associated data binding ciphertexts to row/column/vault, so blobs can be swapped between rows/columns by someone with DB write access. The same key and format are used for the verify marker, credentials and traffic logs (no domain separation).
- Memory hygiene is partial: the argon2 raw output buffer, verify-derived keys and the change-master newKey aren't zeroed. Plaintext values live in JS strings.
- No audit trail of unlock/lock/export/change-master events. sysLog only logs errors.
- Clipboard auto-clear is renderer-only (20s setTimeout in the Credentials window). It is lost if the window closes and it clears whatever the user copied later.
- TOTP: no otpauth:// URI parsing and no per-item algorithm/digits/period storage (renderer always calls cupnetTotp.generate(secret) with defaults).

## 5. How to run / build / test

```bash
npm install --ignore-scripts && npm run rebuild:arm64   # Apple Silicon native build
ELECTRON_RUN_AS_NODE= npm start                          # run the app (clears the Cursor/IDE env that forces headless Node)

# Tests
npm run test:unit        # Vitest (tests/unit/**/*.test.mjs) — some need network (azuretls worker hits httpbin)
npm run test:go          # Go worker unit tests
npm test                 # tests/run-all.sh — node integration suites; test-db needs the Electron binary
npm run test:e2e:smoke   # Playwright + Electron (CUPNET_SKIP_E2E=1 to skip)
npm run build:go:local   # build the Go AzureTLS worker for the current platform

# CupNet mini (mini/README.md): MITM + Go worker for the real Google Chrome, plain Node, no Electron
npm run mini -- start --window --chrome
npm run pack:mini        # standalone dist/CupNet mini.app (or dist/cupnet-mini/): required files only + Go worker + Node
```

**CupNet mini notes:**
- mini reuses `mitm-proxy.js` and `request-interceptor.js`, and injects its JSON rule store (`mini/rule-store.js`) through `setRuleStore`. Never `require('./db')` on mini's path: `scripts/pack-mini.cjs` leaves db.js out, and its `walkRequires` test fails if a new npm package shows up.
- mini logs every body in full (`CUPNET_LOG_BODY_MAX_BYTES` defaults to 256 MB; `CUPNET_LOG_MEDIA_BODIES=1`).
- mini modules:
  - `mini/mini-app.js`: wiring, settings, Repeater, breakpoints.
  - `mini/ui-server.js`: loopback API with token auth and strict CSP.
  - `mini/ui.html`, `mini/ui.js`, `mini/ui.css`: the UI, with no inline scripts. Build DOM with `h()`/`append()`, never native `Element.append` with arrays.
  - `mini/proxy-store.js`:
    - `ProxyStore`: encrypted profiles in `proxies.json` + `proxies.key`.
    - `ProxyManager`: test, connect, kill switch, a self-loop guard, and restore.
  - `mini/ip-monitor.js`: `IpMonitor`, the exit-IP monitor.
    - It gets its context from `ProxyManager.connectionContext()` and feeds results back through `adoptEgress`.
    - `onChange` calls `noteSwitch()`, so a user switch becomes a `switch` event and not a `change`.
    - An answer for a connection that changed during the check is dropped.
    - History is `<data>/ip-history.jsonl`. Events are also written to the session log via `SessionLog.logIp` (kind `ip`).
  - `mini/cookie-jar.js`: `CookieJar`, the cookie manager (MITM path only, no CDP).
    - `observe()` is fed from every logged request. Wire rules (`pin`/`strip`/`block`) and the queued Chrome ops (set, delete, clear-site) run in the `MitmProxy` hooks `onBeforeUpstream` (after identity rewriting) and `onResponseHead` (on every upstream response, buffered or streamed, including the native fallback; mocks never reach it). The ops are injected as `Set-Cookie` / `Clear-Site-Data` into the next first-party response.
    - State is `<data>/cookies.json` (0600). `rev` bumps on every change, and `/api/status.cookies` carries it so the UI polls cheaply.
  - `mini/compare-diff.js`: the request-compare diff (header/query/cookie multimaps, header order, JSON structure, Myers line diff, hunks). It is an IIFE classic script shared by the UI (`window.CupNetDiff`) and Vitest (`module.exports`). Library scripts must not leak top-level names: they share one global lexical scope with `ui.js`.
  - Page agent (Console + Page analyzer, off by default via `settings.pageAgent`):
    - `mini/page-agent.js`: the in-page script, injected through the request-interceptor document-scripts provider (`MiniApp._documentScripts`, `connectSelf`, CSP nonce). `pageAgentMain(cfg, hooks)`: tests pass `{test: true, window: fakeWindow, expose}` so nothing touches the real global and no hello/poll runs.
    - `mini/page-agents.js`: `PageAgents`, the per-document registry (origin + id checked on every call), command queue with long-poll, results and console counts.
    - `mini/page-analyzer.js`: `auditSecurityHeaders` (statuses `good|warn|bad|info`), `techFromHeaders`, `enrichReport`, and `stripAgentFromLoggedDocument`, which removes the agent (and restores the CSP) from the logged HTML so the session shows what the site sent.
    - `/__cupnet/console` and `/__cupnet/agent/*` are answered in `MiniApp._handleAgentEndpoint`; they are reachable by any site, so they never return logged data.
    - `mini/ui-inspect.js`: the Console and Page analyzer views. It loads after `ui.js` and shares its global scope (`h`, `PG`, `CN`, `AN`).
  - `mini/dns-store.js`: `DnsStore`, DNS overrides in `<data>/dns.json` (0600). `toMitmRules()` feeds `MitmProxy.setDnsOverrides({dial, wildcards})`: dial mode keeps URL/SNI/Host/H2 and changes only the TCP target (the Go worker gets `connectTo`, see `azuretls-go/connect_to.go`). Hits and activity are in memory; logged entries carry `dns`.
  - `mini/notes-store.js`: `NotesStore`, Markdown notes in `<data>/notes.json` (0600, atomic). `site` uses the DNS host normalizer (wildcards OK). `cupnet:req/<session>/<seq>` references are rendered as Traffic links by the UI.
  - `mini/site-map.js`: static HTML parse of a session's `Document` responses → pages (opened vs only linked), forms and fields with visibility (`hidden` / `likely`), external hosts. `SiteMapCache` scans each session once (LRU) and the live one incrementally. CSS/JS hiding is out of scope (the Page analyzer sees the live DOM).
  - `mini/share-host.js`: `ShareHost`, the local test host for sealed sessions: 127.0.0.1 only, Host checked, serves only `/` (viewer.html with a CSP script hash computed at start) and `/sealed/<id>.cupsess`. Started by `settings.shareHost` or `cupnet-mini host`. Port 0 = ephemeral (tests).
  - `mini/ui-tools.js`: DNS, Notes, Site map and Share views. Loads after `ui-inspect.js` and shares the global scope.
  - `mini/page-snapshot.js`: `buildSnapshot(log, seq)`, the offline page for Site map → Page, from logged responses only (no fetch); scripts/base/refresh dropped, own CSP; the UI shows it in `<iframe sandbox="">`.
  - WebSockets: `ws-frames.js` (root, shared): `FrameParser`, `encodeFrame`, `relayWebSocket` (byte-exact relay + parsed copy, permessage-deflate inflate for display, injection only at message boundaries). `mitm-proxy.js` calls it in the WS tunnel when `onWebSocket` is set. `mini/ws-hub.js` (`WsHub`) maps relays to the upgrade request seq and logs `kind: 'ws'` entries via `SessionLog.logWs`; `mini/ui-ws.js` is the WebSockets view (hooks `wsIngest`/`wsReset` from `ui.js`).
  - `mini/har.js`: HAR 1.2.
  - `mini/browser-libs.json`: browser libs copied into the pack and served at `/lib/*`.
- `upstream-tunnel.js` (`connectThroughProxy`) gives the raw MITM paths (TLS passthrough, WebSocket) http, https, socks4(a) and socks5(h) upstreams (A-410).
- `MitmProxy.setUpstream()` clears egress blocking. The kill switch is applied in `ProxyManager._applyDirect`.

**Gotchas:** 6 unit files under `tests/unit/` still `import { describe, it } from 'node:test'` — Vitest reports "No test suite found" and PR checks stay red (see AUDIT). `npm run test:go` needs Go on PATH. The azuretls-go worker test hits the network; the concurrency case flakes on a slow link.

## 6. Hardcoded browser fingerprints (single source of truth needed)

> **2026-09-23 update:** `browser-identity-catalog.js` is now the JS source of truth (UA + TLS profile + client hints + navigator.platform per template); `proxy-tls-ua-sync.js` and the Proxy Manager presets derive from it, and `tests/unit/browser-identity-catalog.test.mjs` asserts the UAs match `azuretls-go/profiles.go`. Only Go (`profiles.go`) and the legacy `azure-tls-worker.js` still carry copies. See `docs/ai/PROXY_IDENTITY_REDESIGN.md`.

Browser UA / HTTP2 / TLS profile strings are **copied in 5 places** and drift apart: `azuretls-go/profiles.go`, `azure-tls-worker.js` (BROWSER_PROFILES), `proxy-tls-ua-sync.js`, `proxy-manager-renderer.js` (UA_PRESETS), and `user-agent-utils.js`. The Go worker also always calls `ApplyHTTP2` with the repo string, overriding azuretls' more accurate built-in Firefox/Safari/iOS presets. Current pinned values are Chrome 133 / Firefox 138 / Safari 18.3 / iOS 18.3 / Edge 133 / Opera 119; the Firefox and Safari/iOS HTTP2 strings are wrong. Target values and the exact locations are in PRODUCTION_PLAN §fingerprints. **Recommendation:** move them into one `browser-profiles.json` (read by JS, `//go:embed` in Go).

## 7. Known production gaps (roll-up)

- Single source of truth for browser profiles: the profile data lives in 6+ files (Go, JS worker, UA sync, Proxy Manager renderer/html, IPC whitelists). '__get_profiles__' exists but the app never uses it. Generate from one JSON manifest or query the worker.
- UA, client hints and header set are not aligned with the TLS profile. sec-ch-ua / sec-ch-ua-platform are never rewritten, so Chromium 132 hints go out with a Chrome 133 Windows UA, and Chromium-only headers go out with Firefox/Safari TLS. Electron's full Chrome build number (132.0.6834.210) is sent instead of the reduced 132.0.0.0. The TLS template alone does not change the UA.
- Release packaging is not multi-arch safe. CI builds the Go worker only for the runner arch while producing both x64 and arm64 mac dmg. There is no universal/lipo Go binary, no win32-arm64 worker, the binaries are unsigned and un-notarized, stray binaries (azuretls-worker-test, azuretls-go/azuretls-go-worker) and 58MB of legacy dylibs get packaged, and extraResources ships every platform's binaries.
- No streaming or size limit for upstream responses (whole body base64 in one NDJSON line), no per-request cancellation from MITM into Go (timeouts and aborted client sockets leave Go requests running), and requestId is not propagated.
- No worker health checks or heartbeat. A worker that is alive but wedged (e.g. a blocked clear) is never restarted; only process exit triggers a restart. There is no protocol/version handshake, so a stale binary is used silently.
- Upstream certificate verification is disabled everywhere (InsecureSkipVerify / rejectUnauthorized:false). There is no opt-in strict mode and no pinning.
- The native Node fallback (_nativeHttpsFallback) should be removed or made to honour the proxy, the egress block and the fingerprint.
- The legacy FFI path (azure-tls-worker.js, azuretls/, ensure-cupnet-node, the after-pack hook, ffi optionalDependencies, the missing azuretls-*.js scripts in package.json, and the setup:ffi script pointing to ../greengo22) should be deleted or quarantined. Dangling references include the missing azure-tls-inprocess.js.
- No automated fingerprint regression test (JA3/JA4/Akamai/header order per template against a local or remote echo). test-fingerprints.js is broken and targets the legacy worker.
- ensure-cupnet-node.mjs downloads Node without SHA256 verification. The prebuilt CFFI libraries have no reproducible build script in the repo.
- Timezone and language fingerprinting are incomplete: timezone is applied only when CDP is attached, and the per-tab profile UA is overridden by the global activeFingerprint whenever CDP logging attaches (Emulation.setUserAgentOverride without userAgentMetadata).
- Connection lifetime differs from a real browser: pools are evicted after 25s idle, measured from the last borrow, which causes extra TLS handshakes compared with Chrome's connection reuse.
- Security boundary: remove the privileged electronAPI from preload-view.js for non-internal origins. Expose a minimal bridge only when location is a known internal file:// page, and ALSO validate event.senderFrame.url main-side for new-tab channels (get-cookies, clear-cookies, remove-cookie, ext-proxy:*, get-direct-ip, get-proxy-profiles, connect-proxy-template, set-tab-proxy, set-tab-cookie-group, isolate-tab, get-tabs, set-ui-pref, navigate-to with file://)
- Settings page needs its own privileged bridge (a trusted preload for the settings tab or a separate trusted window) after the WIP change to preload-view.js
- CSP: add meta CSP to new-tab.html, cupnet-guide.html, site-info-popover.html and modal-logging.html by moving inline scripts to files. Drop 'unsafe-inline' script in omnibox-overlay.html. Consider sandbox:true for session-profile-modal (its preload only uses contextBridge/ipcRenderer)
- Overlays: move the credential fill popup and shell toasts into an overlay surface (a WebContentsView like the omnibox, a child window, or reserved space) so they are visible above web content
- Status bar performance: event-driven updates instead of a 3s poll that fires 3 IPCs plus synchronous ps/PowerShell. Cache metrics in main, run getPidMetrics asynchronously, and poll the resources drawer only while it is open
- browser-renderer.js is a 3.4k-line global-scope script. Split it into modules (omnibox, tabs, status bar, credentials, switcher) with unit tests; there is currently no renderer test coverage
- Accessibility: tabs are divs without role=tab/tablist or keyboard focus; the omnibox list lives in another WebContentsView, so aria-controls points to an empty list; status drawers need focus management
- i18n/consistency: a single source for the shortcut list (menu accelerators → keyboard-shortcuts.js → new-tab cheat sheet → guide), a CI check that cupnet-guide.html matches scripts/guide-i18n, and 4-language parity checks
- Favicons were removed (favicon-cache.js deleted in the WIP), so tabs and omnibox show emoji/SVG fallbacks only. The CSS .tab-favicon-img/.site-info-favicon/.item-favicon is left unused
- The Credential save bar 'Never' has no persistence (no never-save list)
- Only the dark theme: the shell forces color-scheme dark although a uiTheme ui-pref exists (used by settings)
- Launch Profile modal should round-trip unknown or unsupported fields (tab.isolated/cookieGroupId, proxy.ja3/profileName) or show them as read-only instead of silently dropping them
- Dev-browser ergonomics: treat localhost[:port], single-label hosts and IPv6 literals as URLs in the omnibox and utils.resolveNavigationUrl
- Packaging: build.files is '**/*', which ships tests/, docs/, scripts/ and the legacy electron-app/ into the app (not browser-ui specific, but it bloats the internal pages' asar)
- No code signing or notarization. Mac uses identity null with no hardened runtime or notarization (Gatekeeper reports the downloaded app as damaged). Windows NSIS has no Authenticode certificate (SmartScreen). No CSC_LINK or APPLE_ID secrets are wired into build-all.yml.
- No Electron fuses and no ASAR integrity. RunAsNode stays enabled, so anyone can run the app binary as Node, and ASAR integrity and OnlyLoadAppFromAsar are off. Configure electronFuses after upgrading to electron-builder 26.
- Packaging uses a denylist ('**/*'). Switch to an explicit allowlist (main.js, main-process/**, preload*.js, *.html, *-renderer.js, required root modules, icons/**, data/**, assets). Drop the asarUnpack of all node_modules/** and unpack only native .node files and the worker binary. The earlier build was 509MB unpacked, with 101MB in app.asar.unpacked and a 111MB bundled node.
- Per-arch mac artifacts: build all Go worker targets (build.sh) or per-arch runners, and add ${arch} to artifactName or build a universal binary. Add a post-pack check that Resources/azuretls-go/bin/<expected name> exists and runs (__init__ handshake).
- The release pipeline runs no tests. build-all.yml has no needs: on unit or e2e jobs, no SBOM or SHA256SUMS, no provenance attestation, and no workflow timeouts or concurrency groups. The pr-checks workflow has no timeout-minutes, so e2e can hang for 6h.
- CI tests run only on macOS. There is no Windows or Linux test matrix, no lint (no ESLint/Prettier config), no type check (checkJs or TS), no coverage threshold, no `npm audit` / CodeQL / gitleaks gate, and no Dependabot or Renovate config.
- E2E depends on public services (httpbin.org, ipinfo.io, example.com). Add a local HTTPS fixture server (self-signed, trusted via the MITM path) so e2e is deterministic and can run offline in CI.
- No auto-update: publish: github produces app-update.yml, but there is no electron-updater dependency or update UI. The draft release has to be published manually, and generate_release_notes is the only changelog.
- Test/debug escape hatches are active in production builds: CUPNET_E2E and CUPNET_SKIP_PROFILE_GATE skip the profile gate and single-instance lock, globalThis.__cupnetAppContext is always exposed, and CUPNET_ALLOW_LEGACY_NODE_WORKER is honored. Gate all of them on !app.isPackaged.
- Repo hygiene: remove the tracked .DS_Store, img2.png, _debug_eof_*.js, debug-main.js, electron-app/app.js (legacy monolith) and the deprecated gen-register-main-ipc.cjs. Ignore .DS_Store and azuretls-go/azuretls-go-worker. Move the 60MB of backups (zip/bundle) and _debug dumps out of the working tree. Commit data/common-passwords-10k.txt.
- Dev portability: the POSIX-only npm scripts (start, debug, test:go, test:db), install.sh installing the x64 app from dist/mac, and run-all.sh are bash- and macOS-centric.
- ensure-cupnet-node downloads from nodejs.org without SHASUMS256 or signature verification (supply chain risk). This goes away once the bundled Node is removed.
- The stability-baseline artifact dumps raw CUPNET_* env values. Redact anything matching TOKEN, KEY, PASS, PROXY_URL.
- No crash reporting or telemetry pipeline (only critical.log and sysLog). No documented release checklist.
- IPC trust boundary is incomplete: none of the in-scope handlers (misc, launcher, settings-toolbar, diagnostics, tracking) use guardIpcHandler/guardIpcListener. Security-sensitive channels (save-security-settings, import-settings-json, reset-settings-to-defaults, set-homepage, save-traffic-opts, save-filter-patterns, get-console-logs-db, save-console-log*, run-ivac-scout, get-direct-ip) should be restricted to trusted shells. preload-view.js (every website) should expose only what new-tab.html strictly needs, ideally only when location is the internal file:// new-tab page.
- No navigation hardening for trusted windows: there is no app-level 'web-contents-created' hook adding will-navigate / setWindowOpenHandler deny rules for shell and tool windows. If a tool window (notes markdown, log viewer response preview) navigates to a remote URL, that page gets the full preload.js API and stays in _trustedShellIds.
- No crash and health handling: no 'render-process-gone' or 'child-process-gone' handlers, no crashReporter, and uncaughtException is swallowed (the process keeps running in an unknown state). There is no watchdog for a hung MITM worker beyond the stats gauges.
- No auto-update (build.publish=github but no electron-updater), no code signing or notarization (mac identity null, hardenedRuntime false, Windows unsigned), no app.setAppUserModelId on Windows (the Notification import is unused), and no protocol or open-url handling in second-instance.
- Packaging hygiene: build.files='**/*' ships tests/, scripts/, docs, electron-app/app.js (dead 5.9k LOC), debug-main.js, data/, todo.md and the Go sources inside the asar. asarUnpack 'node_modules/**' unpacks every dependency.
- Certificate validation is effectively disabled end to end (Chromium verify proc returns 0 for all certs on all sessions including defaultSession, and the Go worker uses InsecureSkipVerify). For production the proc should accept only certificates chained to the in-memory CupNet CA (compare the fingerprint) and return -3 (default verification) otherwise. The upstream worker should verify by default, with a per-rule opt-out.
- Data-at-rest gaps versus the new encryption work: console_logs stores all stdout/stderr in plaintext; ext-ports.json holds the external-port login and password in plaintext without secure-fs; window-state.json, ext-ports.json, critical.log and mitm-ca are global, not per-profile; the CA private key is stored unencrypted (0600).
- Main-thread blocking: os-process-metrics uses execSync (ps or powershell, up to 2s per PID) on every get-app-metrics call. The main window calls it on every update-log-status tick and the Settings > Performance tab every 3s. ui-prefs-store writes synchronously with fsync on every set-ui-pref. confirmExitDialog uses showMessageBoxSync during OS shutdown or logout.
- Settings robustness: a corrupt settings.json is silently replaced by defaults (onboardingComplete=false) and overwritten on the next save, with no .bak backup. There is no real schema migration framework (CURRENT_SETTINGS_VERSION=1). Pending saves are cancelled instead of flushed on quit.
- Test coverage gaps for the runtime lifecycle, IPC scope consistency and settings persistence (see tests). There is no lint step in CI for undefined ctx keys.
- Observability: sys-log 'sys-log-entry' live push is never wired (registerSysLogWindow is unused). critical.log grows without bound on each exit because flushOnExit appends all error and critical entries again with no rotation.
- No retention/size limits for requests, ws_events, screenshots (full-resolution PNG BLOBs) or browser_events; only console_logs is purged (14 days). No VACUUM/auto_vacuum, no DB size indicator, no per-session size stats.
- Encryption at rest is partial even after fixing the leak: url/host (query tokens), ws_events.payload, browser_events.detail (console args, localStorage values), screenshots, console_logs, requests.error, sessions.proxy_info stay plaintext. There is no PRAGMA secure_delete, so plaintext remnants from before the mode switch survive, and old plaintext rows are never migrated or re-encrypted.
- No key id / vault_id stored per encrypted row, no 'redacted' or 'decrypt_failed' flags, and no UI indication. Multi-profile use and lock/unlock periods produce silently empty or redacted rows.
- No FTS strategy for encrypted bodies (after the leak fix, body search only works for plaintext rows); the FTS update trigger is not column-scoped.
- Exports (HAR, bundle, site ZIP) and compare-run run synchronously on the main process that also hosts MITM: no worker_threads, no progress, no cancellation. Bundle and site ZIP build everything in memory.
- Logging/export IPC is not protected by ipc-trusted-sender (guardIpcHandler). open-log-directory accepts an arbitrary renderer path for shell.openPath. browser-storage-activity accepts unauthenticated, unthrottled payloads from any website.
- HAR 1.2 compliance: time/timings.wait can be -1, entries are newest-first, no pageref, empty cookies arrays, empty redirectURL for 3xx, startedDateTime is the completion time, the CDP <base64|...> body marker is not decoded.
- Test coverage gaps: nothing for bundle-utils, diff-utils, site-snapshot-export, cdp-network-logging, mitm-cdp-dedup, screenshot-service, the IPC handlers, or encrypt-mode end-to-end (insert -> DB -> export). e2e always runs with a locked vault.
- Write-queue drops are only logged; there is no UI surfacing or backpressure (stabilityMetrics has gauges, but the logging UI does not show dropped rows).
